Aur yaad rakho, KOH ne sirf carbon dioxide sokhi thi, oxygen nahi. Isliye yeh prayog carbon dioxide ki hi baat karta hai.
Screen par: Sawaal ka dabba: 'yeh nateeja (conclusion) kaise nikla?'; clue: dono hisson ki tulna ki table; 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath bada ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk); table bharti hai: ek hi patti (dono), ek hi paudha (dono), roshni (dono), chlorophyll (dono), CO₂ (bahar haan, andar nahi, KOH ki wajah se), starch (bahar haan, andar nahi); ek hi khaana badla to nateeja badla; akeli nali wali patti par 'shak'; bahar wali patti par 'tulna' ka label; galat soch 'nali ne roshni roki' par kaata nishaan, kaanch ke aar-paar roshni ke teer; KOH sirf CO₂ pakadta, O₂ ko chhod deta.
Kitaab se bahar: Bahar wale hisse se tulna kyun zaroori hai; kaanch ki nali se roshni pahunchti hai; KOH oxygen nahi sokhta.
Jawab: Dono hisse ek hi patti ke, ek hi paudhe par, ek jaisi roshni mein the, aur dono mein chlorophyll tha; farq sirf CO₂ ka tha, kyunki nali ke andar KOH ne CO₂ sokh li. Starch sirf CO₂ wale hisse mein bana, isliye CO₂ zaroori hai. Bahar wala hissa tulna ka kaam karta hai: woh dikhata hai ki isi patti mein starch ban sakta tha.
Aam galti: Galat soch: nali ne roshni rok di, ya KOH ne oxygen hatayi. Nali kaanch ki thi, roshni pahunchi; KOH sirf CO₂ sokhta hai. Bahar wale hisse ke bina akela negative nateeja kuch saabit nahi karta.
p004-i2 Can you explain how this conclusion could be drawn?
sawaal11.1-k8
Ab teeno prayog ek saath rakh kar dekho. Variegated leaf ke safed hisse ko roshni aur carbon dioxide mili, par chlorophyll nahi tha. Black paper ke neeche wale hisse mein chlorophyll tha, par roshni nahi thi. Aur nali ke andar wale hisse mein chlorophyll aur roshni dono the, par carbon dioxide nahi thi. Yaani har prayog mein sirf ek cheez hatayi gayi, aur baaki sab waisa hi rakha gaya. Ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badal kar baaki sab barabar rakhne wali is tulna ko controlled comparison kehte hain. Rasoi mein bhi hum yahi karte: kabhi aag bujhao, kabhi tawa hatao, kabhi aata, aur har baar dekho ki roti bani ya nahi. Agar ek saath aag bhi bujha do aur aata bhi hata do, aur roti na bane, toh kya bataoge? Aag aur aata ek saath hataye, toh aap bata hi nahi paoge ki roti kis kami se nahi bani. Aksar log sochte hain ki photosynthesis ke liye bas yahi teen cheezein chahiye. Par aisa nahi hai: paani bhi utna hi zaroori hai. Toh paani ko bhi aise hata kar kyun nahi parakhte? Paani ko aise hata kar parakhna mushkil hai, kyunki paani ke bina patti khud murjha jaati hai. Phir starch na bane, toh pata nahi chalega ki starch paani ki kami se nahi bana, ya patti ke murjhaane se. Paani ka asli kaam kya hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Teen pattiyan ek line mein: variegated (safed hissa: roshni haan, CO₂ haan, chlorophyll nahi), black paper (chlorophyll haan, roshni nahi), nali (chlorophyll haan, roshni haan, CO₂ nahi); har ek mein sirf ek khaana laal; label: controlled comparison; rasoi ke teen chitra: aag bujhi, tawa gaayab, aata gaayab; phir ek saath aag aur aata gaayab par sawaal ka nishaan; galat soch 'bas teen cheezein' par kaata nishaan, saath mein paani ki boond; murjhaayi patti par '?'; paani ki boond par 'aage'.
Terms: controlled comparison = Ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badal kar baaki sab barabar rakhne wali tulna.
Kitaab se bahar: Rasoi mein ek saath do cheezein hatane ki misaal; paani ko is tarah kyun nahi parakhte (patti murjha jaati hai).
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.1-k9
Ab aaj ki poori baat ek saath jodte hain. Patti mein bana khana starch bankar jama hota hai, aur iodine use neela-kaala karke dikha deta hai. Purana starch hatane ke liye paudhe ko pehle andhere mein rakhte hain, aur patti ka chlorophyll alcohol se nikaal kar test karte hain. Variegated leaf ne dikhaya ki chlorophyll zaroori hai, black paper ne dikhaya ki roshni, aur KOH wali nali ne dikhaya ki CO₂. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: jo khana dikhta hi nahi, uske baare mein hum itne pakke kaise hain, aur patti ko kya-kya chahiye? Jo khana dikhta nahi, uske baare mein hum pakke isliye hain, kyunki uski nishaani dikhti hai: roshni mein rakhi patti mein naya starch milta hai. Aur patti ko kya-kya chahiye, yeh humne ek-ek cheez hata kar jaana. Chlorophyll, light aur CO₂ mein se ek bhi na ho, toh starch nahi banta. Bilkul rasoi ki tarah: aag, tawa aur aata mein se ek bhi na ho, toh dabbe mein nayi roti nahi milti. Par ek samay aisa bhi tha, jab kisi ko pata hi nahi tha ki hawa mein alag-alag gases hoti hain. Tab log dekhte the ki band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai. Usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga? Jar mein hara paudha rakhne par kya hoga, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Ek line mein kahani: patti mein sugar se starch ke daane, iodine se neela-kaala; andhera kamra (destarching), alcohol mein patti ka rang utarna; teen pattiyan: variegated se chlorophyll, black paper se roshni, KOH nali se CO₂; shuru ka sawaal phir: khana dikhta nahi, par neela-kaala nishaan dikhta hai; rasoi: aag + tawa + aata se dabbe mein nayi roti, ek bhi gaayab to dabba khaali; phir purane zamaane ki prayogshala: band kaanch ka jar, jalti mombatti bujhti; jar mein hara paudha rakhte hi bada sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.2 EARLY EXPERIMENTS · 14 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 31 min
Maqsad: Band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai; usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun?
Misaal: jasoos ki file: photosynthesis ek raaz, har vaigyanik ek suraag jodta hai (suraag = prayog ka nateeja); Priestley: hara paudha band hawa theek karta hai; Ingenhousz: sirf roshni mein, aur oxygen ke bulbule sirf hare hisson se; Sachs: glucose chloroplast wale hare hisson mein; Engelmann: neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada kaam ki; oxygen par do shaq, CO₂ aur paani; van Niel ka saancha (A ki jagah oxygen ya sulphur) se faisla: oxygen paani ki; isotope ka nishaan ungliyon ke nishaan jaisa pakka saboot
Kitab ki baatein (103): kitab kehti hai 34 · shabd ka matlab 32 · pehle ki zaroori baat 18 · figure ka nishaan 4 · teacher ki extra baat 15
f1 kitab kehti hai Saral prayogon ki ek lambi kadi ne dheere-dheere photosynthesis ki samajh banayi; in prayogon ke baare mein jaanna dilchasp hai.
f2 kitab kehti hai Joseph Priestley (1733-1804) ne 1770 mein prayogon ki ek series ki, jisne hare paudhon ke badhne mein hawa ki zaroori bhoomika dikhayi.
f3 kitab kehti hai Priestley ne 1774 mein oxygen khoji.
f4 shabd ka matlab: oxygen Oxygen: hawa ki woh gas jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai; formula O₂.
f5 teacher ki extra baat Prayog 1770 mein hue aur oxygen 1774 mein khoji gayi; yaani prayog ke waqt Priestley ko us gas ka naam bhi pata nahi tha.
f6 kitab kehti hai: bell jar Band jagah, yaani bell jar, mein jalti mombatti jaldi bujh jaati hai; band jagah mein chooha bhi jaldi suffocate ho jaata hai.
f7 kitab kehti hai Priestley ka nateeja: jalti mombatti aur saans lene wala jaanwar, dono kisi tarah hawa ko kharab karte hain.
f8 kitab kehti hai: mint Usi bell jar mein mint (pudina) ka paudha rakhne par chooha zinda raha aur mombatti jalti rahi.
f9 kitab kehti hai Priestley ki hypothesis: saans lene wale jaanwar aur jalti mombatti hawa se jo hatate hain, paudhe use hawa mein restore karte hain, yaani lauta dete hain.
f10 shabd ka matlab: bell jar Bell jar: ghanti jaisa ulta kaanch ka bartan jo andar ki hawa ko bahar ki hawa se alag, band rakhta hai; bell = ghanti, jar = bartan.
f11 shabd ka matlab: suffocate Suffocate: saans ke liye zaroori hawa na milne se dam ghutna.
f12 shabd ka matlab: hypothesis Hypothesis: dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye diya gaya vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake; hypothesised = aisa vichaar saamne rakha.
f13 shabd ka matlab: restore Restore: jo ghat gaya ya hat gaya, use phir se bhar dena, lauta dena.
f14 pehle ki zaroori baat Mombatti ka jalna aur jaanwar ki saans, dono ke liye hawa chahiye; dono hawa ki oxygen kharch karte hain.
f15 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.1 mein Priestley ka prayog chaar chitron mein hai: upar ki line bina paudhe ke, neeche ki line paudhe ke saath; dono lines ko jodne wala koi teer nahi.
f16 figure ka nishaan: (a) Chitra (a): band bell jar mein chooha aur jalti mombatti, paudha nahi; teer daayi taraf (b) tak.
f17 figure ka nishaan: (b) Chitra (b): bina paudhe ke jar mein mombatti bujh gayi aur chooha gira pada.
f18 figure ka nishaan: (c) Chitra (c): patton wala gamle ka paudha, chooha aur jalti mombatti ek saath band; teer daayi taraf (d) tak.
f19 figure ka nishaan: (d) Chitra (d): paudha wahin, chooha seedha khada, mombatti ab bhi jal rahi.
f20 teacher ki extra baat Upar aur neeche ki line ek ke baad ek hone wali ghatna nahi, do alag jar hain jinki tulna hai; dono mein farq sirf paudhe ka; chitra ka naam Priestley's experiment.
f21 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: Priestley ne mombatti aur paudhe se prayog kaise kiya hoga? Kuch din baad mombatti jalti hai ya nahi, yeh dekhne ke liye use phir jalana (rekindle) padta; set-up ko chhede bina mombatti kitne tareekon se jalayi ja sakti hai?
f22 shabd ka matlab: rekindle Rekindle: bujhi cheez ko phir se jalana; re = phir se, kindle = jalana.
f23 teacher ki extra baat Jar khole bina mombatti jalane ke tareeke: lens se dhoop ko batti par ek bindu mein jama karna; andar lage patle taar mein bahar se bijli bhej kar use laal-garam karna; andar do taaron ke beech bijli ki chingari.
f24 teacher ki extra baat Jar khola toh bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaayegi, aur pata nahi chalega ki mombatti paudhe ki wajah se jali ya taazi hawa se.
f25 kitab kehti hai Jan Ingenhousz (1730-1799) ne Priestley jaisa set-up ek baar andhere mein aur ek baar dhoop (sunlight) mein rakha.
f26 kitab kehti hai Unhone dikhaya ki jalti mombatti ya saans lete jaanwar se kharab hui hawa ko paudha jis kaam se saaf karta hai, us kaam ke liye sunlight zaroori hai.
f27 shabd ka matlab: purifies the air Purifies the air: hawa ko phir se aisa bana dena ki mombatti jal sake aur jaanwar saans le sake; purify = saaf karna.
f28 teacher ki extra baat Priestley ke prayog kabhi chalte, kabhi nahi; Ingenhousz ke prayog ne bataya ki farq roshni ka tha.
f29 teacher ki extra baat Paudhe din-raat saans lete hain aur oxygen kharch karte hain; roshni mein unka oxygen banana kharch se zyada hota hai, andhere mein sirf kharch hota hai.
f30 kitab kehti hai: aquatic plant Ingenhousz ne ek aquatic plant se prayog kiya: tez dhoop mein hare hisson ke aas-paas chhote bulbule (bubbles) bane, andhere mein nahi.
f31 kitab kehti hai: green parts Baad mein unhone pehchana ki yeh bulbule oxygen ke the; isse dikha ki sirf paudhe ke hare hisse (green parts) oxygen chhod sakte hain.
f32 shabd ka matlab: aquatic plant Aquatic plant: paani mein ugne aur rehne wala paudha; aqua = paani; jaise Hydrilla.
f33 shabd ka matlab: green parts Green parts: paudhe ke hare hisse, jinme chlorophyll hota hai.
f34 shabd ka matlab: bubbles Bubbles: paani ke andar gas ke chhote gole, bulbule.
f35 teacher ki extra baat Oxygen ki ek aasaan pehchaan: sulagti hui teeli oxygen mein bhabhak kar jal uthti hai.
f36 pehle ki zaroori baat Chlorophyll patti ke hare hisson mein hota hai; variegated patti mein starch sirf hare hisson mein bana tha.
f37 kitab kehti hai Lagbhag 1854 mein Julius von Sachs ne saboot diya ki paudhe badhte waqt glucose banate hain; glucose aksar starch bankar jama hota hai.
f38 kitab kehti hai: chloroplast Sachs ke baad ke prayogon ne dikhaya ki hara padarth (chlorophyll) cells ke andar khaas daanon mein hota hai, jinhe baad mein chloroplast kaha gaya.
f39 kitab kehti hai Sachs ne paaya ki glucose paudhe ke hare hisson mein banta hai, aur aksar starch bankar jama hota hai.
f40 shabd ka matlab: chloroplast Chloroplast: plant cell ke andar chlorophyll rakhne wale chhote hisse; chloro = hara, plast = cell ke andar bana chhota hissa.
f41 pehle ki zaroori baat Glucose ek meethi sugar hai jisse cells ko kaam ki energy milti hai; bahut saare glucose judkar starch bante hain.
f42 kitab kehti hai T.W. Engelmann (1843-1909) ne prism se roshni ko uske spectral components mein toda, aur woh rang green alga Cladophora par daale, jo aerobic bacteria ke suspension mein rakhi thi.
f43 kitab kehti hai Bacteria se pata chalaya gaya ki O₂ evolution, yaani oxygen ka nikalna, kahan ho raha hai.
f44 kitab kehti hai Bacteria sabse zyada neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson mein jama hue; is tarah photosynthesis ka pehla action spectrum bana.
f45 kitab kehti hai Yeh action spectrum mota-mota chlorophyll a aur b ke absorption spectra jaisa hai; inhe aage pigments wale hisse mein dekhenge.
f46 shabd ka matlab: spectral components Spectral components: safed roshni ke alag-alag rang; spectrum = rangon ki patti.
f47 shabd ka matlab: suspension Suspension: aisa mishran jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas paani mein tairte rehte hain.
f48 shabd ka matlab: aerobic bacteria Aerobic bacteria: aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye; aero = hawa; isliye woh wahin jama hote hain jahan oxygen zyada ho.
f49 shabd ka matlab: O₂ evolution O₂ evolution: oxygen ka banna aur bahar nikalna; yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi.
f50 shabd ka matlab: action spectrum Action spectrum: kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, uska naqsha; action = kaam.
f51 shabd ka matlab: absorption spectra Absorption spectra: kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; absorb = sokhna, spectra = spectrum ka bahuvachan.
f52 pehle ki zaroori baat Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai; prism, yaani kaanch ka tikona tukda, use indradhanush jaise rangon mein tod deta hai (Class 10).
f53 pehle ki zaroori baat Alga: paani mein rehne wale saral hare jeev, jo photosynthesis karte hain; Cladophora dhaage jaisi lambi hari alga hai.
f54 teacher ki extra baat Hari roshni wale hisse ke paas bahut kam bacteria jama hue.
f55 kitab kehti hai 19th century ke beech tak paudhe ke photosynthesis ki khaas baatein pata thi: paudhe roshni ki energy se CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate banate hain.
f56 kitab kehti hai Oxygen chhodne wale jeevon ke poore kaam ko ek empirical equation se dikhaya gaya.
f57 shabd ka matlab: empirical equation Empirical equation: dekh kar aur naap kar likhi equation, jo sirf yeh batati hai ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, beech ke kadam nahi.
f58 pehle ki zaroori baat Carbohydrate: carbon, hydrogen aur oxygen se bane khane ke padarth, jaise glucose aur starch.
f59 kitab kehti hai Equation: CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂; [CH₂O] carbohydrate ko dikhata hai, jaise glucose, jo chhe carbon wali sugar hai.
f60 shabd ka matlab: [CH₂O] [CH₂O]: carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket, koi asli alag molecule nahi; aisi chhe ikaiyan milkar glucose C₆H₁₂O₆.
f61 pehle ki zaroori baat Chemical equation mein baayi taraf shuru ki cheezein, daayi taraf bani cheezein; teer ke upar Light batata hai ki kaam roshni se chalta hai.
f62 kitab kehti hai Microbiologist Cornelius van Niel (1897-1985) ne purple aur green bacteria par apne kaam se dikhaya ki photosynthesis asal mein ek light-dependent reaction hai.
f63 kitab kehti hai Is reaction mein kisi suitable oxidisable compound ka hydrogen CO₂ ko reduce karke carbohydrate bana deta hai.
f64 shabd ka matlab: microbiologist Microbiologist: bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik; micro = bahut chhota, bio = jeevan.
f65 shabd ka matlab: oxidisable compound Oxidisable compound: aisa yaugik jo apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake.
f66 shabd ka matlab: reduce Reduce: kisi molecule mein hydrogen judna; yahan hydrogen judne se CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai.
f67 shabd ka matlab: light-dependent Light-dependent: jo roshni ke bina na chale.
f68 pehle ki zaroori baat Oxidation: kisi cheez se hydrogen nikalna; reduction: kisi cheez mein hydrogen judna (Class 10).
f69 pehle ki zaroori baat Purple aur green bacteria roshni se photosynthesis karke apna khana banate hain.
f70 kitab kehti hai Van Niel ki aam equation: 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O.
f71 shabd ka matlab: H₂A H₂A: koi bhi hydrogen dene wala yaugik; A ki jagah alag-alag cheez aa sakti hai.
f72 shabd ka matlab: A A: hydrogen dene ke baad donor ka jo hissa bachta hai, uska sanket; paudhon mein oxygen, sulphur bacteria mein sulphur.
f73 shabd ka matlab: hydrogen donor Hydrogen donor: CO₂ ko reduce karne ke liye hydrogen dene wali cheez; donor = dene wala.
f74 kitab kehti hai Hare paudhon mein H₂O hydrogen donor hai, aur woh oxidise hokar O₂ banta hai.
f75 kitab kehti hai: H₂S Kuch jeev photosynthesis mein O₂ nahi chhodte: purple aur green sulphur bacteria mein H₂S hydrogen donor hai, aur oxidation ka nateeja jeev ke hisaab se sulphur ya sulphate hota hai.
f76 shabd ka matlab: H₂S H₂S: hydrogen sulphide, sade ande jaisi badboo wali gas; do hydrogen aur ek sulphur.
f77 teacher ki extra baat Sulphate: sulphur aur oxygen ke atomon se bana ek kan.
f78 kitab kehti hai Bacteria mein nateeja sulphur ya sulphate hai, O₂ nahi; isliye van Niel ne infer kiya ki hare paudhe ki O₂ H₂O se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi.
f79 kitab kehti hai Yeh baad mein radioisotopic techniques se saabit hua; isliye photosynthesis ki sahi equation alag likhi gayi.
f80 shabd ka matlab: infer Infer: seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar nateeja nikalna; inferred = aisa nateeja nikala.
f81 teacher ki extra baat Aksar log sochte hain ki paudhe ki O₂ CO₂ se aati hai, kyunki CO₂ ka carbon khane mein chala jaata hai; par O₂ paani se aati hai.
f82 teacher ki extra baat Agar O₂ CO₂ se aati, toh CO₂ lene wale sulphur bacteria bhi O₂ chhodte; woh nahi chhodte, isliye oxygen ka srot paani hai.
f83 kitab kehti hai Sahi equation: 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂.
f84 shabd ka matlab: C₆H₁₂O₆ C₆H₁₂O₆: glucose ka formula; ek molecule mein 6 carbon, 12 hydrogen aur 6 oxygen atom.
f85 kitab kehti hai C₆H₁₂O₆ glucose hai; nikalne wali O₂ paani se aati hai, yeh radio isotope techniques se saabit hua.
f86 shabd ka matlab: radioisotopic Radioisotopic techniques: nishaan wale isotope ka peechha karke dekhna ki atom kahan gaya; isotope = ek hi element ka alag wazan wala atom.
f87 shabd ka matlab: isotopic tracing Isotopic tracing: isotope ke nishaan se atomon ka raasta pata karna.
f88 teacher ki extra baat 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ne hari alga ko oxygen-18 wala paani diya; nishaan wala oxygen chhodi gayi O₂ mein mila, CO₂ par nishaan lagane par nahi; oxygen-18 bhaari, sthir isotope hai, radioactive nahi.
f89 pehle ki zaroori baat Chapter ki shuruaat mein Calvin ne C¹⁴ ko nishaan bana kar carbon ka peechha kiya tha; ek hi element ke atom neutron ki ginti mein alag ho sakte hain (isotope).
f90 kitab kehti hai Yeh equation ek akeli reaction nahi, balki photosynthesis naam ke kai kadamon wale kaam (multistep process) ka saar hai.
f91 shabd ka matlab: multistep process Multistep process: kai jude kadamon mein poora hone wala kaam; multi = kai, step = kadam.
f92 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: is equation mein baarah paani ke molecule kyun liye gaye?
f93 teacher ki extra baat 6O₂ mein 12 oxygen atom hain aur sab paani se aate hain; ek H₂O mein ek oxygen atom, isliye 12H₂O.
f94 teacher ki extra baat 12H₂O ke 24 hydrogen mein se 12 glucose mein jaate hain, baaki 12 CO₂ ke 6 oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani banate hain; CO₂ ke baaki 6 oxygen glucose mein jaate hain.
f95 teacher ki extra baat Net kharch 6 paani ka (12 gaye, 6 naye bane); par dono taraf likhne se dikhta hai ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai aur daayi taraf ka paani naya hai.
f96 pehle ki zaroori baat Experiments evidence dete hain: prayog ka nateeja kisi explanation ke paksh ya khilaaf saboot banta hai.
f97 pehle ki zaroori baat Hawa (air) mein different gases hoti hain, jaise oxygen, nitrogen aur carbon dioxide.
f98 pehle ki zaroori baat Observation pehle ho sakta hai aur uske cause ki identification baad mein: Priestley ne 1770 mein asar dekha, aur gas 1774 mein pehchani gayi.
f99 pehle ki zaroori baat Closed jar bahar ki air se freely exchange nahi karta: andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se nahi milti.
f100 pehle ki zaroori baat Figure mein sequence arrow, yaani teer, ka meaning hai ki ek haalat ke baad doosri haalat aati hai.
f101 pehle ki zaroori baat Burning (mombatti ka jalna) aur respiration (saans) dono surrounding air par depend karte hain.
f102 pehle ki zaroori baat Jar ki opening se bahar ki air andar aa jaati hai, yaani air exchange ho jaata hai.
f103 pehle ki zaroori baat Fair comparison mein baaki conditions similar rakhi jaati hain aur sirf ek cheez badli jaati hai (11.1).
pehle poori jhalak11.2-k1
Pichhli baar humne dekha ki patti ka bana khana starch bankar jama hota hai, aur iodine use neela-kaala kar deta hai. Teen prayogon se pata chala ki khana banane ke liye patti ko chlorophyll, roshni aur CO₂, teeno chahiye. Yaad rakhein: baaki sab ek jaisa rakh kar sirf ek cheez badlo, tabhi nateeja usi cheez ke naam likha ja sakta hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki dhai sau saal pehle ke vaigyanikon ne bhi isi chaal se ek bada raaz khola. Pichhli baar humne ek sawaal khula chhoda tha: band kaanch ke jar mein jalti mombatti kuch der mein bujh jaati hai. Ab usi jar mein ek hara paudha bhi rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun? Jar mein hara paudha rakhne par kya hota hai, yeh ek prayog ne dikhaya. Par iska kyun poora samajhne mein vaigyanikon ko dedh sau saal se zyada lage. Yeh samajh kisi ek din mein nahi bani. Chhote, saral prayogon ki ek lambi kadi ne dheere-dheere photosynthesis ki tasveer poori ki. Isliye aaj ki kahani ek jasoos ki file jaisi hai: raaz ek hai, aur har vaigyanik usme ek naya suraag jodta hai. Suraag yaani kisi prayog ka nateeja, jo raaz ka ek tukda khol deta hai. Is file mein hum paanch vaigyanikon ke suraag jodenge: Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann aur van Niel. Aur aakhir mein in suraagon se photosynthesis ka poora hisaab ek equation mein likhenge.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: patti par iodine girte hi starch neela-kaala; teen pattiyan ek line mein (variegated, black paper, KOH wali nali) aur unke neeche chlorophyll, roshni, CO₂; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: ek baar mein sirf ek cheez badlo'. Phir band kaanch ka jar, jalti mombatti dheere-dheere bujhti; jar ke bagal mein ek hara paudha aur sawaal ka dabba 'kya hoga, aur kyun?'. Phir ek purani jasoos ki file khulti hai: upar 'Raaz', andar paanch panne jin par naam Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann, van Niel; aakhri panne par khaali equation; kone mein 'suraag = prayog ka nateeja'.
Kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.1) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; jasoos ki file wali misaal jo poori heading ko jodti hai.
Yaad: 11.1-k9, 11.1-k8
p004-i4 It is interesting to learn about those simple experiments that led to a gradual development in our understanding of photosynthesis.
samjhana11.2-k2
Pehla suraag 1770 ka hai, England ke ek vaigyanik Joseph Priestley ke prayogon se. Priestley ka jeevan 1733 se 1804 tak raha, aur 1770 mein unhone prayogon ki ek poori series ki. In prayogon ne dikhaya ki hare paudhon ke badhne mein hawa ka bahut zaroori kaam hai. Aapne shayad yeh naam pehle suna ho: yahi Priestley 1774 mein oxygen gas ke khojkarta bane. Oxygen yaani hawa ki woh gas, jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai. Iska formula O₂ hai, yaani oxygen ke do jude atom. Dhyaan do, prayog 1770 mein hue aur oxygen 1774 mein mili. Yaani prayog karte waqt Priestley ko us gas ka naam bhi nahi pata tha. Ab prayog dekho: unhone kaanch ka ek ulta bartan liya, jo ghanti jaisa dikhta hai. Yeh bartan neeche se band hota hai, isliye andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se mil nahi paati. Aise bartan ko bell jar kehte hain: bell yaani ghanti, aur jar yaani bartan. Dekho, pehle unhone bell jar mein ek jalti mombatti rakhi. Mombatti thodi der jali, phir bujh gayi. Phir unhone band jar mein ek zinda chooha rakha. Woh bhi jaldi hi saans ke liye tadapne laga. Saans ke liye hawa na milne se dam ghutne ko suffocate hona kehte hain. Socho, dono mein kya milta hai: mombatti ko jalne ke liye hawa chahiye, aur chooha ko saans ke liye. Isliye Priestley ne nateeja nikala ki jalti mombatti aur saans lene wala jaanwar, dono kisi tarah hawa ko kharab kar dete hain. Ab asli kamaal dekho: unhone usi bell jar mein pudine ka ek paudha, yaani mint plant, bhi rakh diya. Aur dekho, is baar chooha zinda raha, aur mombatti bhi jalti rahi. Hara paudha andar aisa kya kar raha tha, jisse hawa phir se kaam ki ho gayi? Hara paudha andar kya kar raha tha, iske liye Priestley ne ek vichaar rakha. Saans lete jaanwar aur jalti mombatti hawa se kuch hata dete hain. Paudhe use hawa mein restore kar dete hain, yaani lauta dete hain. Dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye aisa vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake, hypothesis kehlata hai. Priestley ne yahi hypothesis rakha. Aap shayad turant keh do ki paudha oxygen lauta raha tha. Par Priestley ke paas tab us gas ka naam tak nahi tha. Toh jasoos ki file mein pehla suraag likho: band hawa ko jo cheez kharab karti hai, hara paudha use phir theek kar deta hai.
Screen par: Saal 1770 aur Joseph Priestley ka naam (1733-1804); hare paudhe ke paas hawa ke teer. Tareekhon ki line: pehle 1770 (prayog), phir 1774 (oxygen), saath mein O₂ ke do jude atom. Ulta, ghanti jaisa kaanch ka bartan, neeche se band; label: bell = ghanti, jar = bartan. Bell jar mein jalti mombatti bujhti; phir zinda chooha tadapta, label 'suffocate = dam ghutna'; dono ke upar 'hawa kharab'. Phir usi jar mein pudine (mint) ka paudha: chooha chalta-phirta, mombatti jalti; vichaar ka bulbula 'paudha hawa ko restore karta hai (lauta deta hai)', label 'hypothesis'. Jasoos ki file ke pehle panne par suraag likha jaata: 'kharab hawa ko hara paudha theek karta hai'.
Terms: oxygen = Hawa ki woh gas jisse hum saans lete hain aur jisme mombatti jalti hai; formula O₂.; O₂ = Oxygen gas ka formula: ek molecule mein oxygen ke do atom jude hote hain.; bell jar = Ghanti jaisa ulta kaanch ka bartan jo andar ki hawa ko bahar ki hawa se alag band rakhta hai; bell = ghanti, jar = bartan.; suffocate = Saans ke liye hawa na milne se dam ghutna.; hypothesis = Dekhi hui baat ko samjhane ke liye diya gaya vichaar, jise aage prayog se parkha ja sake; hypothesised = aisa vichaar rakha.; restore = Jo hat gaya, use phir se bhar dena, lauta dena.
Kitaab se bahar: Priestley England ke the; prayog (1770) oxygen ki khoj (1774) se pehle hue, isliye tab us gas ka naam pata nahi tha; mombatti aur saans dono ko hawa chahiye.
p004-i5 Joseph Priestley (1733-1804) in 1770 performed a series of experiments that revealed the essential role of air in the growth of green plants. Priestley, you may recall, discovered oxygen in 1774.
p004-i6 Priestley observed that a candle burning in a closed space – a bell jar, soon gets extinguished (Figure 11.1 a, b, c, d). Similarly, a mouse would soon suffocate in a closed space. He concluded that a burning candle or an animal that breathe the air, both somehow, damage the air. But when he placed a mint plant in the same bell jar, he found that the mouse stayed alive and the candle continued to burn. Priestley hypothesised as follows: Plants restore to the air whatever breathing animals and burning candles remove.
figure11.2-k3
Ab Figure 11.1 dekho; isme Priestley ka yahi prayog chaar chitron mein bana hai. Pehle upar wali line dekho, usme paudha nahi hai. Chitra (a) mein band bell jar ke andar ek chooha aur ek jalti mombatti hai. Chitra (a) se ek teer daayi taraf chitra (b) tak jaata hai. Wahan mombatti bujh chuki hai, aur chooha gira pada hai. Neeche wali line mein, chitra (c) mein, wahi chooha aur jalti mombatti hain. Par saath mein patton wala ek gamle ka paudha bhi band hai. Yahan bhi teer daayi taraf chitra (d) tak jaata hai. Wahan paudha wahin hai, chooha seedha khada hai, aur mombatti ab bhi jal rahi hai. Ab ek baarik baat dekho: upar aur neeche ki line ko jodne wala koi teer nahi hai. Upar aur neeche ki line ke beech teer kyun nahi hai? Upar aur neeche ki line ke beech teer isliye nahi hai, kyunki yeh ek ke baad ek hone wali ghatna nahi hai. Yeh do alag jar hain, jinki tulna ho rahi hai. Yaad karo pichhli baar ki chaal: dono jar mein sab kuch ek jaisa hai, farq sirf paudhe ka hai. Isliye jo bhi farq dikha, mombatti ka jalte rehna aur chooha ka bach jaana, woh paudhe ki wajah se hai. Chitra ke neeche iska naam likha hai: Priestley's experiment, yaani Priestley ka prayog.
Screen par: Figure 11.1 ke chaar chitra do line mein. Upar: (a) bell jar mein chooha aur jalti mombatti, paudha nahi; teer daayi taraf (b): mombatti bujhi, chooha gira. Neeche: (c) gamle ka patton wala paudha, chooha aur jalti mombatti; teer daayi taraf (d): paudha, chooha seedha khada, mombatti jalti. Dono line ke beech ki khaali jagah par ungli, aur label 'teer nahi: do alag jar ki tulna'. Dono jar saath-saath, farq wale paudhe par gola. Neeche caption: Priestley's experiment.
Kitaab se bahar: Dono line ke beech teer na hone ka matlab: do alag jar ki tulna, ek ke baad ek ghatna nahi.
Yaad: 11.1-k8
p004-i7 Figure 11.1 shows four bell-jar panels. Upper row: (a) a mouse and a burning candle are enclosed without a plant; a rightward arrow leads to (b), showing an unlit candle and a collapsed mouse. Lower row: (c) a leafy potted plant, mouse and burning candle are enclosed together; a rightward arrow leads to (d), in which the plant remains, the mouse is upright and the candle is still burning. There is no vertical arrow connecting the two rows.
ruko, socho11.2-k4
Ab is prayog par ek mazedaar sawaal socho. Priestley ko kuch din baad dekhna tha ki band jar ki hawa mein mombatti phir se jal sakti hai ya nahi. Iske liye bujhi mombatti ko dobara jalana padta, yaani rekindle karna: re yaani phir se, aur kindle yaani jalana. Par jar kholte hi bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaati, aur poora prayog bigad jaata. Tab pata hi nahi chalta ki mombatti paudhe ki wajah se jali ya taazi hawa ki wajah se. Clue yeh hai: batti tak sirf garmi pahunchani hai, bahar ki hawa nahi. Ruko, pehle khud socho: jar ko chhede bina mombatti ko kitne tareekon se jala sakte ho? Aapne socha hoga ki dhoop ko lens se ek chhote bindu par jama karke batti par daala ja sakta hai. Kaanch ke aar-paar dhoop aaram se aa jaati hai. Aapne dekha hoga ki lens se jama ki gayi dhoop kaagaz tak ko jala deti hai. Doosra tareeka: jar ke andar batti ke paas pehle se ek patla taar laga do, aur bahar se usme bijli bhejo. Bijli se taar laal hokar garam ho jaata hai, aur batti jal uthti hai. Teesra tareeka: andar do taaron ke sire paas-paas rakho, aur unke beech bijli ki chingari chhodo. Teeno tareekon mein ek hi baat hai: garmi andar pahunchti hai, par andar ki hawa bahar ki hawa se nahi milti.
Screen par: Band bell jar mein bujhi mombatti, paudha aur chooha; calendar par kuch din. Shabd: re = phir se, kindle = jalana, rekindle. Jar ka dhakkan khulta aur bahar ki hawa andar ghusti, us par laal kaata nishaan. Clue ka dabba: 'sirf garmi andar, hawa nahi'. 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath bada ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir teen tareeke: lens se dhoop ka chamakta bindu batti par; andar laga patla taar bahar ki battery se laal hota; do taaron ke beech chingari; teeno mein jar band rehta hai.
Terms: rekindle = Bujhi cheez ko phir se jalana; re = phir se, kindle = jalana.
Kitaab se bahar: Jar khole bina mombatti jalane ke teen tareeke: lens se dhoop, bijli se garam taar, bijli ki chingari.
Jawab: Jar khole bina batti tak sirf garmi pahunchani hai: lens se dhoop ko batti par ek bindu mein jama karna, andar lage patle taar mein bahar se bijli bhej kar use laal-garam karna, ya andar do taaron ke beech bijli ki chingari. Jar khola toh taazi hawa andar aa jaayegi aur paudhe ka asar pata nahi chalega.
Aam galti: Galat soch: jar khol kar machis se mombatti jala lo. Isse bahar ki taazi hawa andar aa jaati hai, aur phir mombatti ka jalna paudhe ka saboot nahi rehta.
p004-i13 Can you imagine how Priestley would have conducted the experiment using a candle and a plant? Remember, he would need to rekindle the candle to test whether it burns after a few days. How many different ways can you think of to light the candle without disturbing the set-up?
samjhana11.2-k5
Ab doosre suraag ki baari hai, kyunki Priestley ke prayog mein ek ulajh thi. Kabhi paudha band hawa ko theek kar deta tha, aur kabhi wahi prayog dohraane par kuch nahi hota tha. Ek hi prayog kabhi chale aur kabhi na chale, toh ek jasoos kya sochega? Ek jasoos sochega ki koi ek cheez har baar chupke se badal rahi hai, aur abhi tak kisi ki nazar usme nahi gayi. Yeh cheez pakdi Jan Ingenhousz ne, jinka jeevan 1730 se 1799 tak raha. Unhone Priestley jaisa hi set-up banaya, par use ek baar andhere mein rakha aur ek baar dhoop mein. Yahan bhi wahi chaal hai: sab kuch ek jaisa, sirf roshni badli gayi. Ab nateeja dekho: dhoop wale jar mein paudhe ke saath mombatti jalti rahi. Andhere wale jar mein paudha hote hue bhi mombatti bujh gayi. Toh Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki hawa ko saaf karne ka paudhe ka yeh kaam sunlight ke bina nahi chalta. Hawa ko phir se mombatti aur saans ke laayak bana dene ko angrezi mein purifies the air kehte hain. Purify yaani saaf karna. Ab Priestley wali ulajh bhi sulajh gayi: jab jar ko achhi roshni mili, tab paudhe ne hawa theek ki, aur kam roshni mein nahi. Par andhere wale jar mein paudha tha, phir bhi mombatti kyun bujh gayi? Andhere wale jar mein mombatti isliye bujh gayi, kyunki andhere mein paudha hawa ko theek nahi karta. Ulta woh khud bhi saans leta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki paudhe saans nahi lete, ya sirf raat mein lete hain. Par aisa nahi hai: paudhe din-raat saans lete hain aur oxygen kharch karte hain. Bas roshni mein unka hawa ko theek karne ka kaam saans ke kharch se kahin zyada hota hai. Isliye dhoop mein hawa sudharti hai. Yahi baat aapne black paper wale prayog mein bhi dekhi thi: roshni nahi, toh khana nahi. Jasoos ki file mein doosra suraag likho: hawa ko saaf karne ka yeh kaam paudha sirf roshni mein karta hai.
Screen par: Priestley ka prayog do baar: ek baar mombatti jalti (sahi ka nishaan), ek baar bujhti (?); jasoos ka magnifier '?' par. Jan Ingenhousz ka naam (1730-1799). Do ek jaise jar, dono mein paudha aur mombatti: ek andhere kamre mein, ek dhoop mein. Dhoop wale jar mein mombatti jalti, andhere wale mein bujhti. Label: purifies the air = hawa saaf karna. Andhere wale jar mein paudha bhi saans leta, oxygen ke kan uski taraf jaate; galat soch 'paudhe saans nahi lete' par kaata nishaan; dhoop mein paudhe se nikalti oxygen ka bada teer aur saans ka chhota teer. Kone mein black paper wali patti. File par doosra suraag: 'sirf roshni mein'.
Terms: purifies the air = Hawa ko phir se aisa bana dena ki mombatti jal sake aur jaanwar saans le sake; purify = saaf karna.
Kitaab se bahar: Priestley ke prayog kabhi chalte kabhi nahi, aur farq roshni ka tha; paudhe din-raat saans lete hain, roshni mein unka oxygen banana kharch se zyada hota hai.
Yaad: 11.1-k5
p004-i14 Using a similar setup as the one used by Priestley, but by placing it once in the dark and once in the sunlight, Jan Ingenhousz (1730-1799) showed that sunlight is essential to the plant process that somehow purifies the air fouled by burning candles or breathing animals.
example11.2-k6
Ingenhousz yahin nahi ruke. Ab unhe woh gas dekhni thi jo paudha hawa mein chhod raha tha. Par hawa mein ghuli gas toh dikhti nahi; use aankhon se dekhein kaise? Hawa mein ghuli gas ko aankhon se dekhne ka tareeka unhone paani mein dhoondha. Unhone ek aisa paudha liya jo paani mein hi ugta aur rehta hai. Aise paudhe ko aquatic plant kehte hain, kyunki aqua yaani paani. Hamare taalabon mein milne wala Hydrilla aisa hi ek paudha hai. Paani mein doobe paudhe se jo gas niklegi, woh chhote bulbule bankar upar uthegi, aur hum use dekh payenge. Paani ke andar gas ke in chhote golon ko bubbles kehte hain, yaani bulbule. Ab dekho, tez dhoop mein paudhe ke hare hisson ke aas-paas chhote-chhote bubbles bante dikhe. Andhere mein ek bhi bulbula nahi bana. Kya yeh bulbule paudhe ke har hisse se nikle? Bulbule paudhe ke har hisse se nahi nikle; woh sirf hare hisson ke paas bane. Jo hisse hare nahi the, unke paas kuch nahi bana. Paudhe ke in hare hisson ko green parts kehte hain, aur inme chlorophyll hota hai. Baad mein Ingenhousz ne in bulbulon ki gas pehchaani: woh oxygen thi. Oxygen ki pehchaan ka ek aasaan tareeka aapne lab mein dekha hoga: sulagti hui teeli oxygen mein bhabhak kar jal uthti hai. Toh Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki oxygen chhodne ka kaam paudhe ke sirf hare hisse karte hain. Yaad karo, variegated patti mein bhi starch sirf hare hisson mein bana tha. Jasoos ki file mein teesra suraag: hawa ko theek karne wali gas oxygen hai. Woh sirf roshni mein, sirf hare hisson se nikalti hai.
Screen par: Paani ka bartan, andar Hydrilla jaisa aquatic plant (aqua = paani). Tez dhoop: hare hisson ke paas chhote bubbles upar uthte; andhera: ek bhi bulbula nahi. Paudhe ke hare hisson par gola, label 'green parts'; jo hisse hare nahi, wahan kuch nahi. Bulbulon ki gas ek ulti nali mein jama hoti; sulagti teeli andar daalte hi bhabhak kar jalti; label 'oxygen'. Kone mein variegated patti, sirf hare hisse neele-kaale. File par teesra suraag: 'oxygen, roshni mein, hare hisson se'.
Terms: aquatic plant = Paani mein ugne aur rehne wala paudha; aqua = paani; jaise Hydrilla.; bubbles = Paani ke andar gas ke chhote gole, bulbule.; green parts = Paudhe ke hare hisse, jinme chlorophyll hota hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Hydrilla ka udaharan; sulagti teeli se oxygen ki pehchaan; variegated patti (11.1) se jod.
Yaad: 11.1-k4
p004-i15 Ingenhousz in an elegant experiment with an aquatic plant showed that in bright sunlight, small bubbles were formed around the green parts while in the dark they did not. Later he identified these bubbles to be of oxygen. Hence he showed that it is only the green part of the plants that could release oxygen.
samjhana11.2-k7
Ab tak saare suraag hawa ke the. Par paudha jo khana banata hai, uska kya? Paudha kaun-sa khana banata hai, iska saboot lagbhag 1854 mein Julius von Sachs ne diya. Unhone dikhaya ki paudha badhte waqt glucose banata hai. Aap jaante ho, glucose ek meethi sugar hai, jisse cells ko kaam ki energy milti hai. Yeh glucose aksar starch bankar jama ho jaata hai. Yaad karo, bahut saare glucose judkar hi starch bante hain. Baad ke prayogon mein Sachs ne paudhe ki cell ke andar jhaanka. Unhone dekha ki paudhe ka hara padarth, jise aaj hum chlorophyll kehte hain, poori cell mein faila nahi hota. Woh cell ke andar kuch khaas, chhote daanon mein bhara hota hai. In daanon ko baad mein chloroplast naam diya gaya. Chloro yaani hara, aur plast yaani cell ke andar ka ek chhota bana hua hissa. Sachs ne yeh bhi paaya ki glucose paudhe ke hare hisson mein hi banta hai, aur wahi aksar starch bankar rakha jaata hai. Socho, Ingenhousz ki oxygen bhi hare hisson se nikli, aur Sachs ka glucose bhi hare hisson mein bana. Toh isse kya pata chalta hai? Isse pata chalta hai ki oxygen chhodna aur glucose banana do alag kaam nahi hain. Yeh ek hi kaam ke do hisse hain, jo chloroplast wale hare hisson mein hota hai. Jasoos ki file mein agla suraag: khana glucose hai, woh chloroplast wale hare hisson mein banta hai, aur starch bankar jama hota hai.
Screen par: Saal lagbhag 1854, Julius von Sachs. Badhta paudha, uske andar glucose ke chhote kan; glucose ke motiyon ki zanjeer starch ban kar jama. Patti se cell tak zoom: cell mein hare daane, chlorophyll sirf unke andar; label: chloroplast = chloro (hara) + plast (cell ka chhota hissa). Hare hisse par 'glucose yahan banta hai'. Do teer ek hi hare hisse par: Ingenhousz ki oxygen bahar, Sachs ka glucose andar. File par agla suraag.
Terms: chloroplast = Plant cell ke andar chlorophyll rakhne wale chhote hisse; chloro = hara, plast = cell ke andar bana chhota hissa.
Kitaab se bahar: Glucose meethi sugar hai jo cells ko energy deti hai; oxygen chhodna aur glucose banana ek hi kaam ke do hisse hain.
Yaad: 11.1-k3
p005-i1 It was not until about 1854 that Julius von Sachs provided evidence for production of glucose when plants grow. Glucose is usually stored as starch. His later studies showed that the green substance in plants (chlorophyll as we know it now) is located in special bodies (later called chloroplasts) within plant cells. He found that the green parts in plants is [are] where glucose is made, and that the glucose is usually stored as starch.
samjhana11.2-k8
Roshni toh chahiye, yeh pakka ho gaya. Par ab agla sawaal uthta hai. Kya roshni ka har rang photosynthesis mein barabar kaam karta hai? Roshni ka har rang barabar kaam karta hai ya nahi, yeh T.W. Engelmann ne dekha. Unka jeevan 1843 se 1909 tak raha. Class 10 mein aapne padha tha ki safed roshni asal mein kai rangon ka mel hai. Kaanch ka ek tikona tukda, yaani prism, is roshni ko indradhanush jaise alag-alag rangon mein tod deta hai. Rangon ki is patti ko spectrum kehte hain, aur uske alag-alag rangon ko spectral components. Engelmann ne prism se roshni ko isi tarah uske spectral components mein toda. Phir unhone ek alga li. Alga paani mein rehne wale saral, hare jeev hain, jo paudhon ki tarah photosynthesis karte hain. Yeh ek green alga thi, jiska naam Cladophora hai. Yeh dhaage jaisi lambi hoti hai, isliye uske alag-alag hisson par alag rang daale ja sakte the. Yeh alga ek aise paani mein rakhi gayi, jisme bahut saare bacteria tair rahe the. Aise mishran ko, jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas tairte rehte hain, suspension kehte hain. Yeh aerobic bacteria the: aero yaani hawa, aur aerobic bacteria yaani aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye. Par Engelmann ne bacteria daale hi kyun? Engelmann ne bacteria isliye daale, kyunki aerobic bacteria wahin jama hote hain jahan oxygen zyada ho. Jaise gud ke paas cheentiyan jama ho jaati hain, waise hi yeh bacteria oxygen ke paas jama ho jaate hain. Toh bacteria batate the ki O₂ evolution kahan ho raha hai, yaani oxygen kahan ban kar bahar nikal rahi hai. Dhyaan do, yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi, sirf bahar nikalna hai. Ab nateeja dekho: bacteria sabse zyada neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson par jama hue. Hari roshni wale hisse ke paas bahut kam bacteria the. Matlab neeli aur laal roshni mein alga ne sabse zyada oxygen chhodi, yaani wahan photosynthesis sabse tez chali. Kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, iske naqshe ko action spectrum kehte hain: action yaani kaam. Is tarah photosynthesis ka pehla action spectrum bana. Yeh naqsha mota-mota chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b ke absorption spectra jaisa dikhta hai. Absorption spectra yaani kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; inhe hum aage pigments wale hisse mein dekhenge. Jasoos ki file mein agla suraag: har rang barabar kaam nahi karta, neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada kaam aati hai.
Screen par: Safed roshni prism (kaanch ka tikona tukda) se guzar kar indradhanush jaisi rangon ki patti; label: spectrum, spectral components; T.W. Engelmann (1843-1909). Dhaage jaisi hari alga Cladophora paani mein leti, uske alag hisson par alag rang padte; paani mein tairte nanhe bacteria (suspension), label 'aerobic bacteria: aero = hawa'. Kone mein gud par cheentiyan; bacteria oxygen ki taraf khisakte. Label 'O₂ evolution = oxygen ka nikalna (vikaas nahi)'. Bacteria neeli aur laal roshni wale hisson par jhund mein, hari roshni par bahut kam. Neeche ek graph: har rang par photosynthesis ki oonchai, neele aur laal par chotiyan, naam action spectrum; uske peeche halka doosra graph 'absorption spectra: aage'. File par agla suraag.
Terms: spectral components = Safed roshni ke alag-alag rang; spectrum = rangon ki patti.; suspension = Aisa mishran jisme chhote kan ya jeev ghulte nahi, bas tairte rehte hain.; aerobic bacteria = Aise bacteria jinhe jeene ke liye oxygen chahiye; aero = hawa.; O₂ evolution = Oxygen ka banna aur bahar nikalna; yahan evolution ka matlab vikaas nahi.; action spectrum = Kis rang ki roshni mein photosynthesis kitni hoti hai, uska naqsha; action = kaam.; absorption spectra = Kaun-sa rang koi pigment kitna sokhta hai, uske naqshe; absorb = sokhna.
Kitaab se bahar: Prism aur rangon ka mel (Class 10); alga kya hai aur Cladophora dhaage jaisi kyun kaam aayi; hari roshni par bahut kam bacteria; gud aur cheentiyon ki misaal; 'evolution' yahan vikaas nahi.
p005-i2 Now consider the interesting experiments done by T.W Engelmann (1843 – 1909). Using a prism he split light into its spectral components and then illuminated a green alga, Cladophora, placed in a suspension of aerobic bacteria. The bacteria were used to detect the sites of O₂ evolution. He observed that the bacteria accumulated mainly in the region of blue and red light of the split spectrum. A first action spectrum of photosynthesis was thus described. It resembles roughly the absorption spectra of chlorophyll a and b (discussed in section 11.4).
samjhana11.2-k9
Ab tak ki file padho: 19th century ke beech tak, yaani lagbhag 1850 ke aas-paas, kaafi baatein saaf ho chuki thi. Paudhe roshni ki energy lekar CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate banate hain. Carbohydrate yaani carbon, hydrogen aur oxygen se bane khane ke padarth, jaise glucose aur starch. Oxygen chhodne wale jeevon, jaise hare paudhon, ke is poore kaam ko ek seedhi equation mein likha gaya. Equation mein baayi taraf shuru ki cheezein hoti hain, aur daayi taraf bani hui cheezein. Woh equation thi: CO₂ aur H₂O, yaani paani, roshni ki madad se banate hain [CH₂O] aur O₂. Teer ke upar Light likha hai, kyunki yeh kaam roshni ke bina nahi chalta. Aisi equation, jo bas dekh kar aur naap kar likhi gayi ho ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, empirical equation kehlati hai. Isme beech ke kadam nahi hote; empirical yaani dekhne aur naapne se bana, andar ke kaam ko jaane bina. Ab [CH₂O] ko bracket mein kyun likha? [CH₂O] ko bracket mein isliye likha, kyunki yeh koi asli alag molecule nahi, carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket hai. Aisi chhe ikaiyan jodo, toh chhe carbon wala glucose banta hai, C₆H₁₂O₆. Exam ke liye yeh baarik baat pakki kar lo: [CH₂O] sirf ek ikai hai, glucose ka poora formula nahi. Ab is equation ko jasoos ki nazar se dekho. Oxygen do jagah hai: CO₂ mein bhi, aur H₂O mein bhi. Toh paudhe ne jo O₂ chhodi, uske oxygen atom kahan se aaye? Paudhe ki O₂ ke oxygen atom kahan se aaye, is par do cheezon par shaq hai: CO₂ aur paani. Aksar log sochte hain ki yeh O₂ CO₂ se aati hai. Unka tark hai ki CO₂ ka carbon khane mein chala jaata hai, aur uske do oxygen bach kar bahar nikal jaate hain. Baat seedhi lagti hai, par jasoos kabhi bina saboot ke faisla nahi karta. Is shaq ka faisla bacteria par hue ek kaam se hua, jise hum ab dekhte hain.
Screen par: Calendar par 19th century, 1850 ke aas-paas. Patti mein roshni, CO₂ aur paani se carbohydrate (glucose, starch). Equation likhi jaati: CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂; baayi taraf 'shuru', daayi taraf 'bana', teer par Light. Label: empirical equation = dekh kar likhi, beech ke kadam chhupe. [CH₂O] ke bracket chamakte; chhe [CH₂O] jud kar C₆H₁₂O₆. Jasoos ka magnifier O₂ par: CO₂ ke oxygen aur H₂O ke oxygen, dono par '?' (do shaq). CO₂ se O₂ tak ek teer, us par bada sawaal ka nishaan.
Terms: empirical equation = Dekh kar aur naap kar likhi equation, jo sirf batati hai ki kya andar gaya aur kya bahar aaya, beech ke kadam nahi.; [CH₂O] = Carbohydrate ki ek ikai ka sanket, koi asli alag molecule nahi; aisi chhe ikaiyan milkar glucose C₆H₁₂O₆.; H₂O = Paani ka formula: ek molecule mein hydrogen ke do atom aur oxygen ka ek atom.
Kitaab se bahar: Equation kaise padhte hain (baayi taraf shuru, daayi taraf bana); [CH₂O] asli molecule nahi, ek ikai (exam ki baareek baat); oxygen par do shaq aur aam galat soch.
p005-i3 By the middle of the nineteenth century the key features of plant photosynthesis were known, namely, that plants could use light energy to make carbohydrates from CO₂ and water. The empirical equation representing the total process of photosynthesis for oxygen evolving organisms was then understood as:
p005-i4 CO₂ + H₂O —Light→ [CH₂O] + O₂ where [CH₂O] represented a carbohydrate (e.g., glucose, a six-carbon sugar).
samjhana11.2-k10
Is shaq ka faisla ek vaigyanik ne kiya, jinka naam tha Cornelius van Niel; unka jeevan 1897 se 1985 tak raha. Woh ek microbiologist the: micro yaani bahut chhota, bio yaani jeevan, aur microbiologist yaani bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik. Van Niel ne purple aur green bacteria par kaam kiya, jo paudhon ki tarah roshni se apna khana banate hain. Class 10 mein aapne padha tha: kisi cheez se hydrogen nikal jaaye, toh use oxidation kehte hain. Aur kisi cheez mein hydrogen jud jaaye, toh use reduction kehte hain. Jo yaugik apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake, use oxidisable compound kehte hain. Van Niel ne dikhaya ki photosynthesis asal mein roshni par tiki ek reaction hai, yaani light-dependent reaction. Is reaction mein kisi oxidisable compound ka hydrogen CO₂ ko reduce karta hai, aur CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai. Seedhe shabdon mein: koi cheez apna hydrogen deti hai, aur roshni is kaam ki energy deti hai. Phir woh hydrogen CO₂ se judkar khana bana deta hai. Hydrogen dene wali is cheez ko hydrogen donor kehte hain: donor yaani dene wala. Isse van Niel ne ek aam equation likhi: 2H₂A aur CO₂, Light yaani roshni ki madad se, banate hain 2A, CH₂O aur H₂O. Yahan H₂A koi bhi hydrogen donor ho sakta hai, aur A uska woh hissa hai jo hydrogen dene ke baad bachta hai. Dekho, is equation ki khoobi yeh hai ki yeh har tarah ke photosynthesis par chalti hai. Bas A ki jagah sahi cheez rakhni hai. Jasoos ke haath mein ab ek saancha hai: A ki jagah alag-alag cheez rakho, aur dekho ki bahar kya nikalta hai.
Screen par: Cornelius van Niel (1897-1985); label: micro = chhota, bio = jeevan, microbiologist. Roshni mein bangni (purple) aur hare bacteria. Do chhote chitra: molecule se hydrogen nikla = oxidation, molecule mein hydrogen juda = reduction. Ek molecule apna hydrogen deta (oxidisable compound, hydrogen donor), roshni ki kirnein energy deti, hydrogen CO₂ se judkar carbohydrate banata (reduce). Aam equation: 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O; H₂A aur A par khaali khaane jinme alag cheez rakhi ja sakti hai; jasoos ke haath mein yeh saancha.
Terms: microbiologist = Bacteria jaise nanhe jeevon ko padhne wala vaigyanik; micro = bahut chhota, bio = jeevan.; oxidisable compound = Aisa yaugik jo apna hydrogen dekar khud oxidise ho sake.; reduce = Kisi molecule mein hydrogen judna; yahan hydrogen judne se CO₂ carbohydrate ban jaati hai.; light-dependent = Jo roshni ke bina na chale.; hydrogen donor = CO₂ ko reduce karne ke liye hydrogen dene wali cheez; donor = dene wala.; H₂A = Koi bhi hydrogen dene wala yaugik; A ki jagah alag-alag cheez aa sakti hai.; A = Hydrogen dene ke baad donor ka bacha hissa; paudhon mein oxygen, sulphur bacteria mein sulphur.
Kitaab se bahar: Oxidation aur reduction ki Class 10 wali paribhasha (hydrogen nikalna aur judna); purple aur green bacteria bhi roshni se khana banate hain; equation ek saancha hai jisme A badalta hai.
p005-i5 A milestone contribution to the understanding of photosynthesis was that made by a microbiologist, Cornelius van Niel (1897-1985), who, based on his studies of purple and green bacteria, demonstrated that photosynthesis is essentially a light-dependent reaction in which hydrogen from a suitable oxidisable compound reduces carbon dioxide to carbohydrates. This can be expressed by:
p005-i6 2H₂A + CO₂ —Light→ 2A + CH₂O + H₂O
samjhana11.2-k11
Ab saanche mein hare paudhe rakho. Hare paudhon mein hydrogen dene wala H₂O hai, yaani A ki jagah oxygen hai. Toh equation banti hai: 2H₂O aur CO₂ se 2O, CH₂O aur H₂O. Do oxygen atom milkar ek O₂ ban jaate hain. Yaani paani apna hydrogen deta hai, aur khud oxidise hokar O₂ ban jaata hai. Ab un bacteria ko dekho jo photosynthesis toh karte hain, par O₂ bilkul nahi chhodte. Purple aur green sulphur bacteria mein hydrogen dene wala H₂S hai, yaani hydrogen sulphide. H₂S ek gas hai jisme do hydrogen aur ek sulphur hota hai, aur isi se sade ande jaisi badboo aati hai. Ishaara yeh hai: is baar hamare saanche mein A ki jagah sulphur aa gaya hai, yaani S. Ruko, pehle khud socho: yeh bacteria hawa mein kya chhodenge, aur isse paudhe ki oxygen ke baare mein kya pata chalta hai? Aapne socha hoga ki yeh bacteria oxygen nahi, sulphur chhodenge, aur yahi sach hai. Jeev ke hisaab se yeh oxidation ka nateeja sulphur hota hai ya sulphate. Sulphate sulphur aur oxygen ke atomon se bana ek kan hai. Par in bacteria se O₂ kabhi nahi nikalti. Ab gaur karo: dono mein CO₂ ek hi hai. Badla sirf hydrogen dene wala, aur uske saath badal gaya jo bahar nikla. Socho, agar O₂ CO₂ se aati, toh in bacteria ko bhi O₂ chhodni chahiye thi, kyunki CO₂ toh unke paas bhi hai. Isliye van Niel ne nateeja nikala: hare paudhe jo O₂ chhodte hain, woh H₂O se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi. Toh asal mein O₂ ka srot CO₂ nahi, paani nikla. Seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar aisa nateeja nikalne ko infer karna kehte hain; van Niel ne yahi inferred kiya. Bilkul jasoos ki tarah: chor ko kisi ne dekha nahi, par saboot jod kar pata chal gaya ki woh kaun hai. Par jasoos ki file tab band hoti hai jab pakka saboot mil jaaye, aur woh saboot baad mein radioisotopic techniques se mila.
Screen par: Saanche mein A ki jagah O: 2H₂O + CO₂ → 2O + CH₂O + H₂O, do O mil kar O₂. Phir A ki jagah S: purple aur green sulphur bacteria, H₂S (do H, ek S; sade ande ki badboo ka chhota nishaan); clue ka dabba. 'Ruko, pehle khud socho' ke saath ? aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir bacteria se peele sulphur ke kan ya sulphate nikalte, O₂ par kaata nishaan. Dono saanche saath: CO₂ dono mein ek jaisi, sirf donor badla, aur bahar nikli cheez badli. CO₂ wale shaq par kaata nishaan, paani par 'O₂ ka srot'. Label: infer = saboot jod kar nateeja. Jasoos chor ke pairon ke nishaan jodta; file par 'pakka saboot: baad mein'.
Terms: H₂S = Hydrogen sulphide, sade ande jaisi badboo wali gas; do hydrogen aur ek sulphur.; infer = Seedha dekhe bina, saboot jod kar nateeja nikalna; inferred = aisa nateeja nikala.
Kitaab se bahar: Saanche mein O aur S rakh kar dono equation; H₂S ki badboo; sulphate kya hai; agar O₂ CO₂ se aati toh bacteria bhi chhodte, yeh tark; CO₂ wali galat soch yahan toot-ti hai.
p005-i7 In green plants H₂O is the hydrogen donor and is oxidised to O₂. Some organisms do not release O₂ during photosynthesis. When H₂S, instead is the hydrogen donor for purple and green sulphur bacteria, the ‘oxidation’ product is sulphur or sulphate depending on the organism
p005-i8 and not O₂. Hence, he inferred that the O₂ evolved by the green plant comes from H₂O, not from carbon dioxide. This was later proved by using radioisotopic techniques. The correct equation, that would represent the overall process of photosynthesis is therefore:
samjhana11.2-k12
Yaad karo, chapter ki shuruaat mein Calvin ne C¹⁴ ko haldi jaisa nishaan bana kar carbon ka peechha kiya tha. Wahan humne dekha tha ki ek hi element ke atom neutron ki ginti mein alag ho sakte hain. Inhe isotope kehte hain, aur kuch isotope radiation chhodte hain. Aise nishaan wale isotope se atom ka peechha karke dekhna ki woh kahan pahuncha, radioisotopic techniques kehlata hai. Ise isotopic tracing bhi kehte hain. Isi tarah 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ne ek hari alga ko aisa paani diya, jiske oxygen par nishaan tha, yaani oxygen-18. Aur dekho, alga ne jo O₂ chhodi, usme wahi nishaan wala oxygen mila. Jab nishaan CO₂ ke oxygen par lagaya, tab woh O₂ mein nahi aaya. Exam ke liye yaad rakho: oxygen-18 oxygen ka bas bhaari roop hai, aur yeh radioactive nahi hota. Jasoos ki zubaan mein yeh ungliyon ke nishaan jaisa pakka saboot hai: nikalne wali O₂ paani se hi aati hai. Ab photosynthesis ki sahi equation likho: 6CO₂ aur 12H₂O, Light yaani roshni ki madad se, banate hain C₆H₁₂O₆, 6H₂O aur 6O₂. Yahan C₆H₁₂O₆ glucose hai: iske ek molecule mein chhe carbon, baarah hydrogen aur chhe oxygen atom hote hain. Aur yaad rakho, is equation ki 6O₂ ki saari oxygen paani se aati hai. Par dhyaan rahe, yeh ek akeli reaction nahi hai. Equation sirf shuru aur ant dikhati hai, jabki beech mein kai chhote kadam hote hain. In sab kadamon ke poore kaam ka naam hi photosynthesis hai. Jaise 'aata, paani aur aag se roti' likhne mein gundhna, belna aur sekna, teeno kadam chhup jaate hain. Kai kadamon wale aise kaam ko multistep process kehte hain: multi yaani kai, aur step yaani kadam. Yeh kadam kaun-se hain, yeh aage ke hisson mein ek-ek karke khulenge.
Screen par: Opening ki yaad: C¹⁴ par haldi jaisa nishaan, carbon sugar tak. Ek hi element ke do atom, ek bhaari; label isotope. Paani ke oxygen par chamakta nishaan (oxygen-18), hari alga ke paas: nikalti O₂ mein wahi nishaan; doosre jar mein CO₂ ke oxygen par nishaan, O₂ mein nahi; '1941: Ruben aur Kamen'; neeche 'oxygen-18: bhaari, radioactive nahi'. Ungliyon ke nishaan wala pakka saboot file par. Sahi equation: 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂; C₆H₁₂O₆ ke neeche 6 C, 12 H, 6 O; 6O₂ se paani tak teer. Phir ek seedha teer kai chhote kadamon mein tootta (multistep process: multi = kai, step = kadam); kone mein rasoi: aata, paani, aag se roti, beech mein gundhna, belna, sekna.
Terms: radioisotopic techniques = Nishaan wale isotope ka peechha karke dekhna ki atom kahan gaya; isotope = ek hi element ka alag wazan wala atom.; isotopic tracing = Isotope ke nishaan se atomon ka raasta pata karna.; C₆H₁₂O₆ = Glucose ka formula; ek molecule mein 6 carbon, 12 hydrogen aur 6 oxygen atom.; multistep process = Kai jude kadamon mein poora hone wala kaam; multi = kai, step = kadam.
Kitaab se bahar: 1941 mein Ruben aur Kamen ka oxygen-18 wala prayog (dono taraf nishaan); oxygen-18 radioactive nahi (exam ki baareek baat); rasoi wali roti se multistep ki misaal.
Yaad: 0-k9
p005-i9 6CO₂ + 12H₂O —Light→ C₆H₁₂O₆ + 6H₂O + 6O₂
p005-i10 where C₆H₁₂O₆ represents glucose. The O₂ released is from water; this was proved using radio isotope techniques. Note that this is not a single
p006-i1 reaction but description of a multistep process called photosynthesis.
ruko, socho11.2-k13
Ab is equation mein ek ajeeb baat dekho: paani dono taraf hai. Baayi taraf 12H₂O hai, aur daayi taraf 6H₂O. Toh shuru mein baarah paani ke molecule kyun likhe, seedhe chhe kyun nahi? Baarah paani ke molecule likhne ki wajah oxygen ki ginti mein chhupi hai, toh chalo gin kar dekhte hain. Pehle daayi taraf ki 6O₂ gino. Har O₂ mein do oxygen atom hain, toh chhe O₂ mein baarah oxygen atom hue. Aur van Niel ke suraag se hum jaante hain ki yeh saare oxygen atom paani se aate hain. Socho, ek H₂O mein kitne oxygen atom hote hain, aur baarah oxygen atom ke liye kitne paani chahiye? Ek H₂O mein oxygen ka sirf ek atom hai, isliye baarah atom ke liye baarah paani lagenge. Yahi wajah hai ki baayi taraf 12H₂O likha hai. Toh daayi taraf ke 6H₂O kahan se aaye? Daayi taraf ke 6H₂O naya paani hai, jo is kaam ke dauraan banta hai. Chalo, ab hydrogen bhi gin lete hain: baarah paani ke molecule kul chaubees hydrogen dete hain. Inme se baarah hydrogen glucose mein chale jaate hain. Baaki baarah hydrogen CO₂ ke chhe oxygen ke saath judkar chhe naye paani bana dete hain. Aur CO₂ ke baaki chhe oxygen glucose mein chale jaate hain. Toh dono taraf ka paani alag paani hai. Baayi taraf ke paani ki oxygen O₂ ban jaati hai, aur daayi taraf ke paani ki oxygen CO₂ se aati hai. Agar dono taraf se chhe-chhe paani kaat kar sirf chhe likh dete, toh ginti barabar rehti. Par yeh baat chhup jaati ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai. Exam mein net hisaab bhi poocha jaata hai: baarah paani gaye aur chhe naye bane, toh kul kharch chhe paani ka hua.
Screen par: Equation mein dono taraf paani chamakta: baayi taraf 12H₂O, daayi taraf 6H₂O, aur sawaal ka nishaan. 6O₂ ke moti gine jaate: chhe jode, baarah laal oxygen moti. Har H₂O mein ek laal oxygen moti; baarah paani se baarah laal moti O₂ mein jaate. Phir hydrogen ke 24 safed moti: 12 glucose mein, 12 CO₂ ke 6 neele oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani; CO₂ ke baaki 6 neele moti glucose mein. Daayi taraf ke paani par 'naya'. Kaat kar 6H₂O likhne par chhupi baat par nishaan. Neeche net: 12 - 6 = 6.
Kitaab se bahar: Oxygen aur hydrogen ke atom gin kar 12H₂O ki wajah; daayi taraf ka paani naya hai jiski oxygen CO₂ se aati hai; net kharch 6 paani ka.
Jawab: 6O₂ mein baarah oxygen atom hain, aur saare paani se aate hain; ek H₂O mein ek hi oxygen hota hai, isliye 12H₂O chahiye. 12H₂O ke 24 hydrogen mein se 12 glucose mein jaate hain, baaki 12 CO₂ ke 6 oxygen ke saath 6 naye paani banate hain; CO₂ ke baaki 6 oxygen glucose mein. Isliye daayi taraf ka 6H₂O naya paani hai.
Aam galti: Galat soch: dono taraf ka paani ek hi hai, isliye 6 kaat kar sirf 6H₂O likh do. Ginti barabar rehti hai, par yeh chhup jaata hai ki saari O₂ paani se aati hai aur daayi taraf ka paani naya hai, jiski oxygen CO₂ se aayi.
p006-i2 Can you explain why twelve molecules of water as substrate are used in the equation given above?
poori kahani dobara11.2-k14
Ab jasoos ki poori file ek saath padhte hain. Yaad karo, Priestley ne dikhaya ki hara paudha band jar ki kharab hawa ko phir theek kar deta hai. Ingenhousz ke prayogon se pata chala ki yeh kaam roshni maangta hai, aur oxygen ke bulbule hare hisson se uthte hain. Sachs ne dikhaya ki hare hisson ke chloroplast mein glucose banta hai, jo starch bankar jama hota hai. Engelmann ne dikhaya ki neeli aur laal roshni is kaam mein sabse zyada kaam aati hai. Aur van Niel ne dikhaya ki nikalne wali O₂ paani se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: band jar mein jalti mombatti ke saath ek hara paudha rakh dein, toh kya hoga, aur kyun? Band jar mein hara paudha rakhne par, agar roshni mile, toh mombatti jalti rahegi aur chooha bhi saans leta rahega. Kyunki roshni mein paudhe ke hare hisse paani ke molecule tod kar oxygen chhodte hain, aur CO₂ se glucose banate hain. Mombatti aur chooha jo oxygen kharch karte hain, paudha use paani se nikaal kar hawa mein lauta deta hai. Aur andhere mein paudha oxygen nahi chhodta, sirf saans mein kharch karta hai, isliye tab mombatti bujh jaati hai. Sachs ne bataya tha ki chlorophyll chloroplast naam ke chhote daanon mein rehta hai. Par paudhe mein yeh kaam theek-theek kahan-kahan hota hai, sirf patti mein ya kisi aur hisse mein bhi, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Jasoos ki file ke paanch panne ek saath: Priestley (paudha hawa theek karta), Ingenhousz (roshni, hare hisse, oxygen ke bulbule), Sachs (chloroplast mein glucose, starch), Engelmann (neeli aur laal roshni), van Niel (O₂ paani se). Shuru wala jar phir: roshni mein paudha, mombatti jalti, chooha chalta; paudhe mein paani tootkar oxygen bahar, CO₂ se glucose. Andhere mein wahi jar, mombatti bujhti. Aakhir mein poora paudha, jad, tana aur patti, us par sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.3 WHERE DOES PHOTOSYNTHESIS TAKE PLACE? · 12 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 26 min
Maqsad: Photosynthesis paudhe ke kin-kin hisson mein hota hai, aur un hisson ke andar bhi theek kis jagah?
Misaal: cheeni mill: chloroplast ek mill; do chaardiwari = outer aur inner membrane; dhoop se chalne wala bijlighar = grana ki membranes (light reactions); bijlighar ke kamron ko jodne wale galiyare = stroma lamellae; bijlighar se floor tak jaane wali do cheezein = ATP (kaam ki energy) aur NADPH (hydrogen aur electron); mill ka bada floor = stroma, uski machines = enzymes jo CO₂ se sugar banati hain (dark reactions); godown ki boriyaan = starch granule; purze banane wali workshop = ribosomes
Kitab ki baatein (73): kitab kehti hai 15 · shabd ka matlab 19 · pehle ki zaroori baat 19 · figure ka nishaan 8 · teacher ki extra baat 12
f1 kitab kehti hai Photosynthesis kahan hota hai, iska jawab aap cell wale padhe hue ke aadhar par denge: hari patti mein, ya chloroplast mein; yeh jawab sahi hai.
f2 kitab kehti hai Photosynthesis hari pattiyon mein hota hai, par paudhe ke doosre hare hisson mein bhi hota hai.
f3 pehle ki zaroori baat: plant organs Plant organs: paudhe ke hisse (organs) jad, tana, patti, phool aur phal; patti ek organ hai.
f4 pehle ki zaroori baat: organelles Cells contain organelles: cell ke andar apna-apna kaam karne wale chhote hisse (organelles) hote hain; chloroplast ek organelle hai.
f5 pehle ki zaroori baat: chlorophyll Chlorophyll absorbs light: chloroplast ke andar ka hara chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai.
f6 teacher ki extra baat Patti aur chloroplast, dono jawab sahi hain, bas alag paimaane par: patti organ hai, chloroplast organelle. Galat soch: photosynthesis sirf patti mein hota hai.
f7 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: paudhe ke aur kaun-se hisse hain jahan photosynthesis ho sakta hai? Jawab: hare tane, phool ke hare sepals, kachche hare phal, yaani har hara hissa jisme chloroplast hon aur roshni pahunche.
f8 pehle ki zaroori baat: sepals Stem, sepal and fruit: tana; phool ke neeche ki chhoti hari pattiyan (sepals) jo khilne se pehle kali ko dhakti hain; phal. Jo hissa hara hai, usme chloroplast hote hain.
f9 teacher ki extra baat: Opuntia Opuntia (nagphani) mein pattiyan kaante ban jaati hain, aur tana chapta, mota aur hara ho jaata hai; usme chlorophyll hota hai aur wahi photosynthesis karta hai. Registan mein chaudi pattiyan bahut paani uda deti.
f10 teacher ki extra baat Phir bhi zyadatar paudhon mein sabse zyada khana patti banati hai, kyunki woh chapti aur chaudi hokar sabse zyada roshni pakadti hai.
f11 kitab kehti hai Patti ki mesophyll cells mein bahut saare (large number) chloroplast hote hain.
f12 kitab kehti hai Chloroplast aam taur par mesophyll cells ki deewaron ke saath line mein lagte hain, taaki unhe incident light ki optimum matra mile.
f13 shabd ka matlab: mesophyll Mesophyll: patti ki upar aur neeche ki epidermis ke beech ka hara bhaag; meso = beech, phyll = patti.
f14 shabd ka matlab: incident light Incident light: bahar se aakar kisi satah par girne wali roshni; incident = aakar padne wala.
f15 shabd ka matlab: optimum Optimum: kaam ke liye sabse theek matra, na kam na zyada; optimum maximum (sabse zyada) nahi hai.
f16 pehle ki zaroori baat: cell wall Leaf mesophyll aur cell wall: plant cell ke chaaron taraf mazboot cell wall hoti hai; mesophyll cell ka zyadatar beech ka hissa paani bhara bada vacuole ghere rehta hai, isliye chloroplast kinaaron par rehte hain.
f17 pehle ki zaroori baat: light absorption by chlorophyll Light absorption by chlorophyll: chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai; bahut kam roshni mein kaam dheema hota hai, aur bahut tez roshni chlorophyll aur chloroplast ko nuksaan pahunchati hai.
f18 teacher ki extra baat Mesophyll ki ek cell mein aksar 20 se 40 chloroplast hote hain.
f19 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: chloroplast kab apni chapti satah deewaron ke parallel rakhenge, aur kab incident light ke perpendicular?
f20 pehle ki zaroori baat: parallel and perpendicular Parallel and perpendicular directions: parallel = saath-saath chalne wali, kabhi na milne wali rekhaayein (rail ki patriyan); perpendicular = seedha 90 degree par kaatne wali (farsh aur deewar).
f21 pehle ki zaroori baat: chloroplast flat surface Chloroplast flat surface: chloroplast masoor ke daane (lens) jaisa chapta hota hai; ek chauda chapta chehra aur ek patla kinaara.
f22 teacher ki extra baat Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par jama hote hain; chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur incident light ke perpendicular, isliye zyada roshni pakdi jaati hai. Tez roshni mein woh bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain; chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur roshni ke bhi parallel, sirf kinaara roshni ki taraf, isliye kam roshni aur kam nuksaan.
f23 kitab kehti hai Chloroplast ke andar ek membrane system hai, jisme grana, stroma lamellae aur matrix stroma hain.
f24 shabd ka matlab: grana Grana: thylakoid ki chapti thailiyon ke sikkon jaise dher; ek dher granum, kai dher grana; granum = daana.
f25 shabd ka matlab: stroma lamellae Stroma lamellae: alag-alag grana ko jodne wali patli, chapti, bina dher wali membranes; lamella = patli parat, bahuvachan lamellae.
f26 shabd ka matlab: stroma Stroma: chloroplast ke andar membranes ke chaaron taraf bhara gaadha dravya, yaani matrix.
f27 shabd ka matlab: thylakoid Thylakoid: chloroplast ke andar ki chapti, band membrane wali thaili; inke dher grana bante hain.
f28 pehle ki zaroori baat: chloroplast envelope Chloroplast: cell ka organelle jo do membranes, bahar wali aur andar wali, ke gher (envelope) se ghira hota hai.
f29 pehle ki zaroori baat: membrane versus matrix Membrane versus fluid matrix: membrane = patli jhilli jo ek jagah ko doosri se alag rakhe; matrix = jagah ko bharne wala gaadha dravya.
f30 teacher ki extra baat Stroma aur stroma lamellae alag cheezein hain: stroma dravya hai, stroma lamellae membrane; granum ekvachan hai, grana bahuvachan.
f31 kitab kehti hai Chloroplast mein kaam ka saaf bantwara (clear division of labour) hai.
f32 kitab kehti hai Membrane system roshni ki energy trap karta hai, yaani pakadta hai, aur ATP aur NADPH banata hai.
f33 kitab kehti hai Stroma mein enzymatic reactions sugar banati hain, aur yeh sugar aage starch banati hai.
f34 shabd ka matlab: division of labour Division of labour: ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baantna, jisme har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale; kaam ka bantwara.
f35 shabd ka matlab: ATP ATP: ek molecule jo energy (chemical energy) apne andar lekar chalta hai aur jahan kaam ho wahan de deta hai; poora naam aur banawat aage.
f36 shabd ka matlab: NADPH NADPH: electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule, jo inhe dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai.
f37 shabd ka matlab: enzymatic reactions Enzymatic reactions: enzymes ki madad se chalne wali reactions.
f38 pehle ki zaroori baat: enzymes Enzymes catalyse reactions: enzyme ek protein hai jo reaction ko tez karata hai aur khud nahi badalta.
f39 pehle ki zaroori baat: chemical energy Chemical energy: molecules ke andar band energy; ATP yahi energy lekar chalta hai.
f40 pehle ki zaroori baat: membranes Chloroplast internal membranes and stroma: andar ki membranes (grana aur stroma lamellae) aur unke chaaron taraf ka stroma.
f41 teacher ki extra baat Roshni pakadne wale molecules membrane par ek tay kram mein jade rehte hain, jabki sugar banane wale enzymes stroma ke dravya mein ghule rehte hain; isliye dono kaam alag jagah hote hain.
f42 teacher ki extra baat Stroma mein bani sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai; ATP aur NADPH sugar nahi bante, sirf energy aur hydrogen dekar khaali hote hain aur membrane par phir bhar jaate hain.
f43 kitab kehti hai Pehla set, yaani membranes wali reactions, seedhe roshni se chalti hain (directly light driven), isliye inhe light reactions (photochemical reactions) kehte hain.
f44 shabd ka matlab: light reactions Light reactions: chloroplast ki membranes mein seedhe roshni se chalne wali reactions; roshni band hote hi yeh ruk jaati hain.
f45 shabd ka matlab: photochemical reactions Photochemical reactions: photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik; roshni se seedhe chalne wali rasayanik reactions, light reactions ka doosra naam.
f46 pehle ki zaroori baat: membrane system Light absorption, chloroplast membrane system, ATP and NADPH: membrane system mein chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai, aur wahin ATP aur NADPH bante hain.
f47 kitab kehti hai Doosra set seedhe roshni se nahi chalta, par light reactions ke products, ATP aur NADPH, par tika hai.
f48 kitab kehti hai Isliye, alag pehchaan ke liye, by convention inhe dark reactions (carbon reactions) kehte hain.
f49 kitab kehti hai Iska yeh matlab nahi ki yeh reactions andhere mein hoti hain, ya light-dependent nahi hain.
f50 shabd ka matlab: dark reactions Dark reactions: stroma ki sugar banane wali reactions, jo seedhe roshni se nahi chalti; naam sunkar andhera mat samjho.
f51 shabd ka matlab: carbon reactions Carbon reactions: dark reactions ka doosra naam; inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai.
f52 shabd ka matlab: by convention By convention: bahut pehle se chala aa raha, sabka maana hua naam ya tareeka, chahe woh poori baat na bataye.
f53 pehle ki zaroori baat: light reactions Light reactions produce ATP and NADPH; stroma synthesises sugar: membranes ATP aur NADPH banati hain, aur stroma unse sugar banata hai.
f54 pehle ki zaroori baat: direct versus indirect dependence Direct versus indirect dependence: direct = seedha kisi par tikna; indirect = kisi doosre ke zariye tikna.
f55 teacher ki extra baat Andhere mein ATP aur NADPH banna rukte hi thodi der mein dark reactions bhi ruk jaati hain; yeh zyadatar din mein, light reactions ke saath-saath chalti hain.
f56 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.2: chloroplast ke section ki electron micrograph ka diagrammatic representation. Andaakaar chloroplast outer aur inner membrane se ghira; stroma mein several (kai) grana ke dher, stromal lamellae se jude; chhote bindu ribosomes, naarangi gole-dar-gole starch granules, bhoori boond lipid droplet; aath leader lines, koi process teer nahi.
f57 shabd ka matlab: section Section: andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kisi cheez ka kaata gaya patla tukda.
f58 shabd ka matlab: Diagrammatic representation Diagrammatic representation: kisi photo ya cheez ko saaf rekhaon wale chitra (diagram) ke roop mein dikhana.
f59 shabd ka matlab: electron micrograph Electron micrograph: electron microscope se li gayi bahut badi photo; micro = chhota, graph = chitra.
f60 figure ka nishaan: Outer membrane Sabse bahar ki rekha: chloroplast ke gher ki bahar wali jhilli.
f61 figure ka nishaan: Inner membrane Uske andar ki rekha: gher ki andar wali jhilli; dono milkar do parat ka gher.
f62 figure ka nishaan: Grana Sikkon jaise chapti membranes ke dher; figure mein kai dher.
f63 figure ka nishaan: Stromal lamella Grana ko jodti bina dher wali patli membrane; figure ek patti dikhata hai, isliye ekvachan lamella.
f64 figure ka nishaan: Stroma Membranes ke chaaron taraf ki jagah; chitra mein khaali jaisi, asal mein gaadhe dravya se bhari.
f65 figure ka nishaan: Ribosomes Stroma ke chhote bindu; yeh protein banate hain.
f66 figure ka nishaan: Starch granule Stroma mein naarangi, andaakaar, gole ke andar gole wala daana; jama kiya hua starch.
f67 figure ka nishaan: Lipid droplet Neeche bhoori gol boond; charbi aur tel jaisi cheez, starch nahi.
f68 pehle ki zaroori baat: leader lines Chloroplast compartments, reading diagram leader lines: har label se ek leader line theek us hisse tak jaati hai; uske sire se hissa pehchaano.
f69 pehle ki zaroori baat: ribosomes and lipids Ribosomes synthesise proteins; lipids versus carbohydrates: ribosomes protein banate hain; lipid charbi aur tel hai, starch carbohydrate hai, dono alag.
f70 pehle ki zaroori baat: microscopy Microscopy, section reveals internal structure: microscope chhoti cheez ko bada dikhata hai; kisi cheez ko kaatne par uski andar ki banawat dikhti hai.
f71 teacher ki extra baat Chloroplast ke ribosomes chhote (70S) hote hain, aur chloroplast apne kuch protein khud banata hai.
f72 teacher ki extra baat Kachcha tamatar, hari mirch aur matar ki phali hari hoti hain; inke chloroplast dhoop mein thoda khana banate hain. Pakne par tamatar ke chloroplast laal rang wale chromoplast ban jaate hain aur chlorophyll toot jaata hai.
f73 teacher ki extra baat Money plant jaise paudhon ke hare tane bhi photosynthesis karte hain.
pehle poori jhalak11.3-k1
Pichhli baar humne jasoos ki file poori ki. Paanch vaigyanikon ke suraagon se photosynthesis ki tasveer bani. Ingenhousz ne dikhaya ki oxygen paudhe ke hare hisson se nikalti hai. Sachs ne dikhaya ki glucose chloroplast mein banta hai. Yaad rakhein: yeh kaam paudhe ke hare hisson mein, chloroplast naam ke chhote hare daanon ke andar hota hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki sirf 'hare hisse' kehna kaafi nahi hai. Humein jagah ekdum theek-theek dhoondhni hai. Pichhli film ke aakhir mein humne yahi poochha tha: kya yeh kaam sirf patti mein hota hai? Photosynthesis paudhe ke kin-kin hisson mein hota hai, aur un hisson ke andar bhi theek kis jagah? Photosynthesis ki jagah poochho toh aap turant kahoge: hari patti mein, ya chloroplast mein. Yeh aapne cell wale chapter mein padha tha, aur aapka jawab bilkul sahi hai. Dekho, photosynthesis sach mein paudhon ki hari pattiyon mein hota hai. Par sirf pattiyon mein nahi. Hum dekhenge ki doosre hare hisse bhi yeh kaam karte hain. Aksar hum sochte hain ki khana sirf patti banati hai, par aisa nahi hai. Yaad karo, paudhe ke alag-alag hisson ko organs kehte hain. Jaise jad, tana, patti, phool aur phal. Cell ke andar bhi apna-apna kaam karne wale chhote hisse hote hain, jinhe organelles kehte hain. Chloroplast aisa hi ek organelle hai, aur uske andar hara chlorophyll hota hai jo roshni sokh leta hai. Isliye jahan bhi chloroplast hain aur roshni pahunchti hai, wahan photosynthesis ho sakta hai. Matlab aapke dono jawab sahi hain. Patti ek organ hai, aur chloroplast cell ke andar ka organelle. Aaj hum yeh jagah kadam-dar-kadam dhoondhenge. Pehle paudhe ka hissa, phir uski cell, phir chloroplast, aur aakhir mein chloroplast ke andar ki jagah. Chalo, pehle dekhte hain ki patti ke alawa aur kaun-se hisse hare hote hain.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: jasoos ki file ke paanch panne (Priestley, Ingenhousz, Sachs, Engelmann, van Niel); hare tane se oxygen ke bulbule, chloroplast ke daane mein glucose; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: kaam chloroplast wale hare hisson mein'. Phir ek poora paudha (jad, tana, patti, phool, phal) aur sawaal ka dabba 'Photosynthesis kahan-kahan? Sirf patti?'. Patti par sahi ka nishaan, phir doosre hare hisson par chamak; 'sirf patti' par kaata hua nishaan. Patti se cell, cell se chloroplast, chloroplast ke andar tak zoom ke chaar padaav, har padaav par label: organ, cell, organelle, chloroplast ke andar.
Kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.2) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; organ aur organelle, do paimaane ki jagah.
Yaad: 11.2-k14, 11.2-k7
p006-i4 You would of course answer: in ‘the green leaf’ or ‘in the chloroplasts’, based on what you earlier read in Chapter 8. You are definitely right. Photosynthesis does take place in the green leaves of plants but it does so also in other green parts of the plants.
example11.3-k2
Toh batao, paudhe ke aur kaun-se hisse aapko hare dikhte hain? Hare hisse aapne roz dekhe hain. Naye paudhon ke hare tane, phool ke neeche ki hari pattiyan, aur kachche hare phal. Phool ke neeche ki in chhoti hari pattiyon ko sepals kehte hain. Khilne se pehle yahi kali ko dhak kar rakhti hain. In sab mein photosynthesis ho sakta hai. Inki cells mein bhi chloroplast hote hain, aur in tak roshni pahunchti hai. Ab ek mazedaar misaal dekho: registan ka nagphani, jise Opuntia bhi kehte hain. Nagphani par hari pattiyan dikhti hi nahi. Uske nukile kaante hi uski pattiyan hain, jo badal kar kaante ban gayi hain. Toh jab hari pattiyan hi nahi, toh nagphani apna khana kahan banata hai? Nagphani apna khana apne chapte, mote aur hare tane mein banata hai. Paudhon ki banawat wale chapter mein aapne padha tha ki Opuntia ka tana chapta aur hara ho jaata hai. Usme chlorophyll hota hai. Registan mein chaudi pattiyan bahut paani uda deti hain. Isliye wahan pattiyan kaante ban gayi, aur khana banane ka kaam hare tane ne sambhal liya. Phir bhi zyadatar paudhon mein sabse zyada khana patti hi banati hai. Woh chapti aur chaudi hoti hai, isliye sabse zyada roshni pakadti hai. Toh niyam yaad rakho: jo hissa hara hai aur jis par roshni padti hai, woh khana bana sakta hai.
Screen par: Ek phoolon wala paudha: naya hara tana, phool ke neeche hari sepals (label: sepals), ek kachcha hara phal; har hare hisse ke andar chhote hare chloroplast ke daane aur upar se aati roshni. Phir registan mein nagphani (Opuntia): nukile kaante (label: badli hui pattiyan) aur chapta, mota, hara tana (label: khana yahan banta hai); registan ki dhoop mein chaudi patti se udta paani. Aakhir mein ek chaudi patti par sabse zyada roshni ke teer.
Kitaab se bahar: Opuntia (nagphani) ki misaal, paudhon ki banawat wale chapter se; registan mein pattiyon ka kaante banna.
p006-i5 Can you name some other parts where you think photosynthesis may occur?
samjhana11.3-k3
Chalo, ab patti ke andar chalte hain, kyunki sabse zyada khana wahi banta hai. Patti ki upar aur neeche, dono taraf, cells ki ek patli bahari parat hoti hai. Ise epidermis kehte hain.
