In dono paraton ke beech jo hari cells bhari hoti hain, unhe mesophyll kehte hain. Naam todo toh matlab khul jaata hai: meso yaani beech, aur phyll yaani patti. Yaani patti ke beech ka hissa. Mesophyll ki cells mein bahut saare chloroplast hote hain. Ek cell mein aksar 20 se 40 tak. Isliye samjho ki patti ka asli khana banane wala hissa yahi mesophyll hai. Yaad karo, plant cell ke chaaron taraf ek mazboot deewar hoti hai, jise cell wall kehte hain. Mesophyll cell ka zyadatar beech ka hissa ek bada vacuole ghere rehta hai. Yeh paani se bhari ek thaili hai. Isliye chloroplast cell mein bikhre nahi rehte. Aksar woh deewaron ke saath-saath ek line mein lag jaate hain. Par chloroplast deewaron ke saath hi kyun lagte hain? Deewaron ke saath woh isliye lagte hain, taaki unhe aane wali roshni theek matra mein mile. Bahar se aakar cell par girne wali roshni ko incident light kehte hain. Incident yaani aakar padne wala. Ab ek shabd par gaur karo: chloroplast ko roshni sabse zyada nahi, sabse theek matra mein chahiye. Kaam ke liye sabse theek matra ko optimum kehte hain. Na itni kam ki kaam dheema pade, na itni zyada ki nuksaan ho. Kyunki bahut tez roshni chlorophyll aur chloroplast ke hisson ko nuksaan pahuncha sakti hai. Exam mein yeh baareek baat pakdi jaati hai: optimum ka matlab maximum, yaani sabse zyada, nahi hai. Socho, sardi ki subah hum dhoop ki taraf munh karke baithte hain. Par garmi ki dopahar mein dhoop se bachkar side ho jaate hain. Toh kya chloroplast bhi roshni ke hisaab se apni jagah badalte hain? Haan, chloroplast bhi jagah badalte hain; ab yahi dekhte hain.
Screen par: Ek patti ka cross-section: upar aur neeche patli epidermis ki parat (label), beech mein hari cells (label: mesophyll = meso 'beech' + phyll 'patti'). Ek mesophyll cell paas se: mazboot cell wall, beech mein bada paani bhara vacuole, aur kinaaron par deewar ke saath line mein lage 20-40 hare chloroplast; upar se aati roshni ka teer (label: incident light). Ek paimaana: kam, optimum (beech mein hara), bahut zyada (laal); neeche 'optimum ≠ maximum'. Kone mein do chhote chitra: sardi ki subah dhoop ki taraf munh karke baitha bachcha, garmi ki dopahar mein dhoop se bachkar side baitha bachcha.
Terms: mesophyll = Patti ki upar aur neeche ki epidermis ke beech ka hara bhaag; meso = beech, phyll = patti.; incident light = Bahar se aakar kisi satah par girne wali roshni; incident = aakar padne wala.; optimum = Kaam ke liye sabse theek matra, na kam na zyada; sabse zyada (maximum) nahi.
Kitaab se bahar: Ek mesophyll cell mein 20 se 40 chloroplast (cell wale chapter se); bada vacuole; sardi-garmi ki dhoop ki misaal; optimum aur maximum ka farq.
p006-i6 You would recollect from previous unit that the mesophyll cells in the leaves, have a large number of chloroplasts. Usually the chloroplasts align themselves along the walls of the mesophyll cells, such that they get the optimum quantity of the incident light.
ruko, socho11.3-k4
Pehle chloroplast ki shakal pakki kar lo. Woh gend jaisa gol nahi, masoor ke daane jaisa chapta hota hai. Isliye uska ek chauda, chapta chehra hota hai, aur ek patla kinaara. Ab do shabd yaad karo. Parallel yaani saath-saath chalne wali do rekhaayein, jo kabhi nahi miltin, jaise rail ki do patriyan. Aur perpendicular yaani ek-doosre ko seedha, nabbe degree par kaatne wali, jaise farsh aur deewar. Ab ek mesophyll cell socho, jis par roshni seedhe upar se neeche aa rahi hai. Is cell ki upar aur neeche ki deewarein roshni ko seedha kaat-ti hain. Yaani yeh roshni ke perpendicular hain. Aur bagal ki deewarein roshni ke saath-saath upar se neeche chalti hain. Yaani yeh roshni ke parallel hain. Ek clue lo: chapta chehra roshni ki taraf ho toh zyada roshni pakdi jaati hai. Sirf kinaara ho toh kam. Ruko, pehle khud socho: kam roshni mein chloroplast apna chapta chehra kis taraf rakhenge, aur tez roshni mein kis taraf? Aapne socha hoga ki kam roshni mein chehra roshni ki taraf, aur tez roshni mein kinaara; aap bilkul sahi ho. Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar aur neeche ki deewaron par jama ho jaate hain. Woh apna chapta chehra un deewaron ke parallel rakhte hain. Tab unka chehra roshni ke perpendicular hota hai. Isliye woh girti hui roshni ka zyada se zyada hissa pakad lete hain. Tez roshni mein yahi chloroplast khisak kar bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain. Chapta chehra ab un deewaron ke parallel rehta hai. Bagal ki deewarein roshni ke parallel hain. Isliye ab sirf patla kinaara roshni ki taraf rehta hai, aur kam roshni pakdi jaati hai. Is tarah tez roshni ka nuksaan bach jaata hai. Yeh wahi garmi ki dopahar mein side ho jaane wali baat hai. Gaur karo, dono haalaton mein chloroplast kisi na kisi deewar ke parallel hi lete hain. Farq bas itna hai ki kaun-si deewar. Exam mein aisa sawaal aaye toh pehle roshni ka teer aur deewar bana lo. Phir chehra lagao, uljhan khatam ho jaayegi. Paudha khud hil nahi sakta. Par uske chloroplast cell ke andar khisak kar roshni ke hisaab se jagah badal lete hain.
Screen par: Masoor ka daana aur uske paas chapta chloroplast: chauda chehra aur patla kinaara. Rail ki do patriyan (label: parallel) aur farsh-deewar ka 90° kona (label: perpendicular). Ek mesophyll cell, upar se neeche aati roshni ke teer; upar-neeche ki deewarein 'roshni ke perpendicular', bagal ki deewarein 'roshni ke parallel'. Sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir do cell saath-saath: 'kam roshni' mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par, chehra roshni ki taraf; 'tez roshni' mein bagal ki deewaron par, kinaara roshni ki taraf. Aakhir mein kaagaz par roshni ka teer aur deewar banata haath.
Kitaab se bahar: Kam aur tez roshni mein chloroplast ka khisakna, deewar aur roshni ki disha ke saath; masoor ke daane ki misaal.
Jawab: Kam roshni mein chloroplast upar-neeche ki deewaron par lagte hain: chapta chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur incident light ke perpendicular, taaki zyada roshni pakdein. Tez roshni mein woh bagal ki deewaron par chale jaate hain: chehra un deewaron ke parallel aur roshni ke bhi parallel, sirf kinaara roshni ki taraf, taaki kam roshni pakdein aur nuksaan na ho.
Aam galti: Galat soch: tez roshni mein bhi chloroplast zyada roshni pakadne ke liye chehra roshni ki taraf karenge; ya 'deewar ke parallel' ka matlab hamesha roshni ke perpendicular. Asal mein dekhna padta hai ki kaun-si deewar hai: upar-neeche ki deewar roshni ke perpendicular hai, bagal ki deewar roshni ke parallel.
p006-i7 When do you think the chloroplasts will be aligned with their flat surfaces parallel to the walls? When would they be perpendicular to the incident light?
samjhana11.3-k5
Ab chloroplast ke andar jhaanko: aapko wahan kya-kya dikhta hai? Chloroplast ke andar ka naksha aapne cell wale chapter mein dekha tha. Chalo, use phir se taaza karte hain. Membrane yaani ek patli jhilli, jo ek jagah ko doosri jagah se alag rakhti hai. Jaise ubale ande ke chhilke ke andar ki patli jhilli. Chloroplast ke chaaron taraf aisi do membranes ka gher hota hai, ek bahar wali aur ek andar wali. Is gher ke andar ek gaadha, jelly jaisa dravya bhara rehta hai. Kisi jagah ko bharne wale aise gaadhe dravya ko matrix kehte hain. Chloroplast ke is matrix ka naam stroma hai. Stroma ke beech membranes ka ek poora jaal faila hota hai. Ise chloroplast ka membrane system kehte hain. Is jaal mein chapti, band thailiyan hoti hain, jinhe thylakoid kehte hain. Dekho, yeh thailiyan ek ke upar ek aise rakhi hoti hain, jaise sikkon ka dher. Thylakoid ke aise ek dher ko granum kehte hain, aur kai dheron ko grana. Granum yaani daana, kyunki aam microscope mein yeh hare daanon jaise dikhte hain. Ek chloroplast mein aise kai grana hote hain, jo stroma mein yahan-wahan khade rehte hain. Ab ek dher se doosre dher tak patli, chapti membranes judi rehti hain. Jaise do imaaraton ko jodne wala pul. In jodne wali membranes ko stroma lamellae kehte hain. Lamella yaani patli parat, aur kai hon toh lamellae. Naam mein stroma isliye juda hai, kyunki yeh patli paratein dheron ke beech, stroma mein se hokar guzarti hain. Yahan ek baareek baat dhyan se samjho: stroma aur stroma lamellae ek cheez nahi hain. Stroma ek dravya hai jo sabko ghere hai. Stroma lamellae membranes hain, jo grana ko jodti hain. Toh chloroplast ke andar do tarah ki jagah hui. Ek, membranes, yaani grana aur stroma lamellae; doosri, unke chaaron taraf ka dravya, stroma.
Screen par: Ubale ande ke chhilke ke andar ki patli jhilli (membrane ki misaal). Chloroplast ka bada chitra: bahar do parat ka gher (bahar wali, andar wali membrane); andar gaadha dravya (label: matrix = stroma). Stroma mein chapti band thailiyan (label: thylakoid) sikkon ke dher ki tarah (label: ek dher = granum, kai = grana); halke microscope mein wahi dher hare daanon jaise. Dheron ko jodti patli membranes pul ki tarah (label: stroma lamellae, lamella = patli parat). Do alag rang: membranes hari, stroma halka peela; neeche 'stroma ≠ stroma lamellae'.
Terms: stroma = Chloroplast ke andar membranes ke chaaron taraf bhara gaadha dravya (matrix).; thylakoid = Chloroplast ke andar ki chapti, band membrane wali thaili.; grana = Thylakoid ke sikkon jaise dher; ek dher granum, kai dher grana; granum = daana.; stroma lamellae = Grana ko jodne wali patli, chapti, bina dher wali membranes; lamella = patli parat.
Kitaab se bahar: Thylakoid, granum ka arth (daana) aur lamella ka arth; ande ki jhilli ki misaal; chloroplast ka do membrane wala gher (cell wale chapter se).
p006-i8 You have studied the structure of chloroplast in Chapter 8. Within the chloroplast there is membranous system consisting of grana, the stroma lamellae, and the matrix stroma (Figure 11.2).
figure11.3-k6
Ab Figure 11.2 dekho; isme chloroplast ke andar ka yahi naksha bana hai. Yeh chitra ek khaas microscope ki photo se banaya gaya hai, jise electron microscope kehte hain. Microscope chhoti cheezon ko bada karke dikhata hai. Electron microscope itna bada dikhata hai ki chloroplast ki membranes bhi saaf dikh jaati hain. Electron microscope se li gayi photo ko electron micrograph kehte hain. Micro yaani chhota, aur graph yaani chitra. Yeh photo poore chloroplast ki nahi, uske ek bahut patle kate hue tukde ki hai. Jaise aam ko beech se kaatne par andar ka hissa dikhta hai. Andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kaate gaye aise patle tukde ko section kehte hain. Photo mein bahut kuch ek saath dikhta hai, isliye usse saaf rekhaon wala chitra banaya gaya. Aise chitra ko Diagrammatic representation kehte hain, yaani diagram ke roop mein dikhana. Isliye figure ke neeche likha hai: chloroplast ke section ki electron micrograph ka diagrammatic representation. Har label se ek patli rekha nikalti hai, jo theek us hisse tak jaati hai. Is rekha ke sire ko dekh kar hissa pehchaano. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo: sabse bahar ki rekha par likha hai Outer membrane, yaani bahar ki jhilli. Uske theek andar likha hai Inner membrane, yaani andar ki jhilli. Dono milkar chloroplast ka do parat wala gher banati hain. Ab andar sikkon jaise dher dekho; unpar likha hai Grana. Figure mein aise kai dher bane hain. In dheron ko jodti patli rekha par likha hai Stromal lamella. Yahan ek hi patti dikhayi gayi hai, isliye ekvachan lamella likha hai. Inke chaaron taraf ki jagah par likha hai Stroma. Chitra mein yeh khaali dikhti hai, par asal mein gaadhe dravya se bhari hai. Stroma mein chhote-chhote bindu dikhte hain, aur unpar likha hai Ribosomes. Ribosomes woh chhote kan hain jo protein banate hain. Chloroplast ke ribosomes chhote, 70S, hote hain, aur apne kuch protein yahin banate hain. Ek naarangi, andaakaar daana dikhta hai, jisme gole ke andar gole bane hain. Uspar likha hai Starch granule, yaani starch ka daana. Neeche ki taraf ek bhoori, gol boond par likha hai Lipid droplet. Lipid yaani charbi aur tel jaisi cheezein. Yeh starch nahi hai, isliye chitra ki har gol cheez ko starch mat samajhna. Gaur karo, is figure mein kul aath label hain, par koi teer nahi. Yeh sirf banawat dikhata hai, kaam kaise chalta hai yeh nahi.
Screen par: Figure 11.2 ka saaf, bada chitra. Pehle electron microscope aur uski dhundhli kaali-safed photo; phir aam ko beech se kaatne ka chhota chitra (label: section); phir photo se bana saaf rekhaon wala diagram (label: diagrammatic representation) aur figure ke neeche ka naam. Phir ungli har leader line ke sire par baari-baari: Outer membrane, Inner membrane (do parat ka gher), Grana (kai sikkon jaise dher), Stromal lamella (dheron ko jodti ek patti), Stroma (gaadhe dravya se bhara), Ribosomes (chhote bindu, label 70S), Starch granule (naarangi, gole ke andar gole), Lipid droplet (bhoori boond, saath mein 'starch nahi'). Aakhir mein 'aath label, koi teer nahi'.
Terms: electron micrograph = Electron microscope se li gayi bahut badi photo; micro = chhota, graph = chitra.; section = Andar ki banawat dikhane ke liye kisi cheez ka kaata gaya patla tukda.; Diagrammatic representation = Kisi photo ya cheez ko saaf rekhaon wale chitra (diagram) ke roop mein dikhana.
Kitaab se bahar: Electron microscope aur section ka matlab; chloroplast ke 70S ribosomes; lipid aur starch ka farq; figure mein koi teer nahi.
p006-i12 Section of a chloroplast shown as an oval bounded by outer and inner membranes. Inside the stroma are several stacks of flattened membranes, the grana, connected by stromal lamellae. Small dots represent ribosomes; orange concentrically drawn bodies are starch granules; larger brown dots include the labelled lipid droplet. Eight leader lines identify structures; there are no process arrows.
samjhana11.3-k7
Ab asli sawaal: chloroplast ke andar photosynthesis ka kaam hota kahan hai, membranes par ya stroma mein? Photosynthesis ka kaam chloroplast mein dono jagah hota hai, membranes par bhi aur stroma mein bhi. Par dono jagah ka kaam alag hai. Ise ek cheeni mill ki tarah samjho. Mill ka ek hissa bijli banata hai, aur doosra hissa us bijli se cheeni banata hai. Jab ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baant kar har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale, toh ise division of labour kehte hain. Yaani kaam ka bantwara. Ab dekho, chloroplast mein bhi kaam ka ekdum saaf bantwara hai. Membranes ka kaam hai roshni ki energy ko pakad lena, jaise jaal mein machhli pakdi jaati hai. Roshni pakadne wala chlorophyll inhi membranes mein jada hota hai. Isliye roshni wala kaam yahin hota hai. Pakdi hui is energy se membranes do khaas molecule banati hain: ATP aur NADPH. Dekho, ATP ek molecule hai jo energy ko apne andar band karke chalta hai. Jahan kaam ho, wahan woh yeh energy de deta hai. Yaad karo, molecules ke andar band energy ko humne chemical energy kaha tha. ATP yahi chemical energy lekar chalta hai. Aur NADPH ek molecule hai jo electron aur hydrogen lekar chalta hai. Yaad karo, kisi molecule mein hydrogen judne ko humne reduce hona kaha tha. NADPH yahi hydrogen aur electron dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai. Inke poore naam, aur yeh dono bante kaise hain, yeh aage light reaction mein dekhenge. Abhi ke liye bas itna jaan lo. Ab stroma ka kaam dekho: stroma mein sugar banti hai. Yeh kaam enzymes karte hain. Enzyme ek tarah ka protein hai, jo reaction ko tez kar deta hai aur khud nahi badalta. Enzymes ki madad se chalne wali aisi reactions ko enzymatic reactions kehte hain. Stroma ki enzymatic reactions hawa se aayi CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banati hain. Aur yahi sugar aage starch banati hai, jo starch granule ban kar stroma mein jama rehti hai. Par sugar banane mein energy aur hydrogen, dono lagte hain. Yeh dono stroma ko membranes ke banaye ATP aur NADPH se milte hain. Par yeh dono kaam ek hi jagah kyun nahi hote? Dono kaam ek jagah isliye nahi hote, kyunki roshni pakadne wale molecules membrane par jade rehte hain. Yaad karo, opening mein humne dekha tha ki yeh molecules bikhre nahi, sahi kram mein lage hote hain. Jabki sugar banane wale enzymes stroma ke dravya mein ghule rehte hain. Wahin CO₂ aur baaki saamaan unke paas pahunchta rehta hai. Toh membranes is cheeni mill ka bijlighar hain, aur stroma woh floor jahan cheeni banti hai.
Screen par: Chloroplast ka cutaway, do hisse do rang mein: hari membranes (grana aur stroma lamellae) aur unke chaaron taraf halka peela stroma. Kone mein cheeni mill ka chhota chitra: ek taraf bijlighar, doosri taraf cheeni banane wala floor; label 'division of labour = kaam ka bantwara'. Roshni ke teer membranes par girte, jaal mein phansi energy; membranes se do moti nikalte (labels: ATP = energy lekar chalne wala; NADPH = electron + hydrogen lekar chalne wala) aur stroma mein jaate. Stroma mein enzymes (chhote protein) CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banate, sugar se starch granule. Membrane par pigment molecules ek kram mein jade, aur stroma mein enzymes tairte hue. Aakhir mein jod: membranes = bijlighar, stroma = cheeni wala floor.
Terms: division of labour = Ek bade kaam ko hisson mein baantna, jisme har hissa ek hi kaam sambhale; kaam ka bantwara.; ATP = Ek molecule jo chemical energy apne andar lekar chalta hai aur jahan kaam ho wahan de deta hai.; NADPH = Electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule, jo inhe dekar doosre molecule ko reduce karta hai.; enzymatic reactions = Enzymes ki madad se chalne wali reactions; enzyme = reaction tez karne wala protein.
Kitaab se bahar: Cheeni mill ki misaal; ATP aur NADPH ka abhi ke liye matlab (poora naam aage); enzyme kya hai; sugar ka carbon CO₂ se; dono kaam alag jagah kyun.
Yaad: 0-k12, 11.2-k10
p006-i9 There is a clear division of labour within the chloroplast. The membrane system is responsible for trapping the light energy and also for the synthesis of ATP and NADPH. In stroma, enzymatic reactions synthesise sugar, which in turn forms starch.
analogy11.3-k8
Ab is cheeni mill ko poora khol kar dekho, taaki chloroplast ka har hissa ek saath yaad rahe. Mill ke chaaron taraf do chaardiwari hain; yeh chloroplast ki outer aur inner membrane hain. Mill ka bijlighar koyle se nahi, dhoop se chalta hai. Yeh grana ki membranes hain, jahan roshni pakdi jaati hai. Bijlighar ke alag-alag kamre galiyaron se jude hain; yeh galiyare stroma lamellae hain. Bijlighar se do cheezein floor tak jaati hain. ATP kaam ki energy deta hai, aur NADPH hydrogen aur electron deta hai. Mill ka bada floor stroma hai. Wahan ki machines enzymes hain, jo CO₂ se sugar banati hain. Jo cheeni turant kaam nahi aati, woh boriyon mein bhar kar godown mein rakh di jaati hai. Yeh boriyaan starch granule hain. Mill ki apni chhoti workshop bhi hai, jo machines ke purze banati hai. Yeh ribosomes hain, jo protein banate hain. Dekho, is mill mein har hissa ek hi kaam karta hai, isliye poora kaam tez aur bina gadbad chalta hai. Par yaad rakho, asli mill mein cheeni ganne se banti hai. Chloroplast ki sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai. Aur ATP aur NADPH khud sugar nahi bante. Woh energy aur hydrogen dekar khaali ho jaate hain, aur membrane par laut kar phir bhar jaate hain.
Screen par: Ek cheeni mill ka bada chitra, har hissa chloroplast ke hisse se juda: do chaardiwari = outer aur inner membrane; dhoop se chalta bijlighar = grana ki membranes; bijlighar ke kamron ke beech galiyare = stroma lamellae; bijlighar se floor tak do tokriyan = ATP (energy) aur NADPH (hydrogen + electron); bada floor = stroma, machines = enzymes, CO₂ andar aati, sugar banti; godown ki boriyaan = starch granule; chhoti workshop = ribosomes (protein). Kone mein 'mill: ganna → cheeni' aur 'chloroplast: CO₂ → sugar'; khaali tokriyan wapas bijlighar jaati hain.
Kitaab se bahar: Cheeni mill ki poori misaal, aur woh kahan tak sahi hai.
Kya kis jaisa: Outer aur inner membrane = mill ki do chaardiwari; grana ki membranes = dhoop se chalne wala bijlighar; stroma lamellae = bijlighar ke kamron ko jodne wale galiyare; ATP aur NADPH = bijlighar se floor tak jaane wali do cheezein (kaam ki energy, aur hydrogen-electron); stroma = mill ka floor; enzymes = floor ki machines; starch granule = godown ki boriyaan; ribosomes = purze banane wali workshop.
Kahan tootti hai: Mill ki cheeni ganne se banti hai, par chloroplast ki sugar ka carbon hawa ki CO₂ se aata hai. ATP aur NADPH sugar ka kachcha maal nahi hain; woh sirf energy aur hydrogen-electron dekar khaali hote hain aur membrane par phir bhare jaate hain. Mill ki bijli taar se bahti hai, jabki ATP aur NADPH molecule hain jo stroma mein ghoom kar pahunchte hain.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.3-k9
Ab in dono kaamon ke apne-apne naam bhi hain, aur yeh naam aapko pakke yaad rakhne hain. Socho, membranes wali reactions roshni padte hi chal padti hain. Roshni hi unhe seedhe dhakelti hai. Jaise cheeni mill ka bijlighar tabhi chalta hai, jab us par dhoop padti hai. Jo reactions seedhe roshni se chalti hain, unhe light reactions kehte hain. Inka doosra naam photochemical reactions hai. Photo yaani roshni, aur chemical yaani rasayanik; yaani roshni se chalne wali rasayanik reactions. Yeh wahi membrane system hai jahan chlorophyll roshni sokhta hai, aur jahan ATP aur NADPH bante hain. Inhe light reactions isliye kehte hain, kyunki roshni band hote hi yeh pal bhar mein ruk jaati hain. Light reactions mein electron ka safar kaise chalta hai, yeh hum aage kholenge.
Screen par: Thylakoid ki membrane par roshni girte hi chamak aur kaam shuru; roshni hatate hi sab ruk jaata. Kone mein cheeni mill ka bijlighar: dhoop aate hi chalta, dhoop jaate hi band. Label: light reactions = photochemical reactions (photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik). Membrane mein chlorophyll roshni sokhta, ATP aur NADPH ke moti bante. Aakhir mein membrane par electron ke chhote safar par '?'.
Terms: light reactions = Chloroplast ki membranes mein seedhe roshni se chalne wali reactions.; photochemical reactions = Light reactions ka doosra naam; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
Kitaab se bahar: Roshni band hote hi light reactions ka turant rukna; electron ka safar aage.
Yaad: 0-k12
p006-i10 The former set of reactions, since they are directly light driven are called light reactions (photochemical reactions).
samjhana11.3-k10
Ab doosra kaam dekho, stroma wala: kya ise bhi seedhe roshni chahiye? Stroma ke kaam ko seedhe roshni ki zaroorat nahi, kyunki uske enzymes roshni pakadte hi nahi. Par use light reactions mein bane ATP aur NADPH chahiye. Inke bina sugar ban hi nahi sakti. Matlab yeh kaam roshni par seedhe nahi tika, balki ATP aur NADPH ke zariye tika hai. Seedhe kisi par tikna direct dependence hai, aur kisi doosre ke zariye tikna indirect dependence. Isi farq ko dikhane ke liye in reactions ko dark reactions kehte hain. Inka doosra naam carbon reactions hai, kyunki inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai. Ab cheeni mill yaad karo. Floor ki machines dhoop nahi dekhti, par bijlighar ki bijli se hi chalti hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: shaam ko jab bijlighar band ho jaaye, toh floor kitni der chalega? Aapne socha hoga ki bas thodi der, jab tak bachi hui bijli khatam na ho; bilkul theek. Waise hi andhera hote hi ATP aur NADPH banna ruk jaata hai. Phir thodi der mein dark reactions bhi ruk jaati hain. Aksar log naam sunkar sochte hain ki dark reactions andhere mein hoti hain, par aisa nahi hai. Samjho, dark reactions ko andhere ki koi zaroorat nahi. Yeh zyadatar din mein, light reactions ke saath-saath chalti hain. Yaad karo, light-dependent yaani jo roshni ke bina na chale. Dark reactions bhi light-dependent hain, bas ATP aur NADPH ke zariye. Toh phir aisa bhatkane wala naam kyun rakha gaya? Aisa naam isliye rakha gaya, kyunki yeh by convention chala aa raha hai. By convention yaani bahut pehle se sabka maana hua naam, chahe woh poori baat na bataye. Toh 'dark' ka matlab andhera nahi, sirf itna ki roshni inhe seedhe nahi chalati. Exam ka yeh sabse bada jaal hai: koi kahe ki dark reactions raat mein hi hoti hain, toh woh galat hai. ATP aur NADPH se stroma mein sugar kaise banti hai, yeh bhi hum aage dekhenge.
Screen par: Stroma mein enzymes, jin par roshni ka koi teer nahi; membranes se ATP aur NADPH ke moti stroma mein aate, unse sugar banti. Do teer: roshni → membranes (seedha, label 'direct') aur roshni → ATP + NADPH → stroma (ghuma kar, label 'indirect'). Label: dark reactions = carbon reactions; CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta. Cheeni mill: shaam ko bijlighar band, floor ki machines thodi der chal kar ruk jaati; sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir chloroplast par din ka sooraj: light reactions aur dark reactions saath-saath chalti; 'dark = andhera?' par kaata hua nishaan, aur neeche chhota label 'by convention: purana maana hua naam'.
Terms: dark reactions = Stroma ki sugar banane wali reactions, jo seedhe roshni se nahi chalti par light reactions ke ATP aur NADPH par tiki hain; andhere mein nahi hoti.; by convention = Bahut pehle se chala aa raha, sabka maana hua naam ya tareeka.; carbon reactions = Dark reactions ka doosra naam; inmein CO₂ ka carbon sugar mein judta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Direct aur indirect dependence ka matlab; carbon reactions naam ki wajah; andhere mein dark reactions ka thodi der mein ruk jaana; cheeni mill ki shaam.
Yaad: 11.2-k10
p006-i11 The latter are not directly light driven but are dependent on the products of light reactions (ATP and NADPH). Hence, to distinguish the latter they are called, by convention, as dark reactions (carbon reactions). However, this should not be construed to mean that they occur in darkness or that they are not light-dependent.
asli duniya11.3-k11
Yeh baat aap roz ki sabzi mandi mein bhi pehchaan sakte ho. Aapne dekha hoga, kachcha tamatar hara hota hai, hari mirch hari hoti hai, aur matar ki phali bhi hari hoti hai. In sab ki cells mein chloroplast hote hain, isliye dhoop mein yeh bhi thoda khana bana lete hain. Toh pakne par tamatar laal kyun ho jaata hai? Pakne par tamatar isliye laal hota hai, kyunki uske chloroplast badal kar laal rang wale plastid ban jaate hain. Inhe chromoplast kehte hain. Saath mein uska chlorophyll toot jaata hai, isliye paka laal tamatar khana banana chhod deta hai. Ghar ke money plant ki hari bel bhi, pattiyon ke saath-saath, apne hare tane se thoda khana banati hai.
Screen par: Sabzi mandi ki tokriyan: kachche hare tamatar, hari mirch, matar ki hari phaliyan; har ek ke andar chhote hare chloroplast aur upar dhoop. Phir ek tamatar hara se naarangi se laal hota, andar hare chloroplast laal chromoplast mein badalte. Aakhir mein khidki par money plant ki hari bel, tane par bhi chloroplast ke daane.
Kitaab se bahar: Kachche hare phal, pakne par chloroplast ka chromoplast banna; money plant ka hara tana.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.3-k12
Ab aaj ka poora safar ek saath dohra lo. Humne dekha ki paudhe mein yeh kaam patti mein sabse zyada hota hai, aur hare tane, sepals aur kachche phal mein bhi. Patti ke andar yeh kaam mesophyll ki cells karti hain, jinme dheron chloroplast deewaron se lage rehte hain. Aapne dekha ki kam roshni mein chloroplast apna chehra roshni ki taraf karte hain, aur tez roshni mein apna kinaara. Chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes roshni pakad kar ATP aur NADPH banati hain. Yeh light reactions hain. Stroma ke enzymes unhi se sugar aur phir starch banate hain. Yeh dark reactions hain, jinhe andhere ki zaroorat nahi. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: yeh kaam kin hisson mein, aur unke andar kahan hota hai? Yeh kaam un sab hare hisson mein hota hai, jahan chloroplast hon aur roshni pahunche. Sabse zyada yeh patti ke mesophyll mein hota hai. Aur un hisson ke andar theek jagah hai chloroplast: roshni wala kaam uski membranes par, aur sugar wala kaam stroma mein. Bilkul us cheeni mill ki tarah, jahan bijlighar aur floor ne kaam aapas mein baant rakha hai. Par ek baat abhi baaki hai: membranes roshni pakadti kis cheez se hain, aur patti ka hara rang aakhir kis cheez ka hai? Patti ka hara rang kis cheez se banta hai, aur membranes roshni kaise pakadti hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Ek line mein aaj ka safar: poora paudha (patti, hara tana, sepals, kachcha phal chamakte) → patti ka cross-section (mesophyll) → cell ki deewaron se lage chloroplast (kam roshni mein chehra, tez roshni mein kinaara) → chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae (roshni → ATP + NADPH, label light reactions) aur stroma (sugar → starch, label dark reactions). Shuru ka sawaal phir aata hai, saath mein jawab: hare hisse, mesophyll, chloroplast, membranes aur stroma; kone mein cheeni mill ka bijlighar aur floor. Aakhir mein hari patti par sawaal ka nishaan, 'hara rang kis cheez ka?', jo khula rehta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.4 HOW MANY TYPES OF PIGMENTS ARE INVOLVED IN PHOTOSYNTHESIS? · 15 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 26 min
Maqsad: Agar patti ka hara rang sirf chlorophyll se aata, toh ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein hare ke itne alag-alag shade kyun hote?
Misaal: Ganga aur uski sahayak nadiyaan: Ganga = chlorophyll a (mukhya nadi, apne ilaake ki baarish khud batorti hai, jaise chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai); Yamuna, Ghaghra, Kosi jaisi sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids (accessory = sahayak); baarish ke alag-alag ilaake = roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein milna = pigments ki pakdi energy chlorophyll a tak pahunchna; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga ka kul paani = poora photosynthesis (action spectrum), Ganga ke apne ilaake ka paani = chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum
Kitab ki baatein (84): kitab kehti hai 29 · shabd ka matlab 20 · pehle ki zaroori baat 10 · figure ka nishaan 11 · teacher ki extra baat 14
f1 kitab kehti hai: shades Ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein bhi hare rang ke kai shades dikhte hain; sawaal hai ki aisa kyun aur kaise hota hai.
f2 kitab kehti hai: paper chromatography Is sawaal ka jawab paane ke liye kisi bhi hare paudhe ki patti ke pigments ko paper chromatography se alag kiya ja sakta hai.
f3 kitab kehti hai Chromatography se alag karne par dikhta hai ki patti ka rang ek pigment se nahi, chaar pigments se banta hai.
f4 shabd ka matlab: paper chromatography Paper chromatography: kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko alag karne ka tareeka; chromato = rang, graphy = likhna; solvent kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai aur har pigment ko alag oonchai tak le jaata hai.
f5 pehle ki zaroori baat: mixture Mixture: kai cheezon ka mel jisme har cheez apni pehchaan rakhti hai aur use alag kiya ja sakta hai; patti ke chloroplast mein kai pigments ka mel hota hai (leaves contain chloroplasts).
f6 pehle ki zaroori baat: chlorophyll Chlorophyll: patti ka hara pigment jo roshni pakadta hai; chloro = hara, phyll = patti; yeh chloroplast ki thylakoid membranes mein hota hai.
f7 teacher ki extra baat Chromatography ka naam aur tareeka 1906 mein roosi vaigyanik Mikhail Tsvet ne patti ke pigments alag karte hue diya tha.
f8 pehle ki zaroori baat: solvent Solvent: woh dravya jisme cheezein ghul jaati hain; chromatography mein acetone jaisa solvent patti ke pigments ghol leta hai aur kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai.
f9 teacher ki extra baat Palak ki patti ka ras solvent mein; kaagaz ke lambe tukde par neeche ek gaadha hara bindu; kaagaz ka sira solvent mein aur bindu solvent se upar; solvent upar chadhte hue pigments ko alag-alag oonchai tak le jaata hai: jo pigment zyada ghulta aur kaagaz se kam chipakta hai, woh upar; yeh wazan se tay nahi hota.
f10 kitab kehti hai: Chlorophyll a Chlorophyll a chromatogram mein chamakdaar ya neela-hara (bright or blue green) dikhta hai.
f11 kitab kehti hai: chlorophyll b Chlorophyll b peela-hara (yellow green) dikhta hai.
f12 kitab kehti hai: xanthophylls Xanthophylls peele (yellow) dikhte hain.
f13 kitab kehti hai: carotenoids Carotenoids peele se peela-naarangi (yellow to yellow-orange) dikhte hain.
f14 kitab kehti hai Ab dekhna hai ki photosynthesis mein alag-alag pigments kya kaam karte hain.
f15 shabd ka matlab: chromatogram Chromatogram: alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wala kaagaz, chromatography ka nateeja.
f16 shabd ka matlab: chlorophyll a; chlorophyll b Chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b: dono hare chlorophyll, par molecule thoda alag, isliye unka hara aur unki sokhi roshni thodi alag.
f17 shabd ka matlab: xanthophylls Xanthophylls: xantho = peela, phyll = patti; patti ke peele pigments.
f18 shabd ka matlab: carotenoids Carotenoids: naam carrot (gaajar) se; gaajar ka naarangi rang inhi ka; peele se naarangi pigments.
f19 teacher ki extra baat Chromatogram par neeche se upar: chlorophyll b, chlorophyll a, xanthophylls, aur sabse upar carotenoids.
f20 teacher ki extra baat Xanthophylls bhi carotenoids ke parivaar ke hain, par kaagaz par apni alag lakeer banate hain.
f21 teacher ki extra baat: shades Har patti mein yeh chaar pigments alag-alag matra mein hote hain: chlorophyll zyada to gehra hara, peele pigments ka hissa zyada to halka, peela-sa hara; isliye ek hi paudhe mein hare ke kai shades.
f22 teacher ki extra baat Patjhad mein chlorophyll toot jaata hai aur pehle se maujood peele-naarangi carotenoids dikhne lagte hain.
f23 teacher ki extra baat: stable Andhere mein rakhi patti peeli ya halki hari: chlorophyll lagaataar toot-ta hai aur naya chlorophyll banne ke liye roshni chahiye; peele carotenoids zyada tikau (stable) hain, isliye bache rehte hain.
f24 teacher ki extra baat Chhaanv wali pattiyan aksar zyada gehri hari: kam roshni pakadne ke liye unme chlorophyll zyada (chlorophyll b ka hissa bhi zyada); dhoop wali pattiyon mein chlorophyll kam aur bachaav wale peele pigments ka hissa zyada.
f25 teacher ki extra baat Andhere mein ugi aaloo ki konpal peeli-safed hoti hai aur roshni milte hi hari ho jaati hai.
f26 kitab kehti hai: specific wavelengths Pigments woh padarth hain jinme roshni ko sokhne ki kshamta hai, par sirf kuch khaas wavelengths (specific wavelengths) par.
f27 shabd ka matlab: pigments Pigments: rang wale padarth jo roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths sokhte hain aur baaki lauta dete hain; jo rang lautate hain, wahi unka dikhne wala rang.
f28 shabd ka matlab: absorb Absorb: sokhna; roshni ki energy ko apne andar le lena.
f29 shabd ka matlab: wavelength Wavelength: roshni ki lehar mein ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori; har rang ki apni wavelength, neele ki chhoti, laal ki lambi.
f30 pehle ki zaroori baat: colours of light Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai (light contains different colours); prism use indradhanush jaise rangon mein tod deta hai; har rang ki apni wavelength hai (colour corresponds to wavelength).
f31 pehle ki zaroori baat Koi cheez jis rang ki dikhti hai, woh rang woh lautati hai aur baaki sokh leti hai; kaali cheez lagbhag sab rang sokhti hai, safed sab lautati hai.
f32 teacher ki extra baat Patti hari isliye dikhti hai kyunki chlorophyll hari roshni sabse kam sokhta hai aur use lauta deta hai; hari roshni photosynthesis mein sabse kam kaam aati hai. Galat soch: hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai.
f33 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: duniya mein sabse zyada paya jaane wala plant pigment kaunsa hai? Jawab: chlorophyll a.
f34 teacher ki extra baat Chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur har hari alga mein hota hai; jahan bhi photosynthesis mein oxygen nikalti hai, wahan chlorophyll a hota hai.
f35 kitab kehti hai Graph (Figure 11.3a) dikhata hai ki chlorophyll a alag-alag wavelengths ki roshni kitni sokhta hai.
f36 kitab kehti hai: VIBGYOR Dikhne wali roshni (visible spectrum) ki wavelengths aur VIBGYOR ka kram aap jaante hain.
f37 shabd ka matlab: VIBGYOR VIBGYOR: dikhne wali roshni ke rangon ka kram: violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange, red (baingni, jamuni, neela, hara, peela, naarangi, laal); violet ki wavelength sabse chhoti, red ki sabse lambi.
f38 shabd ka matlab: visible spectrum Visible spectrum: roshni ka woh hissa jo aankh dekh sakti hai, lagbhag 400 se 700 nm.
f39 shabd ka matlab: nanometre Nanometre (nm): lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai; 1 nm = 10⁻⁹ metre, yaani metre ka ek arabvaan hissa.
f40 shabd ka matlab: absorption spectrum Absorption spectrum: ek pigment har wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska graph; absorption spectra iska bahuvachan.
f41 pehle ki zaroori baat: graph Graph: neeche ki leti line aur khadi line; line jitni oonchi, utna zyada; sabse oonchi jagah choti (peak, maximum), sabse neechi jagah minimum; har line (curve) ke paas uska naam (label).
f42 pehle ki zaroori baat: violet; indigo; blue; green; yellow; orange; red Visible light ke saat rang, chhoti se lambi wavelength ke kram mein (visible light has different wavelengths).
f43 pehle ki zaroori baat: horizontal axis; vertical axis Horizontal axis: neeche ki leti line; vertical axis: khadi line (horizontal and vertical graph axes).
f44 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: Figure 11.3a mein chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni kis wavelength (kis rang) par sokhta hai, aur kya kisi aur wavelength par bhi choti hai? Jawab: sabse zyada neele hisse mein, lagbhag 430 nm par; doosri choti laal hisse mein, lagbhag 660 nm par.
f45 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.3a mein teen absorption curves; chlorophyll a ki badi choti neele hisse mein lagbhag 420–430 nm par aur doosri laal hisse mein lagbhag 660–670 nm par; beech ke zyadatar hisse mein absorption kam.
f46 kitab kehti hai Chlorophyll b ki oonchi choti lagbhag 460 nm par aur chhoti laal choti lagbhag 645 nm par.
f47 kitab kehti hai Carotenoids zyadatar neele hisse mein sokhte hain, choudi chotiyan lagbhag 450–480 nm par, aur lagbhag 520 nm ke baad lagbhag zero.
f48 figure ka nishaan: Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments Khadi line ka label: chloroplast ke pigments roshni kitni sokhte hain; is par koi number nahi.

f50 figure ka nishaan: Chlorophyll b Chlorophyll b ka teer us line par jiski oonchi choti neele mein aur chhoti choti laal mein hai.
f51 figure ka nishaan: Carotenoids Carotenoids ka teer us line par jo sirf neele hisse mein oonchi hai, laal mein lagbhag zero.
f52 shabd ka matlab: absorbance Absorbance: koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap; absorb = sokhna.
f53 teacher ki extra baat Graph ki line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi; pigment ko naam wale teer se pehchaante hain. Pigment ka dikhne wala rang uska sokha hua rang nahi: carotenoids peele-naarangi dikhte hain par neeli roshni sokhte hain (pigment colour differs from absorbed colour).
f54 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.3b dikhata hai ki kin wavelengths par paudhe mein photosynthesis sabse zyada hota hai.
f55 kitab kehti hai Jin wavelengths par chlorophyll a sabse zyada sokhta hai, yaani neele aur laal hisse mein, wahin photosynthesis ki rate bhi zyada hai.
f56 shabd ka matlab: action spectrum Action spectrum: har wavelength par photosynthesis kitna hua, uska graph; action = kaam.
f57 pehle ki zaroori baat: O₂ release Photosynthesis mein oxygen (O₂) bahar nikalti hai (photosynthesis releases oxygen); jitna zyada photosynthesis, utni zyada O₂; Engelmann ke bacteria isi O₂ ke paas jama hue the.
f58 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.3b ki kaali line photosynthesis ki rate dikhati hai, jo O₂ nikalne se naapi gayi: neele hisse mein oonchi, hare se peela-hara hisse mein lagbhag 550–570 nm par sabse neechi, laal hisse mein lagbhag 680 nm par sabse oonchi, aur lagbhag 700 nm ke paas tezi se neeche.
f59 kitab kehti hai Beech ke hisse mein bhi photosynthesis ki rate zero nahi hoti.

f61 figure ka nishaan: O₂ O₂: oxygen ka molecule; neeche ka chhota 2 batata hai ki usme oxygen ke do atom hain.
f62 shabd ka matlab: rate of photosynthesis Rate of photosynthesis: ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua; rate = tay samay mein kitna kaam.
f63 kitab kehti hai: chief pigment Isliye chlorophyll a photosynthesis se juda chief pigment, yaani mukhya pigment, hai.
f64 shabd ka matlab: chief pigment Chief pigment: mukhya pigment, sabse bada kaam karne wala; chief ka matlab akela (sole) nahi.
f65 kitab kehti hai: superimposed Figure 11.3c mein photosynthesis ki rate ki kaali line aur chlorophyll a ke absorption ki line ek hi jagah ek ke upar ek rakhi hain (superimposed).
f66 kitab kehti hai Dono mein neele aur laal hisse mein chotiyan hain, par dono poori tarah nahi milti; jahan chlorophyll a kam sokhta hai, wahan bhi kaali line kuch upar rehti hai.
f67 kitab kehti hai Neeche ki leti line par 400, 500, 600 aur 700 ke nishaan; dono lines alag-alag cheezein naapti hain, isliye unki oonchai seedhe nahi milaate.
f68 figure ka nishaan: Rate of photosynthesis Kaali line ka naam Rate of photosynthesis: yahi action spectrum.
f69 figure ka nishaan: Absorption Doosri line ka naam Absorption: sirf chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum, saare pigments ka nahi.
f70 figure ka nishaan: Light absorbed Khadi line par likha label: Light absorbed.
f71 figure ka nishaan: Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm) Neeche ki leti line ka label: Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm), nishaan 400, 500, 600, 700.
f72 shabd ka matlab: superimposed Superimposed: tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhna; super = upar.
f73 kitab kehti hai: one-to-one overlap Sawaal: Figure 11.3c dekh kar kya chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur photosynthesis ke action spectrum mein poora one-to-one overlap hai? Jawab: nahi.
f74 shabd ka matlab: one-to-one overlap One-to-one overlap: har wavelength par dono lines ka ekdum saath-saath chalna, ek line doosri par poori chadh jaaye.
f75 kitab kehti hai Teeno graph milkar dikhate hain ki zyadatar photosynthesis spectrum ke neele aur laal hisse mein hota hai; kuch photosynthesis dikhne wali roshni ki baaki wavelengths par bhi hota hai.
f76 kitab kehti hai: accessory pigments Roshni pakadne ka mukhya pigment chlorophyll hai, par thylakoid ke doosre pigments, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids, bhi roshni sokhte hain aur energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain; inhe accessory pigments kehte hain.
f77 kitab kehti hai: wider range of wavelength Accessory pigments aane wali roshni ki zyada choudi range (wider range of wavelength) ko photosynthesis ke kaam laate hain.
f78 kitab kehti hai: photo-oxidation Accessory pigments chlorophyll a ko photo-oxidation se bachate bhi hain.
f79 shabd ka matlab: accessory pigments Accessory pigments: sahayak pigments; accessory = saath dene wala, madad karne wala.
f80 shabd ka matlab: photo-oxidation Photo-oxidation: tez roshni ki zyada energy se pigment ka kharaab hokar toot jaana; photo = roshni; oxidation = molecule ka apne electron ya hydrogen kho dena.
f81 teacher ki extra baat Carotenoids zyada energy ko garmi bana kar surakshit nikaal dete hain, isliye tez dhoop mein chlorophyll a bacha rehta hai.
f82 pehle ki zaroori baat: thylakoid Thylakoid: chloroplast ki chapti band thailiyan; inki membranes mein saare pigments baithe hain (chloroplast thylakoids).
f83 teacher ki extra baat Jis paudhe mein chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho, woh photosynthesis nahi kar payega: baaki pigments ki pakdi energy bhi chlorophyll a tak hi jaati hai aur aage ka kaam wahi shuru karta hai; chlorophyll b aur doosre pigments choudi range aur bachaav ke liye hain.
f84 figure ka nishaan: VIBGYOR Graph ki leti line par rang VIBGYOR ke kram mein baayein se daayein: violet sabse chhoti wavelength par, red sabse lambi par; isiliye neeli choti baayein aur laal choti daayein dikhti hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.4-k1
Pichhli baar humne photosynthesis ki jagah dhoondhi: paudhe ke hare hisse, sabse zyada patti ka mesophyll, aur uski cells mein chloroplast. Chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes roshni pakadti hain, aur stroma mein sugar banti hai. Yaad rakhein: roshni pakadne ka kaam chloroplast ki membranes par, yaani thylakoid ki thailiyon par, hota hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki in membranes mein aisa kya baitha hai jo roshni ko pakad leta hai. Pichhli film ke aakhir mein humne do baatein khuli chhodi thi. Pehli, patti ka hara rang kis cheez ka hai; doosri, membranes roshni kaise pakadti hain. Pehli baat par aap turant kahoge: hara rang chlorophyll ka hai, kyunki chloro yaani hara aur phyll yaani patti. Par ek baat par gaur karo: ek hi paudhe ki nayi pattiyan aksar halki hari hoti hain, purani gehri hari, aur kuch peeli-si. Agar patti ka hara rang sirf chlorophyll se aata, toh ek hi paudhe ki pattiyon mein hare ke itne alag-alag shade kyun hote? Hare ke itne shade kyun hote hain, yahi aaj ka pehla sawaal hai. Uske saath judega doosra sawaal: patti roshni pakadti kaise hai. Rang ka raaz kholne ka ek seedha tareeka hai: patti ke rang ko uske hisson mein tod kar dekho. Aapne shayad kaali sketch pen ki line geele kaagaz par dekhi ho. Paani upar chadhte hue usme se neela, baingni aur peela rang alag kar deta hai. Kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko aise alag karne ke tareeke ko paper chromatography kehte hain. Naam todo toh kaam samajh aata hai: chromato yaani rang, aur graphy yaani likhna. Kaagaz par har rang apni jagah khud likh deta hai. Yahi tareeka kisi bhi hare paudhe ki patti par lagao. Tab patti ke pigments ka mel khul jaata hai, aur har pigment alag dikhta hai. Dilchasp baat yeh hai ki chromatography bani hi patti ke pigments alag karne ke liye thi. 1906 mein roosi vaigyanik Mikhail Tsvet ne yeh tareeka diya. Toh aaj hum teen kadam chalenge. Pehle patti ke rang alag karenge, phir dekhenge ki kaun-sa rang kaun-si roshni pakadta hai, aur aakhir mein ki mukhya kaam kaun karta hai.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: hare paudhe se patti, patti ke mesophyll se cell, cell ke chloroplast; chloroplast ke andar grana aur stroma lamellae ki membranes par roshni ke teer, stroma mein sugar; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: roshni pakadne ka kaam thylakoid ki membranes par'. Phir ek hi paudhe ki teen pattiyan: nayi halki hari, purani gehri hari, ek peeli-si; sawaal ka dabba 'Itne shade kyun?'. Kaali sketch pen ki line wala geela kaagaz, paani chadhte hi neela, baingni aur peela rang alag hote. Label: paper chromatography (chromato = rang, graphy = likhna); kone mein '1906, Mikhail Tsvet'. Aakhir mein teen kadam ki patti: rang alag, kaun kaun-si roshni, mukhya kaun.
Terms: paper chromatography = Kaagaz par mile hue rangon ko alag karne ka tareeka; chromato = rang, graphy = likhna.
Kitaab se bahar: Pichhli film (11.3) ki yaad aur uska khula sawaal; nayi aur purani pattiyon ke shade; kaali syaahi ki chromatography; chromato + graphy; 1906 mein Mikhail Tsvet.
Yaad: 11.3-k12, 11.3-k5, 11.1-k2
p007-i2 Looking at plants have you ever wondered why and how there are so many shades of green in their leaves – even in the same plant? We can look for an answer to this question by trying to separate the leaf pigments of any green plant through paper chromatography. A chromatographic separation of the leaf pigments shows that the colour that we see in leaves is not due to a single pigment but due to four
example11.4-k2
Chalo, palak ki ek patti lekar yeh karke dekhte hain. Pehle hum patti ko acetone jaise solvent mein peeste hain; solvent yaani woh dravya jisme cheezein ghul jaati hain. Patti ke saare rang usme ghul jaate hain, aur gehra hara ras ban jaata hai. Ab kaagaz ka ek lamba tukda lo, aur uske neeche wale sire se thoda upar is ras ka ek gaadha hara bindu lagao. Kaagaz ka sira solvent mein dubao, par dhyan rahe ki bindu solvent se upar rahe. Ab dekho, solvent dheere-dheere kaagaz mein upar chadhta hai, jaise tissue paper ka kona paani mein dubane par paani upar chadh jaata hai. Chadhta hua solvent bindu ke rangon ko bhi apne saath le chalta hai. Par har rang ek jitna upar nahi jaata; aisa kyun? Har rang alag oonchai tak isliye jaata hai, kyunki har pigment ka ghulna alag hai. Jo solvent mein zyada ghulta hai aur kaagaz se kam chipakta hai, woh tez bhaagta hai. Jo pigment kam ghulta hai aur kaagaz se zyada chipakta hai, woh peechhe reh jaata hai. Dhyan do, yeh alag hona pigment ke wazan se nahi hota, ghulne aur chipakne se hota hai. Ab thodi der baad kaagaz ko dekho. Us ek hare bindu ki jagah wahan chaar alag rangon ki lakeerein ban gayi hain. Alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wale is kaagaz ko chromatogram kehte hain. Ab ungli rakh kar neeche se upar chalo: sabse neeche peela-hara rang hai, yeh chlorophyll b hai. Uske upar chamakdaar, neela-hara rang hai; yeh chlorophyll a hai. Phir peele rang ki lakeer aati hai; yeh xanthophylls hain. Aur sabse upar peele se naarangi rang ki lakeer hai; yeh carotenoids hain. Matlab jo ek hara bindu dikh raha tha, woh asal mein chaar pigments ka mel tha. Chlorophyll a aur chlorophyll b, dono hare chlorophyll hain; bas unka molecule thoda alag hai, isliye unka hara bhi thoda alag hai. Xanthophylls ka naam todo: xantho yaani peela, aur phyll yaani patti; matlab patti ka peela pigment. Carotenoids ka naam carrot, yaani gaajar, se aaya hai; gaajar ka naarangi rang inhi pigments ka hai. Xanthophylls bhi asal mein carotenoids ke hi parivaar ke hain, par kaagaz par apni alag lakeer banate hain.
Screen par: Lab ki mez: palak ki patti acetone mein peesi jaati, gehra hara ras banta; kaagaz ke lambe tukde par neeche ek gaadha hara bindu; tukde ka sira solvent mein, bindu solvent se upar. Solvent kaagaz mein upar chadhta (kone mein paani mein dooba tissue paper), saath mein rang upar khisakte; chhota label 'wazan se nahi: ghulna + chipakna'. Thodi der baad chaar lakeerein, neeche se upar ungli ke saath: peela-hara (chlorophyll b), chamakdaar neela-hara (chlorophyll a), peela (xanthophylls), peela-naarangi (carotenoids); poore kaagaz par label chromatogram. Naam ke hisse: xantho = peela, phyll = patti; carotenoids ke paas ek gaajar; xanthophylls aur carotenoids ek parivaar ke ghere mein.
Terms: chromatogram = Alag hue rangon ki lakeeron wala kaagaz, chromatography ka nateeja.; chlorophyll a = Chamakdaar ya neela-hara chlorophyll; photosynthesis ka mukhya pigment.; chlorophyll b = Peela-hara chlorophyll.; xanthophylls = Patti ke peele pigments; xantho = peela, phyll = patti.; carotenoids = Peele se naarangi pigments; naam carrot (gaajar) se.
Kitaab se bahar: Palak ki chromatography ke kadam (acetone jaisa solvent, bindu, solvent ka chadhna, tissue paper); ghulne aur chipakne se alag hona, wazan se nahi; chromatogram par lakeeron ka kram; naam ke hisse (xantho, carrot); xanthophylls carotenoid parivaar mein.
p007-i3 pigments: Chlorophyll a (bright or blue green in the chromatogram), chlorophyll b (yellow green), xanthophylls (yellow) and carotenoids (yellow to yellow-orange). Let us now see what roles various pigments play in photosynthesis.
samjhana11.4-k3
Ab aaj ke pehle sawaal par lautte hain. Aksar hum sochte hain ki patti ka hara rang sirf ek pigment, chlorophyll, ka hai, par aisa nahi hai. Patti ka rang chaar pigments ke mel se banta hai: do hare, aur do peele-naarangi. Aur har patti mein yeh chaaron alag-alag matra mein hote hain. Socho, hare rang mein thoda peela milao, toh hara halka aur peela-sa ho jaata hai. Aur hara zyada daalo, toh rang gehra ho jaata hai. Patti mein bhi yahi hota hai. Chlorophyll zyada ho toh patti gehri hari dikhti hai, aur peele pigments ka hissa zyada ho toh halki, peeli-si hari. Hare ke itne shade isliye hote hain, kyunki har patti mein in chaar pigments ka mel alag hai. Nayi aur purani patti, dhoop aur chhaanv ki patti, sab mein yeh mel badal jaata hai. Par sirf rang gin lena kaafi nahi hai. Asli baat yeh hai ki photosynthesis mein in alag-alag pigments ka kaam kya hai. Aur iske liye pehle hum samjhenge ki koi pigment asal mein karta kya hai.
Screen par: Ek taraf ek hara bindu, doosri taraf usse nikle chaar rang; 'sirf chlorophyll?' par kaata hua nishaan. Phir rang ki pyali mein hare mein peela milte hi hara halka hota, hara badhte hi gehra. Teen pattiyan (nayi, purani, chhaanv wali), har ek ke saath chaar pigments ki chhoti pattiyon ka alag-alag hissa: gehri hari patti mein chlorophyll ki patti lambi, halki hari mein peele pigments ki patti badi. Aakhir mein sawaal: 'In pigments ka kaam kya?'
Kitaab se bahar: Shades ka kaaran: chaar pigments ki alag-alag matra; rang milaane ki misaal.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i4
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.4-k4
Yaad karo, pigment ko humne rang dene wala padarth kaha tha, jo roshni ka kuch hissa sokh leta hai. Ab isi kuch hisse wali baat ko thoda khol kar dekhte hain. Safed roshni kai rangon ka mel hai; prism se bani indradhanush jaisi rangon ki patti aapne Engelmann ke prayog mein dekhi thi. Jab roshni kisi cheez par padti hai, toh woh cheez kuch rangon ko apne andar le leti hai. Baaki rangon ko woh lauta deti hai. Roshni ko apne andar le lene ko absorb karna kehte hain; absorb yaani sokhna. Jo rang lautta hai, wahi hamari aankh tak pahunchta hai, aur cheez hamein usi rang ki dikhti hai. Isliye kaali shirt dhoop mein jaldi garam hoti hai: woh lagbhag saare rang sokh leti hai. Safed shirt saare rang lauta deti hai, isliye kam garam hoti hai. Ab apni patti ko dekho: woh hari dikhti hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: toh chlorophyll kaun-si roshni sabse kam sokhta hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki hari roshni; bilkul sahi. Chlorophyll hari roshni sabse kam sokhta hai aur use lauta deta hai, isliye patti hari dikhti hai. Aksar log ulta sochte hain ki hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta hoga. Par asal mein yahi roshni sabse kam kaam aati hai. Yaad karo, Engelmann ke prayog mein bhi hari roshni ke paas sabse kam bacteria jama hue the. Matlab koi pigment har rang ki roshni barabar nahi sokhta; woh kuch khaas rangon ko hi chunta hai. Yeh rang ek-doosre se alag kaise hote hain, yeh samajhne ke liye roshni ki lehar socho. Talaab mein patthar phenko, toh paani par lehrein uthti hain: ek chot, phir neechai, phir agli chot. Roshni bhi lehron mein chalti hai. Ek lehar ki chot se agli lehar ki chot tak ki doori ko wavelength kehte hain. Har rang ki apni wavelength hoti hai: neeli roshni ki lehar chhoti, aur laal roshni ki lehar lambi. Ab pigments ki poori pehchaan suno: pigments woh padarth hain jo roshni ko sokh sakte hain, par sirf kuch khaas wavelengths par. Kuch khaas yaani specific; har pigment ki apni pasand ki wavelengths hoti hain, aur wahi uski pehchaan hai.
Screen par: Safed roshni prism se indradhanush jaisi patti mein bant-ti. Ek laal gend par roshni: laal teer wapas aankh tak, baaki rang gend mein samaate (label: absorb = sokhna). Dhoop mein kaali aur safed shirt: kaali par saare teer andar, safed se sab wapas. Hari patti par roshni: neele aur laal teer andar, hara teer wapas; sawaal ka dabba, bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). 'Hara pigment hari roshni sabse zyada sokhta?' par kaata hua nishaan; kone mein Engelmann ki alga, hare hisse par sabse kam bacteria. Talaab mein lehrein, chot se agli chot tak teer (label: wavelength); neeli roshni ki chhoti lehar, laal ki lambi. Neeche: pigments = kuch khaas (specific) wavelengths sokhne wale padarth.
Terms: absorb = Sokhna; roshni ki energy apne andar le lena.; wavelength = Roshni ki lehar mein ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori.; pigments = Roshni ko kuch khaas (specific) wavelengths par sokhne wale padarth.
Kitaab se bahar: Cheez ka rang = lautaaya hua rang; kaali aur safed shirt; patti hari kyun aur hari roshni sabse kam kaam ki kyun; talaab ki lehron se wavelength; neeli lehar chhoti, laal lambi.
Yaad: 11.1-k2, 11.2-k8
p007-i4 Pigments are substances that have an ability to absorb light, at specific wavelengths.
sawaal11.4-k5
Ab ek mazedaar sawaal: aap batao, poori duniya mein sabse zyada kaun-sa plant pigment milta hoga? Poori duniya mein sabse zyada milne wala plant pigment chlorophyll a hai. Kyunki chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur har hari alga mein hota hai, chahe woh khet mein ho ya samudra mein. Jahan bhi photosynthesis mein oxygen nikalti hai, wahan chlorophyll a zaroor milta hai. Isliye ab hum graphs mein sabse pehle chlorophyll a ko hi parkhenge.
Screen par: Duniya ka globe: hare khet, jungle aur samudra ki hari alga chamakte; sawaal ka dabba 'sabse zyada kaun-sa plant pigment?'; jawab ka label chlorophyll a, aur har jagah se oxygen ke bulbule.
Kitaab se bahar: Chlorophyll a har hare paudhe aur alga mein; oxygen chhodne wale photosynthesis mein hamesha.
p007-i5 Can you guess which is the most abundant plant pigment in the world?
samjhana11.4-k6
Kaun-sa pigment kis wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, yeh ek graph par saaf dikhta hai. Engelmann wale prayog mein humne absorption spectra ka naam suna tha: pigments kis rang ko kitna sokhte hain, uske naqshe. Ek akele pigment ke aise graph ko absorption spectrum kehte hain; spectra iska bahuvachan hai. Ab is graph ki do seedhi lines ko pehchaano. Neeche ki leti line, yaani horizontal axis, par roshni ki wavelength hai. Is line par rang baayein se daayein ek kram mein chalte hain, jise aap VIBGYOR naam se jaante ho. VIBGYOR yaani violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange aur red: baingni, jamuni, neela, hara, peela, naarangi aur laal. Violet ki taraf wavelength sabse chhoti hai, aur red ki taraf sabse lambi. Aankh ko dikhne wali yeh roshni, yaani visible spectrum, lagbhag 400 se 700 nanometre tak ki hai. Nanometre, yaani nm, lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai hai: ek nm metre ka ek arabvaan hissa hai, yaani 10⁻⁹ metre. Socho, aapke ek baal ki motai mein bhi roshni ki sau se zyada lehrein samaa jaayein. Ab khadi line, yaani vertical axis, par dikhaya jaata hai ki pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai. Line jitni oonchi, sokhna utna zyada; aur jahan line sabse oonchi ho, us jagah ko choti, yaani peak, kehte hain. Yahi graph Figure 11.3a hai; chalo, ab ise saath-saath padhte hain.
Screen par: Khaali graph banta hai: neeche leti line (horizontal axis) par baayein se daayein VIBGYOR ki rangeen patti, violet se red tak, neeche 400 se 700 nm; label visible spectrum. Kone mein ek baal ki motai, uske aar-paar bahut saari chhoti lehrein (nm = metre ka arabvaan hissa, 10⁻⁹ metre). Phir khadi line (vertical axis) uthti, label 'kitna sokha'; ek namoone ki line jiski sabse oonchi jagah par 'choti (peak)'. Upar label: absorption spectrum; aakhir mein 'Figure 11.3a'.
Terms: absorption spectrum = Ek pigment har wavelength ki roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska graph.; VIBGYOR = Dikhne wali roshni ke rangon ka kram: violet, indigo, blue, green, yellow, orange, red.; visible spectrum = Aankh ko dikhne wali roshni, lagbhag 400 se 700 nm.; nanometre (nm) = Lambaai ki bahut chhoti ikaai; 1 nm = 10⁻⁹ metre.
Kitaab se bahar: VIBGYOR ke Hindi naam; visible spectrum lagbhag 400–700 nm; nm = 10⁻⁹ metre aur baal ki motai; graph ki do lineon ka padhna, choti (peak).
Yaad: 11.2-k8
p007-i6 Let us study the graph showing the ability of chlorophyll a pigment to absorb lights of different wavelengths (Figure 11.3 a). Of course, you are familiar with the wavelength of the visible spectrum of light as well as the VIBGYOR.
figure11.4-k7
Figure 11.3a par ungli rakh kar chalo. Khadi line par likha hai: Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments. Absorbance yaani koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap; is line par koi number nahi diya gaya. Graph mein teen lines hain, aur har line par ek teer se pigment ka naam likha hai. Dhyan do, line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi hai; pigment ko sirf naam wale teer se pehchaano. Pehle Chlorophyll a naam wali line dekho. Ab aap batao: chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni kis wavelength par sokhta hai? Chlorophyll a sabse zyada roshni neele hisse mein, lagbhag 420 se 430 nm par, sokhta hai. Kya iski koi doosri choti bhi hai? Haan, iski doosri choti laal hisse mein hai, lagbhag 660 se 670 nm par. Beech mein, hare aur peele hisse mein, yeh line kaafi neeche rehti hai; yaani wahan sokhna kam hai. Ab Chlorophyll b naam wali line dekho: iski oonchi choti neele mein, lagbhag 460 nm par hai. Iski laal choti chhoti hai, lagbhag 645 nm par; dono chlorophyll ki chotiyan paas-paas hain, par ek jagah nahi. Aur Carotenoids naam wali line sirf neele hisse mein oonchi hai, lagbhag 450 se 480 nm par. Lagbhag 520 nm ke baad yeh line zameen se lag jaati hai; matlab carotenoids laal roshni lagbhag nahi sokhte. Yahan ek baareek baat chhupi hai: carotenoids dikhte peele-naarangi hain, par sokhte neeli roshni hain. Kyunki jo rang woh lautate hain, wahi hamein dikhta hai; sokha hua rang toh aankh tak pahunchta hi nahi.
Screen par: Figure 11.3a ka saaf, bada chitra; ungli ka nishaan har label par baari-baari: khadi line ka label 'Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments' (koi number nahi) → teen lines aur unke naam ke teer, chhota label 'line ka rang ≠ pigment ka rang' → Chlorophyll a ki line: neele mein lagbhag 420–430 nm par badi choti, laal mein lagbhag 660–670 nm par doosri, beech mein neechi → Chlorophyll b: lagbhag 460 nm par oonchi choti, lagbhag 645 nm par chhoti → Carotenoids: lagbhag 450–480 nm par chotiyan, lagbhag 520 nm ke baad zameen se lagi. Kone mein ek naarangi gaajar: neela teer andar, naarangi teer wapas.
Terms: absorbance = Koi pigment roshni kitni sokhta hai, uska naap.
Kitaab se bahar: Graph ki line ka rang pigment ka rang nahi; carotenoids naarangi dikhte hain par neeli roshni sokhte hain.
p007-i11 Figure 11.3a: three absorption curves against wavelength. The cyan chlorophyll a curve has a major blue-region peak near 420–430 nm and a red-region peak near 660–670 nm, with low absorption across much of the intervening region. The green chlorophyll b curve has a tall peak near 460 nm and a smaller red-region peak near 645 nm. The yellow carotenoid curve absorbs mainly in the blue region, with broad peaks around 450–480 nm, and falls to near baseline beyond about 520 nm. The vertical axis reads 'Absorbance of light by chloroplast pigments'. The wavelength scale is shared with the lower panels.
p007-i7 From Figure 11.3a can you determine the wavelength (colour of light) at which chlorophyll a shows the maximum absorption? Does it show another absorption peak at any other wavelengths too? If yes, which one?
samjhana11.4-k8
Sokhna ek baat hai; par kya sokhi hui har roshni se photosynthesis bhi utna hi hota hai? Sokhi hui roshni se photosynthesis kitna hota hai, yeh dekhne ke liye ek doosra graph banaya gaya, Figure 11.3b. Yaad karo, Engelmann ne alag-alag rangon mein photosynthesis naapa tha, aur us naqshe ko action spectrum kaha tha. Figure 11.3b bhi ek action spectrum hai; bas yeh aaj ke naapne ke tareekon se bana hai. Iski khadi line par likha hai: Rate of photosynthesis (measured by O₂ release). Rate yaani ek tay samay mein kitna kaam hua; toh yeh line batati hai ki ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua. Aur photosynthesis kitna hua, yeh nikalti hui O₂ se naapa gaya: jitni zyada oxygen nikli, utna zyada photosynthesis hua. O₂ yaani oxygen ka molecule; neeche likha chhota 2 batata hai ki isme oxygen ke do atom hain. Ab is line par ungli chalao, aur dekho: neele hisse mein yeh oonchi hai. Hare se peela-hara hisse mein, lagbhag 550 se 570 nm par, yeh sabse neeche aa jaati hai. Phir laal hisse mein, lagbhag 680 nm par, yeh phir sabse oonchi ho jaati hai. Lagbhag 700 nm ke paas yeh tezi se gir jaati hai. Par dhyan do, beech ke hisse mein bhi yeh line kabhi zero tak nahi girti. Ab aap dono graph milao; kya dikhta hai? Dono graph milane par dikhta hai ki jahan chlorophyll a sabse zyada sokhta hai, wahin photosynthesis ki rate bhi zyada hai. Yeh do jagah hain: neela hissa aur laal hissa. Matlab chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur action spectrum, dono ki chotiyan neele aur laal mein milti hain.
Screen par: Figure 11.3a halka padta, uske theek neeche Figure 11.3b (wahi wavelength ki line): khadi line ka label 'Rate of photosynthesis (measured by O₂ release)', kone mein ghadi aur patti se nikalte O₂ ke bulbule; O₂ = do oxygen atom. Kaali line par ungli: neele mein oonchi, lagbhag 550–570 nm par neechi (par zero se upar), lagbhag 680 nm par sabse oonchi, lagbhag 700 nm par tez girti. Phir dono graph upar-neeche: neele aur laal ki chotiyan seedhi lakeeron se judti, label 'absorption spectrum ↔ action spectrum'; kone mein Engelmann ka pehla action spectrum.
Terms: rate of photosynthesis = Ek tay samay mein kitna photosynthesis hua.
Kitaab se bahar: Rate ka matlab; O₂ ke chhote 2 ka matlab; Engelmann ke action spectrum se jod.
Yaad: 11.2-k8
p007-i17 Figure 11.3b: black action-spectrum curve shows photosynthetic rate measured by O₂ release against wavelength. The curve is high in the blue region, falls toward a broad minimum in the green to yellow-green region around 550–570 nm, rises again to a red-region maximum around 680 nm, and drops steeply near 700 nm. It is not zero across the intermediate visible region. Horizontal positions align with the shared wavelength scale below.
p007-i8 Now look at Figure 11.3b showing the wavelengths at which maximum photosynthesis occurs in a plant. Can you see that the wavelengths at which there is maximum absorption by chlorophyll a, i.e., in the blue and the red regions, also shows higher rate of photosynthesis.
samjhana11.4-k9
Ab socho: jo pigment sabse zyada roshni pakadta hai, aur jiske saath-saath photosynthesis bhi chalta hai, woh kaun hua? Sabse zyada roshni pakadne wala yeh pigment chlorophyll a hi hai. Kyunki dono graphs ki chotiyan usi ki chotiyon ke saath chalti hain. Isliye kehte hain ki chlorophyll a photosynthesis se juda chief pigment hai. Chief yaani mukhya, sabse bada kaam karne wala. Par dhyan rakhna, chief ka matlab akela nahi hota. Toh agar chlorophyll a mukhya hai, toh baaki teen pigment kya kar rahe hain?
Screen par: Dono graphs ki neeli aur laal chotiyan chlorophyll a ke naam par aakar milti; label 'chief pigment = mukhya pigment'; neeche 'chief ≠ akela'. Chlorophyll a ke paas teen aur pigment (chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids), har ek par sawaal ka nishaan.
Terms: chief pigment = Mukhya pigment, jiske saath photosynthesis sabse zyada chalta hai; akela nahi.
Kitaab se bahar: Chief ka matlab akela nahi.
p007-i9 Hence, we can conclude that chlorophyll a is the chief pigment associated with photosynthesis.
analogy11.4-k10
Is baat ko Ganga aur uski sahayak nadiyon se samjho. Ganga Himalaya se nikalti hai, aur raaste bhar apne ilaake ki baarish ka paani batorti hai. Par Ganga har ilaake tak nahi pahunchti. Door ke ilaakon ki baarish ka paani Yamuna, Ghaghra aur Kosi jaisi nadiyaan batorti hain. Phir woh yeh paani Ganga mein la kar mila deti hain. Isliye inhe Ganga ki sahayak nadiyaan kehte hain; sahayak yaani madad karne wali. Ab jod kar dekho: baarish ke alag-alag ilaake roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths hain. Ganga chlorophyll a hai, jo apni pasand ki neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai. Aur chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids sahayak nadiyon jaise hain. Yeh doosri wavelengths ki roshni pakadte hain, aur uski energy chlorophyll a tak pahuncha dete hain. Isliye Bihar aur Bengal tak pahunchte-pahunchte Ganga mein bahut saara paani sahayak nadiyon ka hota hai.
Screen par: Bharat ka naksha: Himalaya se nikalti Ganga, us par baarish; door ke ilaakon mein Yamuna, Ghaghra aur Kosi apne ilaake ki baarish batorti aur Ganga mein milti; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga chaudi hoti jaati. Saath mein jod: baarish ke ilaake = alag wavelengths; Ganga = chlorophyll a (neeli aur laal roshni); sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein = energy chlorophyll a tak.
Kitaab se bahar: Ganga aur sahayak nadiyon ki misaal (Himalaya, Yamuna, Ghaghra, Kosi, Bihar-Bengal).
Kya kis jaisa: Ganga = chlorophyll a (mukhya pigment, neeli aur laal roshni khud pakadta hai); sahayak nadiyaan = chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids (accessory = sahayak); baarish ke alag-alag ilaake = roshni ki alag-alag wavelengths; sahayak nadi ka paani Ganga mein milna = pigments ki pakdi energy chlorophyll a tak jaana; Bihar-Bengal tak Ganga ka kul paani = poora photosynthesis (action spectrum).
Kahan tootti hai: Nadiyaan paani jaisa padarth dhoti hain, jabki pigments sirf energy aage dete hain, koi padarth nahi; pigments ek hi thylakoid membrane mein paas-paas baithe hote hain, door-door nahi; aur nadi wala chitra photo-oxidation se bachaav nahi dikhata.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
figure11.4-k11
Ab Figure 11.3c dekho; isme do graph ek hi jagah, ek ke upar ek rakhe gaye hain. Tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhne ko superimposed kehte hain; super yaani upar. Neeche ki leti line par likha hai: Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm). Is par 400, 500, 600 aur 700 ke nishaan hain, jo upar ke graphs ki seedh mein hi hain. Khadi line par likha hai: Light absorbed. Ek kone mein dono lines ke naam likhe hain. Kaali line ke saamne likha hai Rate of photosynthesis; yeh wahi action spectrum hai. Doosri line ke saamne likha hai Absorption; yeh sirf chlorophyll a ka absorption spectrum hai, saare pigments ka nahi. Dhyan do, dono lines alag-alag cheezein naapti hain, isliye unki oonchai ko seedhe mat milao; sirf unka aakaar milao. Ab dekho, dono lines ki chotiyan neele aur laal hisse mein saath-saath hain.
Screen par: Figure 11.3c: do lines ek ke upar ek aakar baithti (label superimposed, super = upar). Ungli neeche ki line par: 'Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm)', nishaan 400, 500, 600, 700, upar ke graphs ki seedh mein → khadi line 'Light absorbed' → kone ke naam: kaali line 'Rate of photosynthesis' (action spectrum), doosri line 'Absorption' (sirf chlorophyll a). 'Oonchai mat milao, aakaar milao' ka label; neele aur laal par dono chotiyan saath chamakti.
Terms: superimposed = Tulna ke liye ek graph ko doosre ke theek upar rakhna.
Kitaab se bahar: Dono lines ki oonchai alag-alag naap hai; sirf aakaar milaana.
p007-i20 Figure 11.3c superimposes a black rate-of-photosynthesis curve and a cyan chlorophyll a absorption curve. Both have prominent blue- and red-region responses, but they do not overlap completely. The black curve retains a broad intermediate response where chlorophyll a absorption is low. Legend: black line 'Rate of photosynthesis'; cyan line 'Absorption'. Vertical axis: 'Light absorbed'. Horizontal axis: 'Wavelength of light in nanometres (nm)', with ticks 400, 500, 600 and 700. No common numerical vertical scale is given for the two different quantities.
ruko, socho11.4-k12
Abhi humne dekha ki dono lines neele aur laal mein saath-saath chotiyan banati hain. Agar do lines har wavelength par ekdum saath-saath chalein, toh kaha jaata hai ki unka one-to-one overlap hai. Socho, phir aage badhenge: kya Figure 11.3c mein chlorophyll a ke absorption spectrum aur action spectrum ka one-to-one overlap hai? Nahi, Figure 11.3c mein in dono ka one-to-one overlap nahi hai. Hare aur peele hisse mein, lagbhag 500 se 600 nm ke beech, chlorophyll a bahut kam sokhta hai. Phir bhi wahan photosynthesis ki kaali line kaafi upar rehti hai. Matlab wahan bhi photosynthesis ho raha hai, jabki chlorophyll a wahan ki roshni zyada pakad hi nahi raha. Toh wahan ki roshni pakad kaun raha hai? Wahan ki roshni pakad rahe hain baaki pigments, jo apni pakdi energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain. Bilkul Ganga ki tarah: jis ilaake tak Ganga khud nahi pahunchti, wahan ka paani sahayak nadiyaan le aati hain.
Screen par: Figure 11.3c par sawaal 'one-to-one overlap?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi. Phir lagbhag 500–600 nm wala hissa roshan: chlorophyll a ki line neeche, kaali line upar; beech ki khaali jagah par sawaal 'yahan roshni kaun pakad raha?'. Phir doosre pigments ki chhoti chamak, unse chlorophyll a ki taraf teer; kone mein Ganga mein milti sahayak nadi.
Terms: one-to-one overlap = Har wavelength par do lines ka ekdum saath-saath chalna.
Kitaab se bahar: Lagbhag 500–600 nm par chlorophyll a ka kam sokhna aur wahan bhi photosynthesis.
Jawab: Nahi. Hare-peele hisse (lagbhag 500–600 nm) mein chlorophyll a bahut kam sokhta hai, phir bhi photosynthesis hota hai; yaani wahan ki roshni doosre pigments pakad kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain. Neele aur laal mein mel batata hai ki chlorophyll a mukhya hai, aur adhoora mel batata hai ki woh akela nahi.
Aam galti: Galat soch 1: mel poora nahi, toh chlorophyll a zaroori nahi; galat, kyunki neele-laal ka mel use mukhya banata hai. Galat soch 2: hare hisse mein photosynthesis zero hai; galat, rate kam hai par zero nahi. Aur dono lines ki oonchai alag-alag cheezon ki naap hai, unhe seedhe nahi milaate.
p007-i10 But by looking at Figure 11.3c can you say that there is a complete one-to-one overlap between the absorption spectrum of chlorophyll a and the action spectrum of photosynthesis?
samjhana11.4-k13
Teeno graph milkar batate hain ki zyadatar photosynthesis spectrum ke neele aur laal hisse mein hota hai. Par dikhne wali roshni ki baaki wavelengths par bhi thoda photosynthesis zaroor hota hai. Ab dekhte hain ki aisa hota kaise hai. Roshni pakadne wala mukhya pigment chlorophyll hai, yeh hum jaan chuke hain. Par thylakoid ki membranes mein uske saath teen aur pigments baithe hain: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Yeh bhi roshni sokhte hain, par us roshni ki energy apne paas nahi rakhte. Yeh woh energy chlorophyll a ko de dete hain. Isi madad ki wajah se inhe accessory pigments kehte hain; accessory yaani sahayak, saath dene wala. Bilkul Ganga ki sahayak nadiyon ki tarah, jo apna paani Ganga mein mila deti hain. Inka pehla fayda: aane wali roshni ki zyada chaudi range photosynthesis ke kaam aa jaati hai. Jo wavelengths chlorophyll a khud nahi pakad paata, woh bhi bekaar nahi jaati. Inka doosra fayda hai bachaav: yeh chlorophyll a ko photo-oxidation se bachate hain. Socho, dhoop mein kai din sookhte rangeen kapde ka rang dheere-dheere ud jaata hai. Tez roshni ki zyada energy rang ke molecule ko hi tod deti hai. Tez dhoop mein chlorophyll ke saath bhi aisa ho sakta hai: zyada energy se woh kharaab hokar toot sakta hai. Photo yaani roshni, aur oxidation yaani molecule ka apne electron ya hydrogen kho dena. Roshni se hone wale is nuksaan ko photo-oxidation kehte hain. Carotenoids yeh faltu energy apne upar le lete hain, aur use garmi bana kar surakshit nikaal dete hain. Isliye tez dhoop mein bhi chlorophyll a bacha rehta hai. Ab ek sawaal socho: kisi paudhe mein chlorophyll b khoob ho, par chlorophyll a bilkul na ho, toh kya woh photosynthesis karega? Aise paudhe mein photosynthesis nahi ho payega, kyunki baaki pigments ki pakdi energy bhi aakhir chlorophyll a tak hi jaani hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki roshni ka asli kaam chlorophyll a se hi shuru hota hai; abhi ke liye bas itna jaan lo. Toh paudhe ko chlorophyll b aur baaki pigments kyun chahiye? Paudhe ko baaki pigments isliye chahiye, kyunki woh zyada wavelengths ki roshni pakadte hain. Saath hi woh chlorophyll a ko tez roshni se bachate hain.
Screen par: Teeno graph saath, neele aur laal hisse roshan, beech ka hissa halka par zero nahi. Phir thylakoid membrane ka paas ka chitra: chlorophyll a ke chaaron taraf chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids; har ek apni alag rang ki roshni pakadta aur energy ki chamak chlorophyll a tak jaati; label 'accessory = sahayak', kone mein sahayak nadi Ganga mein milti. Phir dhoop mein sookhta rangeen kapda, rang feeka hota; tez dhoop mein chlorophyll a par 'photo-oxidation' ka khatra, carotenoids faltu energy ko garmi ki lehron mein badal kar bahar karte. Aakhir mein do paudhe: 'sirf chlorophyll b' wala paudha bina photosynthesis ke (kaata hua nishaan), aur saare pigments wala paudha.
Terms: accessory pigments = Chlorophyll a ke sahayak pigments (chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids), jo roshni pakad kar energy use dete hain aur use bachate hain.; photo-oxidation = Tez roshni ki zyada energy se pigment ka kharaab hokar toot jaana.
Kitaab se bahar: Rangeen kapde ka rang udna; oxidation ka matlab; carotenoids faltu energy ko garmi bana kar nikaalte hain; chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho toh photosynthesis nahi (exercise 5 ke liye).
Yaad: 11.3-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i2
p008-i1 These graphs, together, show that most of the photosynthesis takes place in the blue and red regions of the spectrum; some photosynthesis does take place at the other wavelengths of the visible spectrum. Let us see how this happens. Though chlorophyll is the major pigment responsible for trapping light, other thylakoid pigments like chlorophyll b, xanthophylls and carotenoids, which are called accessory pigments, also absorb light and transfer the energy to chlorophyll a. Indeed, they not only enable a wider range of wavelength of incoming light to be utilised for photosynthesis but also protect chlorophyll a from photo-oxidation.
asli duniya11.4-k14
Yeh baatein aapke aas-paas roz dikhti hain. Patjhad mein pattiyan peeli aur naarangi kyun ho jaati hain? Patjhad mein pattiyan peeli isliye hoti hain, kyunki unka chlorophyll toot jaata hai. Tab pehle se chhupe peele-naarangi carotenoids dikhne lagte hain. Yeh peela rang naya nahi bana; woh hare rang ke peeche pehle se maujood tha. Waise hi aap koi gamla kai din andhere mein rakh do, toh uski pattiyan peeli ya halki hari pad jaati hain. Kyunki chlorophyll lagaataar toot-ta rehta hai, aur naya chlorophyll banne ke liye roshni chahiye. Peele carotenoids zyada tikau, yaani stable, hote hain; isliye woh bache rehte hain aur patti peeli dikhti hai. Andhere mein ugi aaloo ki konpal bhi isi wajah se peeli-safed hoti hai, aur roshni milte hi hari ho jaati hai. Ab aap ek hi paudhe ki chhaanv wali aur dhoop wali pattiyon ko milao. Chhaanv wali pattiyan aksar zyada gehri hari hoti hain. Kyunki kam roshni mein zyada se zyada roshni pakadne ke liye unme chlorophyll zyada banta hai. Chlorophyll b ka hissa bhi badh jaata hai. Dhoop wali pattiyon ko roshni ki kami nahi hoti, isliye unme chlorophyll kam aur bachaav wale peele pigments ka hissa zyada hota hai.
Screen par: Patjhad ke ped: hari patti peeli-naarangi hoti, andar chlorophyll tootkar ghat-ta aur pehle se maujood carotenoids dikhte. Andhere kamre mein rakha gamla, pattiyan dheere-dheere peeli; andhere mein ugi aaloo ki peeli-safed konpal, dhoop mein hari hoti. Ek hi paudha: chhaanv wali patti gehri hari (chlorophyll zyada, chlorophyll b badha hua), dhoop wali halki hari (peele pigments ka hissa zyada).
Kitaab se bahar: Patjhad, andhere mein peeli patti aur aaloo ki konpal, chhaanv aur dhoop ki pattiyan: inki baatein body nahi padhati; exercise 6 aur 7 ke liye.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i3, p022-i4
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.4-k15
Ab aaj ki poori baat hum ek saath dohra lete hain. Paper chromatography ne dikhaya ki patti ka rang chaar pigments ka mel hai: chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Har pigment roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths hi sokhta hai, aur jo rang lautata hai, wahi uska rang dikhta hai. Chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai, aur photosynthesis bhi wahin sabse tez hota hai. Isliye woh chief pigment hai. Baaki teen accessory pigments doosri wavelengths pakad kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain, aur use tez roshni se bachate hain. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se dekho: ek hi paudhe mein hare ke itne rang kyun dikhte hain? Ek hi paudhe mein hare ke alag-alag rang isliye dikhte hain, kyunki har patti mein chaaron pigments alag matra mein hain. Aur patti roshni aise pakadti hai: kai pigment milkar alag-alag roshni pakadte hain, aur saari energy chlorophyll a tak pahunchti hai. Bilkul Ganga ki tarah, jisme door-door ki sahayak nadiyon ka paani aakar milta hai. Par ek baat abhi khuli hai: yeh saare pigments membrane mein kaise jame hote hain, aur chlorophyll a tak pahunchi energy se aage kya hota hai? Pigments membrane mein kaise jame hain aur energy aage kya karti hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Ek line mein aaj ka safar: chromatogram ki chaar lakeerein → hari patti par roshni (hara teer wapas) → Figure 11.3a aur 11.3b ki neeli-laal chotiyan chlorophyll a par milti (chief pigment) → teen accessory pigments se chlorophyll a tak energy ke teer aur bachaav. Shuru ka sawaal phir aata hai, saath mein jawab: teen pattiyan aur unme chaar pigments ka alag mel; kone mein Ganga aur sahayak nadiyaan. Aakhir mein thylakoid membrane par pigments ke dher par sawaal ka nishaan 'kaise jame hain, energy aage kya karti hai?', jo khula rehta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.5 WHAT IS LIGHT REACTION? · 9 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 22 min
Maqsad: Ek akela pigment molecule ek second mein bas das-ek baar roshni pakad paata hai; toh is thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam chalta kaise hai?
Misaal: aatishi sheesha (magnifying lens): sheeshe ka bada chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain; chamakta bindu = reaction centre, woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan saari energy milti hai; kaagaz ka sulagna = reaction centre se electron ka uchhal kar primary acceptor tak jaana, yaani rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; sheeshe ke bina har jagah thodi dhoop = akele pigment ki thodi-si pakad
