Kitab ki baatein (55): kitab kehti hai 10 · shabd ka matlab 19 · pehle ki zaroori baat 11 · figure ka nishaan 4 · teacher ki extra baat 11
f1 kitab kehti hai: Photochemical Light reactions ka doosra naam 'Photochemical' phase hai; isme chaar kaam aate hain: light absorption (roshni sokhna), water splitting (paani ka tootna), oxygen release (oxygen ka nikalna), aur high-energy chemical intermediates ATP aur NADPH ka banna.
f2 shabd ka matlab: high-energy chemical intermediates High-energy chemical intermediates: energy se bhare rasayanik molecule jo beech ki kadi hain; roshni ki energy inme bhar kar sugar banane tak pahunchti hai; yahan ATP aur NADPH.
f3 shabd ka matlab: phase Phase: kisi kaam ka ek daur; photosynthesis ka roshni wala daur Photochemical phase.
f4 pehle ki zaroori baat Light absorption by pigments: pigments roshni ki kuch khaas wavelengths sokhte hain; chlorophyll a mukhya hai aur sahayak pigments apni energy use dete hain (pichhli film).
f5 pehle ki zaroori baat Water as a molecule: paani (H₂O) ek molecule hai, hydrogen ke do atom aur oxygen ka ek atom; water splitting mein yahi molecule toot-ta hai.
f6 pehle ki zaroori baat Chemical energy: molecule ke bandhanon mein jama energy, jo reaction mein kaam aati hai; jaise khaane ki energy.
f7 pehle ki zaroori baat ATP energy lekar chalne wala molecule aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar chalne wala molecule; dono roshni wale hisse mein bante hain aur stroma mein sugar banane ke kaam aate hain.
f8 teacher ki extra baat Roshni ko na jama kiya ja sakta hai, na stroma tak bheja ja sakta hai; isliye uski energy pehle ATP aur NADPH mein bhari jaati hai.
f9 kitab kehti hai: protein complexes Light reaction mein several protein complexes kaam karte hain.
f10 kitab kehti hai Pigments do alag (discrete) photochemical light harvesting complexes (LHC) mein jame hote hain, jo Photosystem I (PS I) aur Photosystem II (PS II) ke andar hain.
f11 kitab kehti hai PS I aur PS II ke naam unki khoj ke kram se rakhe gaye, light reaction mein kaam karne ke kram se nahi.
f12 shabd ka matlab: protein complexes Protein complexes: kai protein molecule judkar bana bada jhund jo milkar ek kaam karta hai; complex = kai hisson ka juda hua samooh.
f13 shabd ka matlab: light harvesting complexes Light harvesting complexes (LHC): roshni batorne wale pigments aur proteins ke jhund; harvest = fasal batorna.
f14 shabd ka matlab: LHC LHC: light harvesting complex ka chhota roop.
f15 shabd ka matlab: discrete Discrete: alag-alag, apni seema wale; dono LHC ek-doosre se alag hain.
f16 shabd ka matlab: Photosystem Photosystem: photo = roshni, system = kai hisson ka mila-jula intezaam; pigments, proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ka poora jhund jo roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai.
f17 shabd ka matlab: PS I; PS II PS I: Photosystem I ka chhota roop; PS II: Photosystem II ka chhota roop.
f18 pehle ki zaroori baat Proteins can form molecular complexes: proteins cell ke bade molecule hain jo aapas mein judkar complexes bana sakte hain.
f19 pehle ki zaroori baat Chloroplast thylakoids: chloroplast ki chapti thailiyan, jinki membrane mein pigments baithe hain aur roshni pakadne ka kaam hota hai.
f20 teacher ki extra baat Kaam ke kram mein pehle PS II aata hai, phir PS I (aage electron ke raaste mein); PS I pehle khoja gaya, isliye I.
f21 kitab kehti hai LHC hundreds (sainkdon) pigment molecules se bane hain, jo proteins se bound (kas kar jude) hote hain.
f22 kitab kehti hai Har photosystem mein ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigments light harvesting system banate hain, jise antennae bhi kehte hain (Figure 11.4).
f23 kitab kehti hai Antenna ke pigments alag-alag wavelengths ki roshni sokh kar photosynthesis ko zyada efficient banate hain.
f24 shabd ka matlab: antennae Antennae: antenna ka bahuvachan; jaise TV ya mobile ka antenna hawa ki tarangein pakadta hai, waise yeh pigments roshni pakadte hain; light harvesting system ka doosra naam.
f25 shabd ka matlab: light harvesting system Light harvesting system: roshni batorne wala intezaam; har photosystem ke saare pigment, ek reaction centre chlorophyll a ko chhod kar.
f26 shabd ka matlab: efficient Efficient: kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam; yahan aane wali roshni ka zyada hissa kaam aana.
f27 shabd ka matlab: bound Bound: kas kar jude hue; pigment proteins par apni jagah jude rehte hain.
f28 pehle ki zaroori baat Photosystem organisation aur pigment–protein binding: ek photosystem mein bahut saare pigment proteins par apni jagah jame hote hain.
f29 pehle ki zaroori baat Accessory-pigment energy transfer: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids doosri wavelengths sokh kar energy chlorophyll a ko dete hain (pichhli film).
f30 teacher ki extra baat Ek akela chlorophyll molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein bas kuch hi, lagbhag das, baar roshni pakadta hai; sainkdon pigment apni energy ek hi jagah bhejte hain, taaki woh jagah lagaataar kaam kare.
f31 teacher ki extra baat Proteins pigments ko sahi doori aur disha mein bithaate hain, taaki energy ek molecule se paas ke molecule tak jaldi pahunche.
f32 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.4 (The light harvesting complex): hare pigment molecules ka jhund; ek photon kinare ke pigment par padta hai; laal zigzag raasta jhund se reaction centre tak; reaction centre se chauda neela teer upar Primary acceptor tak; leader lines reaction centre aur several pigment molecules dikhati hain.
f33 figure ka nishaan: Photon Photon: baayein, laal nishaan jo kinare ke ek hare pigment par aata hai; roshni ki energy ka ek packet.
f34 figure ka nishaan: Pigment molecules Pigment molecules: daayein, kai leader lines hare ovals ko dikhati hain; antenna ke pigment, sab ek rang mein bane par asal mein kai tarah ke.
f35 figure ka nishaan: Reaction centre Reaction centre: jhund ke beech, neele teer ki jad mein, jahan laal zigzag khatam hota hai; ek khaas chlorophyll a.
f36 figure ka nishaan: Primary acceptor Primary acceptor: upar ka peela oval, jahan reaction centre se upar jaata neela teer pahunchta hai.
f37 shabd ka matlab: photon Photon: roshni ki energy ka sabse chhota packet; roshni aise packeton mein aati hai.
f38 shabd ka matlab: reaction centre Reaction centre: reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech ki jagah; photosystem ka woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan pahunchi energy se electron nikalta hai.
f39 shabd ka matlab: primary acceptor Primary acceptor: primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala; reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle lene wala molecule.
f40 pehle ki zaroori baat Antenna pigments aur reaction-centre chlorophyll: antenna energy batorta hai, reaction centre ka chlorophyll a electron chhodta hai.
f41 pehle ki zaroori baat Light excites electrons: energy milne par electron oonchi, zyada energy wali haalat mein uchhalta hai (excite hona); bahut energy mile toh molecule chhod sakta hai.
f42 teacher ki extra baat Zigzag aur neela teer alag cheezein: zigzag mein energy pigment se pigment jaati hai, koi electron nahi chalta; neele teer mein ek electron reaction centre se primary acceptor tak jaata hai; yahin roshni ki energy rasayanik badlaav banti hai.
f43 teacher ki extra baat Figure mein sab pigment ek hare rang mein bane hain, par antenna mein kai tarah ke pigment hote hain.
f44 kitab kehti hai Ek akela chlorophyll a molecule reaction centre banata hai; dono photosystems mein reaction centre alag hai.
f45 kitab kehti hai PS I ke reaction centre chlorophyll a ka absorption peak 700 nm par hai, isliye P700; PS II mein absorption maxima 680 nm par hai, isliye P680.
f46 shabd ka matlab: absorption peak Absorption peak: absorption spectrum ki sabse oonchi choti; woh wavelength jahan pigment sabse zyada roshni sokhta hai.
f47 shabd ka matlab: absorption maxima Absorption maxima: maximum ka bahuvachan; sabse zyada sokhne ki jagah, peak ka hi doosra naam.
f48 shabd ka matlab: P700 P700: P = pigment, 700 = choti 700 nm par; PS I ka reaction centre.
f49 shabd ka matlab: P680 P680: PS II ka reaction centre; choti 680 nm par.
f50 pehle ki zaroori baat Reaction centre versus antenna; wavelength: lehar ki ek chot se agli chot tak ki doori; absorption peak: absorption spectrum ke graph ki sabse oonchi choti (pichhli film).
f51 teacher ki extra baat P700 aur P680 dono chlorophyll a hi hain; akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm par hoti hai, aur reaction centre mein proteins ka maahaul choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiskaata hai.
f52 teacher ki extra baat 680, 700 se kam hai: PS II ka reaction centre thodi chhoti wavelength chahta hai, PS I thodi lambi.
f53 teacher ki extra baat Ek photon ka safar: lagbhag 450 nm ki neeli roshni PS II ke antenna ke kinare carotenoid par; energy chlorophyll b, phir chlorophyll a molecules se P680 tak, second ke arabvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein; P680 ka electron PS II ke primary acceptor tak.
f54 teacher ki extra baat Har kood mein energy ka thoda hissa garmi ban jaata hai, isliye energy zyadatar reaction centre ki taraf hi badhti hai.
f55 teacher ki extra baat Reaction centre na ho toh antenna ki pakdi energy garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaati hai, koi electron nahi chhootta; isliye chlorophyll a ke bina photosynthesis nahi hota.
pehle poori jhalak11.5-k1
Pichhli film mein humne patti ke hare rang ko tod kar dekha. Usme chaar pigments mile: chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls aur carotenoids. Chlorophyll a neeli aur laal roshni sabse zyada sokhta hai, aur baaki teen sahayak pigments apni pakdi energy usi ko saunp dete hain. Yaad rakhein: patti mein jitni bhi roshni pakdi jaaye, uski energy aakhir chlorophyll a tak hi pahunchti hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki pigments membrane mein kaise jame hain, aur chlorophyll a tak pahunch kar energy karti kya hai. Yahi do baatein pichhli film ne khuli chhodi thi. Par isse pehle ek ajeeb baat suno. Ek akela pigment molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein bas das-ek baar roshni pakad paata hai. Samjho, molecule ki duniya mein das baar bahut dheema hai. Wahan kai kaam second ke laakhvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein ho jaate hain. Toh jab ek akela pigment itni kam roshni pakadta hai, toh is thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam chalta kaise hai? Thodi-si pakad se patti ka kaam kaise chalta hai, yahi aaj ka sawaal hai. Iske jawab mein woh dono khuli baatein bhi khul jaayengi. Par jawab se pehle dekh lo ki roshni wale is hisse mein kul kitne kaam hote hain. Yaad karo, roshni se seedhe chalne wali reactions ko humne light reactions kaha tha, aur unka doosra naam photochemical reactions. Isi hisse ko Photochemical phase bhi kehte hain; phase yaani kisi kaam ka ek daur. Dekho, is daur mein kul chaar kaam hote hain. Pehla hai light absorption, yaani pigments ka roshni sokhna, jo hum dekh chuke hain. Doosra hai water splitting, yaani paani ke molecule ka tootna. Teesra hai oxygen release, yaani usi tootne se oxygen ka bahar nikalna. Aur chautha hai do khaas molecule ka banna: ATP aur NADPH. Yaad karo, ATP energy lekar chalta hai, aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar chalta hai. In dono ko high-energy chemical intermediates kehte hain. High-energy yaani energy se bhare, chemical yaani rasayanik, aur intermediates yaani beech ki kadi. Par roshni ki energy ko seedhe sugar banane mein kyun nahi lagaya jaata, beech mein yeh kadi kyun? Roshni ki energy seedhe sugar mein isliye nahi lagti, kyunki roshni ko jama nahi kiya ja sakta. Use stroma tak bheja bhi nahi ja sakta. Isliye uski energy pehle ATP aur NADPH mein bhar di jaati hai, aur yahi dono stroma tak jaakar sugar banwate hain. Paani kaise toot-ta hai aur ATP-NADPH kaise bante hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge. Aaj hum shuruaat par rukenge: roshni pakadne ka intezaam kaisa hai.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: chromatogram ki chaar lakeerein (chlorophyll a, chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids); teen sahayak pigments se energy ke teer chlorophyll a tak; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: energy aakhir chlorophyll a tak'. Thylakoid membrane par pigments ke dher par do sawaal ke nishaan: 'kaise jame?', 'energy aage kya karti?'. Ek akela pigment molecule dhoop mein, saath mein ek second ki ghadi aur bas das chamak gini jaati; sawaal ka dabba 'Itni thodi pakad se kaam kaise chalta hai?'. Phir roshni wale daur ka naksha: label 'light reactions = photochemical reactions = Photochemical phase (phase = daur)'; chaar khaane baari-baari roshan: 1 light absorption (pigment par roshni), 2 water splitting (paani ka molecule toot-ta), 3 oxygen release (oxygen ke bulbule), 4 ATP aur NADPH ke moti; label 'high-energy chemical intermediates'. Aakhir mein roshni thylakoid par hi rukti, aur ATP aur NADPH ke moti stroma mein sugar ki taraf jaate.
Terms: phase = Kisi kaam ka ek daur; roshni wala daur Photochemical phase kehlata hai.; high-energy chemical intermediates = Energy se bhare rasayanik molecule jo roshni aur sugar ke beech ki kadi hain: ATP aur NADPH.
Kitaab se bahar: Ek akela pigment molecule tez dhoop mein bhi ek second mein lagbhag das baar hi roshni pakadta hai, aur molecule ki duniya mein yeh dheema hai; roshni jama ya stroma tak bheji nahi ja sakti, isliye ATP aur NADPH beech ki kadi hain.
Yaad: 11.4-k15, 11.3-k9, 11.3-k7
p008-i3 Light reactions or the ‘Photochemical’ phase include light absorption, water splitting, oxygen release, and the formation of high-energy chemical intermediates, ATP and NADPH.
samjhana11.5-k2
Toh pehli baat: pigments membrane mein jame kaise hain, bikhre hue ya kisi tarteeb mein? Pigments membrane mein bikhre hue nahi, ek pakki tarteeb mein jame hain. Unhe is tarteeb mein proteins pakad kar rakhte hain. Proteins ko aap khaane mein daal aur doodh se jaante ho. Par cell ke andar proteins kaam karne wale bade molecule bhi hain. Kai protein molecule aapas mein judkar ek bada jhund bana sakte hain, jo milkar ek hi kaam karta hai. Aise jhund ko protein complex kehte hain. Complex yaani kai hisson ka juda hua samooh, jaise kai purzon se bani ek machine. Dekho, light reaction ka kaam koi akela molecule nahi karta. Ismein several, yaani kai, protein complexes milkar kaam karte hain. Yeh sab chloroplast ki thylakoid membrane mein baithe hain, wahi chapti thailiyan jinhe hum pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Ab pigments ki baat karte hain. Woh proteins se jud kar do alag jhund banate hain, jo roshni ko batorte hain. Jaise kisan khet mein ghoom-ghoom kar fasal batorta hai, waise hi yeh jhund aane wali roshni ko batorte hain. Isliye inhe light harvesting complexes kehte hain, chhote mein LHC; harvest yaani fasal batorna. Samjho, yeh do LHC ek-doosre se bilkul alag hain, jise discrete kehte hain. Aur dono photochemical hain, yaani roshni se rasayanik kaam shuru karte hain. Har LHC apne proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ke saath milkar ek poora intezaam banata hai. Yeh intezaam roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai. Is poore intezaam ko Photosystem kehte hain: photo yaani roshni, aur system yaani kai hisson ka mila-jula intezaam. Aise do photosystem hain: Photosystem I, chhote mein PS I, aur Photosystem II, chhote mein PS II. Dono LHC inhi ke andar baithe hain. Ab naam dekh kar batao: PS I pehle kaam karta hoga ya PS II? PS I pehle kaam karta hai ya PS II, iska jawab naam se nahi milta. Naam kaam ke kram se rakhe hi nahi gaye. Aksar hum sochte hain ki PS I pehle kaam karta hoga, kyunki ek pehle aata hai do se; par aisa nahi hai. Dono ke naam unki khoj ke kram se rakhe gaye hain. Jo photosystem pehle khoja gaya, use I mila, aur baad wale ko II. Light reaction mein toh pehle PS II kaam karta hai, phir PS I. Yeh aage hum electron ke raaste mein dekhenge. Aisa isliye hua, kyunki vaigyanikon ne PS I ko pehle pehchaana, aur dono ke kaam ka asli kram baad mein samajh aaya. Bilkul class ke roll number ki tarah: roll number admission ke kram se milta hai. Roll number ek wala bachcha line mein sabse aage khada ho, yeh zaroori nahi. Exam mein yeh baat aksar poochhi jaati hai, isliye pakki kar lo. PS I aur PS II ke number khoj ka kram batate hain, kaam ka nahi.
Screen par: Thylakoid membrane ka paas ka chitra: pigments bikhre nahi, proteins par apni jagah jame. Kone mein daal aur doodh, phir cell ke andar protein ke bade molecule; kai protein judkar ek jhund (label: protein complex, kai purzon wali machine). Membrane mein kai protein complexes ek line mein (label: several = kai). Pigments proteins se jud kar do alag jhund banate; kone mein khet mein fasal batorta kisan; label 'light harvesting complexes (LHC), harvest = fasal batorna', 'discrete = alag-alag'. Har LHC ek bade ghere ke andar: label Photosystem (photo = roshni, system = mila-jula intezaam); do ghere: Photosystem I (PS I) aur Photosystem II (PS II). Sawaal 'PS I pehle kaam karta hai?' par kaata hua nishaan; ek patti par 'khoj: pehle PS I, phir PS II', neeche 'kaam: pehle PS II, phir PS I'. Kone mein class ka register: roll number admission ke kram se, line mein bachche alag kram mein.
Terms: protein complexes = Kai protein molecule judkar bana bada jhund jo milkar ek kaam karta hai; complex = kai hisson ka juda hua samooh.; light harvesting complexes (LHC) = Proteins se jude pigments ke jhund jo roshni batorte hain; harvest = fasal batorna.; LHC = Light harvesting complex ka chhota roop.; discrete = Alag-alag, apni seema wale.; Photosystem = Pigments, proteins aur ek khaas chlorophyll a ka poora intezaam jo roshni pakad kar light reaction shuru karta hai; photo = roshni, system = mila-jula intezaam.; PS I = Photosystem I ka chhota roop; pehle khoja gaya photosystem.; PS II = Photosystem II ka chhota roop; baad mein khoja gaya, par kaam mein pehle aata hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Proteins khaane se jaane-pehchaane, cell mein kaam karne wale molecule; protein complex = kai purzon wali machine; harvest = fasal batorna; PS I pehle pehchaana gaya aur kaam ka kram baad mein samajh aaya; roll number ki misaal; exam ki baareek baat.
Yaad: 11.3-k5, 11.4-k13
p008-i4 Several protein complexes are involved in the process. The pigments are organised into two discrete photochemical light harvesting complexes (LHC) within the Photosystem I (PS I) and Photosystem II (PS II). These are named in the sequence of their discovery, and not in the sequence in which they function during the light reaction.
samjhana11.5-k3
Ab shuru wale sawaal ka jawab isi intezaam mein chhupa hai. Ek LHC mein ek-do nahi, sainkdon pigment molecule hote hain. Sab proteins se kas kar jude, yaani bound, hote hain. Proteins inhe sahi doori aur sahi disha mein bithaate hain. Isse energy ek molecule se paas wale molecule tak jhat se pahunch jaati hai. Ab ek photosystem ke andar jhaanko. Usme ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigments ek hi kaam karte hain. Woh roshni pakadte hain aur uski energy aage badhaate hain. Roshni pakadne wale is poore jhund ko light harvesting system kehte hain, aur iska doosra naam antennae hai. Aapne ghar ki chhat par TV ka antenna ya mobile tower ka antenna dekha hoga; woh hawa mein aati tarangein pakadta hai. Hamare pigments bhi waise hi roshni pakadte hain. Antennae, antenna ka bahuvachan hai, kyunki yahan sainkdon pigment milkar pakadte hain. Toh is antenna mein kaun-kaun se pigment baithe hain? Is antenna mein woh sab pigment baithe hain jo humne kaagaz par alag kiye the. Chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids, aur bahut saare chlorophyll a bhi. Yaad karo, har pigment apni pasand ki wavelengths sokhta hai. Chlorophyll a aur b neeli aur laal ke alag-alag hisse sokhte hain, aur carotenoids neeli roshni. Isliye jab itne alag pigment ek saath baithe hon, toh roshni ke zyada rang pakde jaate hain, aur kam roshni bekaar jaati hai. Kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam ko efficient hona kehte hain. Antenna ke pigments isi tarah photosynthesis ko zyada efficient banate hain. Ab doosra fayda dekho, jo shuru wale sawaal ka seedha jawab hai. Yaad karo, akela pigment ek second mein bas das-ek baar hi roshni pakad paata tha. Par jab sainkdon pigment apni-apni pakdi energy ek hi jagah bhejte hain, toh kya hota hai? Us ek jagah tak har second sainkdon baar energy pahunchti hai. Isliye woh jagah kabhi khaali nahi baithti, aur roshni ka kaam lagaataar chalta rehta hai. Toh thodi-si pakad se bhi kaam isliye chal jaata hai, kyunki pigments akele nahi, sainkdon ki toli mein kaam karte hain. Woh ek jagah kaun-si hai, aur wahan kya hota hai, yeh pehle ek misaal se samjho.
Screen par: Ek LHC ka paas ka chitra: protein ke dhaanche par sainkdon pigment molecule apni-apni jagah kase hue (label: bound = kas kar jude), paas-paas aur sahi disha mein. Ek photosystem ke andar: beech mein ek alag chamakta chlorophyll a, baaki saare pigment uske chaaron taraf; poore ghere par label 'light harvesting system = antennae'. Kone mein chhat ka TV antenna aur mobile tower, hawa ki tarangein pakadte. Antenna ke pigments alag rangon mein: chlorophyll b, xanthophylls, carotenoids aur bahut saare chlorophyll a; har pigment apne rang ki roshni pakadta, kam roshni bekaar; label 'efficient = kam barbaadi, zyada kaam'. Phir ek akele pigment par ek second mein das chamak, aur sainkdon pigments se energy ke teer ek jagah aakar har second sainkdon chamak; neeche: 'thodi pakad + badi toli = lagaataar kaam'.
Terms: bound = Kas kar jude hue; pigment proteins par apni jagah jude rehte hain.; light harvesting system = Har photosystem ke woh saare pigment jo roshni pakad kar energy aage badhaate hain (ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar).; antennae = Light harvesting system ka doosra naam; antenna ka bahuvachan, kyunki sainkdon pigment milkar roshni pakadte hain.; efficient = Kam barbaadi mein zyada kaam; aane wali roshni ka zyada hissa kaam aana.
Kitaab se bahar: Proteins pigments ko sahi doori aur disha mein bithaate hain; TV aur mobile antenna ki misaal; sainkdon pigment ek hi jagah energy bhejte hain, isliye woh jagah lagaataar kaam karti hai (efficiency ka doosra kaaran, akele pigment ki lagbhag das baar pakad ke saamne).
Yaad: 11.4-k13, 11.4-k4
p008-i5 The LHC are made up of hundreds of pigment molecules bound to proteins. Each photosystem has all the pigments (except one molecule of chlorophyll a) forming a light harvesting system also called antennae (Figure 11.4). These pigments help to make photosynthesis more efficient by absorbing different wavelengths of light.
analogy11.5-k4
Socho, aap dhoop mein ek aatishi sheesha, yaani bada lens, kaagaz ke upar pakad kar khade ho. Sheeshe ka bada chehra apne poore hisse par padi dhoop ko ek chhote-se chamakte bindu par le aata hai. Thodi hi der mein usi bindu par kaagaz sulagne lagta hai, aur patla-sa dhuaan uthta hai. Par sheeshe ke bina bhi wahi dhoop kaagaz par padti hai; tab kaagaz kyun nahi sulagta? Sheeshe ke bina kaagaz isliye nahi sulagta, kyunki tab har jagah thodi-thodi dhoop padti hai. Kisi ek jagah par itni energy nahi jamti. Ab jodo: aatishi sheeshe ka bada chehra antenna ke sainkdon pigment hain, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain. Chamakta bindu woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule hai, jahan saari energy aakar milti hai. Aur kaagaz ka sulagna woh pal hai, jab us bindu par pahunchi energy se asli rasayanik kaam shuru hota hai. Is khaas bindu ka naam kya hai, aur wahan asal mein kya sulagta hai, yeh hum ab figure mein dekhenge.
Screen par: Dhoop mein aatishi sheesha (bada lens) kaagaz ke upar: sheeshe ke poore chehre se roshni ke teer ek chamakte bindu par milte, bindu par kaagaz sulagta aur patla dhuaan uthta. Phir bina sheeshe ke wahi kaagaz, har jagah halki dhoop, kuch nahi sulagta. Phir jod: sheeshe ka chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment; chamakta bindu = ek khaas chlorophyll a; kaagaz ka sulagna = rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; bindu par sawaal ka nishaan 'iska naam?'.
Kitaab se bahar: Aatishi sheeshe (magnifying lens) ki misaal.
Kya kis jaisa: Aatishi sheeshe ka bada chehra = antenna ke sainkdon pigment, jo roshni ki energy batorte hain; chamakta bindu = reaction centre, woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan saari energy milti hai; kaagaz ka sulagna = wahan electron ka uchhal kar primary acceptor tak jaana, yaani rasayanik kaam ki shuruaat; sheeshe ke bina har jagah thodi dhoop = akele pigment ki thodi-si pakad, jisse kuch shuru nahi hota.
Kahan tootti hai: Sheesha roshni ko mod kar ek jagah laata hai, jabki pigments roshni nahi, uski energy ek molecule se paas ke molecule ko aage dete hain; sheesha saare rang ek saath modta hai, jabki antenna mein alag pigment alag rang pakadte hain; aur reaction centre jalta nahi, uska ek electron nikal kar doosre molecule ke paas jaata hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
figure11.5-k5
Ab Figure 11.4 dekho; iske neeche likha hai The light harvesting complex, yaani wahi roshni batorne wala jhund. Dekho, beech mein hare-hare ovals ka ek jhund hai. Uske paas likha hai Pigment molecules, aur kai patli lines alag-alag ovals ki taraf ishaara karti hain. Yahi antenna ke pigment hain. Figure mein sab ek hi hare rang mein bane hain, par asal mein inme kai tarah ke pigment hote hain. Baayein taraf likha hai Photon, aur ek laal nishaan kinare ke ek hare pigment ki taraf aata dikhta hai. Roshni chhote-chhote packet mein aati hai, aur roshni ki energy ke ek packet ko photon kehte hain. Toh yeh laal nishaan roshni ka ek packet hai, jo antenna ke kinare wale pigment par pada. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo. Us pigment se ek laal zigzag raasta, pigment se pigment hota hua, jhund ke beech ki taraf jaata hai. Raasta zigzag isliye hai, kyunki energy ek pigment se paas wale pigment tak kood-kood kar jaati hai. Koi pigment apni jagah se nahi hilta. Zigzag jahan khatam hota hai, wahan likha hai Reaction centre; yeh woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule hai. Yahi hamare aatishi sheeshe ka chamakta bindu hai, jahan antenna ki saari energy aakar milti hai. Reaction yaani rasayanik badlaav, aur centre yaani beech ki jagah. Jis jagah pahunchi energy se rasayanik badlaav shuru hota hai, use reaction centre kehte hain. Ab dekho, reaction centre se ek chauda neela teer upar jaata hai, aur ek peele oval tak pahunchta hai. Yeh neela teer ek electron ka safar dikhata hai. Iska raaz samajhne ke liye pehle ek clue suno. Yaad karo, jab kisi atom ko energy milti hai, toh uska electron oonchi, zyada energy wali haalat mein uchhal jaata hai. Ise electron ka excite hona kehte hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: agar reaction centre ke electron ko itni energy mil jaaye ki woh molecule mein tik hi na paaye, toh kya hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki woh electron molecule chhod kar bahar nikal jaayega; aapne bilkul sahi socha. Dekho, reaction centre ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur paas baitha ek molecule use turant pakad leta hai. Us peele oval par yahi likha hai: Primary acceptor. Primary yaani pehla, aur acceptor yaani lene wala. Toh primary acceptor woh molecule hai jo uchhla electron sabse pehle leta hai. Isliye figure mein zigzag aur neela teer alag rang ke hain. Zigzag mein sirf energy aage badhti hai, koi electron nahi chalta. Neele teer mein ek electron sach mein ek molecule se doosre tak jaata hai. Yahi woh pal hai jab roshni ki energy rasayanik kaam ban jaati hai: ek electron apni jagah chhod kar doosre molecule ke paas chala gaya. Reaction centre ka khoya electron kahan se bharta hai, aur primary acceptor use aage kahan deta hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Figure 11.4 ka saaf, bada chitra; neeche caption 'The light harvesting complex'. Ungli baari-baari: hare ovals ka jhund aur 'Pigment molecules' ki kai lines (chhota label: sab ek rang mein bane, asal mein kai pigment) → baayein 'Photon' aur laal nishaan kinare ke pigment par (label: photon = roshni ki energy ka packet) → laal zigzag pigment se pigment hota beech tak, energy ki chamak koodti aur pigment apni jagah → zigzag ke ant par 'Reaction centre', kone mein aatishi sheeshe ka chamakta bindu; label reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech → reaction centre se chauda neela teer upar peele oval tak. Phir clue: atom mein electron energy lekar oonchi haalat mein uchhalta; sawaal 'tik na paaye toh?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Electron molecule chhod kar uchhalta, peela oval use pakadta: label 'Primary acceptor (primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala)'. Aakhir mein zigzag (sirf energy) aur neela teer (ek electron) ke alag label; reaction centre par electron ki khaali jagah par chhota '?'.
Terms: photon = Roshni ki energy ka sabse chhota packet; roshni aise packeton mein aati hai.; reaction centre = Photosystem ka woh ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule jahan pahunchi energy se electron nikal kar rasayanik badlaav shuru hota hai; reaction = rasayanik badlaav, centre = beech ki jagah.; primary acceptor = Reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle lene wala molecule; primary = pehla, acceptor = lene wala.
Kitaab se bahar: Photon ka matlab; zigzag kyun; figure mein sab pigment ek rang mein par asal mein kai tarah ke; electron ka excite hona aur bahut energy par molecule chhod dena; zigzag (energy) aur neela teer (electron) ka farq; khoye electron ki baat aage.
Yaad: 11.4-k4
p008-i6 The light harvesting complex is drawn as a cluster of green pigment molecules. A red incoming photon strikes a peripheral pigment. A red zigzag route passes through the pigment cluster towards the reaction centre. A broad upward blue arrow leads from the reaction centre to the yellow oval labelled Primary acceptor. Leader lines identify the reaction centre and several pigment molecules.
samjhana11.5-k6
Toh har photosystem mein reaction centre kitne molecule se banta hai? Har photosystem mein reaction centre sirf ek akele chlorophyll a molecule se banta hai. Antenna ke sainkdon pigment isi ek ke liye energy batorte hain. Par dono photosystems ka reaction centre ek jaisa nahi hai. Dono ki roshni ki pasand thodi alag hai. Yaad karo, absorption spectrum mein sabse oonchi jagah ko humne choti, yaani peak, kaha tha. Jis wavelength par kisi pigment ki sabse oonchi choti ho, use uska absorption peak kehte hain. Isi ko absorption maxima bhi kehte hain; maxima, maximum ka bahuvachan hai, yaani sabse zyada sokhne wali jagah. PS I ke reaction centre wale chlorophyll a ka absorption peak 700 nm par hai. Isliye use P700 kehte hain. PS II ke reaction centre ki absorption maxima 680 nm par hai, aur use P680 kehte hain. Aap naam aise padho: P yaani pigment, aur saath ka number batata hai ki uski choti kitne nm par hai. Toh kya P700 aur P680 do naye pigment hain, jo kaagaz par alag dikhe hi nahi? P700 aur P680 koi naye pigment nahi hain; dono chlorophyll a hi hain, bas ek khaas jagah par baithe hue. Pichhli film mein akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660 se 670 nm par thi. Reaction centre ka chlorophyll a apne proteins ke beech baitha hai. Yahi maahaul uski choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiska deta hai. Ek baat aur dhyan se dekho: 680, 700 se kam hai. Yaani PS II ka reaction centre thodi chhoti wavelength ki roshni chahta hai, aur PS I thodi lambi. Exam ke liye yeh jodi pakki rakho: PS I ke saath P700 aur 700 nm; PS II ke saath P680 aur 680 nm. Do photosystem, do alag reaction centre, aur dono milkar kaise kaam karte hain, yeh aage hum electron ke raaste mein dekhenge.
Screen par: Ek photosystem: beech mein ek akela chlorophyll a (reaction centre), chaaron taraf antenna. Do photosystem saath-saath, dono ke reaction centre alag rang ki chamak mein. Absorption spectrum ka graph, sabse oonchi jagah par 'choti (peak)'; label 'absorption peak = absorption maxima (maxima = maximum ka bahuvachan)'. PS I ke reaction centre par 700 nm ki choti aur naam P700; PS II par 680 nm ki choti aur naam P680; naam ke hisse: P = pigment, number = choti ka nm. 'Naya pigment?' par kaata hua nishaan, dono par 'chlorophyll a'. Pichhli film ka chlorophyll a graph (laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm) aur reaction centre ki choti thoda daayein khiski, proteins ke ghere ke saath. 680 aur 700 ki tulna: 680 thoda baayein (chhoti wavelength). Aakhir mein exam ki jodi ka card: PS I – P700 – 700 nm; PS II – P680 – 680 nm.
Terms: absorption peak = Woh wavelength jahan kisi pigment ka sokhna sabse zyada ho; graph ki sabse oonchi choti.; absorption maxima = Absorption peak ka hi doosra naam; maxima = maximum ka bahuvachan.; P700 = PS I ka reaction centre, ek chlorophyll a jiski choti 700 nm par hai; P = pigment.; P680 = PS II ka reaction centre, ek chlorophyll a jiski choti 680 nm par hai.
Kitaab se bahar: P = pigment; akele chlorophyll a ki laal choti lagbhag 660–670 nm (Figure 11.3a), aur reaction centre mein proteins ka maahaul choti ko thoda lambi wavelength ki taraf khiskaata hai; 680 aur 700 ki tulna; exam ki jodi.
Yaad: 11.4-k6, 11.4-k7
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p008-i12 The single chlorophyll a molecule forms the reaction centre. The reaction centre is different in both the photosystems. In PS I the reaction centre chlorophyll a has an absorption peak at 700 nm, hence is called P700, while in PS II it has absorption maxima at 680 nm, and is called P680.
example11.5-k7
Chalo, ab ek photon ke saath shuru se aakhir tak poora safar karke dekhte hain. Socho, neeli roshni ka ek photon, lagbhag 450 nm wala, PS II ke antenna ke kinare baithe ek carotenoid molecule par padta hai. Dekho, carotenoid us photon ko sokh leta hai, par uski energy apne paas nahi rakhta. Yeh energy paas ke chlorophyll b tak jaati hai. Phir ek chlorophyll a se doosre chlorophyll a tak koodti jaati hai. Har kood mein energy ka thoda-sa hissa garmi ban jaata hai, isliye woh zyadatar aage, reaction centre ki taraf hi badhti hai. Socho, second ke ek arabvein hisse se bhi kam samay mein yeh energy P680 tak pahunch jaati hai. Tab P680 ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur PS II ka primary acceptor use pakad leta hai. Bilkul aatishi sheeshe ki tarah: dhoop kinare par padi thi, par kaagaz beech ke bindu par sulga. Ab batao, is safar mein electron kisne diya: carotenoid ne ya P680 ne? Is safar mein electron P680 ne diya; carotenoid ne sirf roshni pakad kar uski energy aage badhaayi. Matlab photon sainkdon pigments mein se kisi ek ne pakda, par rasayanik kaam ek hi jagah shuru hua: reaction centre par.
Screen par: PS II ka antenna, kinare par ek naarangi carotenoid; neeli roshni ka ek photon (lagbhag 450 nm) us par girta. Energy ki chamak carotenoid se chlorophyll b, phir ek chlorophyll a se doosre par koodti, har kood par halki garmi ki lehar, aur chamak P680 ki taraf hi badhti; kone mein ghadi 'second ke arabvein hisse se kam'. P680 par pahunch kar uska electron uchhalta, PS II ka primary acceptor use pakadta. Kone mein aatishi sheesha: dhoop kinare par, kaagaz beech ke bindu par sulagta. Sawaal ka dabba 'electron kisne diya: carotenoid ya P680?', jawab P680 par sahi ka nishaan.
Kitaab se bahar: Ek photon ka safar: lagbhag 450 nm ki neeli roshni PS II ke antenna ke carotenoid par, chlorophyll b aur chlorophyll a se P680 tak, second ke arabvein hisse se kam samay mein; har kood mein thodi energy garmi banti hai, isliye energy reaction centre ki taraf badhti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
ruko, socho11.5-k8
Ab ek line ko dhyan se parkho, jisme bahut se bachche phisal jaate hain. Humne kaha tha ki har photosystem mein ek chlorophyll a molecule ko chhod kar baaki saare pigment antenna banate hain. Socho, phir aage badhenge: toh kya antenna mein chlorophyll a hota hi nahi? Antenna mein chlorophyll a hota hai, aur woh bhi sainkdon molecule. Aksar hum sochte hain ki chlorophyll a sirf reaction centre mein hota hai, par aisa nahi hai. Ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar ka matlab hai ki sirf ek khaas molecule alag hai. Baaki sainkdon chlorophyll a antenna mein hi roshni pakadte hain. Samjho, wahi ek khaas chlorophyll a reaction centre hai, aur baaki saare pigment uske liye energy batorte hain. Ab ulta socho: agar kisi photosystem ke antenna mein pigment bharpoor hon, par uska reaction centre na ho, toh kya hoga? Reaction centre na ho, toh antenna roshni pakadta rahega, par koi electron nahi uchhlega. Kyunki energy ko electron ke uchhalne mein badalne ka kaam sirf reaction centre karta hai. Pakdi hui energy bas garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaayegi. Isi wajah se jis paudhe mein chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a bilkul na ho, usme photosynthesis nahi ho sakta. Usme reaction centre banane wala molecule hi nahi hai.
Screen par: Line ka card: 'ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar baaki saare pigment antenna'; sawaal ka dabba 'antenna mein chlorophyll a nahi?'. Antenna ke andar sainkdon chlorophyll a chamakte, beech mein sirf ek khaas chlorophyll a alag rang mein (reaction centre); 'chlorophyll a sirf reaction centre mein' par kaata hua nishaan. Phir ek photosystem jiska reaction centre gayab: antenna roshni pakadta, energy beech tak aakar garmi aur halki laal chamak ban kar nikal jaati, koi electron nahi uchhalta. Aakhir mein do paudhe: sirf chlorophyll b wala (photosynthesis nahi, kaata hua nishaan) aur chlorophyll a wala.
Kitaab se bahar: Reaction centre ke bina pakdi energy garmi ya halki chamak ban kar nikal jaati hai; chlorophyll b ho par chlorophyll a na ho toh photosynthesis nahi (exercise 5 ke liye).
Yaad: 11.4-k13
Jawab: Antenna mein bhi sainkdon chlorophyll a hote hain; sirf ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule reaction centre banta hai. Reaction centre ke bina antenna roshni pakad leta hai par koi electron nahi chhootta, isliye light reaction shuru nahi hoti; isi wajah se chlorophyll a ke bina, sirf chlorophyll b se, photosynthesis nahi hota.
Aam galti: Galat soch 1: 'ek chlorophyll a ko chhod kar' ka matlab antenna mein chlorophyll a nahi; galat, antenna mein bahut chlorophyll a hai. Galat soch 2: pigment bahut hon toh reaction centre ki zaroorat nahi; galat, roshni pakadna aur electron chhodna alag kaam hain. Galat soch 3: P700 aur P680 alag pigment hain; galat, dono chlorophyll a hain.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i2
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.5-k9
Ab aaj ka poora safar ek saath dohra lete hain. Light reactions, yaani Photochemical phase, mein chaar kaam hote hain. Roshni sokhna, paani ka tootna, oxygen ka nikalna, aur ATP aur NADPH ka banna. Pigments proteins se jud kar do alag LHC banate hain, jo PS I aur PS II ke andar hain. Inke number khoj ke kram se hain, kaam ke kram se nahi. Humne dekha ki har photosystem mein sainkdon pigment antenna banate hain, aur alag-alag rang ki roshni pakad kar uski energy beech tak bhejte hain. Beech mein ek akela chlorophyll a reaction centre hai; wahan electron uchhal kar primary acceptor ke paas jaata hai. PS I ka reaction centre P700 hai, jiski choti 700 nm par hai. PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, jiski choti 680 nm par hai. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: akele pigment ki pakad itni dheemi hai, phir bhi patti roshni ka kaam lagaataar kaise kar leti hai? Patti roshni ka kaam lagaataar isliye kar leti hai, kyunki sainkdon pigment milkar roshni batorte hain. Woh saari energy ek reaction centre ko dete hain, jo kabhi khaali nahi baithta. Bilkul aatishi sheeshe ki tarah, jo poore chehre ki dhoop ek bindu par la kar kaagaz sulga deta hai. Par ab ek nayi baat khadi ho gayi hai. Reaction centre ne apna electron de diya, toh woh electron aage kahan jaata hai, aur do photosystem aapas mein kaise jude hain? Electron aage kahan jaata hai aur dono photosystem aapas mein kaise jude hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Aaj ka safar ek line mein: Photochemical phase ke chaar khaane (roshni sokhna, paani tootna, oxygen nikalna, ATP aur NADPH) → proteins par jame pigments ke do LHC, PS I aur PS II ke ghere (khoj ka kram, kaam ka nahi) → antenna ke sainkdon pigment se energy beech ke reaction centre tak → electron uchhal kar primary acceptor tak → P700 (700 nm) aur P680 (680 nm) ke card. Shuru ka sawaal laut kar aata: akela pigment das chamak, poori toli se reaction centre par lagaataar chamak; kone mein aatishi sheesha kaagaz sulgaata. Aakhir mein primary acceptor se aage ek khaali raasta aur do photosystem ke beech sawaal ka nishaan, jo khula rehta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.6 THE ELECTRON TRANSPORT · 11 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 23 min
Maqsad: Har baar roshni padne par dono photosystem apna ek electron chhod dete hain; toh yeh electron jaate kahan hain, aur photosystem khaali kyun nahi ho jaate?
Misaal: roller coaster: gaadi = electron; do motor wali chain = roshni ke do dhakke (pehla PS II par, doosra PS I par); har choti = electron acceptor; pehli lambi utaar = PS II se PS I tak cytochromes ki kadi (downhill); doosri, aur bhi oonchi choti = PS I ka acceptor (greater redox potential); aakhri utaar = NADP⁺ tak; oonche platform wala station = NADP⁺, jahan electron rukta hai; poore raaste gaadi wahi rehti hai = electron kharch nahi hota; oonchai = energy
Kitab ki baatein (68): kitab kehti hai 13 · shabd ka matlab 16 · pehle ki zaroori baat 15 · figure ka nishaan 14 · teacher ki extra baat 10
f1 kitab kehti hai PS II ke reaction centre ka chlorophyll a 680 nm wavelength ki laal roshni sokhta hai.
f2 kitab kehti hai Isse electron excited hote hain aur atom ke nucleus se door wale orbit mein kood jaate hain.
f3 kitab kehti hai In electrons ko ek electron acceptor pakadta hai, jo inhe aage electron transport system ko de deta hai.
f4 shabd ka matlab: excited Excited: energy paakar electron ka zyada energy wali, uchhli hui haalat mein pahunchna; excited = uttejit.
f5 shabd ka matlab: orbit Orbit: nucleus ke chaaron taraf electron ke ghoomne ka raasta; nucleus se jitna door ka orbit, wahan tikne ke liye utni zyada energy.
f6 shabd ka matlab: electron acceptor Electron acceptor: doosre molecule se electron lene wala molecule; accept = lena; PS II ka pehla acceptor wahi primary acceptor hai.
f7 pehle ki zaroori baat PS II and P680: PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, ek chlorophyll a jiski absorption choti 680 nm par; kaam mein PS II pehle aata hai, number khoj ke kram se pade (pichhli film).
f8 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron and atomic nucleus: atom ke beech mein nucleus (proton aur neutron), aur uske chaaron taraf chhote, negative charge wale electron ghoomte hain (Class 9; chapter ki shuruaat).
f9 pehle ki zaroori baat Photon absorption: roshni photon naam ke packeton mein aati hai; antenna ke pigment photon sokh kar uski energy reaction centre tak bhejte hain (pichhli film).
f10 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron acceptor (pichhli film): reaction centre se uchhla electron sabse pehle primary acceptor pakadta hai.
f11 teacher ki extra baat Excited electron apne molecule mein der tak nahi tikta; paas baitha acceptor use turant le leta hai.
f12 kitab kehti hai Electron transport system cytochromes se bana hai (Figure 11.5).
f13 kitab kehti hai Electrons ka yeh chalna oxidation-reduction, yaani redox potential, scale par downhill hai.
f14 kitab kehti hai Electron transport chain se guzarte hue electron kharch nahi hote; woh aage PS I ke pigments tak de diye jaate hain.
f15 kitab kehti hai Saath-saath PS I ke reaction centre ke electron 700 nm wavelength ki laal roshni paakar excite hote hain, aur ek doosre acceptor molecule ko de diye jaate hain, jiska redox potential greater hai.
f16 shabd ka matlab: cytochromes Cytochromes: electron lekar aage dene wale rangeen proteins; cyto = cell, chrome = rang; inke beech ka loha (iron) electron leta aur deta hai.
f17 shabd ka matlab: redox potential Redox potential: koi molecule electron ko kitne zor se pakadta hai ya kitni aasaani se chhodta hai, iska maap; redox = reduction + oxidation; is maap se molecules ko upar-neeche rakhne par redox potential scale banti hai.
f18 shabd ka matlab: electron transport system Electron transport system: electron ko aage pahunchane wala intezaam; transport = ek jagah se doosri jagah pahunchana, system = kai hisson ka milkar kaam karne wala intezaam.
f19 shabd ka matlab: downhill Downhill: down = neeche, hill = pahadi; redox potential scale par electron ka apne aap upar se neeche utarna, jismein uski kuch energy nikalti hai.
f20 shabd ka matlab: electron transport chain Electron transport chain: electron ko aage dene wale molecules ki kadi; chain = zanjeer, jo kadiyon se banti hai.
f21 shabd ka matlab: greater redox potential Greater redox potential wala acceptor: PS I ka doosra acceptor, jo scale par PS II wale acceptor se bhi oonchi jagah baitha hai; wahan pahunch kar electron sabse zyada energy wali haalat mein hota hai.
f22 pehle ki zaroori baat Light absorption by reaction-centre pigments: reaction centre ka chlorophyll a roshni ki energy paakar apna electron chhodta hai (pichhli film).
f23 pehle ki zaroori baat Electrons and oxidation-reduction: molecule se electron (ya hydrogen) nikalna oxidation hai, aur milna reduction (11.2 aur 11.4 mein dekha).
f24 pehle ki zaroori baat PS II and PS I: do photosystem; PS II ka reaction centre P680, PS I ka P700; kaam mein pehle PS II, phir PS I (pichhli film).
f25 teacher ki extra baat PS I ka electron chale jaane se uski jagah khaali hoti hai; kadi se aaya PS II ka electron yahi khaali jagah bharta hai, isliye dono photosystem ek line mein jude hain aur PS I khaali nahi hota.
f26 teacher ki extra baat Redox potential scale par upar woh molecule jo electron aasaani se chhodte hain, neeche woh jo use kas kar pakadte hain; isliye electron apne aap upar se neeche jaata hai, kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf.
f27 kitab kehti hai Phir yeh electron dobara downhill jaate hain, is baar energy-rich NADP⁺ ke ek molecule tak.
f28 kitab kehti hai In electrons ke judne se NADP⁺ reduce hota hai.
f29 shabd ka matlab: NADP⁺ NADP⁺: electron lene ko taiyaar, khaali carrier molecule (khaali thaila); electron paakar yahi bhara hua NADPH banta hai.
f30 shabd ka matlab: energy-rich Energy-rich: energy se bhara; electron paakar bana NADPH energy se bhara hota hai.
f31 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron gain as reduction: kisi molecule ko electron milna hi uska reduction hai.
f32 pehle ki zaroori baat PS I excitation: PS I ka P700 700 nm ki roshni paakar excite hota hai aur apna electron doosre acceptor ko deta hai (abhi dekha).
f33 teacher ki extra baat Ek NADP⁺ ko NADPH banne ke liye do electron chahiye; exam ke liye: khaali NADP⁺, bhara NADPH, dono ke naam aapas mein nahi badalte.
f34 kitab kehti hai NADP⁺ reduce hokar NADPH + H⁺ banta hai.
f35 kitab kehti hai Electron ka poora raasta, PS II se uphill acceptor tak, electron transport chain se neeche PS I tak, electron ka excite hona, doosre acceptor tak jaana, aur aakhir mein downhill NADP⁺ tak jo reduce hokar NADPH + H⁺ banta hai, apne khaas aakaar ki wajah se Z scheme kehlata hai (Figure 11.5).
f36 kitab kehti hai Yeh aakaar tab banta hai jab saare carriers redox potential scale par ek kram mein rakhe jaayein.
f37 shabd ka matlab: Z scheme Z scheme: do baar roshni se upar uthte electron ka poora raasta, jo carriers ko scale par rakhne par karvat lete Z jaisa dikhta hai; scheme = khaaka.
f38 shabd ka matlab: carrier Carrier: electron lekar agle molecule ko dene wala molecule; carry = dhona, jaise cycle ka carrier saamaan dhota hai.
f39 shabd ka matlab: uphill Uphill: up = upar; scale par electron ka upar chadhna, jo apne aap nahi hota; iske liye roshni ki energy lagti hai.
f40 shabd ka matlab: H⁺ H⁺: hydrogen atom jiska ek electron nikal gaya; bacha sirf ek proton, aur plus ka nishaan uska positive charge.
f41 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron transport between photosystems: PS II ka electron cytochromes ki kadi se utar kar PS I tak jaata hai (abhi dekha).
f42 pehle ki zaroori baat Light-driven electron excitation: roshni electron ko oonchi energy tak uthaati hai, PS II par 680 nm aur PS I par 700 nm (abhi dekha).
f43 pehle ki zaroori baat NADP⁺ reduction: NADP⁺ electron paakar reduce hota hai aur NADPH banta hai (abhi dekha).
f44 teacher ki extra baat Membrane mein electron Z ke aakaar mein nahi chalta; Z energy ka chitra hai, membrane ka raasta nahi (exam ki aam galatfahmi).
f45 teacher ki extra baat Ek NADP⁺ do electron aur H⁺ lekar NADPH banta hai; NADPH + H⁺ mein saath likha H⁺ hisaab poora rakhta hai.
f46 teacher ki extra baat Exam mein Z scheme aksar adhoora dekar poochha jaata hai; kram: PS II, uska acceptor, kadi, PS I, uska acceptor, aur NADP⁺.
f47 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.5 (Z scheme of light reaction): baayein Photosystem II, daayein Photosystem I; Light se peele dashed teer dono LHC tak; PS II ke LHC se laal teer upar uske e⁻ acceptor tak; phir laal teer Electron transport system se neeche PS I ke LHC tak; is system ke upar ADP + iP se ATP; PS I ke LHC se doosra laal teer upar uske e⁻ acceptor tak, phir NADP⁺ se NADPH; PS II ke neeche H₂O se 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O], aur ek teer electron PS II ke LHC tak.
f48 figure ka nishaan: Photosystem II Photosystem II: upar baayein bracket, jo PS II aur uske electron acceptor ko gherta hai.
f49 figure ka nishaan: Photosystem I Photosystem I: daayein bracket, jo PS I aur uske electron acceptor ko gherta hai.
f50 figure ka nishaan: Light Light: baayein peela gola; usse peele dashed teer dono LHC tak, yaani roshni dono photosystem par padti hai.
f51 figure ka nishaan: LHC LHC (baayein, hara oval): PS II ka roshni batorne wala jhund; roshni aur paani se aaya electron yahin pahunchte hain, aur yahin se teer upar acceptor tak.
f52 figure ka nishaan: LHC LHC (daayein, hara oval): PS I ka roshni batorne wala jhund; roshni aur kadi se aaya electron yahin pahunchte hain, aur yahin se doosra teer upar.
f53 figure ka nishaan: e⁻ acceptor e⁻ acceptor (baayein, gulabi oval): PS II ke LHC se upar jaata laal teer yahin pahunchta hai; yahan se electron kadi ki taraf.
f54 figure ka nishaan: e⁻ acceptor e⁻ acceptor (daayein, gulabi oval): PS I ke LHC se doosra upar jaata teer yahin pahunchta hai; yahan se electron NADP⁺ ki taraf.
f55 figure ka nishaan: Electron transport system Electron transport system: beech ka baingani dabba, PS II ke acceptor aur PS I ke LHC ke beech neeche utarte laal teeron par; cytochromes wali kadi.
f56 figure ka nishaan: ADP + iP ADP + iP: dabbe ke upar mudi teer ki shuruaat; ATP banne ki shuruaati cheezein, electron ke utaar ke saath.
f57 figure ka nishaan: ATP ATP: usi mudi teer ka ant; electron ke utaar ke saath ATP banta hai (kaise, yeh aage).
f58 figure ka nishaan: NADP⁺ NADP⁺: daayein aakhri mudi teer par, khaali acceptor jahan electron ka safar khatam hota hai.
f59 figure ka nishaan: NADPH NADPH: usi aakhri teer par NADP⁺ ke upar likha bhara hua product.
f60 figure ka nishaan: H₂O H₂O: PS II ke neeche paani, jahan se electron dene wala teer shuru hota hai.
f61 figure ka nishaan: 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]: ek paani ke molecule ka hisaab, do electron, do H⁺ aur oxygen ka ek atom; ek teer electron PS II ke LHC tak le jaata hai.
f62 shabd ka matlab: e⁻ e⁻: electron ka nishaan; e = electron, upar ka chhota minus = uska negative charge.
f63 pehle ki zaroori baat Photosystems: do photosystem, har ek mein roshni batorne wala LHC aur ek reaction centre (pichhli film).
f64 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron transfer: electron ek molecule se doosre molecule tak jaata hai; dene wala oxidise, lene wala reduce hota hai (abhi dekha).
f65 pehle ki zaroori baat ATP and NADPH as light-reaction products: ATP energy lekar aur NADPH electron aur hydrogen lekar stroma tak jaate hain, jahan sugar banti hai (11.3, 11.5).
f66 teacher ki extra baat [O] yaani oxygen ka ek atom; aise do atom milkar O₂ gas banti hai, isliye figure ka [O] O₂ nahi hai.
f67 teacher ki extra baat Ek NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon lagte hain: do electron, aur har electron ke peeche ek photon PS II par aur ek PS I par.
f68 teacher ki extra baat Kuch kharpatwar-naashak dawaiyaan (weed-killer) PS II ke aage ki kadi mein electron ka raasta rok deti hain; tab PS I ki khaali jagah nahi bharti, NADPH nahi banta aur paudha bhookha mar jaata hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.6-k1
Pichhli film mein humne dekha ki har photosystem ke sainkdon pigment milkar roshni batorte hain. Unki saari energy ek hi jagah pahunchti hai: reaction centre par. Wahan ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai, aur primary acceptor use pakad leta hai. Yeh kaam PS II mein P680 karta hai, aur PS I mein P700. Yaad rakhein: roshni ka asli kaam yahi hai, reaction centre ke ek electron ko uthakar doosre molecule tak pahunchana. Aage hum dekhenge ki primary acceptor ke baad electron kis raaste jaata hai, aur do photosystem ek-doosre se kaise bandhe hain. Par pehle ek ajeeb baat par gaur karo. Socho, patti par din bhar roshni padti hai, aur har baar dono photosystem apna ek electron chhod dete hain. Toh yeh saare electron jaate kahan hain, aur itne electron dekar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi ho jaate? Electron kahan jaate hain aur photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote, yahi aaj ka sawaal hai. Iska jawab ek safar mein chhupa hai, jo electron teen padaav mein poora karta hai. Pehle padaav par PS II hai. Wahan roshni electron ko upar uchhaalti hai, aur ek acceptor use pakad leta hai. Doosre padaav mein electron molecules ki ek kadi se neeche utar kar PS I tak aata hai. Phir wahan roshni use doosri baar uchhaalti hai. Aur teesre padaav mein electron phir neeche utarta hai. Woh ek khaas molecule tak pahunch kar ruk jaata hai. Aur ek hi electron ko roshni ka dhakka do baar kyun chahiye, yeh bhi aaj saaf ho jaayega.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: ek photosystem ke sainkdon pigment se energy ke teer beech ke reaction centre tak; P680 (PS II) aur P700 (PS I) ke reaction centre se electron uchhal kar primary acceptor tak; neeche dabba 'Yaad rakhein: roshni ek electron ko uthakar doosre molecule tak pahunchati hai'. Phir thylakoid membrane par PS II aur PS I saath-saath, din bhar dhoop; dono par roshni padti, dono se ek-ek electron uchhalta; sawaal ka dabba 'electron kahan jaate hain? photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote?'. Phir safar ka naksha teen khaano mein: 1 PS II par roshni ka dhakka aur acceptor; 2 molecules ki kadi se neeche utar kar PS I tak, wahan doosra dhakka; 3 phir neeche utar kar ek khaas molecule par ruk jaana (naam ki jagah '?'); kone mein chhota sawaal '2 dhakke kyun?'.
Kitaab se bahar: Opening recap of 11.5 (sainkdon pigment, reaction centre, primary acceptor, P680 aur P700) aur uske do khule sawaal; aaj ka sawaal: din bhar electron chhod kar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi hote.
Yaad: 11.5-k9, 11.5-k6
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6-k2
Ab pehle padaav par chalte hain, yaani PS II par. Yaad karo, kaam mein PS II pehle aata hai, chahe uska number do ho. Number toh khoj ke kram se pade the. PS II ka reaction centre P680 hai, yaani ek khaas chlorophyll a molecule. Yeh 680 nm wavelength wali laal roshni sokhta hai. Ab sochiye, roshni ki yeh energy jaati kahan hai? Roshni ki yeh energy seedhe reaction centre ke ek electron ko milti hai. Yaad karo, har atom ke beech mein nucleus hota hai, aur electron uske chaaron taraf ghoomte rehte hain. Electron nucleus ke chaaron taraf jis raaste par ghoomta hai, use orbit kehte hain. Nucleus se jitna door ka orbit ho, wahan tikne ke liye electron ko utni hi zyada energy chahiye. Isliye roshni ki energy milte hi electron nucleus se door wale orbit mein kood jaata hai. Bilkul waise, jaise aap koi achhi khabar sunkar khushi se uchhal padte ho. Energy paakar electron ki is uchhli hui, zyada energy wali haalat ko excited kehte hain; excited yaani uttejit. Ab samjho, excited electron apne molecule mein zyada der tik nahi paata. Paas baitha ek molecule use turant pakad leta hai. Jo molecule doosre molecule se electron le leta hai, use electron acceptor kehte hain; accept yaani lena. Humne pichhli film mein isi pehle acceptor ko primary acceptor kaha tha, yaani sabse pehle lene wala. Toh kya acceptor yeh electron apne paas rakh leta hai? Acceptor yeh electron apne paas nahi rakhta. Woh use turant aage, ek electron transport system ko de deta hai. Transport ka matlab aap jaante ho: ek jagah se doosri jagah pahunchana. System yaani kai hisson ka milkar kaam karne wala intezaam. Matlab electron transport system woh intezaam hai, jo electron ko aage pahunchata hai. Yeh intezaam kin molecules se bana hai, aur electron ko kahan le jaata hai, yeh hum agle padaav mein dekhenge.
Screen par: Thylakoid membrane par PS II ka ghera; kone mein patti 'kaam ka kram: pehle PS II (number khoj se)'. Reaction centre P680 (ek chlorophyll a) par 680 nm ki laal roshni ka teer; sawaal ka chhota dabba 'energy kahan jaati?'. Phir paas se ek atom: beech mein nucleus, chaaron taraf orbit ke ghere, ek electron andar wale ghere par (label: orbit; door ka orbit = zyada energy); roshni ki energy aate hi electron door wale ghere mein koodta. Kone mein khushkhabri sunkar uchhalta insaan; label 'excited = uttejit'. Phir excited electron molecule se nikalta aur paas ka molecule use pakad leta (label: electron acceptor, accept = lena; wahi primary acceptor). Acceptor ke paas sawaal 'rakh lega?' par kaata nishaan; electron aage ek dhundhle intezaam ki taraf, label 'electron transport system: transport = pahunchana, system = intezaam', uspar '?' 'kin molecules se bana?'.
Terms: orbit = Nucleus ke chaaron taraf electron ke ghoomne ka raasta; door ka orbit = zyada energy.; excited = Energy paakar electron ka zyada energy wali, uchhli hui haalat mein pahunchna; uttejit.; electron acceptor = Doosre molecule se electron lene wala molecule; accept = lena.; electron transport system = Electron ko aage pahunchane wala intezaam; transport = pahunchana, system = mila-jula intezaam.
Kitaab se bahar: Orbit ka matlab aur 'door ka orbit = zyada energy' (Class 9 ka atom); khushi se uchhalne ki misaal; excited electron ka der tak na tikna; PS II ka pehla acceptor hi pichhli film ka primary acceptor; transport aur system ke matlab.
Yaad: 11.5-k2, 11.5-k6, 11.5-k5, 0-k7
p008-i14 In photosystem II the reaction centre chlorophyll a absorbs 680 nm wavelength of red light causing electrons to become excited and jump into an orbit farther from the atomic nucleus. These electrons are picked up by an electron acceptor which passes them to an electrons [electron] transport
analogy11.6-k3
Is poore safar ko ek mele ke roller coaster se samjho. Roller coaster ki gaadi pehle dheere-dheere ek oonchi chadhai par chadhti hai. Use ek motor wali chain upar kheenchti hai; gaadi apne aap upar nahi ja sakti. Choti par pahunch kar gaadi apne aap neeche utarti hai. Utaar par woh ek ke baad ek mod paar karti jaati hai. Neeche pahunchte hi ek doosri chain use phir upar kheenchti hai, aur is baar pehle se bhi oonchi choti tak. Wahan se gaadi doosri utaar par neeche aati hai. Aakhir mein woh ek oonche platform wale station par ruk jaati hai. Ab batao, station toh oonchai par hai; pehli utaar ke baad gaadi wahan apne aap kyun nahi pahunch sakti? Pehli utaar ke baad gaadi station tak apne aap isliye nahi pahunch sakti, kyunki woh neeche aa chuki hoti hai. Oonchai par chadhne ke liye use phir zor chahiye, aur yahi zor doosri chain deti hai. Ab jodo: roller coaster ki gaadi hai electron. Dono motor wali chain hain roshni ke do dhakke, pehla PS II par aur doosra PS I par. Har choti ek electron acceptor hai, jo upar pahunche electron ko pakad leta hai. Pehli lambi utaar woh kadi hai, jisse electron PS II se PS I tak neeche utarta hai. Aur oonche platform wala station woh khaas molecule hai, jahan electron ka safar khatam ho jaata hai. Gaur karo, poore raaste mein gaadi wahi rehti hai; badalti hai toh bas uski oonchai. Electron ke liye yahi oonchai uski energy hai: upar matlab zyada energy, aur neeche matlab kam.
Screen par: Mele ka roller coaster, bagal se: ek gaadi motor wali chain par dheere-dheere pehli oonchi chadhai chadhti (chain ki tak-tak); choti se gaadi apne aap ghumaavdaar utaar par neeche; neeche doosri chain use aur bhi oonchi choti tak kheenchti; wahan se doosri utaar, aur aakhir mein oonche platform wale station par gaadi rukti. Sawaal ka dabba 'pehli utaar ke baad station tak kyun nahi?': gaadi platform ke neeche ruki, upar chadhne ke liye chain. Phir jod ke label, ek-ek karke: gaadi = electron; do chain = roshni ke do dhakke (PS II, PS I); choti = electron acceptor; pehli utaar = PS II se PS I tak ki kadi; station = khaas molecule (naam '?'). Aakhir mein gaadi par nishaan 'wahi gaadi', aur bagal mein ek khadi patti 'oonchai = energy'.
Kitaab se bahar: Mele ke roller coaster ki misaal: do motor wali chadhai, beech ki utaar, oonche platform wala station.
Kya kis jaisa: Roller coaster ki gaadi = electron; dono motor wali chain = roshni ke do dhakke, pehla PS II par (680 nm) aur doosra PS I par (700 nm); har choti = electron acceptor; pehli lambi utaar = PS II se PS I tak cytochromes ki kadi, jahan electron apne aap downhill utarta hai; doosri, aur oonchi choti = PS I ka acceptor (greater redox potential); aakhri utaar = NADP⁺ tak; oonche platform wala station = NADP⁺, jahan electron ka safar khatam hota hai; poore raaste gaadi wahi rehti hai = electron kharch nahi hota; oonchai = energy, isliye pehli utaar ke baad doosra dhakka chahiye.
Kahan tootti hai: Electron sach mein upar-neeche nahi jaata: oonchai yahan energy hai, membrane mein jagah nahi. Gaadi ek patri par khud chalti hai, par electron ek molecule se doosre molecule ke paas jaata hai, jo use lete aur aage dete hain. Asli roller coaster ghoom kar shuru wale station par laut aata hai, par is raaste mein electron aakhri molecule (NADP⁺) par ruk jaata hai, wapas PS II par nahi aata. Motor aur chain bijli se chalte hain, yahan dhakka roshni deti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6-k4
Ab doosra padaav: PS II ke acceptor ke baad electron jaata kahan hai? Acceptor ke baad electron molecules ki ek kadi mein jaata hai, jismein kai khaas proteins baithe hain. Yeh proteins electron ko ek pal ke liye lete hain, aur phir agle ko de dete hain. Inke beech mein loha, yaani iron, ka ek atom hota hai; wahi electron ko leta aur aage deta hai. In proteins ko cytochromes kehte hain: cyto yaani cell, aur chrome yaani rang, kyunki yeh rangeen proteins hain. Isliye electron ko aage le jaane wala yeh intezaam cytochromes wala electron transport system kehlata hai. Ab dekho ki electron is kadi mein kis disha mein chalta hai. Yaad karo, molecule se electron nikal jaaye toh oxidation hota hai, aur electron mil jaaye toh reduction. Har molecule electron ko alag zor se pakadta hai. Koi use kas kar pakadta hai, koi aasaani se chhod deta hai. Pakad ke is zor ke maap ko redox potential kehte hain; redox yaani reduction aur oxidation, dono milkar. Ab saare molecules ko is maap ke hisaab se upar-neeche ek line mein khada karo; yahi redox potential scale hai. Upar woh molecule aate hain jo electron aasaani se chhod dete hain. Neeche woh, jo use kas kar pakadte hain. Isliye electron apne aap upar se neeche jaata hai, kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf. Is utaar mein uski kuch energy nikalti hai. Electron ke is apne aap neeche utarne ko downhill jaana kehte hain; down yaani neeche, aur hill yaani pahadi. Bilkul roller coaster ki pehli utaar ki tarah, jahan gaadi bina motor ke neeche aati hai. Aksar hum sochte hain ki energy dete-dete electron raaste mein kharch ho jaata hai; par aisa nahi hai. Electron transport chain se guzarte hue electron kharch nahi hote. Wahi electron ek molecule se agle molecule tak badhta jaata hai. Chain yaani zanjeer: jaise zanjeer kadiyon se banti hai, waise hi yeh kadi bhi kai molecules se bani hai. Kharch hoti hai toh sirf uski thodi energy. Bilkul waise, jaise roller coaster ki gaadi utaar par apni oonchai khoti hai. Aur yeh kadi electron ko le kahan jaati hai? Yeh kadi electron ko seedhe PS I ke pigments tak pahuncha deti hai.
Screen par: PS II ke acceptor se aage molecules ki ek kadi (zanjeer ki kadiyon jaisi); kadi mein kuch proteins chamakte, electron ek pal unke paas rukta aur aage jaata; ek protein ke beech loha (Fe) ka atom electron leta-deta; label 'cytochromes: cyto = cell, chrome = rang', proteins halke rangeen. Label 'cytochromes wala electron transport system'. Phir ek khadi seedhi jaisi patti: molecules upar-neeche khade, upar 'electron aasaani se chhodte', neeche 'kas kar pakadte'; kone mein 'oxidation = electron nikalna, reduction = electron milna'; label 'redox potential = pakad ka maap; redox = reduction + oxidation'; patti par likha 'redox potential scale'. Electron upar se neeche ek-ek molecule par utarta, har kadam par halki chamak (energy nikalti); label 'downhill: down = neeche, hill = pahadi'; kone mein roller coaster ki pehli utaar. 'Electron kharch?' par kaata nishaan: wahi electron har kadam par chamakta rehta, sirf oonchai ghatti. Aakhir mein kadi ka sira PS I ke pigments tak pahunchta.
Terms: cytochromes = Electron lekar aage dene wale rangeen proteins; cyto = cell, chrome = rang; inme loha (iron) electron leta-deta hai.; redox potential = Molecule electron ko kitne zor se pakadta ya kitni aasaani se chhodta hai, iska maap; redox = reduction + oxidation.; downhill = Redox potential scale par electron ka apne aap upar se neeche utarna, jismein energy nikalti hai; down = neeche, hill = pahadi.
Kitaab se bahar: Cytochromes mein heme ka loha (iron) electron leta-deta hai aur inka rang inhi se hai (cyto + chrome); redox potential ko 'pakad ka zor' aur scale ko upar-neeche ki line ki tarah samjhaya; downhill = down + hill; chain = zanjeer; electron kharch nahi hota, sirf uski energy ghatti hai (roller coaster ki utaar).
Yaad: 11.2-k10, 11.4-k13
p009-i1 system consisting of cytochromes (Figure 11.5). This movement of electrons is downhill, in terms of an oxidation-reduction or redox potential scale. The electrons are not used up as they pass through the electron transport chain, but are passed on to the pigments of photosystem PS I. Simultaneously, electrons in the reaction centre of PS I are also excited when they receive red light of wavelength 700 nm and are transferred to another accepter [acceptor] molecule that has a greater redox potential.
samjhana11.6-k5
Ab kadi ke doosre sire par chalo, jahan PS I baitha hai. PS II ka electron abhi kadi mein neeche utar hi raha hota hai. Theek usi waqt PS I par bhi roshni padti hai. PS I ke reaction centre, yaani P700, ke electron 700 nm wavelength wali laal roshni paakar excite ho jaate hain. Yeh excited electron ek doosre acceptor molecule ke paas chala jaata hai. Yeh doosra acceptor redox potential scale par PS II wale acceptor se bhi oonchi jagah baitha hai. Isliye ise greater redox potential wala acceptor kaha jaata hai. Wahan pahunch kar electron sabse zyada energy wali haalat mein hota hai. Yeh doosra dhakka isliye zaroori hai, kyunki kadi se utar kar electron neeche aa chuka hota hai. Roller coaster mein yahi doosri chain hai, jo gaadi ko pehle se bhi oonchi choti tak le jaati hai. Ab socho, PS I ka apna electron toh chala gaya, toh uski jagah khaali ho gayi. Ek clue par dhyan do: PS II ka electron kadi par neeche aa raha hai. Aur yeh kadi seedhe PS I tak jaati hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: PS I ki yeh khaali jagah kaun bharta hoga? Aapne socha hoga ki PS II se aaya electron hi use bharta hai; aapne bilkul sahi socha. Kadi PS II wale electron ko PS I ke pigments tak pahunchati hai. Wahi electron P700 ki khaali jagah bhar deta hai. Isliye PS I baar-baar electron dekar bhi khaali nahi hota: uski bharpaai PS II se aate electron karte rehte hain. Yahi woh jod hai jo pichhli film ne khula chhoda tha: dono photosystem electron ke ek hi safar se jude hain. Pehle PS II, phir kadi, phir PS I; dono ek line mein, ek ke baad ek kaam karte hain.
Screen par: Kadi ka doosra sira aur wahan PS I ka ghera; kadi par neeche utarta PS II ka electron, aur usi waqt PS I par 700 nm ki laal roshni (dono saath-saath, label 'usi waqt'). P700 ka electron uchhal kar ek doosre acceptor tak, jo scale ki patti par PS II wale acceptor se bhi upar bana; label 'greater redox potential wala acceptor: sabse zyada energy'. Kone mein roller coaster ki doosri chain, oonchi choti tak. Phir P700 par electron ki khaali jagah ka chamakta ghera; clue ka chhota teer kadi ke sire se PS I ki taraf. Sawaal 'Ruko, pehle khud socho: khaali jagah kaun bharega?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir kadi se aaya PS II ka electron P700 ki khaali jagah mein baith jaata; label 'bharpaai'. Aakhir mein ek line: PS II → kadi → PS I, aur upar 'dono photosystem jude'.
Kitaab se bahar: Book ka 'greater redox potential' (PS I ka doosra acceptor) figure ki tarah scale par oonchi jagah aur zyada energy ke roop mein padhaya; electrochemistry ki bhasha mein is acceptor ka reduction potential zyada negative hota hai, yeh baat sirf yahan record mein. PS II ka electron PS I ki khaali jagah bharta hai, yeh 'passed on to the pigments of PS I' ka matlab; ruko, pehle khud socho isi mod par.
Yaad: 11.5-k6
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6-k6
Ab teesra aur aakhri padaav: PS I ke doosre acceptor se electron aage kidhar badhta hai? PS I ke acceptor ke baad electron phir downhill jaata hai, yaani apne aap neeche utarta hai. Bilkul roller coaster ki doosri utaar ki tarah, jo gaadi ko aakhri station tak le jaati hai. Yeh station ek khaas molecule hai, jo electron lene ke liye khaali baitha rehta hai. Socho, bazaar jaate waqt haath mein ek khaali thaila, jo saamaan bharne ka intezaar kar raha ho. Is khaali molecule ka naam NADP⁺ hai, aur electron aate hi yeh bharne lagta hai. Yaad karo, kisi molecule ko electron milna hi uska reduction hai. Isliye electron judte hi NADP⁺ reduce ho jaata hai; yahi is padaav ka asli kaam hai. Ek NADP⁺ ko poora bharne ke liye do electron chahiye. Bhar kar yahi molecule energy-rich, yaani energy se bhara, NADPH ban jaata hai. NADPH ko aap pehle se jaante ho: yeh electron aur hydrogen lekar stroma tak jaata hai, jahan sugar banti hai. Exam ke liye yeh pakki kar lo: khaali thaila NADP⁺ hai, aur bhara thaila NADPH. Dono ke naam aapas mein mat badalna. Toh electron ka safar yahin khatam hota hai; roller coaster ki gaadi station par ruk gayi.
Screen par: PS I ke acceptor se electron ek doosri utaar par neeche; kone mein roller coaster ki doosri utaar aur oonche platform wala station. Station ki jagah ek molecule, uske paas khaali thaile ki chhoti tasveer (bazaar jaata haath); label 'NADP⁺ = khaali thaila'. Do electron aakar judte, thaila bharta; label 'electron milna = reduction', 'NADP⁺ reduce hua'; ginti '2 electron'. Bhara thaila: label 'NADPH = energy-rich, energy se bhara'; NADPH stroma ki taraf jaata, wahan sugar ke moti. Exam ka card: 'khaali NADP⁺, bhara NADPH, naam mat badlo'. Aakhir mein roller coaster ki gaadi station par rukti.
Terms: NADP⁺ = Electron lene ko taiyaar khaali carrier molecule (khaali thaila); electron paakar NADPH banta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Khaali aur bhare thaile ki misaal; ek NADP⁺ ko do electron chahiye; book ka 'energy-rich NADP⁺' yahan bhare hue NADPH par lagaya, kyunki energy se bhara asal mein NADPH hai; exam ke liye NADP⁺ aur NADPH ka farq.
Yaad: 11.3-k7, 11.2-k10
p009-i2 These electrons then are moved downhill again, this time to a molecule of energy-rich NADP⁺. The addition of these electrons reduces NADP⁺
samjhana11.6-k7
Yaad karo, hydrogen sabse chhota atom hai: beech mein ek proton, aur bahar ek electron. Woh electron nikal jaaye, toh jo bachta hai use H⁺ likhte hain; plus ka nishaan uske positive charge ka hai. Ab NADP⁺ ke bharne ko aksar NADPH + H⁺ likha jaata hai. Ek NADP⁺ do electron aur H⁺ lekar NADPH banta hai. Hisaab poora rakhne ke liye saath mein ek H⁺ likha jaata hai. Ab is poore safar ko shuru se aakhir tak ek saath dekho. Roshni ke bina electron kabhi upar nahi chadhta; upar chadhne ke liye use energy chahiye. PS II par roshni ne electron ko acceptor tak upar chadhaya; is chadhai ko uphill jaana kehte hain, up yaani upar. Phir electron transport chain se neeche PS I tak aaya, wahan dobara excite hua, aur doosre acceptor tak chadha. Aakhir mein downhill utar kar woh NADP⁺ tak pahuncha, aur use NADPH + H⁺ bana diya. Is raaste mein jo bhi molecule electron lekar agle ko deta hai, use carrier kehte hain; carry yaani dhona. Jaise cycle ke peeche ka carrier saamaan dhota hai, waise yeh molecule electron dhote hain. Ab saare carriers ko redox potential scale par unki jagah rakho, aur electron ke raaste ki lakeer kheencho. Lakeer pehle upar jaati hai, phir neeche, phir dobara upar, aur aakhir mein neeche. Is lakeer ko ek karvat lita do, toh angrezi ka akshar Z dikhta hai. Isi khaas aakaar ki wajah se is poore raaste ko Z scheme kehte hain; scheme yaani khaaka. Z ka aakaar isliye banta hai, kyunki electron do baar roshni se upar uthta hai. Aur dono baar uske baad woh neeche utarta hai. Bilkul roller coaster ki patri jaisa: do chadhai, beech mein lambi utaar, aur aakhri utaar station tak. Kai bachche sochte hain ki electron membrane mein sach mein Z ke aakaar mein chalta hai. Par asal mein Z sirf energy ka chitra hai, membrane ka raasta nahi. Exam mein Z scheme aksar adhoora dekar poochha jaata hai. Isliye iska kram pakka yaad rakho: PS II, acceptor, kadi, PS I, acceptor, aur NADP⁺.
Screen par: Hydrogen ka chhota atom: beech mein ek proton, bahar ek electron; electron udkar chala jaata, bacha proton, label 'H⁺: plus = positive charge'. Phir NADP⁺ ke bhare thaile ke paas likha 'NADPH + H⁺'; chhota hisaab: NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH, saath mein ek H⁺. Phir poora safar ek lakeer ki tarah: PS II par roshni, electron upar acceptor tak (label 'uphill: up = upar'), kadi se neeche PS I tak, phir roshni se upar doosre acceptor tak, aakhir mein neeche NADP⁺ tak. Label 'carrier: carry = dhona', kone mein cycle ke peeche ka carrier jis par saamaan bandha. Saare carriers redox potential scale par apni jagah, unhe jodti lakeer: upar, neeche, upar, neeche. Lakeer karvat leti hai aur akshar Z ban jaata hai; label 'Z scheme (scheme = khaaka)'. Kone mein roller coaster ki patri usi aakaar mein. 'Membrane mein Z raasta?' par kaata nishaan: membrane mein carriers baithe, Z sirf energy ke graph par. Aakhir mein exam ka kram card: PS II → acceptor → kadi → PS I → acceptor → NADP⁺.
Terms: H⁺ = Hydrogen atom jiska electron nikal gaya; bacha sirf ek proton, plus = positive charge.; uphill = Scale par electron ka upar chadhna, jo roshni ki energy se hi hota hai; up = upar.; carrier = Electron lekar agle molecule ko dene wala molecule; carry = dhona.; Z scheme = Do baar roshni se upar uthte electron ka poora raasta, jo carriers ko redox potential scale par rakhne par karvat lete Z jaisa dikhta hai; scheme = khaaka.
Kitaab se bahar: H⁺ ka matlab (proton); NADPH + H⁺ ke likhne ka hisaab (NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ → NADPH + H⁺); carrier ki cycle wali misaal; lakeer ko karvat lita kar Z dekhna; Z membrane ka raasta nahi, energy ka chitra hai (exam ki galatfahmi); adhoore Z scheme wale exam sawaal ke liye kram.
p009-i3 to NADPH + H⁺. This whole scheme of transfer of electrons, starting from the PS II, uphill to the acceptor, down the electron transport chain to PS I, excitation of electrons, transfer to another acceptor, and finally down hill to NADP⁺ reducing it to NADPH + H⁺ is called the Z scheme, due to its characteristic shape (Figure 11.5). This shape is formed when all the carriers are placed in a sequence on a redox potential scale.
figure11.6-k8
Ab Figure 11.5 dekho; iske neeche likha hai Z scheme of light reaction, yaani light reaction ka Z jaisa khaaka. Upar baayein ek bracket par likha hai Photosystem II, aur daayein doosre bracket par Photosystem I. Har bracket apne photosystem ke saath uske acceptor ko bhi gherta hai. Baayein ek peela gola hai, jis par likha hai Light, yaani roshni. Usse peele dashed teer dono taraf ke hare ovals tak jaate hain, aur dono ovals par likha hai LHC. LHC wahi roshni batorne wale jhund hain, jo humne pichhli film mein dekhe. Peele teer batate hain ki roshni dono photosystem par padti hai. Ab ungli rakh kar chalo, baayein PS II ke LHC se. Yahan se ek laal teer seedha upar ek gulabi oval tak jaata hai, jis par likha hai e⁻ acceptor. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai: e yaani electron, aur upar ka chhota minus uska negative charge. Yeh upar jaata teer pehli chadhai hai: roshni ne electron ko uthaya, aur acceptor ne use pakda. Ab ungli neeche le chalo: laal teer ek baingani dabbe se hote hue neeche utarte hain. Dabbe par likha hai Electron transport system. Yahi cytochromes wali kadi hai, aur teer ka neeche jaana electron ka downhill utaar hai. Is dabbe ke upar ek mudi hui teer dekho: ADP + iP se ATP. Matlab electron ke is utaar ke saath ATP banne ka kaam bhi juda hai. ADP aur iP kya hain, aur utaar se ATP banta kaise hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge. Laal teer utaar ke baad daayein PS I ke LHC tak pahunchte hain. Wahan se doosra laal teer phir upar jaata hai, aur doosre gulabi oval par bhi likha hai e⁻ acceptor. Is acceptor se electron aakhir mein ek mudi hui teer par NADP⁺ tak jaata hai. Usi teer par likha hai NADPH. Dekho, yahi hamara station hai: khaali NADP⁺ electron paakar bhara hua NADPH ban gaya. Ab PS II ke neeche dekho: wahan likha hai H₂O, aur usse ek teer 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] tak jaata hai. Wahan se ek teer electron ko PS II ke LHC tak pahunchata hai. Matlab PS II ki khaali jagah ka rishta paani se hai. Bracket wala [O] yaani oxygen ka ek atom; aise do atom milkar O₂ gas banti hai. Yeh hissa abhi bas pehchaan lo; iski poori kahani aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Figure 11.5 ka saaf, bada chitra; neeche caption 'Z scheme of light reaction'. Ungli baari-baari: upar baayein bracket 'Photosystem II' aur daayein bracket 'Photosystem I' (dono apne acceptor samet) → baayein peela gola 'Light', usse peele dashed teer dono hare ovals tak, dono par 'LHC' → baayein LHC se laal teer upar gulabi oval 'e⁻ acceptor' tak (chhota label: e⁻ = electron, minus = negative charge; kone mein roller coaster ki pehli chadhai) → laal teer neeche baingani dabbe 'Electron transport system' se hote hue (label: cytochromes wali kadi, downhill) → dabbe ke upar mudi teer 'ADP + iP → ATP' halki chamak mein, uspar 'aage' ka chhota nishaan → teer daayein LHC tak → wahan se doosra laal teer upar doosre gulabi oval 'e⁻ acceptor' tak → mudi teer par 'NADP⁺' aur uske upar 'NADPH' (kone mein station par ruki gaadi) → PS II ke neeche 'H₂O' aur teer '2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]' tak, ek teer electron ko PS II ke LHC tak; label '[O] = oxygen ka ek atom; 2 [O] = O₂'; is hisse par 'aage' ka nishaan.
Terms: e⁻ = Electron ka nishaan; e = electron, minus = negative charge.
Kitaab se bahar: e⁻ ka matlab; peele dashed teer roshni ke, laal teer electron ke; ADP + iP → ATP electron ke utaar par bana hai, iska tareeka aage; [O] oxygen ka ek atom hai, O₂ nahi (exam ki baareek baat); paani wala hissa sirf pehchaan, poori baat aage.
Yaad: 11.5-k2
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p009-i4 Z scheme of light reaction. Photosystem II is shown on the left and Photosystem I on the right. Dashed yellow arrows from Light reach both LHC ovals. A red arrow rises from the PS II LHC to its e⁻ acceptor; red arrows then descend through an Electron transport system to the PS I LHC. ADP + iP → ATP is drawn above this transport system. A second red arrow rises from the PS I LHC to its e⁻ acceptor, followed by transfer toward NADP⁺ reduction to NADPH. Beneath PS II, H₂O → 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] is shown, with an arrow supplying electrons to its LHC.
example11.6-k9
Chalo, ab ginti karke dekhte hain ki ek NADPH banane mein kitni roshni lagti hai. Humne dekha ki ek NADP⁺ ko poora bharne ke liye do electron chahiye. Ab pehle electron ka safar dekho. PS II par ek photon padta hai, aur P680 ka electron uchhal kar PS II ke acceptor tak jaata hai. Usi waqt roshni ka ek aur packet P700 ko jagaata hai, aur uska electron PS I ke apne acceptor par pahunch jaata hai. PS II wala electron cytochromes ki kadi se utar kar P700 ki khaali jagah bhar deta hai. Aur P700 wala electron downhill utar kar NADP⁺ tak pahunch jaata hai. Toh NADP⁺ tak ek electron pahunchane ke peeche do photon lage: ek PS II par, aur ek PS I par. Ab batao, ek poore NADPH ke liye kul kitne photon lagenge? Ek poore NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon lagenge. Do electron hain, aur har electron ke peeche do photon. Inme se do photon PS II par padte hain, aur do PS I par. Aur dhyan do, poore raaste mein dono electron kahin kharch nahi hue; bas unki energy upar-neeche hoti rahi. Roller coaster mein socho toh do gaadiyaan, har ek ne do chadhai chadhi, aur dono station tak pahunchi.
Screen par: Ginti ka board: 'ek NADPH = ?'. Pehle '2 electron' likha.
