Phir Z ka raasta: PS II par ek photon (roshni ka packet) girta, P680 ka electron uchhal kar acceptor tak; udhar PS I par ek photon, P700 ka electron doosre acceptor tak; PS II wala electron kadi se utar kar P700 ki khaali jagah mein; P700 wala electron neeche NADP⁺ tak. Board par '1 electron = 2 photon (1 PS II + 1 PS I)'. Sawaal 'ek NADPH = kitne photon?', phir '2 × 2 = 4': do photon PS II par, do PS I par. Dono electron ke paas 'kharch nahi' ka nishaan. Kone mein do roller coaster gaadiyaan, har ek do chadhai chadh kar station par.
Kitaab se bahar: Ek NADPH ke liye kam se kam chaar photon (do electron, har electron ke peeche PS II aur PS I par ek-ek); asli patti mein kuch roshni bekaar jaane se thodi zyada lagti hai, yeh sirf record mein.
Yaad: 11.5-k7

ruko, socho11.6-k10
Socho, phir aage badhenge: agar beech ki kadi kahin ruk jaaye, aur PS II ka electron PS I tak na pahunche, toh kya hoga? Agar kadi ruk jaaye, toh PS I ki khaali jagah bharne wala koi nahi rahega. PS I ek-do baar electron dekar khaali baitha rahega, aur NADP⁺ tak electron pahunchna band ho jaayega. Isliye NADPH banna ruk jaayega, aur stroma mein sugar banne ka kaam bhi thap pad jaayega. Kuch kharpatwar-naashak dawaiyaan, yaani weed-killer, theek yahi karti hain: woh is kadi mein electron ka raasta rok deti hain. Isliye un dawaiyon se kharpatwar ka paudha roshni mein khada rehkar bhi bhookha mar jaata hai. Matlab NADPH banane ke liye dono photosystem ek-doosre par tike hain. Beech ki kadi hi un dono ko jodti hai.
Screen par: Z ka raasta; beech ki kadi par laal 'ruka' ka nishaan; sawaal ka dabba 'kadi ruk jaaye toh?'. PS II ka electron kadi par atka; P700 ki khaali jagah khaali hi chamakti; NADP⁺ tak jaata teer dhundhla aur band; NADPH ki jagah '0', stroma mein sugar ki machine thami. Phir khet mein kharpatwar par dawai ka chhidkaav (label: weed-killer), kadi par 'rok'; dhoop mein khada kharpatwar murjha jaata. Aakhir mein do photosystem aur beech ki kadi, upar 'ek-doosre par tike'.
Kitaab se bahar: Kuch weed-killer (jaise DCMU aur atrazine wali dawaiyaan) PS II ke aage electron ka raasta rok deti hain; kadi rukne par NADPH banna ruk jaata hai.
Jawab: Kadi ruk jaaye toh PS II ka electron PS I tak nahi pahunchta, isliye P700 ki khaali jagah nahi bharti; PS I ek-do baar electron dekar ruk jaata hai, NADP⁺ tak electron jaana band hota hai aur NADPH nahi banta, to stroma mein sugar banna bhi rukta hai; kuch weed-killer is kadi ko rok kar yahi karte hain. Isliye NADPH ke liye dono photosystem ek-doosre par tike hain.
Aam galti: Galat soch 1: PS I ko roshni mil rahi hai, toh woh akele hi NADPH banata rahega; galat, uski khaali jagah PS II ke electron se hi bharti hai. Galat soch 2: kadi bas ek raasta hai, uske rukne se kuch nahi bigadta; galat, wahi dono photosystem ko jodti hai. Galat soch 3: electron raaste mein kharch ho jaate hain, isliye kami hoti hai; galat, electron kharch nahi hote, unka aage jaana rukta hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.6-k11
Chalo, aaj ke safar ko shuru se aakhir tak ek baar phir chalein. PS II ka P680, 680 nm ki laal roshni se excite hota hai, aur uska electron acceptor tak chadhta hai. Phir electron cytochromes ki kadi mein downhill utarta hai, kharch hue bina, aur PS I tak pahunchta hai. PS I ka P700, 700 nm ki roshni se excite hota hai. Uska electron doosre, aur bhi oonche acceptor tak jaata hai. Wahan se electron downhill utar kar NADP⁺ ko reduce karta hai, aur NADPH + H⁺ banta hai. Saare carriers ko redox potential scale par rakhne se yeh raasta Z jaisa dikhta hai, isliye iska naam Z scheme hai. Ab wahi sawaal jisse shuru kiya tha: itne electron chhod kar bhi photosystem khaali kyun nahi padte, aur electron pahunchte kahan hain? Photosystem isliye khaali nahi padte, kyunki ek ka chhoda electron doosre ki kami poori karta hai. PS II ka electron PS I ko milta hai. PS I ka electron NADP⁺ tak pahunch kar ruk jaata hai, aur NADPH ban kar stroma ke kaam aata hai. Aur PS II ki khaali jagah ka rishta, jaisa figure ne dikhaya, paani se hai. Roller coaster mein bhi yahi tha: gaadi wahi rahi, bas do baar upar kheenchi gayi aur do baar neeche utri. Par ek baat abhi bhi khuli hai: paani jaisa seedha-saadha molecule apne electron chhodta kaise hai, aur PS II ko lagaataar electron kaise milte rehte hain? Paani apne electron kaise chhodta hai aur PS II ko lagaataar electron kaise milte hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Aaj ka safar Z ki ek lakeer par: PS II (P680, 680 nm) se electron upar acceptor tak → cytochromes ki kadi par neeche (kharch nahi) → PS I (P700, 700 nm) se aur bhi oonche acceptor tak → neeche NADP⁺, jo NADPH + H⁺ banta; carriers scale par aur Z ka aakaar. Shuru ka sawaal laut kar aata: 'electron kahan? photosystem khaali kyun nahi?'; jawab ke teer: PS II ka electron PS I ki khaali jagah mein, PS I ka electron NADP⁺ par ruk kar NADPH, NADPH stroma ki taraf; PS II ki khaali jagah ke neeche paani ki boond. Kone mein roller coaster ki wahi gaadi, do chadhai aur do utaar. Aakhir mein paani ki boond par bada sawaal ka nishaan 'paani electron kaise chhodta hai?', jo khula rehta hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.6.1 Splitting of Water · 9 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 20 min
Maqsad: PS II ko din bhar naye electron milte kahan se hain, aur shaant-sa paani unhe deta kaise hai?
Misaal: dahi ka bilona: dahi = paani; dahi mein chhupa makhan = paani ke electron; mathani ki rassi kheenchne wala haath = P680, jo har roshni ke baad ek baar kheenchta hai aur ek electron alag karta hai; bahar nikala makhan = PS II ki khaali jagah bharne wala electron; matke mein bachi chhachh = H⁺ aur O₂; matka = thylakoid ki thaili, matke ka andar = lumen, matke ke bahar = stroma; mathani matke ke andar ghoomti hai = water splitting complex inner side par; dahi poora makhan aur chhachh mein bant jaata hai = atom aur charge ka barabar hisaab; roz naya dahi = jad se aata lagaataar paani
Kitab ki baatein (40): kitab kehti hai 11 · shabd ka matlab 6 · pehle ki zaroori baat 12 · figure ka nishaan 0 · teacher ki extra baat 11
f1 kitab kehti hai Sawaal uthta hai: PS II lagaataar electron kaise deta rehta hai (How does PS II supply electrons continuously?).
f2 pehle ki zaroori baat Electrons leaving PS II during excitation: roshni se P680 ka electron excite hokar acceptor ke paas chala jaata hai, aur PS II mein ek electron kam ho jaata hai (11.5, 11.6).
f3 kitab kehti hai PS II se aage gaye electrons ki jagah bharni zaroori hai (the electrons must be replaced).
f4 kitab kehti hai: splitting of water Yeh bharpaai paani ke tootne (splitting of water) se mile electron karte hain.
f5 kitab kehti hai Paani ka tootna PS II se juda hai.
f6 kitab kehti hai Paani toot kar 2H⁺, [O] aur electron deta hai.
f7 kitab kehti hai: net products Isse oxygen banti hai, jo photosynthesis ke net products mein se ek hai.
f8 kitab kehti hai PS I se gaye electrons ki bharpaai PS II karta hai.
f9 shabd ka matlab: splitting of water Splitting of water: paani ke molecule ka toot kar hisson mein bantna; split = todna, hisson mein baantna.
f10 shabd ka matlab: net products Net products: poori kriya mein jo gaya aur jo bana, dono ka hisaab jodne ke baad aakhir mein bachne wali cheezein; net = kul milakar bacha; photosynthesis ke net products: sugar aur oxygen.
f11 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron loss from excited reaction centres: excite hua electron reaction centre chhod kar acceptor tak jaata hai, aur centre mein uski jagah khaali rehti hai (11.6 mein P700 ki khaali jagah).
f12 pehle ki zaroori baat Electron transport from PS II to PS I: PS II ka electron cytochromes ki kadi se utar kar PS I ki khaali jagah bharta hai (11.6).
f13 pehle ki zaroori baat Net hisaab (11.2): photosynthesis ki equation mein baarah paani gaye aur chhe naye bane, toh net kharch chhe paani ka.
f14 pehle ki zaroori baat Redox potential scale (11.6): har molecule electron ko alag zor se pakadta hai; electron apne aap kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf jaata hai.
f15 teacher ki extra baat Paani apne electron bahut kas kar pakadta hai (scale par bahut neeche); roshni se electron kho kar P680 jeevit duniya ka sabse taaqatwar electron kheenchne wala molecule ban jaata hai aur paani se electron kheench leta hai, har roshni ke saath ek.
f16 teacher ki extra baat Roshni seedhe paani nahi todti; roshni P680 ka electron uchhaalti hai, aur khaali P680 paani ke electron kheenchta hai; roshni ke bina yeh nahi hota, isliye exam mein ise photolysis of water bhi kehte hain (photo = roshni, lysis = todna).
f17 teacher ki extra baat Exam: PS I ko electron seedhe PS II se (kadi ke raaste) milta hai; par NADP⁺ tak pahunchne wala har electron asal mein paani se chala tha.
f18 pehle ki zaroori baat Oxygen from water (11.2): van Niel ke suraag aur 1941 ke oxygen-18 wale prayog ne dikhaya ki patti ki O₂ paani se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi.
f19 kitab kehti hai: 4e⁻ Do paani ke molecule toot kar chaar H⁺, oxygen ka ek molecule aur chaar electron dete hain: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻.
f20 shabd ka matlab: 4e⁻ 4e⁻: chaar electron; aage ka 4 unki ginti, e⁻ electron ka nishaan, upar ka minus har electron ka negative charge.
f21 pehle ki zaroori baat Chemical equation coefficients and subscripts: formula ke aage ka bada number molecule ki ginti, neeche ka chhota number molecule ke andar atom ki ginti, upar ka chhota nishaan charge; teer = banta hai.
f22 pehle ki zaroori baat Electric charges on electrons and protons: electron par negative, proton yaani H⁺ par positive charge; paani ka molecule bina charge ka (Class 9; 11.6).
f23 teacher ki extra baat Hisaab: teer ke dono taraf chaar hydrogen aur do oxygen atom; charge bhi barabar, daayein chaar plus aur chaar minus milkar zero, jaise paani; ek O₂ ke liye do paani aur chaar electron (anupaat 2 : 4 : 1 : 4).
f24 teacher ki extra baat Figure 11.5 ka 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O] ek paani ka hisaab hai; do paani par sab dugna: 4e⁻ + 4H⁺ + O₂.
f25 teacher ki extra baat P680 ek baar mein ek hi electron kheenchta hai; isliye chaar electron chaar baar mein, har baar ek roshni ke baad, nikalte hain, aur chauthe ke baad O₂ ka ek molecule nikalta hai.
f26 teacher ki extra baat Ek O₂ ke chaar electron se do NADPH bante hain; har electron ke peeche do photon (PS II aur PS I par ek-ek), isliye ek O₂ ke saath kam se kam aath photon lagte hain (exam).
f27 kitab kehti hai: water splitting complex Water splitting complex PS II se juda hai.
f28 kitab kehti hai: inner side PS II khud thylakoid ki membrane ki inner side par baitha hai.
f29 shabd ka matlab: water splitting complex Water splitting complex: PS II ke saath juda paani todne wala hissa; complex = kai proteins se mil kar bani ek machine.
f30 shabd ka matlab: inner side Inner side: thylakoid membrane ka andar wala chehra, jo lumen ki taraf hai; inner = andar ka; bahar wala chehra outer side.
f31 shabd ka matlab: lumen Lumen: thylakoid ki band thaili ke andar ki ghiri hui jagah.
f32 pehle ki zaroori baat Thylakoid membrane and enclosed lumen: chloroplast mein chapti band thailiyan (thylakoid); unki deewar thylakoid membrane, ek patli jhilli; uske andar ghiri jagah lumen (11.3).
f33 pehle ki zaroori baat Thylakoid lumen versus outer stroma: thaili ke andar lumen, thailiyon ke bahar chloroplast ka gaadha dravya stroma (11.3).
f34 pehle ki zaroori baat Water splitting at PS II: paani PS II ke paas toot kar uski khaali jagah bharta hai (isi film mein abhi dekha).
f35 teacher ki extra baat PS II lumen mein tairta nahi; woh membrane mein dhansa hai, aur uska paani todne wala hissa lumen ki taraf munh kiye hai.
f36 teacher ki extra baat Is complex mein manganese ke chaar atom, ek calcium aur chloride hote hain, jo paani ke electron sambhaal kar PS II tak pahunchate hain (exam: manganese, calcium, chloride); oxygen yahin nikalti hai, isliye naam oxygen evolving complex bhi.
f37 kitab kehti hai Sawaal: paani tootne se bane protons (H⁺) aur O₂ kahan chhootte honge, lumen mein ya membrane ki outer side par?
f38 pehle ki zaroori baat Lumen-facing water-splitting complex: paani todne wala complex lumen ki taraf khula hai (abhi dekha).
f39 teacher ki extra baat Jawab: H⁺ aur O₂ pehle lumen mein chhootte hain, kyunki paani lumen ki taraf wale complex par toot-ta hai; electron PS II ko jaate hain; O₂ chhota molecule hai, membranes paar karke patti se bahar nikal jaati hai; H⁺ membrane aasaani se paar nahi karte, lumen mein jama hote hain (unka kya hota hai, aage).
f40 teacher ki extra baat Hawa ki lagbhag saari oxygen kabhi na kabhi kisi patti ya alga mein paani ke tootne se bani hai; jad ka kheencha paani patti mein toot kar hamari saans ki oxygen banta hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.6.1-k1
Pichhli film mein humne electron ka poora safar dekha: PS II se carriers ki kadi, phir PS I, aur aakhir mein NADP⁺. Roshni ne ek hi electron ko do baar upar uthaya, bilkul roller coaster ki gaadi ki tarah. Aakhir mein NADP⁺ use lekar NADPH ban gaya. Yaad rakhein: PS I ki khaali jagah PS II se aaya electron bharta hai, isliye dono photosystem ek hi line mein bandhe hain. Aage hum dekhenge ki is line ke bilkul shuru mein, PS II ki apni khaali jagah kaun bharta hai. Pichhli film ka figure ek ishaara chhod gaya tha: PS II ki khaali jagah ka rishta paani se hai. Par yahan ek ajeeb baat hai, use dhyan se socho. Dhoop mein PS II pal-pal apna electron aage bhejta rehta hai, aur yeh din bhar chalta hai. Agar har baar uska ek electron kam hota jaata, toh woh kuch hi pal mein khaali ho jaata. Aur paani ko toh aap roz peete ho; woh kisi ko apne aap electron nahi deta. Toh PS II ko din bhar naye electron milte kahan se hain, aur shaant-sa paani unhe deta kaise hai? PS II ko din bhar electron kahan se aur kaise milte hain, yahi aaj ka sawaal hai. Pichhli film ne bhi yahi sawaal khula chhoda tha. Aapke man mein bhi yahi aayega ki PS II itne lagaataar electron de kaise paata hai. Iska jawab hum teen kadam mein dhoondhenge. Pehle, PS II ki khaali jagah bharne wale electron kahan se aur kaise aate hain. Phir, do paani ke molecule tootne par kya-kya banta hai, uski poori ginti. Aur aakhir mein, yeh tootna thaili ke kis taraf hota hai, aur bani cheezein kahan jaati hain.
Screen par: Pichhli film ki yaad: Z ka raasta ek chalti lakeer par, PS II se carriers ki kadi, PS I, aur NADP⁺ jo NADPH banta; kone mein roller coaster ki gaadi, do chadhai ke saath. Dabba 'Yaad rakhein: PS I ki khaali jagah PS II ka electron bharta hai'. PS II par khaali ghera, uske neeche paani ki boond par chhota ishaara-teer. Phir dhoop mein PS II se ek ke baad ek electron nikalte, ginti badhti (1, 2, 3 ...); ek pal ke liye PS II khaali hota dikhe, uspar sawaal 'na bhare toh?'. Kone mein shaant paani ka gilaas. Sawaal ka dabba 'electron kahan se? paani deta kaise hai?'. Aakhir mein teen khaano ka naksha: 1 khaali jagah kaun aur kaise bharta; 2 do paani ka hisaab; 3 thaili ke kis taraf.
Kitaab se bahar: Opening recap of 11.6 (Z ka raasta, roller coaster, PS I ki bharpaai PS II se, figure ka paani wala ishaara) aur uska khula sawaal; aaj ka sawaal: PS II din bhar khaali kyun nahi hota aur shaant paani electron kaise deta hai; teen kadam ka naksha.
Yaad: 11.6-k11, 11.6-k5, 11.6-k8
p009-i21 You would then ask, How does PS II supply electrons continuously? The
samjhana11.6.1-k2
Pehle ek chhota sawaal: roshni ne P680 ka electron aage bhej diya, toh PS II mein kya bacha? Electron aage bhej dene ke baad PS II mein ek khaali jagah bachi, bilkul waisi jaisi humne P700 par dekhi thi. Yeh jagah na bhare, toh agli roshni aane par PS II ke paas aage bhejne ko electron hi nahi hoga. Isliye PS II se gaye har electron ki jagah bharni padti hai; gayi cheez ki jagah nayi cheez rakhni padti hai. Yeh bharpaai paani karta hai. Paani ke electron PS II ki khaali jagah bharte hain. Par paani ka molecule apne electron aise hi alag nahi karta. Electron nikalte hi paani ka molecule toot jaata hai. Isliye paani ke is tootne ko splitting of water kehte hain; split yaani todna, ya hisson mein baant dena. Dekho, yeh tootna PS II se juda hai. Paani PS II ke paas hi toot-ta hai, aur uske electron seedhe PS II ko milte hain. Yaad karo, Figure 11.5 mein PS II ke neeche likha tha: H₂O se 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]. Yahi ek paani ka hisaab hai: woh toot kar 2H⁺, [O] aur electron deta hai. 2H⁺ ka matlab hai do hydrogen ion, aur [O] ka matlab hai oxygen ka sirf ek atom. Jab aise do atom aapas mein judte hain, tab O₂ banti hai. Ab ek naya shabd samjho: net. Net ka matlab hai kul milakar bacha hua. Jaise subah das roti bani aur aath kha li, toh net do roti bachi. Yaad karo, photosynthesis ki equation mein baarah paani gaye aur chhe naye bane, toh net kharch chhe paani ka hua tha. Isi tarah poori photosynthesis ka hisaab jodne ke baad jo cheezein aakhir mein bachti hain, unhe net products kehte hain. Paani ke tootne se bani oxygen inhi net products mein se ek hai; doosri hai sugar. Ab PS I ki baat. Uski khaali jagah bharne wale electron PS II deta hai, jaisa humne pichhli film mein dekha. Aksar bachche sochte hain ki paani apne electron seedhe PS I ko bhi deta hai, par aisa nahi hai. Paani sirf PS II ko bharta hai. Phir PS II ka electron kadi se hokar PS I ko bharta hai; har photosystem apne peeche wale se bharta hai. Exam ke liye yeh baareek baat pakki kar lo: PS I ko electron seedhe PS II se milta hai. Par NADP⁺ tak pahunchne wala har electron asal mein paani se hi chala tha.
Screen par: PS II par chamakta khaali ghera; kone mein P700 ki waisi hi khaali jagah ki chhoti yaad. Agli roshni aati par aage bhejne ko kuch nahi, chhota kaata nishaan. Phir paani ka molecule PS II ke paas: uske electron khaali jagah mein jaate, aur molecule toot jaata; label 'splitting of water: split = todna'. Figure 11.5 ka chhota tukda: H₂O se 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]; labels '2H⁺ = do hydrogen ion', '[O] = oxygen ka ek atom'; do [O] judkar O₂. Roti ka chhota drishya: 10 bani, 8 khaayi, net 2; kone mein 12H₂O gaye, 6H₂O bane, net 6. Label 'net products: sugar aur O₂'. Phir ek line: paani → PS II → kadi → PS I; 'paani → PS I' wala seedha teer kaata gaya. Exam card: 'PS I ko seedhe PS II se; har electron asal mein paani se'.
Terms: splitting of water = Paani ke molecule ka toot kar hisson mein bantna; split = todna.; net products = Poori kriya ka hisaab jodne ke baad aakhir mein bachne wali cheezein; net = kul milakar bacha.
Kitaab se bahar: Gayi cheez ki jagah nayi rakhna (book ka 'replaced'); split = todna; net ka roti wala arth aur 11.2 ka net hisaab yaad dilaya; photosynthesis ke net products: sugar aur oxygen; exam ki baareek baat: PS I ko seedhe PS II se, par har electron asal mein paani se.
Yaad: 11.6-k5, 11.6-k8, 11.2-k13
p009-i22 electrons that were moved from photosystem II must be replaced. This is achieved by electrons available due to splitting of water. The splitting of water is associated with the PS II; water is split into 2H⁺, [O] and electrons. This creates oxygen, one of the net products of photosynthesis. The electrons needed to replace those removed from photosystem I are provided by photosystem II.
samjhana11.6.1-k3
Ab asli sawaal par aate hain: paani apne electron dene ko raazi kaise hota hai? Paani apne electron dene ko raazi hota hi nahi. Yeh samajhne ke liye pichhli film ki redox potential scale yaad karo. Har molecule electron ko alag zor se pakadta hai, aur electron apne aap kamzor pakad se mazboot pakad ki taraf jaata hai. Paani is scale par bahut neeche hai. Matlab woh apne electron bahut kas kar pakadta hai. Yahi wajah hai ki paani itna shaant aur tika hua molecule hai. Toh phir PS II paani se electron le kaise leta hai? PS II paani se electron isliye le paata hai, kyunki roshni ke baad uski pakad paani se bhi zyada mazboot ho jaati hai. Dekho, roshni ne P680 ka electron uchhaal kar acceptor ko de diya. Ab P680 mein ek electron kam hai, aur woh use wapas paane ke liye bechain hai. Electron kho kar P680 jeevit duniya ka sabse taaqatwar electron kheenchne wala molecule ban jaata hai. Isliye woh paas baithe paani se ek electron kheench leta hai. Paani ne diya nahi, P680 ne kheench liya. Aksar log sochte hain ki roshni seedhe paani ko tod deti hai, par aisa nahi hai. Roshni sirf P680 ka electron uchhaalti hai. Paani isliye toot-ta hai, kyunki khaali hua P680 uske electron kheench leta hai. Phir bhi yeh kaam roshni ke bina nahi hota. Isliye exam mein ise photolysis of water bhi kehte hain: photo yaani roshni, aur lysis yaani todna. Aur jitni baar roshni P680 ko khaali karti hai, utni hi baar woh paani se ek naya electron kheench leta hai. Socho, isi tarah PS II ki khaali jagah baar-baar, din bhar bharti rehti hai.
Screen par: Sawaal ka dabba 'paani electron dene ko raazi kaise?'. Pichhli film ki khadi patti (redox potential scale): upar kamzor pakad, neeche mazboot pakad, electron upar se neeche utarta. Paani ka molecule patti ke bahut neeche, uske electron kas kar jude. Sawaal 'PS II le kaise leta?'. Roshni P680 par padti, uska electron uchhal kar acceptor tak jaata; P680 par khaali ghera chamakta, aur patti par P680 paani se bhi neeche khisak jaata (sabse mazboot pakad). P680 ki taraf paani se ek electron kheencha jaata; label 'diya nahi, kheencha gaya'. 'Roshni → paani' ka seedha teer kaata gaya; sahi kram teen khaano mein: roshni → P680 khaali → paani se electron. Exam card 'photolysis of water: photo = roshni, lysis = todna'. Phir har nayi roshni ke saath ek naya electron kheencha jaata, baar-baar; PS II ka ghera har baar bhar jaata.
Kitaab se bahar: Paani ki electron par mazboot pakad aur roshni ke baad P680 ki usse bhi mazboot pakad (jeevit duniya ki sabse taaqatwar), 11.6 ki redox potential scale se; galat soch: roshni seedhe paani todti hai; exam ka naam photolysis of water (photo = roshni, lysis = todna). Book sirf itna kehti hai ki electron paani ke tootne se milte hain; kaise, yeh teacher ki taraf se.
Yaad: 11.6-k4, 11.6-k2
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
analogy11.6.1-k4
Is poori baat ko ghar ke ek kaam se samjho: matke mein dahi bilona, yaani mathani se mathna. Dahi mein makhan chhupa hota hai, par woh apne aap bahar nahi aata. Mathani ki rassi ko baar-baar kheenchna padta hai, ek baar is taraf, ek baar us taraf. Thodi der mathne ke baad makhan alag hokar upar tair aata hai, aur matke mein chhachh bach jaati hai. Makhan haath se nikaal kar kaam ke liye le jaate hain, par chhachh matke ke andar hi rehti hai. Ab batao, matke mein jo dahi daala tha, kya uska kuch hissa gaayab ho gaya? Matke ka dahi kahin gaayab nahi hua; woh poora ka poora makhan aur chhachh mein bant gaya. Ab jodo: dahi hai paani, aur usme chhupa makhan hai paani ke electron. Mathani ki rassi kheenchne wala haath hai P680. Har roshni ke baad woh ek baar kheenchta hai, aur ek electron alag karta hai. Bahar nikala gaya makhan woh electron hai, jo PS II ki khaali jagah bharne jaata hai. Aur matke mein bachi chhachh hai paani ke baaki hisse: H⁺ aur oxygen. Chhachh bhi bekaar nahi jaati, log use shauk se peete hain. Waise hi paani tootne se bani oxygen bhi bade kaam ki hai. Aur matke ka andar aur bahar kis jagah ke nishaan hain, yeh thodi der mein samjhenge.
Screen par: Rasoi mein mitti ka matka, andar dahi, beech mein lakdi ki mathani, uski rassi do haathon mein; rassi baar-baar is taraf, us taraf kheenchi jaati. Makhan ke gole upar tairte, matke mein patli chhachh. Haath makhan nikaal kar katori mein rakhta, chhachh matke mein hi. Sawaal ka dabba 'dahi gaayab hua?': taraazu jaisa chhota nishaan, dahi = makhan + chhachh. Phir jod ke label ek-ek karke: dahi = paani; makhan = electron; kheenchne wala haath = P680 (har roshni ke baad ek kheench); nikala makhan = PS II ki khaali jagah ki taraf; chhachh = H⁺ aur oxygen. Kone mein gilaas se chhachh peeta insaan. Aakhir mein matke par chhota '?' aur 'andar-bahar = ?'.
Kitaab se bahar: Dahi bilone ki misaal (matka, mathani, makhan, chhachh).
Kya kis jaisa: Dahi ka bilona: dahi = paani (H₂O); dahi mein chhupa makhan = paani ke electron; baar-baar mathani ki rassi kheenchna = har roshni ke baad P680 ka paani se ek electron kheenchna; bahar nikala makhan = PS II ki khaali jagah bharne wala electron; matke mein bachi chhachh = H⁺ aur O₂; matka = thylakoid ki thaili, uska andar = lumen, bahar = stroma (k6, k7); dahi poora makhan aur chhachh mein bant jaata hai = paani ke atom aur charge poore hisaab mein rehte hain (k5); roz naya dahi = jad se aata lagaataar paani (k9).
Kahan tootti hai: Dahi aur makhan haath ki taaqat se alag hote hain, par paani ke electron P680 ki kheench se nikalte hain, jo roshni se paida hoti hai. Makhan dahi mein pehle se chhote kanon ki tarah tairta hai, jabki electron paani ke molecule ka hissa hain aur unke nikalte hi molecule toot jaata hai. Bilona ek saath sab alag karta hai, par P680 ek baar mein ek hi electron kheenchta hai. Makhan matke se bahar le jaate hain, par electron membrane mein baithe PS II ke paas jaate hain, thaili se bahar stroma mein nahi phenke jaate. Chhachh peene ki cheez hai; H⁺ aur O₂ ka apna-apna kaam hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
example11.6.1-k5
Ab ek nahi, do paani ke molecule lekar poora hisaab karte hain. Pehle equation padhna yaad karo. Formula ke aage ka bada number molecule ginta hai, aur neeche ka chhota number molecule ke andar ke atom. Upar likha chhota plus ya minus charge batata hai. Aur teer ka matlab hai 'banta hai'. Ab yeh equation dekho: 2H₂O se 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. Baayein 2H₂O yaani paani ke do molecule hain, aur har molecule mein do hydrogen aur ek oxygen. Teer ke baad pehle 4H⁺ hai, yaani chaar hydrogen ion. Phir O₂, yaani oxygen ka ek poora molecule. Aakhir mein chaar electron hain. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai, aur upar ka minus har electron ka negative charge. Aage likha 4 unki ginti hai, isliye in chaar electron ko 4e⁻ likhte hain. Ab ginti milao: kya teer ke dono taraf atom barabar hain? Teer ke dono taraf atom barabar hain. Baayein do paani mein chaar hydrogen aur do oxygen hain. Daayein, chaar H⁺ mein chaar hydrogen hain, aur O₂ mein do oxygen. Ek bhi atom na naya bana, na gaayab hua. Ab charge bhi milakar dekho. Yaad karo, electron par negative charge hota hai, proton par positive, aur paani ke molecule par koi charge nahi. Toh kya daayein taraf ka kul charge bhi zero aata hai? Daayein taraf ka kul charge bhi zero aata hai: chaar H⁺ ke chaar plus aur chaar electron ke chaar minus aapas mein kat jaate hain. Bilone mein bhi yahi tha: jitna dahi gaya, utna hi makhan aur chhachh bana. Isliye exam ke liye yeh anupaat ginti se yaad rakho, ratt kar nahi: do paani, chaar H⁺, ek O₂, aur chaar electron. Figure 11.5 mein ek paani ka hisaab tha, 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]. Yahan do paani hain, toh sab kuch dugna ho gaya. Ab ek baareek baat samjho: P680 ek baar mein sirf ek electron kheench sakta hai. Isliye yeh chaar electron chaar baar mein nikalte hain, har baar ek nayi roshni ke baad. Chauthe electron ke baad hi O₂ ka ek molecule bahar aata hai. Pichhli film mein humne dekha tha ki ek NADPH ko do electron chahiye, aur har electron ke peeche do photon lagte hain. Toh batao, ek O₂ ke saath kitne NADPH bane, aur kitne photon lage? Ek O₂ ke saath chaar electron nikle, toh do NADPH bane. Aur kam se kam aath photon lage: chaar PS II par, aur chaar PS I par.
Screen par: Hisaab ka board. Chhote label: aage ka bada number = molecule ki ginti, neeche ka chhota number = atom, upar ka nishaan = charge, teer = banta hai. Equation ubharti: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. Do paani ke molecule ke model (har ek mein do safed hydrogen, ek laal oxygen); models toot kar chaar H⁺, ek O₂ (do laal gole jude) aur chaar electron (chhote minus wale gole) bante; label '4e⁻: 4 = ginti, e⁻ = electron, minus = charge'. Ginti ki do patti: baayein 4 H, 2 O; daayein 4 H, 2 O, sahi ka nishaan. Charge ki patti: baayein 0; daayein +4 aur −4 = 0. Kone mein bilone ka chhota taraazu. Exam card '2 paani : 4 H⁺ : 1 O₂ : 4 e⁻'. Figure 11.5 ka tukda '2e⁻ + 2H⁺ + [O]' aur uske aage '× 2'. Phir P680 par chaar roshni ek-ek karke, har baar ek electron nikalta (ginti 1, 2, 3, 4), chauthe ke baad O₂ ka bulbula. Aakhir mein: 4 electron → 2 NADPH, photon ki ginti 4 (PS II) + 4 (PS I) = 8.
Terms: 4e⁻ = Chaar electron; 4 = ginti, e⁻ = electron ka nishaan, minus = negative charge.
Kitaab se bahar: Equation padhne ke niyam (aage ka number, neeche ka number, upar ka charge, teer); atom aur charge ka barabar hisaab; anupaat 2 : 4 : 1 : 4; Figure 11.5 ka ek paani wala hisaab dugna; P680 ek baar mein ek electron, chaar roshni ke baad ek O₂; ek O₂ ke saath do NADPH aur kam se kam aath photon (exam).
Yaad: 11.6-k8, 11.6-k9, 11.6-k7
p009-i23 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻
samjhana11.6.1-k6
Ab teesra kadam: paani ka yeh tootna thaili ke kis taraf hota hai? Paani ka tootna kis taraf hota hai, yeh jaanne ke liye pehle thaili ka naksha taaza karo. Yaad karo, chloroplast ke andar chapti, band thailiyan hoti hain, jinhe thylakoid kehte hain. Har thaili ki deewar ek patli jhilli hai, yaani thylakoid membrane. Aur thailiyon ke bahar chloroplast ka gaadha dravya, stroma, bhara hai. Thaili band hai, isliye uske andar ek chhoti-si jagah chaaron taraf se ghiri rehti hai. Thaili ke andar ki is band jagah ko lumen kehte hain. Toh membrane ke do chehre hue: ek andar wala, jo lumen ki taraf hai, aur ek bahar wala, jo stroma ki taraf hai. Andar wale chehre ko membrane ki inner side kehte hain; inner yaani andar ka. Bahar wala chehra outer side kehlata hai. Abhi humne dekha ki paani PS II ke paas toot-ta hai. Paani todne wala yeh khaas hissa PS II se hi juda hai. Is hisse ko water splitting complex kehte hain. Complex ka matlab aap jaante ho: kai proteins se mil kar bani ek machine. Aur PS II khud thylakoid membrane ki inner side par baitha hai, isliye water splitting complex bhi andar ki taraf hai. Dhyan do, PS II lumen mein tairta nahi. Woh membrane mein dhansa hai, aur uska paani todne wala hissa lumen ki taraf munh kiye hai. Bilkul mathani ki tarah, jo matke ke andar ghoomti hai, bahar nahi. Toh matka hai thylakoid ki thaili, matke ke andar ki jagah lumen, aur matke ke bahar ka hissa stroma. Is complex ke beech manganese ke chaar atom hote hain, saath mein calcium aur chloride. Yahi paani ke electron sambhaal kar PS II tak pahunchate hain. Isliye exam mein poochha jaata hai ki paani ke tootne mein kaun-se tatva madad karte hain: manganese, calcium aur chloride. Oxygen yahin se nikalti hai, isliye is complex ko oxygen evolving complex bhi kehte hain; evolve yaani nikalna, jaisa humne O₂ evolution mein dekha tha.
Screen par: Sawaal ka dabba 'kis taraf?'. Chloroplast ka cutaway: gaadhe stroma mein chapti thailiyon ke dher; ek thaili paas aati hai. Thaili ki deewar (label thylakoid membrane, patli jhilli), bahar gaadha dravya (label stroma), andar ghiri jagah halki chamak mein (label lumen). Membrane ke do chehre teer se: andar ki taraf 'inner side (inner = andar)', bahar 'outer side'. Membrane mein dhansa PS II; uske lumen wale chehre par ek chhota hissa chamakta (label water splitting complex; complex = kai proteins ki machine). PS II ke lumen mein tairne wala galat chitra kaata gaya; sahi chitra: dhansa PS II, munh lumen ki taraf. Kone mein matke ke andar ghoomti mathani; labels: matka = thaili, andar = lumen, bahar = stroma. Complex ke andar chaar baingani gole (Mn), ek Ca aur kuch Cl; exam card 'manganese, calcium, chloride'. Label 'oxygen evolving complex: evolve = nikalna'.
Terms: lumen = Thylakoid ki band thaili ke andar ki ghiri hui jagah.; inner side = Thylakoid membrane ka andar wala chehra, jo lumen ki taraf hai; inner = andar ka.; water splitting complex = PS II ke saath juda paani todne wala hissa; complex = kai proteins se bani machine.
Kitaab se bahar: Lumen ka arth (aage ke headings ke liye bhi); inner aur outer side; PS II membrane mein dhansa hai, sirf uska paani todne wala hissa lumen ki taraf (book ki 'inner side' ki sahi tasveer); matka-thaili jod; complex ke manganese, calcium aur chloride (exam); oxygen evolving complex naam.
Yaad: 11.3-k5
p009-i24 We need to emphasise here that the water splitting complex is associated with the PS II, which itself is physically located on the inner side of the membrane of the thylakoid.
ruko, socho11.6.1-k7
Ab ek clue pakdo: paani isi complex par toot-ta hai, aur complex ka munh lumen ki taraf hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: paani tootne se bane H⁺ aur O₂ pehle kahan chhootenge, lumen mein, ya membrane ki outer side par, stroma ki taraf? Aapne socha hoga ki lumen mein, aur yahi theek jawab hai. H⁺ aur O₂ lumen mein isliye chhootte hain, kyunki paani wahin toot-ta hai jahan complex ka munh hai, yaani thaili ke andar. Bilone mein bhi chhachh matke ke andar hi rehti hai; bahar sirf makhan nikaala jaata hai. Electron alag raasta lete hain. Woh membrane mein baithe PS II ki khaali jagah mein jaate hain. Toh kya yeh O₂ hamesha lumen mein hi band rehti hai? O₂ lumen mein band nahi rehti. Woh bahut chhota molecule hai, aur membranes ke aar-paar fail kar patti se bahar nikal jaati hai. Matlab O₂ ke banne ki jagah lumen hai, par uski manzil bahar ki hawa hai. Par H⁺ itni aasaani se membrane paar nahi kar paate. Isliye woh lumen mein hi jama hote jaate hain. Lumen mein jama hote in H⁺ ka kya hota hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Thaili ka cutaway; complex ka munh lumen ki taraf, uspar chhota clue ka teer. Sawaal 'Ruko, pehle khud socho: H⁺ aur O₂ kahan? lumen ya outer side?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir complex par paani toot-ta: chaar H⁺ aur O₂ andar lumen mein girte, electron PS II ki khaali jagah mein jaate; kone mein matke mein chhachh, bahar katori mein makhan. Sawaal 'O₂ band rahegi?': O₂ ke bulbule membranes paar karke patti se hawa mein; label 'bani lumen mein, manzil hawa'. H⁺ lumen mein jama hote, unki ginti badhti; uspar '?' aur 'aage'.
Kitaab se bahar: Book ke sawaal ka jawab (book khud jawab nahi deti): H⁺ aur O₂ pehle lumen mein; O₂ ke banne ki jagah aur manzil alag; H⁺ membrane aasaani se paar nahi karte, lumen mein jama hote hain (unka kya hota hai, yeh aage 11.6.3 mein); ruko, pehle khud socho isi mod par.
Jawab: Water splitting complex lumen ki taraf khula hai, aur paani wahin toot-ta hai; isliye H⁺ aur O₂ pehle lumen mein chhootte hain. Electron PS II ki khaali jagah mein jaate hain. O₂ chhota molecule hai, baad mein membranes paar karke patti se bahar nikal jaati hai; H⁺ membrane aasaani se paar nahi karte, isliye lumen mein jama hote hain.
Aam galti: Galat soch 1: H⁺ aur O₂ stroma mein chhootte hain; galat, stroma thaili ke bahar hai aur complex ka munh andar hai. Galat soch 2: paani ke electron seedhe PS I ko jaate hain; galat, woh PS II ko jaate hain. Galat soch 3: O₂ hamesha lumen mein band rehti hai; galat, banne ki jagah aur aakhri manzil alag hain. Galat soch 4: poora PS II lumen mein tairta hai; galat, woh membrane mein dhansa hai.
p009-i25 Then, where are the protons and O₂ formed likely to be released – in the lumen? or on the outer side of the membrane?
asli duniya11.6.1-k8
Ab apni saans par ek pal dhyan do. Aap jo oxygen saans mein lete ho, uski lagbhag saari kabhi na kabhi kisi patti ya alga mein paani tootne se bani hai. Pehle humne dekha tha ki 1941 ke oxygen-18 wale prayog ne saabit kiya: nikalne wali O₂ paani se aati hai, CO₂ se nahi. Aaj humne dekha ki yeh hota kahan aur kaise hai: PS II ke paas, thaili ke andar, paani toot kar. Matlab jad jo paani mitti se kheench kar upar bhejti hai, wahi paani patti mein toot kar aapki saans ki oxygen banta hai. Agli baar aap kisi paudhe ko paani do, toh yaad rakhna ki usi paani se hawa ki oxygen bhi banegi.
Screen par: Ek bachcha khule mein gehri saans leta. Hawa ki oxygen ke kan peeche ki taraf chalte: patti ke chhote chhedon se cell, chloroplast, thaili ke andar, PS II ke paas paani ka tootna. Kone mein 1941 ka oxygen-18 wala nishaan laga paani aur usi nishaan wali O₂. Phir paudhe ki jad mitti se paani kheenchti, paani tane se patti tak, patti mein toot kar O₂ bahar, bachche ki saans tak. Aakhir mein ek bachcha gamle mein paudhe ko paani deta.
Kitaab se bahar: Hawa ki lagbhag saari oxygen photosynthesis mein paani ke tootne se bani hai; jad ke paani se saans ki oxygen tak ka raasta.
Yaad: 11.2-k12

poori kahani dobara11.6.1-k9
Chalo, aaj ki poori baat ek baar shuru se jodte hain. Roshni P680 ka electron uchhaal kar aage bhejti hai, aur PS II mein ek khaali jagah reh jaati hai. Electron kho kar P680 sabse taaqatwar kheenchne wala ban jaata hai, aur paani se ek electron kheench leta hai. Paani ke is tootne ko splitting of water kehte hain, aur yeh PS II se juda hai. Do paani ke molecule tootte hain, toh chaar H⁺, ek O₂ aur chaar electron milte hain; atom aur charge dono barabar rehte hain. Yeh kaam water splitting complex karta hai, jo PS II ke saath membrane ki inner side par, lumen ki taraf hai. Isliye H⁺ aur O₂ lumen mein chhootte hain. O₂ baad mein patti se bahar nikal jaati hai, aur H⁺ lumen mein jama hote hain. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: PS II ki khaali jagah har baar kaun bharta hai, aur paani apne electron chhodta kaise hai? PS II ki khaali jagah har baar paani ka electron bharta hai. Paani khud nahi chhodta; roshni se khaali hua P680 use kheench leta hai, aur paani toot jaata hai. Aur paudhe ko jad se paani lagaataar milta rehta hai, isliye yeh bharpaai bhi din bhar lagaataar chalti hai. Bilkul bilone ki tarah: matke mein dahi aata raha, toh makhan bhi nikalta raha, aur chhachh matke mein bachti rahi. Ab poora raasta ek line mein dekho: paani se PS II, phir kadi, phir PS I, aur aakhir NADP⁺. Ab tak electron ek hi disha mein chala hai. Par kya electron hamesha isi seedhe raaste par chalta hai? Agar PS I ke baad NADP⁺ tak pahunchne ka raasta hi na mile, toh electron ka kya hoga, aur kuch banega bhi ya nahi? Raasta na mile toh electron kahan jaata hai aur kya banta hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Aaj ki kahani ek patti par chalti: P680 par roshni, electron aage, khaali ghera; P680 paani se electron kheenchta; paani toot-ta (label splitting of water); do paani se chaar H⁺, ek O₂, chaar e⁻ aur barabar ginti ki patti; thaili mein inner side par complex, H⁺ aur O₂ lumen mein, O₂ bahar ki taraf, H⁺ jama. Shuru ka sawaal laut kar: 'khaali jagah kaun bharta? paani kaise chhodta?'; jawab ke teer: paani → PS II, P680 kheenchta. Jad se paani lagaataar upar aata; kone mein bilone ka matka, makhan nikalta rehta. Phir poora raasta ek line: paani → PS II → kadi → PS I → NADP⁺, ek hi disha ka teer. Aakhir mein PS I ke aage NADP⁺ wala raasta band (rok ka nishaan), electron par bada '?', jo khula rehta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Opening question again with its answer; jad se lagaataar paani = lagaataar bharpaai; paani se NADP⁺ tak ek hi disha (agli film ki yaad ke liye); agli film ka sawaal khula chhoda; non-cyclic raaste ki shuruaat paani se aur O₂ tabhi jab PS II paani tode (exercise 9b ke liye).
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.6.2 Cyclic and Non-cyclic Photo-phosphorylation · 7 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 14 min
Maqsad: Agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega, aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?
Misaal: relay daud: electron baton, carriers khiladi; non-cyclic mein baton NADP⁺ ke haath tak, cyclic mein track ka ek chakkar
Kitab ki baatein (45): kitab kehti hai 14 · shabd ka matlab 13 · pehle ki zaroori baat 7 · figure ka nishaan 7 · teacher ki extra baat 4

recap11.6.2-k1
Pichhli film mein humne dekha ki PS II ka khoya hua electron paani se aata hai. Do paani ke molecule tootte hain, aur chaar electron, chaar H⁺ aur ek O₂ milte hain. Usse pehle Z scheme mein electron ka poora safar dekha tha: paani se PS II, phir carriers ki kadi, phir PS I, aur aakhir mein NADP⁺. Yaad rakhein: is safar mein electron ek hi disha mein chalta hai, paani se shuru hokar NADP⁺ par khatam. Aage hum dekhenge ki yeh safar hamesha aise hi chalta hai ya nahi. Ab socho: agar PS I ke aage NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta hi band ho, toh roshni se uchhla electron kahan jaayega? Aur kya tab bhi kuch banega?
Screen par: Z scheme ka raasta ek chalti line ki tarah: paani, PS II, carriers ki kadi, PS I, NADP⁺; paani ke do molecule toot kar 4 e⁻, 4 H⁺ aur ek O₂; yaad rakhein: electron ek hi disha mein, paani se NADP⁺ tak (ek teer); aakhri line par PS I ke aage NADP⁺ wala raasta band.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.2-k2
Is sawaal ka jawab isi film mein milega. Par usse pehle ek zaroori cheez pakki kar lo: ATP. Har jeev ko kaam ke liye energy chahiye: saans lene ke liye, chalne ke liye, ek naya molecule banane ke liye. Yeh energy jeev kuch cheezon se nikaalte hain, jaise khaane ka glucose. Yaad karo, oxidation ka matlab hai electron dena, aur reduction ka matlab hai electron lena. Jo cheezein oxidation mein electron de sakti hain, unhe oxidisable substances kehte hain. Glucose aisi hi ek cheez hai. Glucose se energy kaise nikalti hai, yeh aap aage respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki aisi cheezon se energy nikalti hai. Nikli hui energy jeev turant kharch nahi karte. Use molecules ke andar band karke rakh lete hain. Band karke isliye rakhte hain kyunki energy ki zaroorat har jagah aur har pal hoti hai, aur band energy ko jahan chaaho wahan le ja sakte ho. Socho ek gulel, jiski rubber ko kheench kar pakad liya. Energy us khinchi hui rubber mein band hai; chhodoge toh kaam karegi. Molecule mein atom aapas mein bandhanon se jude hote hain, jinhe chemical bonds kehte hain. In bonds mein bhi aise hi energy band rehti hai; ise bond energy kehte hain. ATP ek aisa hi khaas molecule hai. Yeh apne chemical bonds mein energy lekar chalta hai, aur cell ke har kaam tak pahunchata hai. ATP ka poora naam hai adenosine triphosphate. Tri yaani teen, toh isme teen phosphate jude hain. ADP yaani adenosine diphosphate. Di yaani do, toh isme do hi phosphate hain. ATP ko ek bhari hui battery samjho, aur ADP ko ek khaali hoti battery. Khaali battery ko phir se bharna hai, toh ADP mein ek phosphate aur jodna padta hai. Aur is jodne mein energy lagti hai. Jo phosphate akela hai, kisi molecule se juda nahi, use inorganic phosphate kehte hain. Chhote mein ise Pᵢ likhte hain; i yaani inorganic. ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banane ko phosphorylation kehte hain. Phosphate jodna, isliye naam phosphorylation. Cell yeh kaam do jagah karta hai: mitochondria mein aur chloroplast mein. Mitochondria wala tareeka bhi aap respiration wale chapter mein padhoge. Hamari kahani chloroplast ki hai. Chloroplast mein yeh charging roshni se hoti hai, bilkul waise jaise solar charger dhoop se phone charge karta hai. Roshni ki madad se ADP aur inorganic phosphate se ATP banane ko photo-phosphorylation kehte hain, kyunki photo ka matlab hi roshni hai.
Screen par: Energy ki zaroorat ke chhote drishya (saans, chalna, naya molecule); glucose se energy; oxidation = electron dena, reduction = electron lena; gulel ki khinchi rubber (apni tasveer); atomon ke beech chemical bond mein band energy; ATP: adenosine ke saath teen phosphate, ADP: do phosphate; bhari aur khaali battery (apni tasveer); ADP + Pᵢ → ATP, jodne mein energy; mitochondria aur chloroplast; dhoop se phone bharta solar charger (apni tasveer); roshni ke saath ADP + Pᵢ → ATP.
Terms: oxidisable substances = jo oxidation mein electron de sakein; bond energy = chemical bonds mein band energy; inorganic phosphate = akela phosphate, Pᵢ; phosphorylation = ADP mein phosphate jodkar ATP banana; Photo-phosphorylation = roshni ki madad se phosphorylation
p009-i27 Living organisms have the capability of extracting energy from oxidisable substances and store this in the form of bond energy. Special substances like ATP, carry this energy in their chemical bonds. The process through which
p010-i1 ATP is synthesised by cells (in mitochondria and chloroplasts) is named phosphorylation. Photo-phosphorylation is the synthesis of ATP from ADP and inorganic phosphate in the presence of light.
samjhana11.6.2-k3
Ab Figure 11.5 wali Z scheme yaad karo, jo hum pehle padh chuke hain. Usme do photosystem the: pehle PS II, phir PS I. Dono par roshni padti thi. Dono ke beech carriers ki ek kadi thi, jo electron ko ek se doosre tak aage deti hai. Is kadi ko electron transport chain kehte hain: electron ka transport, yaani le jaana, ek chain mein. Ise ek relay daud ki tarah socho. Har khiladi baton ko agle khiladi ke haath mein deta hai, aur baton aage badhta jaata hai. Yahan baton hai electron, aur khiladi hain carriers. Roshni do jagah zor ka dhakka deti hai: PS II par aur PS I par. Is daud mein baton paani se chalta hai. PS II aur PS I se hokar, aakhir mein NADP⁺ ke haath mein pahunchta hai. NADP⁺ electron le leta hai aur NADPH ban jaata hai. Baton wahin ruk gaya; woh wapas shuru par nahi aata. Toh is raaste mein do cheezein banti hain: ATP aur NADPH. Saath mein paani tootne se O₂ bhi nikalti hai. Aksar ise NADPH + H⁺ likhte hain. Iska matlab bas itna hai ki NADPH ke saath ek H⁺ bhi likha jaata hai. Dhyan do, electron ek seedhe raaste par aage gaya, aur jahan se chala tha wahan wapas nahi lauta. Raasta gol nahi hai. Isliye ise non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation kehte hain. Cyclic yaani gol chakkar wala, aur non-cyclic yaani jo gol chakkar na ho. Yaad rakho: non-cyclic mein dono photosystem ek ke baad ek kaam karte hain, pehle PS II, phir PS I. Aur ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai. Yahan exam ki ek baareek baat hai: naam PS I hai, par kaam mein woh doosre number par aata hai. Number kaam ke kram se nahi pade; PS I pehle khoja gaya tha, isliye uska naam I pada.
Screen par: Z scheme: PS II aur PS I, dono par roshni, beech mein carriers ki kadi; relay daud (apni tasveer): baton electron, khiladi carriers; electron paani se NADP⁺ tak pahunch kar NADPH; ATP, NADPH aur O₂; seedha raasta jo shuru par wapas nahi aata; kaam ka kram PS II pehle, PS I baad mein, aur naam ka kram khoj se (PS I pehle khoja gaya).
Terms: electron transport chain = carriers ki kadi jo electron aage deti hai; non-cyclic = jo gol chakkar na ho
Kitaab se bahar: PS I aur PS II ke naam khoj ke kram se pade, yeh kitaab mein nahi; exam ki baareek baat ke roop mein joda.
Kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki PS I pehle kaam karta hai kyunki uska number pehla hai.
p010-i2 When the two photosystems work in a series, first PS II and then the PS I, a process called non-cyclic photo-phosphorylation occurs. The two photosystems are connected through an electron transport chain, as seen earlier – in the Z scheme. Both ATP and NADPH + H⁺ are synthesised by this kind of electron flow (Figure 11.5).
figure11.6.2-k4
Ab Figure 11.6 dekho. Yahan ek bilkul alag raasta hai. Sabse upar bracket mein likha hai Photosystem I. Matlab is figure mein sirf PS I ka kaam dikhaya gaya hai; ise kahin-kahin PSI bhi likha milta hai. Neeche likha hai Chlorophyll P 700. Yeh PS I ka reaction centre hai, jo lagbhag 700 nm ki roshni pakadta hai. Peeli dashed teer par Light likha hai. Roshni seedhe P 700 par padti hai. Roshni padte hi P 700 ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai. Ungli rakho: gulabi teer seedhe upar e⁻ acceptor tak jaata hai. e⁻ electron ka nishaan hai. Aur e⁻ acceptor woh molecule hai jo uchhla hua electron sabse pehle pakadta hai. Ab acceptor se ek mudi hui gulabi teer neeche Electron transport system tak aati hai. Yeh carriers ki wahi kadi hai. Ab ungli aage le chalo. Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer kahan jaati hai? Is kadi ke baad gulabi teer wapas P 700 par hi pahunchti hai, wahin jahan se electron chala tha. Electron ne ek gol chakkar poora kiya: P 700 se acceptor, phir kadi, phir wapas P 700. Isi gol chakkar ki wajah se ise cyclic kehte hain. Relay wali daud yaad hai? Yahan baton kisi ko diya hi nahi gaya. Khiladi track ka ek chakkar lagakar wahin laut aaya jahan se chala tha. Ab kadi ke paas ek kaali mudi teer dekho: ADP + Pi se ATP. Yaani is chakkar ke saath ATP banta hai. Isliye ise cyclic photophosphorylation kehte hain: roshni se, ek gol chakkar ke saath, ATP banana. Aur yeh bhi dekho ki figure mein kya nahi hai: na PS II hai, na NADP⁺ tak jaane wala raasta.
Screen par: Cyclic photophosphorylation ka figure jaisa drishya, har label tabhi jab line uska naam le: Photosystem I bracket, Chlorophyll P 700, Light ki peeli dashed teer, upar e⁻ acceptor tak gulabi teer, Electron transport system, wapas P 700 tak teer, ADP + Pi → ATP; relay mein khiladi track ka chakkar lagakar lautta (apni tasveer); aakhir mein dikhe ki PS II aur NADP⁺ ka raasta nahi hai.
Terms: cyclic = gol chakkar wala
p010-i3 Cyclic photophosphorylation: a dashed yellow arrow carries Light to Chlorophyll P 700. A straight pink arrow rises from P 700 to the e⁻ acceptor. A curved pink arrow leads from the acceptor down to the Electron transport system, and another pink arrow returns from that system to P 700. Beside the transport system, a curved black arrow indicates ADP + Pi → ATP. The Photosystem I bracket identifies the PSI pathway. No PS II or NADP⁺-reduction branch is shown.
samjhana11.6.2-k5
Toh shuruaat ka sawaal: jab NADP⁺ tak jaane ka raasta na ho, toh electron kahan jaata hai? Jab NADP⁺ tak raasta na ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se hokar wapas PS I par laut aata hai. Aisa tab hota hai jab sirf PS I kaam kar raha ho. Electron PS I ke andar hi chakkar lagata rehta hai. Is gol bahav ke saath jo phosphorylation hoti hai, wahi cyclic photo-phosphorylation hai. Par chloroplast mein aisa kahan ho sakta hai, yeh samajhne ke liye pehle uske andar ka naksha yaad karo. Chloroplast mein thylakoid ki chapti thailiyan sikkon ki tarah ek ke upar ek rakhi hoti hain. Inke dher grana kehlate hain. Do dheron ko jodne wali patli, bina dher wali membrane bhi hoti hai, jaise do imaraton ke beech ka galiyara. Inhe hum pehle stroma lamellae ke naam se dekh chuke hain; lamella yaani patli parat. Grana ki membrane mein dono photosystem hote hain, PS I bhi aur PS II bhi. Par stroma lamellae ki membrane mein PS II nahi hota. Aur NADP reductase enzyme bhi nahi hota. NADP reductase woh enzyme hai jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banne ka kaam karata hai. Reductase yaani reduction karaane wala. Toh socho, stroma lamellae mein uchhle electron ko NADP⁺ tak pahunchane wala enzyme hi nahi hai. Isliye electron ke paas wapas lautne ke siva koi raasta nahi. Isliye stroma lamellae ko cyclic photo-phosphorylation ki ek sambhav jagah maana jaata hai. Nateeja pakka yaad rakho: gol raaste mein sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH + H⁺ nahi banta. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge. Par aisa nahi hai: gol raaste mein electron NADP⁺ tak jaata hi nahi, aur PS II ke bina paani bhi nahi tootta, isliye sirf ATP banta hai. Ek aur haalat hai. Yaad karo, roshni lehron mein chalti hai, aur ek lehar ki chot se agli chot tak ki doori wavelength hai. PS II ka centre P680 hai, jo lagbhag 680 nm ki roshni chahta hai. PS I ka P700, 700 nm tak ki roshni bhi pakad leta hai. Jab sirf 680 nm se aage ki roshni mile, toh PS II theek se nahi jaagta. Sirf PS I chalta hai, aur cyclic photo-phosphorylation hoti hai. Aksar log ise ulta samajh lete hain, par 680 nm se aage ka matlab hai 680 se zyada, yaani lambi wavelength, kam wavelength nahi. Paudhe ko yeh gol raasta kyon chahiye, iska ek bada kaaran hum aage Calvin cycle mein dekhenge. Abhi bas itna jaan lo ki kabhi paudhe ko NADPH se zyada ATP chahiye hota hai.
Screen par: Electron wapas PS I par; chloroplast ka naksha: grana sikkon ke dher jaise, unhe jodti stroma lamellae galiyare jaisi; grana ki membrane mein PS I aur PS II, stroma lamellae mein sirf PS I (PS II aur NADP reductase nahi); lautta electron; sirf ATP; 680 nm aur 700 nm ki lehrein, 680 se lambi lehar.
Terms: NADP reductase = reduction karaane wala enzyme jo NADP⁺ se NADPH banwata hai
p010-i12 When only PS I is functional, the electron is circulated within the photosystem and the phosphorylation occurs due to cyclic flow of electrons (Figure 11.6). A possible location where this could be happening is in the stroma lamellae. While the membrane or lamellae of the grana have both PS I and PS II the stroma lamellae membranes lack PS II as well as NADP reductase enzyme. The excited electron does not pass on to NADP⁺ but is cycled back to the PS I complex through the electron transport chain (Figure 11.6). The cyclic flow hence, results only in the synthesis of ATP, but not of NADPH + H⁺. Cyclic photophosphorylation also occurs when only light of wavelengths beyond 680 nm are available for excitation.
example11.6.2-k6
Ab ek electron ko pakad kar stroma lamellae mein uska poora safar chalate hain. Roshni P 700 par padi, aur uska ek electron energy lekar uchhal gaya. e⁻ acceptor ne use pakda, aur woh carriers ki kadi mein aage badha. Kadi ke aage use NADP⁺ tak le jaane wala NADP reductase mila hi nahi, kyunki yahan woh enzyme hai hi nahi. Toh electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa gaya, aur agli roshni ka intezaar karne laga. Is ek chakkar ke saath ATP bana. NADPH ek bhi nahi bana, aur O₂ bhi nahi nikli.
Screen par: Stroma lamellae mein ek electron ka safar, kadam-kadam ek hi tasveer par: P 700 par roshni, electron uchhla, e⁻ acceptor, kadi, NADP reductase ki khaali jagah, wapas P 700; ATP bana, NADPH aur O₂ nahi.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
ruko, socho11.6.2-k7
Ruko, pehle khud socho: agar kisi patti par sirf 700 nm wali roshni pade, toh usme NADPH banega ya nahi? Aapne socha hoga ki NADPH nahi banega, aur yahi sahi hai. Aisi roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, aur sirf ATP banega. Ab dono raaste ek saath rakh kar dekho. Seedhe raaste, yaani non-cyclic, mein electron paani se chalta hai: pehle PS II, phir PS I, aur aakhir NADP⁺. ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno bante hain. Gol raaste, yaani cyclic, mein sirf PS I kaam karta hai, aur electron wapas P 700 par aata hai. Sirf ATP banta hai; na NADPH, na O₂. Ab shuruaat ka sawaal phir se: NADP⁺ ka raasta band ho, toh electron kahan jaayega, aur kya kuch banega? Raasta band ho, toh electron carriers ki kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700 par aa jaata hai. Aur tab bhi kuch banta hai: ATP, bas NADPH nahi. Ab ek ajeeb baat par gaur karo, jo hamari agli film ka sawaal hai: dono raaston mein ATP bana, par electron ne kahin bhi ADP ko chhua tak nahi. Toh ATP bana kaise? ATP bana kaise, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Sawaal screen par; dono raaste saath-saath: non-cyclic (paani, PS II, PS I, NADP⁺; ATP, NADPH, O₂) aur cyclic (PS I se wapas P 700; sirf ATP); shuruaat ka sawaal phir screen par, NADP⁺ ka band raasta; electron kadi se ghoom kar wapas P 700, ATP bana, NADPH nahi; aakhir mein electron aur ADP ke beech khaali jagah, sawaal khula.
Jawab: Sirf 700 nm ki roshni mein sirf PS I chalega, electron gol chakkar lagayega, sirf ATP banega, NADPH nahi.
Aam galti: Yeh sochna ki light reaction mein hamesha ATP, NADPH aur O₂ teeno banenge.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.6.3 Chemiosmotic Hypothesis · 19 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 32 min
Maqsad: Jab koi bhi electron ADP tak pahuncha hi nahi, toh ADP ko teesra phosphate jodne ki taakat mili kahan se?
Misaal: baandh: deewaar = thylakoid membrane; deewaar ke peeche ooncha ruka paani = lumen mein jama H⁺ (high); neeche ki taraf kam paani = stroma (low H⁺); upar-neeche paani ka fark = proton gradient, yaani dhalaan; paani upar chadhane wala pump = electron ka safar (paani ka tootna, PQ ki naav, FNR) jo H⁺ andar bharta aur stroma se nikaalta hai; deewaar ka ek hi gate = CF₀ ka transmembrane channel; girte paani se ghoomti turbine = CF₁ ki badalti shakal; bijli = ATP; deewaar mein daraar = chhedon wali membrane (checkpoint)
Kitab ki baatein (96): kitab kehti hai 25 · shabd ka matlab 23 · pehle ki zaroori baat 15 · figure ka nishaan 21 · teacher ki extra baat 12
f1 kitab kehti hai: chemiosmotic hypothesis Chloroplast mein ATP asal mein kaise banta hai, yeh samjhaane ke liye chemiosmotic hypothesis rakhi gayi hai.
f2 kitab kehti hai Respiration ki tarah photosynthesis mein bhi ATP ka banna ek membrane ke aar-paar proton gradient banne se juda hai.
f3 kitab kehti hai Photosynthesis mein yeh membrane thylakoid ki membrane hai.
f4 kitab kehti hai Ek fark: chloroplast mein protons membrane ke andar ki taraf, yaani lumen mein, jama hote hain.
f5 kitab kehti hai Respiration mein electron ETS se guzarte hain, aur protons mitochondria ke intermembrane space mein jama hote hain (Chapter 12).
f6 shabd ka matlab: chemiosmotic hypothesis Mitchell ka samjhaane wala vichaar ki ATP ka banna membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ke fark aur unke bahav se juda hai.
f7 shabd ka matlab: chemiosmotic Chemi = chemical kaam (yahan ATP banana); osmotic = membrane ke aar-paar jaana (yahan H⁺ ka).
f8 shabd ka matlab: proton gradient Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki maatra ka fark: protons ki dhalaan.
f9 shabd ka matlab: gradient Dhalaan: ek taraf zyada, doosri taraf kam; cheez apne aap zyada se kam ki taraf jaati hai.
f10 shabd ka matlab: intermembrane space Mitochondria ki bahar aur andar wali membrane ke beech ki patli jagah; inter = beech, space = jagah.
f11 pehle ki zaroori baat Thylakoid ek band thaili hai: uski deewar thylakoid membrane, andar ki jagah lumen, aur bahar chloroplast ka dravya stroma (11.3, 11.6.1).
f12 pehle ki zaroori baat Mitochondria ki do membrane hoti hain, ek bahar wali aur ek andar wali (cell ka chapter).
f13 pehle ki zaroori baat ATP aur ADP: ADP mein ek phosphate (Pᵢ) jodne se ATP banta hai, aur is jodne mein energy lagti hai; ATP energy lekar chalne wala molecule hai (11.6.2).
f14 pehle ki zaroori baat H⁺: hydrogen atom se electron nikal jaaye toh bacha proton; isliye H⁺ ko hydrogen ions ya proton bhi kehte hain (11.6).
f15 teacher ki extra baat 1961 se pehle vaigyanik electron se ADP tak energy le jaane wala ek beech ka molecule dhoondhte rahe, jo kabhi nahi mila; 1961 mein Peter Mitchell ne chemiosmotic vichaar diya, aur 1978 mein unhe chemistry ka Nobel Prize mila.
f16 pehle ki zaroori baat Pichhli film (11.6.2): seedhe (non-cyclic) raaste mein ATP aur NADPH, gol (cyclic) raaste mein sirf ATP; dono mein electron ne ADP ko seedhe nahi chhua.
f17 kitab kehti hai Proton gradient kaise banta hai, yeh jaanne ke liye electron ke activation aur unke transport ke kadam dobara dekhne hain (Figure 11.7).
f18 shabd ka matlab: activation of electrons Roshni ki energy se electron ka jaagna aur uchhalna (excite hona); activation = jagaana.
f19 pehle ki zaroori baat Roshni se reaction centre ka electron excite hokar primary acceptor tak jaata hai, phir carriers ki kadi se aage badhta hai; yahi electron transport hai (11.5, 11.6).
f20 kitab kehti hai Paani ka tootna membrane ki inner side par hota hai, isliye paani se bane protons ya hydrogen ions thylakoid ke lumen mein jama hote hain.
f21 shabd ka matlab: protons or hydrogen ions Dono naam H⁺ ke hain; H⁺ asal mein ek proton hi hai.
f22 pehle ki zaroori baat Water splitting: paani PS II ke paas toot-ta hai, 2H₂O se 4H⁺, O₂ aur 4e⁻; tootne wala hissa inner side par, lumen ki taraf (11.6.1).
f23 teacher ki extra baat Membrane ki beech ki parat tel jaisi hai; charge wale H⁺ use apne aap paar nahi kar paate, isliye lumen mein ruk kar jama hote hain; paani ke H⁺ lumen ke andar hi paida hote hain, bahar se nahi aate.

f48 kitab kehti hai Electron jab photosystems se guzarte hain, tab protons membrane ke aar-paar le jaaye jaate hain.

f60 kitab kehti hai Isliye chloroplast mein stroma ke protons ghatte hain aur lumen mein protons jama hote hain.

f71 shabd ka matlab: breakdown of this gradient Dhalaan ka ghatte-ghatte mitna; H⁺ nasht nahi hote, dono taraf ka fark kam hota hai.
f72 shabd ka matlab: transmembrane Membrane ke aar-paar faila; trans = aar-paar.
f73 pehle ki zaroori baat Electrochemical gradient aur ATP synthase: H⁺ ki dhalaan aur ATP banane wala enzyme, jo ADP aur inorganic phosphate jodta hai.
f74 kitab kehti hai ATP synthase ke do hisse hain: CF₀ thylakoid membrane mein dhansa hai aur ek transmembrane channel banata hai, jisse protons ka facilitated diffusion hota hai.
f75 kitab kehti hai Doosra hissa CF₁ thylakoid membrane ki bahar ki satah par, stroma ki taraf, nikla hua hai.

f77 shabd ka matlab: CF₁ ATP synthase ka stroma ki taraf nikla sir, jahan ATP banta hai.
f78 shabd ka matlab: facilitated diffusion Protein ke diye raaste se zyada se kam ki taraf apne aap failna, bina energy; facilitated = madad diya hua.
f79 shabd ka matlab: ATP synthase ATP banane wala enzyme; synthase = banane wala; do hisse CF₀ aur CF₁.
f80 pehle ki zaroori baat Membrane mein proteins dhanse hote hain, jo raasta ya machine ka kaam karte hain (cell ka chapter).
f81 pehle ki zaroori baat Pumping (dhalaan ke ulte, active) mein energy lagti hai; diffusion (dhalaan ke saath) apne aap hota hai.

f84 teacher ki extra baat CF₀ ka ek hissa H⁺ ke guzarne par turbine ki tarah ghoomta hai, aur yahi ghoomna CF₁ ki shakal badalta hai.
f85 teacher ki extra baat Energy ka safar: roshni se electron mein, electron se H⁺ ki dhalaan mein, aur dhalaan se ATP mein.
f86 kitab kehti hai Chemiosmosis ke liye membrane, proton pump, proton gradient aur ATP synthase chahiye.
f87 kitab kehti hai Energy lagakar protons membrane ke paar pump kiye jaate hain, taaki thylakoid lumen mein protons ki zyada maatra, yaani gradient, bane.
f88 kitab kehti hai ATP synthase ke channel se protons wapas diffuse hote hain; isse nikli energy ATP synthase enzyme ko chalaati hai, jo ATP banata hai.
f89 shabd ka matlab: chemiosmosis Membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ki dhalaan bana kar aur unhe ATP synthase se wapas baha kar ATP banana.
f90 shabd ka matlab: proton pump Energy laga kar H⁺ ko dhalaan ke ulte bharne wala; yahan electron ka safar aur PQ.
f91 teacher ki extra baat Jagendorf ka prayog (1966): andhere mein thylakoid ko pH 4 ke ghol mein rakha, phir pH 8 ke ghol mein ADP aur Pᵢ ke saath daala; bina roshni aur bina electron ke ATP bana.
f92 teacher ki extra baat Membrane mein chhed hon toh H⁺ CF₀ ke bina laut jaate hain, dhalaan nahi banti, ATP nahi banta, energy garmi ban jaati hai; kuch rasayan membrane ko aisa bana dete hain.
f93 kitab kehti hai Electron ke chalne se bane NADPH ke saath yeh ATP turant stroma ki biosynthetic reaction mein kaam aata hai, jo CO₂ ko fix karti hai aur sugar banati hai.
f94 shabd ka matlab: fixing CO₂ CO₂ ke carbon ko pakad kar kisi molecule ka pakka hissa bana dena.
f95 shabd ka matlab: biosynthetic Jeev ke andar naye molecule banane wala; bio = jeevan, synthesis = banana.

recap11.6.3-k1
Pichhli film mein humne roshni se ATP banne ke do raaste dekhe the. Seedhe raaste mein electron paani se chal kar NADP⁺ tak jaata hai. Is raaste mein ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai. Gol raaste mein electron ghoom kar wapas P 700 par laut aata hai, aur sirf ATP banta hai. Yaad rakhein: dono raaston mein ATP bana, lekin electron ne ADP ko kabhi seedhe haath tak nahi lagaya. Aage hum dekhenge ki ATP asal mein kis jagah, aur kis tareeke se banta hai. Toh socho: jab koi bhi electron ADP tak pahuncha hi nahi, toh ADP ko teesra phosphate jodne ki taakat mili kahan se?
Screen par: Pichhli film ke dono raaste saath-saath: seedha raasta (paani, PS II, kadi, PS I, NADP⁺; ATP aur NADPH) aur gol raasta (PS I se wapas P 700; sirf ATP); dabba 'Yaad rakhein: electron ne ADP ko seedhe nahi chhua'; ADP ke molecule par teesre phosphate ki khaali jagah, uspar sawaal 'taakat kahan se?'.
Kitaab se bahar: Opening recap of 11.6.2 aur uska khula sawaal (ATP bana kaise), aaj ke sawaal ke roop mein: ADP ko teesra phosphate jodne ki energy kahan se aayi.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k7, 11.6.2-k3
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
kahani11.6.3-k2
Is sawaal ka jawab dhoondhne se pehle hamein ek purani galatfahmi todni padegi. Aksar hum sochte hain ki electron jab kadi par neeche utarta hai, toh uski energy seedhe ADP mein chali jaati hai. Par aisa nahi hai. Socho, ek zamaane mein bade-bade vaigyanik bhi yahi maante the. Woh saalon tak aise molecule ko dhoondhte rahe, jo electron se energy lekar seedhe ADP ko de de. Bahut dhoondha gaya, par aisa koi molecule kabhi mila hi nahi. Phir 1961 mein Peter Mitchell naam ke ek vaigyanik ne bilkul nayi baat kahi. Unhone kaha ki beech mein aisa koi molecule hai hi nahi. ATP ka banna ek membrane se juda hai. Pehle bahut logon ne yeh baat nahi maani. Par baad ke prayogon ne Mitchell ko sahi saabit kiya. Isi kaam ke liye 1978 mein unhe chemistry ka Nobel Prize mila. Mitchell ke is samjhaane wale vichaar ko chemiosmotic hypothesis kehte hain. Hypothesis ka matlab aap pehle dekh chuke ho: ek samjhaane wala vichaar, jise baad mein prayog parakhte hain. Ab chemiosmotic shabd ko do hisson mein todo. Chemi yaani chemical kaam; yahan woh kaam hai ATP ka banna. Osmotic yaani kisi cheez ka membrane ke aar-paar jaana; yahan H⁺ ka aar-paar jaana. Dekho, naam hi bata deta hai ki membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ke jaane se ATP banta hai. H⁺ ke jaane se ATP kaise banta hai, yeh samajhne ke liye chalo, pehle H⁺ ko phir se pehchano.
Screen par: Kadi par utarta electron aur ADP ke beech seedha teer, jis par kaata nishaan; purani prayogshala mein vaigyanik ek 'beech wale molecule' ko dhoondhte, khaali haath; 1961: Peter Mitchell aur unka vichaar, membrane ka ek tukda; 1978 ka Nobel Prize; label 'chemiosmotic hypothesis' do tukdon mein: chemi = chemical kaam (ATP banana), osmotic = membrane ke aar-paar jaana (H⁺ ka).
Terms: chemiosmotic hypothesis = Mitchell ka vichaar ki ATP ka banna membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ke jaane se juda hai; chemi = chemical kaam, osmotic = membrane ke aar-paar jaana.
Kitaab se bahar: Galat soch (electron ki energy seedhe ADP mein) aaj ke sawaal ka darwaza; Mitchell ki kahani: beech wale molecule ki lambi khoj, 1961 ka vichaar, 1978 ka Nobel Prize (kitaab mein nahi); hypothesis 11.2 mein khula, yahan sirf yaad; chemi aur osmotic ke hisse.
Kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki electron ki energy seedhe ADP ko milti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.3-k3
Pichhli filmon mein humne dekha tha ki hydrogen ke atom se uska electron nikal jaaye, toh sirf ek proton bachta hai. Usi akele proton ko H⁺ likhte hain, aur uske upar ka plus uska positive charge batata hai. Ab ek purani baat yaad karo. Jahan koi cheez zyada bhari ho, wahan se woh apne aap kam wali jagah ki taraf failne lagti hai. Kamre ke ek kone mein agarbatti jalao, toh thodi der mein uski khushboo poore kamre mein fail jaati hai. Dekho, khushboo ko failne ke liye kisi dhakke ki zaroorat nahi padti. Zyada se kam ki taraf jaana uska apna swabhaav hai. Is apne aap failne ko diffusion kehte hain, yaani zyada se kam ki taraf khud-ba-khud jaana. Ab ulta socho: kam wali jagah se zyada wali jagah mein cheez bharni ho, toh kya woh apne aap jaayegi? Kam wali jagah se zyada wali jagah mein cheez apne aap nahi jaati. Use dhakelne mein zor, yaani energy, lagti hai. Isko ek baandh se samjho, jo aaj ki poori kahani mein hamare saath chalega. Baandh ki deewaar ke peeche paani ooncha ruka hota hai. Deewaar ke doosri taraf paani bahut neeche hota hai. Paani hamesha oonchai se neeche ki taraf bahna chahta hai, bina kisi ke dhakele. Aur neeche ka paani wapas upar chadhana ho, toh pump chalana padta hai, jisme bijli kharch hoti hai. Kisi cheez ki maatra mein aise ek taraf zyada aur ek taraf kam wale fark ko gradient kehte hain. Gradient ka seedha matlab hai dhalaan, jaise pahaad ki dhalaan. Ab socho, baandh ke paani ki jagah H⁺ hain: membrane ke ek taraf bahut saare H⁺, aur doosri taraf thode-se. H⁺ ke is upar-neeche wale fark ko proton gradient kehte hain, yaani protons ki dhalaan. Aur baandh ke paani ki tarah H⁺ bhi zyada wali taraf se kam wali taraf bahna chahte hain. Chalo, ab dekhte hain ki chloroplast mein yeh dhalaan kis membrane ke aar-paar banti hai.
Screen par: Hydrogen atom: ek proton, ek electron; electron nikalta, bacha proton = H⁺ (plus ka nishaan); kamre ke kone mein agarbatti, khushboo dheere-dheere poore kamre mein (zyada se kam), label 'diffusion'; ulti disha ka teer 'energy chahiye'; baandh (apni tasveer): deewaar ke peeche ooncha paani, doosri taraf neecha; pump paani upar chadhata, bijli ka meter ghoomta; label 'gradient = dhalaan'; phir paani ki jagah H⁺: membrane ke ek taraf bahut H⁺, doosri taraf thode, label 'proton gradient'.
Terms: proton gradient = Membrane ke dono taraf H⁺ ki maatra ka fark; protons ki dhalaan.
Kitaab se bahar: H⁺ ki pehchaan 11.6 se; diffusion aur agarbatti (Class 9); baandh, jo poori heading mein saath chalta hai; gradient = dhalaan.
Yaad: 11.6-k7
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.3-k4
Jaise respiration mein, waise hi photosynthesis mein bhi ATP ka banna ek membrane ke aar-paar bani proton gradient se juda hai. Photosynthesis mein yeh membrane hai thylakoid ki membrane. Yaad karo, thylakoid ek chapti, band thaili hai, jo chloroplast ke gaadhe dravya, yaani stroma, mein doobi rehti hai. Ise aise socho: paani se bhari balti mein ek band gubbara dooba hua hai. Gubbare ki jhilli thylakoid membrane hai, aur gubbare ke andar ki jagah lumen hai. Bahar balti ka paani stroma hai. Chloroplast mein H⁺ is gubbare ke andar, yaani lumen mein, jama hote hain. Yahan ek fark dhyan se samjho, kyunki exam mein yahi baat ulat-palat kar poochhi jaati hai. Respiration mitochondria mein hoti hai, aur mitochondria ki do membrane hoti hain: ek bahar wali, aur ek andar wali. Dono membranes ke beech ki patli-si jagah ko intermembrane space kehte hain. Inter yaani beech, aur space yaani jagah. Wahan electron ek electron transport system, yaani ETS, se guzarte hain. Aur H⁺ isi beech wali jagah mein jama hote hain. Toh dekho, chloroplast mein H⁺ thaili ke bilkul andar jama hote hain, aur mitochondria mein do membranes ke beech. Mitochondria ki yeh poori baat aap aage respiration ke chapter mein padhoge. Abhi hamare saamne sawaal yeh hai ki chloroplast mein yeh H⁺ lumen mein pahunchte kaise hain.
Screen par: Balti mein dooba band gubbara (apni tasveer): jhilli = thylakoid membrane, andar = lumen, bahar ka paani = stroma; lumen mein H⁺ jama hote; phir mitochondria ka cutaway: bahar aur andar ki do membrane, beech ki patli jagah (label 'intermembrane space: inter = beech'), ETS se guzarte electron aur beech mein jama H⁺; exam card: chloroplast mein lumen, mitochondria mein intermembrane space.
Terms: intermembrane space = Mitochondria ki do membranes ke beech ki patli jagah; inter = beech, space = jagah.
Kitaab se bahar: Balti mein gubbare ki misaal; exam ki baareek baat: chloroplast mein H⁺ lumen mein, mitochondria mein intermembrane space mein (Chapter 12 ka vistaar aage).
Yaad: 11.6.1-k6
p010-i14 Let us now try and understand how actually ATP is synthesised in the chloroplast. The chemiosmotic hypothesis has been put forward to explain the mechanism. Like in respiration, in photosynthesis too, ATP synthesis is linked to development of a proton gradient across a membrane. This time these are the membranes of thylakoid. There is one difference though, here the proton accumulation is towards the inside of the membrane, i.e., in the lumen. In respiration, protons accumulate in the intermembrane space of the mitochondria when electrons move through the ETS (Chapter 12).
pehle poori jhalak11.6.3-k5
H⁺ lumen mein kaise jama hote hain, yeh jaanne ke liye hamein electron ka safar ek baar phir dekhna padega. Roshni padte hi reaction centre ka electron energy lekar uchhalta hai. Is jaagne ko activation of electrons kehte hain. Activation yaani jagaana, kaam ke liye taiyaar karna. Phir electron carriers ki kadi se aage badhta hai, aur isi ko electron ka transport kehte hain. Dekho, is safar mein teen jagah aisi hain, jahan lumen aur stroma ke H⁺ badalte hain. Pehli jagah: paani ka tootna, jo thaili ke andar hota hai. Doosri jagah: ek khaas carrier, jo stroma se H⁺ utha kar andar chhod aata hai. Teesri jagah: NADPH ka banna, jisme stroma ke H⁺ kharch hote hain. Figure 11.7 mein yeh teeno dikhaye gaye hain; chalo, ek-ek karke dekhte hain.
Screen par: Thylakoid ki thaili: roshni padti, electron uchhalta (label 'activation of electrons'); electron carriers ki kadi par chalta (label 'transport'); teen jagah ke number: 1 paani ka tootna (andar), 2 khaas carrier (stroma se andar), 3 NADPH ka banna (stroma mein).
Terms: activation of electrons = Roshni ki energy se electron ka jaagna aur uchhalna; activation = jagaana.
Kitaab se bahar: Teen jagah ka naksha, jo kitaab ke (a), (b), (c) hain.
Yaad: 11.6-k8
p010-i15 Let us understand what causes the proton gradient across the membrane. We need to consider again the processes that take place during the activation of electrons and their transport to determine the steps that cause a proton gradient to develop (Figure 11.7).
samjhana11.6.3-k6
Pehli jagah se shuru karte hain: paani ka tootna. Yaad karo, paani PS II ke paas toot-ta hai. Aur paani todne wala hissa membrane ki inner side par, lumen ki taraf khula hai. Isliye paani ke tootne se bane H⁺ seedhe lumen mein hi chhootte hain. Do paani ke molecule tootte hain, toh chaar H⁺ ek saath lumen mein aa jaate hain. H⁺ asal mein ek proton hi hai, isliye use kabhi proton kehte hain, kabhi hydrogen ion. Protons or hydrogen ions, dono ka matlab ek hi hai. Ab socho, kya yeh H⁺ lumen se nikal kar bahar stroma mein chale jaate hain? Yeh H⁺ lumen se nikal kar bahar nahi ja paate, kyunki membrane unhe aasaani se aar-paar nahi jaane deti. Membrane ke beech ki parat tel jaisi hai, aur charge wale H⁺ tel ki parat se apne aap nahi guzar paate. Toh dekho, lumen mein H⁺ ki ginti badhti jaati hai, aur yahi dhalaan ki pehli eent hai. Baandh ki bhasha mein, deewaar ke peeche paani ka level thoda upar chadh gaya. Exam ki ek baareek baat yaad rakho: paani ke yeh H⁺ kahin bahar se laaye nahi gaye. Yeh lumen ke andar hi paida hue.
Screen par: Thaili ka cutaway: membrane mein dhansa PS II, uska paani todne wala hissa lumen ki taraf; do paani toot kar chaar H⁺ andar lumen mein; label 'H⁺ = proton = hydrogen ion'; H⁺ membrane se takra kar wapas, membrane ki beech ki tel jaisi parat; lumen mein H⁺ ki ginti badhti; kone mein baandh ke peeche paani ka level thoda upar.
Terms: protons or hydrogen ions = H⁺ ke do naam; H⁺ asal mein ek proton hi hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Membrane ki tel jaisi parat charge wale H⁺ ko rokti hai (teacher ki taraf se); exam ki baareek baat: paani ke H⁺ lumen ke andar hi bante hain.

p010-i16 (a) Since splitting of the water molecule takes place on the inner side of the membrane, the protons or hydrogen ions that are produced by the splitting of water accumulate within the lumen of the thylakoids.
figure11.6.3-k7
Ab Figure 11.7 par ungli rakho; yahi aaj ki hamari poori kahani ka naksha hai. Dekho, beech mein gulabi rang ki ek band jhilli hai; uspar likha hai Thylakoid membrane. Jhilli ke andar ki jagah par likha hai Lumen (high H⁺), yaani andar H⁺ zyada hain. Jhilli ke bahar likha hai Stroma (low H⁺), yaani bahar H⁺ kam hain. Upar se narangi teer aa rahe hain, jin par likha hai Light; roshni do jagah padti hai. Baayein P680 wala PS II hai, aur daayein P700 wala PS I. PS II ke neeche H₂O se ½O₂ aur H⁺ banne ka teer hai. Ise Oxidation of water kehte hain, kyunki paani apne electron kho raha hai. Dhyan do, ½O₂ ka matlab aadha molecule nahi hai. Ek paani se oxygen ka sirf ek atom milta hai, aur do paani milkar ek poori O₂ dete hain. Ab electron ka raasta ungli se pakdo: PS II se electron pehle PQ par jaata hai. PQ ka poora naam Plastoquinone hai, aur H⁺ lene ke baad yahi PQH₂ ban jaata hai. PQ se electron peele rang ke Cytochrome B6f complex par pahunchta hai. Wahan se PC, yaani Plastocyanin, electron ko PS I tak le jaata hai. PS I par roshni dobara dhakka deti hai, aur electron upar Fd par pahunchta hai. Fd ka poora naam ferredoxin hai, aur yeh electron ko FNR naam ke enzyme tak le jaata hai. FNR wahi NADP reductase hai jo humne pichhli film mein dekha tha. Iska poora naam ferredoxin NADP⁺ reductase hai. FNR ke paas NADP⁺ + H⁺ se NADPH banne ka teer hai. Socho, yeh poora raasta wahi Z scheme hai, bas yahan har carrier membrane mein apni sahi jagah par dikhaya gaya hai. Ab figure ke H⁺ dekho: lumen mein jagah-jagah H⁺ likhe hain, aur hare teer unka aana-jaana dikhate hain. Neeche ek machine hai jiska naam ATP synthase hai. Iske do hisse CF₀ aur CF₁ likhe hain, aur paas mein ADP + Pᵢ se ATP banne ka teer hai. Baayein neeche ek nokdaar patti par High se Low likha hai. H⁺ ke teer, yeh patti aur yeh machine kya kehte hain, yeh ab hum ek-ek karke kholte hain.
