p011-i1 A closed thylakoid membrane encloses a lumen with high H⁺; the surrounding stroma has low H⁺. Light reaches P680 PS II and P700 PS I. Electron flow is shown from PS II through plastoquinone, cytochrome B6f and plastocyanin to PS I, then through Fd and FNR to NADP⁺ reduction. Water oxidation at PS II supplies lumen H⁺ and releases ½O₂. Proton movement from the stroma through the carrier pathway adds H⁺ to the lumen. Green arrows converge on lumen H⁺ and show its return through the CF₀–CF₁ ATP synthase to the stroma, accompanying ADP + Pᵢ → ATP. A wedge marks a high-to-low electrochemical potential gradient from lumen to stroma.
samjhana11.6.3-k8
Ab doosri jagah dekho, jo sabse dilchasp hai. Yaad karo, roshni se uchhla electron sabse pehle reaction centre ke primary acceptor ke paas jaata hai. PS II ka yeh primary acceptor membrane ki bahar wali taraf, yaani stroma ki taraf, baitha hai. Carriers do tarah ke hote hain: kuch sirf electron uthate hain, aur kuch electron ke saath H⁺ bhi utha lete hain. Jo sirf electron le jaaye, woh electron carrier hai. Jo electron ke saath H⁺ bhi le jaaye, use H carrier kehte hain. Electron aur H⁺ milkar ek poora hydrogen atom ban jaate hain, isliye iska naam H carrier pada. Yahan primary acceptor apna electron kisi electron carrier ko nahi, balki ek H carrier ko deta hai. Yeh H carrier wahi PQ hai, jo humne figure mein dekha. Socho, PQ ko electron mila; ab woh apne saath H⁺ kahan se lega? PQ apne saath H⁺ stroma se hi leta hai, kyunki woh membrane ki stroma wali taraf khada hai. Dekho, phir PQ membrane ke andar hi tair kar doosri taraf, inner side tak pahunchta hai. Wahan woh apna electron agle electron carrier, yaani Cytochrome B6f, ko de deta hai. Electron chala gaya, toh saath wala H⁺ akela reh gaya, aur use lumen mein chhod diya jaata hai. Ise nadi ki ek naav ki tarah socho, jo is kinare se sawaari utha kar us kinare utaar deti hai. Is kinara hai stroma, us kinara hai lumen, aur sawaari hai H⁺. Isliye electron ke har safar ke saath stroma ka ek H⁺ kam hota hai, aur lumen ka ek H⁺ badhta hai. Asal mein PQ ek baar mein do electron aur do H⁺ uthata hai, aur tab woh PQH₂ kehlata hai. Baandh mein yeh pump ka kaam hai: neeche ka paani utha kar deewaar ke peeche upar bhar dena. Aur is pump ko chalane ki energy electron ke chalne se aati hai.
Screen par: Membrane ka cutaway: PS II ka primary acceptor stroma wali taraf; do tarah ke carrier (sirf electron / electron ke saath H⁺); PQ stroma se H⁺ uthata, membrane ke andar tair kar inner side tak, electron Cytochrome B6f ko, H⁺ lumen mein; nadi ki naav (apni tasveer): is kinare stroma, us kinare lumen, sawaari H⁺; label 'PQ + 2e⁻ + 2H⁺ = PQH₂'; kone mein baandh ka pump paani upar chadhata.
Terms: H carrier = Electron ke saath H⁺ bhi le jaane wala carrier; electron + H⁺ = hydrogen.
Kitaab se bahar: Yeh H carrier PQ (plastoquinone) hai, jo membrane ke andar tairta hai (kitaab naam nahi leti, figure dikhata hai); naav ki misaal; PQ ek baar mein do electron aur do H⁺.
p011-i20 (b) As electrons move through the photosystems, protons are transported across the membrane. This happens because the primary acceptor of electron which is located towards the outer side of the membrane transfers its electron not to an electron carrier but to an H carrier. Hence, this molecule removes a proton from the stroma while transporting an electron. When this molecule passes on its electron to the electron carrier on the inner side of the membrane, the proton is released into the inner side or the lumen side of the membrane.
samjhana11.6.3-k9
Ab teesri jagah chalo, electron ke safar ke bilkul aakhir mein. Yaad karo, NADP⁺ electron lekar NADPH banta hai, aur electron lene ko hi reduction kehte hain. Is reduction ko karaane wala enzyme NADP reductase hai, jo figure mein FNR likha hai. Yeh enzyme membrane ki stroma wali taraf, yaani stroma side par, baitha hai. Electron toh PS I ke acceptor se Fd ke raaste is enzyme tak pahunch jaate hain. Par NADP⁺ ko NADPH banne ke liye sirf electron kaafi nahi. Saath mein H⁺ bhi chahiye. Toh NADPH banne ke liye yeh H⁺ kahan se aate hain? NADPH banne ke liye H⁺ stroma se hi aate hain, kyunki enzyme stroma ki taraf baitha hai. Matlab dekho, NADPH ke har molecule ke saath stroma ka ek aur H⁺ kam ho jaata hai. Socho, ek taraf PQ stroma ke H⁺ andar bhar raha hai, aur doosri taraf FNR bhi stroma ke H⁺ kharch kar raha hai. Dono milkar stroma ko H⁺ se aur khaali kar dete hain. Baandh mein yeh aisa hai jaise neeche wali taraf se paani alag se nikaal liya jaaye, taaki fark aur bada ho jaaye.
Screen par: Membrane ki stroma side par baitha enzyme (label 'NADP reductase = FNR'); PS I ke acceptor se Fd ke raaste electron aate; NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ se NADPH, H⁺ stroma se uthaya jaata; stroma mein H⁺ ki ginti ghat-ti; ek taraf PQ H⁺ andar bharta, doosri taraf FNR stroma ke H⁺ kharch karta; kone mein baandh ki neeche wali taraf se paani nikaala jaata.
Terms: stroma side = Membrane ka woh chehra jo stroma ki taraf hai.
Kitaab se bahar: NADP reductase = figure ka FNR; electron Fd se aate hain, enzyme sirf reaction karaata hai.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k5
p011-i21 (c) The NADP reductase enzyme is located on the stroma side of the membrane. Along with electrons that come from the acceptor of electrons of PS I, protons are necessary for the reduction of NADP⁺ to NADPH+ H⁺. These protons are also removed from the stroma.
samjhana11.6.3-k10
Chalo, ab teeno kaam ek saath jodo. Lumen mein paani ke tootne se H⁺ badhe, aur PQ ne stroma se laakar aur H⁺ bhar diye. Udhar stroma mein PQ ne H⁺ uthaye, aur NADPH banne mein bhi H⁺ kharch hue. Toh chloroplast ke andar stroma mein H⁺ ghatte jaate hain, aur lumen mein jama hote jaate hain. Isse thylakoid membrane ke aar-paar ek proton gradient ban jaata hai: andar bahut, bahar thode. Saath mein lumen ka pH bhi kaafi gir jaata hai, aur yeh girna naapa ja sakta hai. pH kya hai, yeh Class 10 se yaad karo: pH batata hai ki kisi ghol mein H⁺ kitne hain. Jitne zyada H⁺, utna kam pH, aur ghol utna zyada khatta, yaani acidic. Aap jaante ho, nimbu ka ras isliye khatta hai, kyunki usme H⁺ bahut hain aur uska pH kam hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki H⁺ badhe toh pH bhi badhega. Par asal mein ulta hota hai: H⁺ badhe toh pH ghatta hai. Lumen ka pH lagbhag 5 tak gir jaata hai, jabki stroma ka pH lagbhag 8 rehta hai. pH ka har ek number das guna fark hai. Toh teen number ke fark ka matlab hai lumen mein lagbhag hazaar guna zyada H⁺. Ab figure ki nokdaar patti dekho; uspar High Electrochemical Potential Gradient Low likha hai. Iska mota sira lumen ki taraf High hai, aur patla sira stroma ki taraf Low. Yahi dhalaan ki disha hai. Electrochemical do hisson se bana hai: electro yaani charge ka fark, aur chemical yaani H⁺ ki ginti ka fark. Lumen mein H⁺ ginti mein bhi zyada hain, aur unka positive charge bhi wahan zyada jama hai. Dono milkar H⁺ ko bahar ki taraf dhakelte hain. Socho, baandh ki deewaar ke peeche ab paani poori oonchai tak bhar chuka hai.
Screen par: Teeno kaam ek saath: lumen mein H⁺ badhte (paani se, PQ se), stroma mein ghat-te (PQ, NADPH); label 'proton gradient: andar bahut, bahar thode'; pH ki patti 0 se 14, zyada H⁺ = kam pH = khatta; nimbu ka ras (apni tasveer); 'H⁺ badhe to pH badhe' par kaata nishaan; lumen pH lagbhag 5, stroma pH lagbhag 8, label 'lagbhag 1000 guna H⁺'; figure ki nokdaar patti High Electrochemical Potential Gradient Low; electro = charge, chemical = ginti; baandh ke peeche paani poori oonchai par.
Kitaab se bahar: pH aur khattapan Class 10 se; nimbu ki misaal; lumen pH lagbhag 5 aur stroma lagbhag 8, hazaar guna H⁺ (kitaab sirf 'naapne laayak kami' kehti hai); galat soch: H⁺ badhe toh pH badhe; electro + chemical ke hisse.
p011-i22 Hence, within the chloroplast, protons in the stroma decrease in number, while in the lumen there is accumulation of protons. This creates a proton gradient across the thylakoid membrane as well as a measurable decrease in pH in the lumen.
samjhana11.6.3-k11
Ab ginti karke dekho ki sirf do paani ke molecule par kya hota hai. Do paani tootne se chaar H⁺ seedhe lumen mein aaye. Unke chaar electron PQ se hokar gaye, aur PQ stroma se chaar H⁺ utha kar lumen mein chhod aaya. Toh lumen mein kul aath H⁺ badh gaye. Wahi chaar electron aakhir mein do NADPH banate hain, aur har NADPH stroma ka ek H⁺ le leta hai. Toh batao, stroma se kul kitne H⁺ kam hue? Stroma se kul chhe H⁺ kam hue: chaar PQ ne uthaye, aur do NADPH ne liye. Par lumen mein aath badhe aur stroma se chhe ghate; yeh ginti barabar kyun nahi hai? Ginti barabar isliye nahi hai, kyunki paani ke chaar H⁺ stroma se aaye hi nahi. Woh lumen ke andar hi paida hue. Isliye dekho, dhalaan dono taraf se badhti hai: andar H⁺ judte hain, aur bahar se ghat-te hain.
Screen par: Ginti ka board: do paani se 4 H⁺ lumen mein; 4 electron PQ se, PQ stroma se 4 H⁺ lumen mein; lumen +8; 4 electron se 2 NADPH, stroma −2; stroma kul −6; sawaal '8 aur 6 barabar kyun nahi?'; paani ke 4 H⁺ lumen ke andar hi bane, stroma se nahi.
Kitaab se bahar: Do paani ke molecule par H⁺ ki ginti (8 lumen mein, 6 stroma se), teacher ki taraf se.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.3-k12
Ab ek seedha sawaal: hum is proton gradient ke peeche itna kyun pade hain? Hum proton gradient ke peeche isliye pade hain, kyunki ATP ki asli kahani isi mein chhupi hai. Socho, baandh ke peeche ruka paani bijli kab banata hai: jab woh ruka rahe, ya jab woh neeche bahe? Baandh ka paani bijli tab banata hai jab woh neeche bahta hai. Ruka hua paani sirf taakat sambhaal kar rakhta hai. H⁺ ke saath bhi bilkul yahi hota hai. Jab lumen ke H⁺ wapas stroma mein bahte hain, tab dhalaan dheere-dheere kam hoti hai, aur isi se ATP banta hai. Dhalaan ke is kam hone ko breakdown of this gradient kehte hain. Breakdown yaani tootna, ghatte-ghatte mit jaana. Yaad rakho, isme H⁺ nasht nahi hote; bas dono taraf ka fark ghatta hai. Par membrane toh H⁺ ko aar-paar jaane hi nahi deti, toh yeh H⁺ bahar nikalenge kahan se? H⁺ bahar membrane ke ek khaas darwaze se nikalte hain, jo ATP synthase naam ki machine ke CF₀ hisse mein bana hai. Yeh darwaza ek nali jaisa raasta hai, jo membrane ke is paar se us paar tak jaata hai. Membrane ke aar-paar faile aise raaste ko transmembrane channel kehte hain. Trans yaani aar-paar, aur channel yaani nali. Dekho, is nali se H⁺ lumen se nikal kar stroma mein aate hain, aur dhalaan par neeche bahte hain. Baandh mein yahi woh gate hai, jahan se paani turbine ki taraf girta hai.
Screen par: Sawaal 'proton gradient ke peeche kyun?'; baandh: ruka paani bijli nahi banata, behta paani turbine ghumata (apni tasveer); lumen se stroma ki taraf H⁺ bahte, dhalaan ghat-ti (label 'breakdown of this gradient': H⁺ nasht nahi, fark ghata); membrane par rok, ek hi darwaza: ATP synthase ka CF₀; nali membrane ke aar-paar (label 'transmembrane channel: trans = aar-paar'); H⁺ nali se stroma mein; kone mein baandh ka gate.
Terms: breakdown of this gradient = Dhalaan ka ghatte-ghatte mitna; H⁺ nasht nahi hote, fark kam hota hai.; transmembrane = Membrane ke aar-paar faila; trans = aar-paar.
Kitaab se bahar: Baandh ka sawaal (ruka paani ya behta paani); exam ki baareek baat: breakdown mein H⁺ nasht nahi hote.
p011-i23 Why are we so interested in the proton gradient?
p011-i24 This gradient is important because it is the breakdown of this gradient that leads to the synthesis of ATP. The gradient is broken down due to the movement of protons across the membrane to the stroma through the transmembrane
samjhana11.6.3-k13
Chalo, ab is machine ko paas se dekhte hain. ATP synthase ek enzyme hai, aur synthase ka matlab hai kuch banane wala; yeh enzyme ATP banata hai. Iske do hisse hain, aur dono ke naam mein CF aata hai; yahan C ka matlab hai chloroplast. Pehla hissa CF₀ hai, jo thylakoid membrane ke andar dhansa hua hai. CF₀ hi woh transmembrane channel banata hai, jisse H⁺ aar-paar jaate hain. Doosra hissa CF₁ hai, jo membrane se bahar nikla hua hai, aur stroma ki taraf munh kiye hai. Baandh se samjho toh CF₀ woh gate wali nali hai, aur CF₁ uske munh par lagi turbine. Ab socho, H⁺ ko is nali ki zaroorat kyun padti hai? H⁺ ko nali ki zaroorat isliye padti hai, kyunki membrane ki tel jaisi parat charge wale H⁺ ko seedhe nahi nikalne deti. CF₀ ka protein unhe ek aasaan raasta de deta hai, ek tarah ki madad. Aur H⁺ zyada se kam ki taraf ja rahe hain, isliye unhe dhakelne mein koi energy nahi lagti. Aise madad wale raaste se apne aap failne ko facilitated diffusion kehte hain. Facilitated yaani madad diya hua, aur diffusion yaani zyada se kam ki taraf failna, jaisa agarbatti mein dekha. Yaad rakho: H⁺ ko lumen mein bharne mein energy lagti hai, par lumen se bahar aane mein nahi.
Screen par: ATP synthase paas se: membrane mein dhansa CF₀ (nali), uske upar stroma ki taraf nikla CF₁ (sir); label 'C = chloroplast'; kone mein baandh ki gate wali nali aur uske munh par turbine; H⁺ tel jaisi parat se takrate, CF₀ ki nali se aasaani se guzarte; label 'facilitated = madad diya hua, diffusion = zyada se kam'; exam card 'andar bharna: energy; bahar aana: energy nahi'.
Terms: CF₀ = ATP synthase ka membrane mein dhansa hissa; H⁺ ki nali.; CF₁ = ATP synthase ka stroma ki taraf nikla sir, jahan ATP banta hai.; facilitated diffusion = Protein ke diye raaste se zyada se kam ki taraf apne aap failna, bina energy.
Kitaab se bahar: CF mein C = chloroplast; tel jaisi parat aur charge wale H⁺ (teacher ki taraf se); exam ki baareek baat: pumping mein energy, facilitated diffusion mein nahi.
p012-i1 channel of the CF₀ of the ATP synthase. The ATP synthase enzyme consists of two parts: one called the CF₀ is embedded in the thylakoid membrane and forms a transmembrane channel that carries out facilitated diffusion of protons across the membrane. The other portion is called CF₁ and protrudes on the outer surface of the thylakoid membrane on the side that faces the stroma.
samjhana11.6.3-k14
Ab sabse zaroori baat: H⁺ ke behne se ATP banta kaise hai? H⁺ ke behne se ATP aise banta hai: jab H⁺ CF₀ se guzarte hain, toh dhalaan ki energy CF₁ tak pahunchti hai. Dekho, yeh energy CF₁ ki shakal badal deti hai. Is enzyme jaise kisi bhi protein ki shakal ya banawat ko uski conformation kehte hain. Conformation yaani uska aakaar. Toh shakal ke is badalne ko conformational change kehte hain. Ise apni mutthi se samjho: haath khula, phir band, phir khula. Aapke khule haath mein ADP aur Pᵢ aakar baith jaate hain. Mutthi band hoti hai, toh dono ko daba kar jod deti hai, aur ATP ban jaata hai. Mutthi phir khulti hai, ATP bahar aa jaata hai, aur nayi ADP aur Pᵢ andar aa jaati hain. Jab tak H⁺ behte rahenge, tab tak yeh khulna aur band hona chalta rahega. Isliye dekho, ek nahi, balki kai saare ATP bante jaate hain, aur har ATP energy se bhara hota hai. Asal mein CF₀ ka ek hissa H⁺ ke guzarne par turbine ki tarah ghoomta hai. Yahi ghoomna CF₁ ki shakal badalta hai. Baandh mein bhi girta paani turbine ghumata hai, aur ghoomti turbine bijli banati hai. Yahan bijli ki jagah ATP banta hai. Socho, ATP mein band yeh energy asal mein kahan se aayi? ATP mein band yeh energy asal mein roshni ki hai: roshni se electron mein, electron se H⁺ ki dhalaan mein, aur dhalaan se ATP mein.
Screen par: CF₀ se guzarte H⁺, energy CF₁ tak; CF₁ ki shakal badalti (label 'conformation = protein ka aakaar'); mutthi (apni tasveer): khule haath mein ADP aur Pᵢ, mutthi band, ATP bana, haath khula, ATP bahar, nayi ADP aur Pᵢ; ATP ke kai molecule ek ke baad ek; CF₀ ka hissa turbine jaisa ghoomta; kone mein baandh ki turbine aur bijli; energy ka safar: roshni, electron, H⁺ ki dhalaan, ATP.
Terms: conformational change = Protein ki shakal (conformation) ka badalna.
Kitaab se bahar: Mutthi ki misaal (Navin ka 'bilkul yahi tha'); CF₀ ka ghoomna turbine jaisa (teacher ki taraf se); energy ka poora safar roshni se ATP tak.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k2
p012-i2 The break down of the gradient provides enough energy to cause a conformational change in the CF₁ particle of the ATP synthase, which makes the enzyme synthesise several molecules of energy-packed ATP.
example11.6.3-k15
Ab ek prayog dekho, jisne Mitchell ki baat ko pakka kar diya. 1966 mein Andre Jagendorf naam ke vaigyanik ne chloroplast se thylakoid ki thailiyan alag nikaal li. Unhone in thailiyon ko andhere mein ek khatte ghol mein rakha, jiska pH lagbhag 4 tha. Kuch der mein thailiyon ka lumen bhi khatta ho gaya, yaani andar H⁺ bhar gaye. Phir unhone thailiyon ko jaldi se ek kam khatte ghol mein daala, jiska pH lagbhag 8 tha. Saath mein unhone ADP aur Pᵢ bhi milaaye. Gaur karo, poore prayog mein na roshni thi, na koi electron chal raha tha. Bas andar H⁺ bahut the, aur bahar bahut kam. Ruko, pehle khud socho: bina roshni aur bina electron ke, kya in thailiyon mein ATP banega? Aapne socha hoga ki roshni ke bina kuch nahi banega, par ATP bana, aur khoob bana. ATP isliye bana, kyunki H⁺ ki dhalaan taiyaar thi, aur andar ke H⁺ ATP synthase se bahar bahne lage. Matlab ATP banane ke liye asli zaroorat H⁺ ki dhalaan ki hai. Roshni aur electron ka kaam bas yeh dhalaan banana hai. Yahi prayog Mitchell ki chemiosmotic hypothesis ki sabse saaf jeet bana.
Screen par: Prayogshala, 1966: chloroplast se alag ki gayi thylakoid ki thailiyan; andhera (bulb band ka nishaan); thailiyan pH 4 ke khatte ghol mein, lumen mein H⁺ bharte; phir pH 8 ke ghol mein, saath mein ADP aur Pᵢ; 'roshni nahi, electron nahi' ke do nishaan; sawaal 'Ruko, pehle khud socho: ATP banega?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second); phir ATP ke molecule bante, H⁺ ATP synthase se bahar bahte.
Kitaab se bahar: Jagendorf ka 1966 ka prayog (kitaab mein nahi): chemiosmosis ka sabse saaf saboot; ruko, pehle khud socho isi mod par.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.3-k16
Ab tak ki saari baat ek line mein dekho: pehle H⁺ ko andar bharo, phir unhe ek darwaze se bahar bahne do. Membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ki dhalaan bana kar, aur unhe ATP synthase se wapas baha kar ATP banane ko chemiosmosis kehte hain. Yaad rakho, chemiosmosis ke liye chaar cheezein zaroori hain. Pehli, ek membrane, jo H⁺ ko apne aap aar-paar na jaane de. Doosri, ek proton pump, jo energy laga kar H⁺ ko dhalaan ke ulte, thylakoid lumen mein bhar de. Pump yaani zor laga kar kisi cheez ko upar ya andar dhakelna. Yahan yeh kaam electron ka safar aur PQ karte hain. Teesri, khud proton gradient, yaani H⁺ ki dhalaan, jisse lumen mein H⁺ bahut zyada ho jaate hain. Aur chauthi, ATP synthase, jiske channel se H⁺ wapas bahte hain. H⁺ ke is wapas bahne se itni energy nikalti hai ki ATP synthase enzyme chal padta hai, aur ATP banata hai. Socho, baandh mein bhi yahi chaar cheezein hain: deewaar, paani upar chadhane wala pump, upar-neeche paani ka fark, aur turbine. Inme se ek bhi hata do, toh bijli nahi banegi; isliye chemiosmosis mein bhi chaaron zaroori hain.
Screen par: Ek line mein poora tareeka: H⁺ andar bharte, phir darwaze se bahar; label 'chemiosmosis'; chaar khaane: membrane, proton pump (electron ka safar aur PQ), proton gradient (lumen mein bahut H⁺), ATP synthase; har khaane ke saath baandh ki tasveer: deewaar, pump, upar-neeche paani, turbine; ek khaana hatate hi bijli ka bulb bujhta.
Terms: chemiosmosis = Membrane ke aar-paar H⁺ ki dhalaan bana kar aur unhe ATP synthase se wapas baha kar ATP banana.; proton pump = Energy laga kar H⁺ ko dhalaan ke ulte bharne wala; yahan electron ka safar aur PQ.
Kitaab se bahar: Exam ki baareek baat: yahan pump koi alag machine nahi, electron ka safar aur PQ hi H⁺ bharte hain; chaaron cheezein baandh se jodi.
p012-i3 Chemiosmosis requires a membrane, a proton pump, a proton gradient and ATP synthase. Energy is used to pump protons across a membrane, to create a gradient or a high concentration of protons within the thylakoid lumen. ATP synthase has a channel that allows diffusion of protons back across the membrane; this releases enough energy to activate ATP synthase enzyme that catalyses the formation of ATP.
ruko, socho11.6.3-k17
Socho, baandh ki deewaar mein kahin daraar pad jaaye, toh paani turbine tak pahunchne se pehle hi bah jaayega. Ab aap batao: agar thylakoid membrane mein chhote-chhote chhed ho jaayein, toh kya electron chalte rahenge, aur kya ATP banega? Membrane mein chhed hon, toh bhi electron chalte rahenge, aur H⁺ andar bharte bhi rahenge. Par H⁺ CF₀ ke bajaay chhedon se hi wapas bah jaayenge, aur dhalaan kabhi ban hi nahi paayegi. Isliye ATP nahi banega; energy bas garmi ban kar bekaar chali jaayegi. Kuch rasayan sach mein membrane ko aisa hi chhedon wala bana dete hain. Tab electron chalne par bhi ATP ruk jaata hai. Isse saaf hai ki electron ka chalna aur ATP ka banna seedhe jude nahi. Dekho, dono ke beech H⁺ ki dhalaan ka pul hai.
Screen par: Baandh ki deewaar mein daraar, paani turbine se pehle hi bahta (apni tasveer); sawaal ka dabba; chhedon wali thylakoid membrane: electron chalte, H⁺ andar jaate par chhedon se laut aate; CF₀ khaali, ATP nahi; garmi ki lehrein; aakhir mein electron aur ATP ke beech 'H⁺ ki dhalaan' ka pul.
Kitaab se bahar: Chhedon wali membrane ka sawaal aur aise rasayan (teacher ki taraf se), chemiosmosis ki chaar cheezon ki jaanch.
Jawab: Electron chalte rahenge aur H⁺ andar bhi jaayenge, par chhedon se wapas bah jaayenge; dhalaan nahi banegi, CF₀ se H⁺ nahi guzrenge, isliye ATP nahi banega; energy garmi ban jaayegi.
Aam galti: Galat soch: electron chal rahe hain toh ATP apne aap banega. Galat, kyunki ATP ko H⁺ ki dhalaan aur CF₀–CF₁ se unka bahav chahiye; iske liye membrane ka band rehna zaroori hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.6.3-k18
Ab dekho ki yeh ATP banta kahan hai. CF₁ ka munh stroma ki taraf hai, isliye naya bana ATP seedhe stroma mein chhootta hai. Aur FNR bhi stroma side par baitha hai, isliye NADPH bhi stroma mein hi banta hai. Socho, electron ke chalne se bana NADPH aur yeh ATP turant stroma mein hi kaam aate hain. Stroma mein ek biosynthetic reaction chalti hai, jo hawa ke CO₂ ko pakad kar sugar banati hai. Biosynthetic yaani jeev ke andar naye molecule banane wala. Bio yaani jeevan, aur synthesis yaani banana. CO₂ ke carbon ko pakad kar kisi molecule ka pakka hissa bana dene ko fixing CO₂ kehte hain. Matlab ATP aur NADPH ko kahin door nahi jaana padta; jahan bante hain, wahin kaam aate hain. Yaad karo, sugar banane wale enzyme bhi stroma mein hi rehte hain.
Screen par: CF₁ ka munh stroma ki taraf, ATP stroma mein chhootta; FNR stroma side par, NADPH bhi stroma mein; dono turant stroma ki sugar banane wali reaction mein; hawa ka CO₂ pakad kar sugar ka molecule (label 'fixing CO₂'); label 'biosynthetic: bio = jeevan, synthesis = banana'; stroma mein enzyme.
Terms: fixing CO₂ = CO₂ ke carbon ko pakad kar kisi molecule ka pakka hissa bana dena.
Kitaab se bahar: ATP aur NADPH dono stroma ki taraf bante hain, isliye wahin kaam aate hain (CF₁ aur FNR ki jagah se).
p012-i4 Along with the NADPH produced by the movement of electrons, the ATP will be used immediately in the biosynthetic reaction taking place in the stroma, responsible for fixing CO₂, and synthesis of sugars.
poori kahani dobara11.6.3-k19
Chalo, ab aaj ka poora safar ek saath dekhte hain, roshni se ATP tak. Roshni electron ko uchhaalti hai, aur electron carriers ki kadi par aage badhta hai. Raaste mein paani ka tootna lumen mein H⁺ chhodta hai, aur PQ stroma ke H⁺ utha kar lumen mein bharta hai. Stroma side par NADPH banne mein stroma ke aur H⁺ kharch hote hain. Andar H⁺ bahut aur bahar thode: yahi proton gradient hai, aur isse lumen khatta ho jaata hai. Membrane H⁺ ko seedhe bahar nahi jaane deti. Woh sirf ATP synthase ke CF₀ channel se stroma mein laut sakte hain. Unke behne ki energy CF₁ ki shakal badalti hai, aur CF₁ ADP aur Pᵢ ko jod kar kai ATP banata hai. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se socho: jab electron ne ADP ko chhua hi nahi, toh teesre phosphate ko jodne ki energy aayi kahan se? Teesre phosphate ko jodne ki energy H⁺ ki dhalaan se aayi. Electron ne apni energy H⁺ ko lumen mein bharne mein lagayi, aur lautte H⁺ ne woh energy ATP synthase ko de di. Bilkul baandh ki tarah: pump ne paani upar chadhaya, aur girte paani ne turbine ghuma kar bijli banayi. Isi poore tareeke ko chemiosmosis kehte hain, aur yahi Mitchell ki chemiosmotic hypothesis thi. Ab ATP aur NADPH dono stroma mein taiyaar hain, aur turant kaam mein lagne wale hain. Par stroma mein CO₂ se sugar banane mein yeh dono kaise kharch hote hain, aur kya us kaam ke liye roshni chahiye, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Aaj ki kahani ek patti par: roshni, electron uchhla, kadi; paani ka tootna (lumen mein H⁺), PQ ki naav (stroma se lumen), FNR (stroma ke H⁺ kharch); proton gradient aur pH ki patti; CF₀ ki nali se H⁺ laut-te, CF₁ ki mutthi ATP banati; shuru ka sawaal laut kar, jawab ke teer: electron se H⁺ ki dhalaan, dhalaan se ATP; kone mein baandh, pump, turbine, bijli; label 'chemiosmosis'; aakhir mein stroma mein ATP aur NADPH, CO₂ aur sugar ke beech '?' khula.
Kitaab se bahar: Shuru ka sawaal phir se, uske jawab ke saath; agli film (11.7) ka sawaal khula chhoda: stroma mein ATP aur NADPH kaise kharch hote hain aur kya us kaam ko roshni chahiye.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.7 WHERE ARE THE ATP AND NADPH USED? · 9 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 20 min
Maqsad: Stroma ka sugar banane wala kaam roshni par kitna tika hai, aur hawa ki CO₂ ka carbon sugar tak pahunchne se pehle sabse pehle kahan rukta hai?
Misaal: marathon: start line = CO₂ ka stroma mein aana; raaste ke mod = beech ke padaav; finish line = sugar; pehla mod = first product (PGA); laal topi wale dhavak = ¹⁴C wale carbon; race ke ek ghante baad ki photo = der tak di gayi ¹⁴C wali CO₂ (nishaan har padaav mein bikhra); kuch second baad ki photo = Calvin ka kuch second wala prayog (sab pehle mod par); alag pehle mod wali do marathon = C₃ aur C₄
Kitab ki baatein (64): kitab kehti hai 22 · shabd ka matlab 19 · pehle ki zaroori baat 12 · figure ka nishaan 0 · teacher ki extra baat 11
f1 pehle ki zaroori baat Pichhli film (11.6.3): lumen ke H⁺ CF₀ ki nali se stroma mein lautte hain to CF₁ ATP banata hai; FNR stroma side par hai, isliye NADPH bhi stroma mein banta hai.
f2 pehle ki zaroori baat Roshni ka kaam ATP aur NADPH banane tak hai; roshni khud sugar nahi banati, sugar stroma mein banti hai (11.3, 11.6.3).

f23 kitab kehti hai Ab dekhna hai ki biosynthetic phase mein ATP aur NADPH kaise use hote hain (kis kadam mein kitne, 11.7.2 mein).

f30 pehle ki zaroori baat Poori equation sirf shuruaat aur aakhir batati hai; beech ke padaav nahi.

f34 pehle ki zaroori baat Isotopes aur radio-activity (shuruaat): ek element ke atom neutron ki ginti mein alag ho sakte hain; radioactive atom radiation chhodte hain, jise yantra pakad lete hain; ¹⁴C paudhe mein aam carbon jaisa hi chalta hai.
f35 shabd ka matlab: algal Algal: algae ka; algae paani mein rehne wale chhote, saral hare jeev hain jo photosynthesis karte hain.

f41 shabd ka matlab: PGA PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: P = phospho (us par phosphate laga hai), G = glyceric (teen carbon ki reedh), A = acid.
f42 kitab kehti hai Calvin ne poora biosynthetic raasta khojne mein bhi yogdaan diya; isliye ise unke naam par Calvin cycle kaha gaya.

f44 kitab kehti hai PGA mein kitne carbon atom hain?

f47 teacher ki extra baat Glycerine (glycerol) ki reedh bhi teen carbon ki hai; glyceric acid usi parivaar ka hai.
f48 teacher ki extra baat Kuch second ke prayog mein ¹⁴C PGA ke teen carbon mein se sirf ek, acid wale sire ke carbon par mila; CO₂ ne ek carbon diya, baaki do kisi aur molecule se aaye.
f49 kitab kehti hai Vaigyanikon ne jaanna chaaha ki kya sab paudhon mein CO₂ fixation ka pehla product PGA hi hai, ya kuch paudhon mein koi aur.
f50 kitab kehti hai Bahut tarah ke paudhon par prayogon se ek aur group mila, jisme CO₂ fixation ka pehla stable product phir organic acid tha, par chaar carbon wala.
f51 kitab kehti hai: oxaloacetic acid Yeh acid oxaloacetic acid, yaani OAA, nikla.

f64 shabd ka matlab: OAA OAA: oxaloacetic acid ka chhota naam (oxalo, acetic, acid ke pehle akshar).
f53 shabd ka matlab: first stable product First stable product: CO₂ fix hone ke baad bana pehla tikau molecule, jo turant toot na jaaye.
f54 shabd ka matlab: CO₂ fixation CO₂ fixation: CO₂ ke carbon ka kisi molecule mein pakka bandh jaana (fixing CO₂).
f55 shabd ka matlab: CO₂ assimilation CO₂ assimilation: CO₂ ke carbon ka paudhe ke apne molecules ka hissa ban jaana; assimilation = pach kar apna hissa banna.
f56 kitab kehti hai Tab se photosynthesis mein CO₂ assimilation do mukhya prakaar ka maana gaya: pehla product C₃ acid (PGA) wale paudhon ka C₃ pathway, aur C₄ acid (OAA) wale paudhon ka C₄ pathway.
f57 shabd ka matlab: C₃ pathway C₃ pathway: jin paudhon mein CO₂ fixation ka pehla product teen carbon wala PGA hai, unka raasta.
f58 shabd ka matlab: C₄ pathway C₄ pathway: jin paudhon mein pehla product chaar carbon wala OAA hai, unka raasta.
f59 kitab kehti hai In dono groups ke paudhon mein aur bhi jude hue gun hain, jo aage padhenge (11.8).
f60 pehle ki zaroori baat Molecule ke carbon gine ja sakte hain: CO₂ mein ek, PGA mein teen, OAA mein chaar.
f61 teacher ki extra baat C₃ ka 3 aur C₄ ka 4 pehle tikau product ke carbon ginte hain, CO₂ ke molecule nahi.
f62 teacher ki extra baat Gehun aur chawal C₃ paudhe hain, makka aur ganna C₄; zyadatar paudhe C₃ hain.
f63 teacher ki extra baat Sirf bahar se dekh kar paudha C₃ hai ya C₄, pakka nahi kaha ja sakta; pehchaan andar bane pehle product se hoti hai (patti ki banawat, 11.8).
pehle poori jhalak11.7-k1
Pichhli film mein humne lumen mein bhare H⁺ dekhe the. Jab woh CF₀ ki nali se stroma mein laut-te hain, toh CF₁ ki mutthi ATP bana deti hai. Aur FNR ke stroma side par baithne se NADPH bhi stroma mein hi banta hai. Matlab roshni wala kaam poora hote hi stroma mein do cheezein taiyaar milti hain: ATP aur NADPH. Yaad rakhein: roshni ka kaam yahin tak hai; roshni ATP aur NADPH banati hai, sugar nahi. Aage hum dekhenge ki stroma mein yeh dono kis kaam mein lagte hain, aur us kaam ka roshni se kya rishta hai. Toh aaj ka sawaal suno. Stroma ka sugar banane wala kaam roshni par kitna tika hai, aur hawa ki CO₂ ka carbon sugar tak pahunchne se pehle sabse pehle kahan rukta hai? Is sawaal ke do hisse hain, aur hum dono ko ek-ek karke kholenge. Pehle ek seedha prayog dekhenge, jo bataayega ki roshni band karne par sugar ke kaam ka kya hota hai. Phir hum Calvin ke saath CO₂ ke carbon ka peechha karenge. Dekhenge ki woh sabse pehle kis molecule mein pakda gaya.
Screen par: Pichhli film ka aakhri drishya: thylakoid membrane mein CF₀ ki nali se H⁺ stroma mein laut-te, CF₁ ki mutthi se ATP ke moti chhootte; stroma side par FNR se NADPH; stroma mein ATP aur NADPH ke moti taiyaar; dabba 'Yaad rakhein: roshni ATP aur NADPH banati hai, sugar nahi'; phir stroma mein hawa se aata CO₂ ka ek carbon aur door ek sugar ka molecule, beech mein do sawaal: 'roshni par kitna tika?' aur 'carbon pehle kahan?'; neeche do khaane: prayog, aur Calvin ke saath carbon ka peechha.
Kitaab se bahar: Opening recap of 11.6.3 aur uska khula sawaal (stroma mein ATP aur NADPH ka kaam, aur us kaam ka roshni se rishta), aaj ke sawaal ke roop mein.
Yaad: 11.6.3-k19, 11.6.3-k18
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.7-k2
Chalo, pehle gin lete hain ki roshni wale kaam se kya-kya nikalta hai. Light reaction se teen cheezein nikalti hain: ATP, NADPH aur O₂. Yaad karo, yeh O₂ paani ke tootne se aati hai. Ab dekho, in teeno ka aage ka safar ek jaisa nahi hai. O₂ chloroplast mein rukti nahi, woh apne aap bahar nikal jaati hai. Chloroplast ke andar O₂ zyada banti hai, aur bahar kam hai. Isliye woh agarbatti ki khushboo ki tarah zyada se kam ki taraf failti hai. Isi ko kehte hain ki O₂ chloroplast se diffuse karke bahar jaati hai. Phir woh patti ke chhote chhedon, yaani stomatal pores, se hawa mein chali jaati hai. Wahi O₂ hum saans mein lete hain. Par ATP aur NADPH bahar nahi jaate; woh stroma mein hi rukte hain. Unka kaam hai un reactions ko chalaana, jo paudhe ka khana banati hain. Ab socho, khana bahut bada shabd hai: roti, daal, phal, sab khana hi kehlaate hain. Isliye theek-theek kahein toh chloroplast mein seedhe sugar banti hai. Paudhe ka baaki khana baad mein isi sugar se banta hai. Aksar hum sochte hain ki paudha O₂ banane ke liye hi photosynthesis karta hai, par aisa nahi hai. Paudhe ki asli kamaai sugar hai. O₂ to paani todne par bacha hua hissa hai, jo bahar chala jaata hai. Haan, yahi bacha hua hissa hum jaise jeevon ke liye saans ka sahaara hai.
Screen par: Chloroplast ka realistic cutaway: thylakoid membranes se teen cheezein nikalti: ATP aur NADPH (moti) aur O₂ (chhote bulbule); O₂ ke bulbule chloroplast ki deewar se bahar failte, zyada se kam ki taraf (kone mein agarbatti ki khushboo); phir patti ke stomatal pore se hawa mein, aur ek bachche ki saans; ATP aur NADPH stroma mein hi rukte, enzymes ke paas; thaali mein roti, daal, phal ('khana'), aur chloroplast mein sugar ka molecule; 'O₂ hi maksad?' par kaata nishaan, sugar par 'kamaai'.
Kitaab se bahar: Diffusion 11.6.3 se (agarbatti); O₂ stomatal pores se hawa mein (shuruaat ka stomatal pore); galat soch: paudha O₂ ke liye photosynthesis karta hai; O₂ paani todne ka bacha hissa (teacher ki taraf se).
Yaad: 11.6.1-k8, 11.6.3-k3, 0-k2
Kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki photosynthesis ka maksad O₂ banana hai; paudhe ke liye asli product sugar hai.
p012-i6 We learnt that the products of light reaction are ATP, NADPH and O₂. Of these O₂ diffuses out of the chloroplast while ATP and NADPH are used to drive the processes leading to the synthesis of food, more accurately, sugars.
samjhana11.7-k3
Ab stroma ke is sugar wale kaam ko uska naam do. Yaad karo, pichhli film mein biosynthetic ka matlab dekha tha: bio yaani jeevan, aur synthesis yaani banana. Sugar bhi ek naya molecule hai, jise jeevit cell apne andar khud banati hai. Isliye photosynthesis ke is sugar banane wale daur ko biosynthetic phase kehte hain. Phase yaani kisi bade kaam ka ek daur, jaise light reaction is kaam ka pehla daur tha. Is daur ko chahiye ATP aur NADPH, aur saath mein CO₂ aur H₂O. CO₂ isme carbon laati hai, aur H₂O yaani paani bhi in reactions mein kharch hota hai. Ab gaur karo, is list mein roshni ka naam hi nahi hai. Yeh daur roshni par seedhe nahi tika; yeh light reaction ke products, ATP aur NADPH, par tika hai. Ab sawaal uthta hai: yeh pakka kaise ho ki ise roshni seedhe nahi chahiye, sirf roshni ke banaye ATP aur NADPH chahiye? Roshni seedhe chahiye ya nahi, yeh pakka karne ka tareeka bahut seedha hai. Roshni mein kaam karti patti par achanak andhera kar do. Andhera hote hi light reactions usi pal ruk jaati hain, yeh hum dekh chuke hain. Aksar hum sochte hain ki tab sugar banna bhi usi pal ruk jaata hoga, par aisa nahi hota. Sugar banna kuch der chalta rehta hai, aur phir ruk jaata hai. Ab phir se roshni do, toh sugar banna phir se shuru ho jaata hai. Kuch der isliye chalta hai, kyunki stroma mein pehle se bane ATP aur NADPH bache hote hain. Jab yeh bache hue ATP aur NADPH kharch ho jaate hain, aur naye bante nahi, toh kaam ruk jaata hai. Roshni lautte hi membranes phir naye ATP aur NADPH banane lagti hain. Tab stroma ka kaam phir chal padta hai. Dekho, is prayog ka har hissa ek alag baat saabit karta hai. Kuch der chalte rehna dikhata hai ki roshni ise seedhe nahi chalati. Warna yeh bhi pal bhar mein ruk jaata. Aur phir ruk jaana dikhata hai ki yeh roshni ke banaye ATP aur NADPH ke bina nahi chal sakta. Bilkul cheeni mill ki tarah. Bijlighar band hote hi floor ki machines bachi hui bijli se thodi der chal kar ruk jaati hain.
Screen par: Usi stroma mein enzymes, label 'biosynthetic phase (bio = jeevan, synthesis = banana, phase = daur)'; zaroorat ki list: ATP, NADPH, CO₂, H₂O, aur khaali jagah jahan 'roshni' nahi likha; phir prayog ki timeline ek patti par: roshni on (sugar ke molecule bante); roshni off (light reactions turant band, sugar thodi der aur bante, ATP aur NADPH ke moti ghatte, phir sab ruk); roshni phir on (naye moti, sugar phir); 'usi pal ruk jaata hai?' par kaata nishaan; kone mein cheeni mill: bijlighar band, floor ki machines thodi der chal kar rukti.
Terms: biosynthetic phase = Photosynthesis ka sugar banane wala daur; bio = jeevan, synthesis = banana, phase = daur.
Kitaab se bahar: Biosynthetic 11.6.3 se, phase 11.5 se; direct aur indirect, aur cheeni mill 11.3 se; andhere mein bache ATP aur NADPH se kaam ka kuch der chalna (teacher ki taraf se); galat soch: andhera hote hi sugar banna usi pal rukta hai; prayog ka har hissa kya saabit karta hai.
Yaad: 11.6.3-k18, 11.5-k1, 11.3-k9, 11.3-k10, 11.3-k8
Kahan tootti hai: Yeh sochna ki andhera hote hi sugar banna usi pal ruk jaata hai; woh bache ATP aur NADPH se kuch der chalta hai.
p012-i7 This is the biosynthetic phase of photosynthesis. This process does not directly depend on the presence of light but is dependent on the products of the light reaction, i.e., ATP and NADPH, besides CO₂ and H₂O. You may wonder how this could be verified; it is simple: immediately after light becomes unavailable, the biosynthetic process continues for some time, and then stops. If then, light is made available, the synthesis starts again.
ruko, socho11.7-k4
Ab ek sawaal par ruk kar socho. Is par aap doston ke saath baith kar charcha bhi kar sakte ho. Yaad karo, is biosynthetic phase ko dark reaction bhi kaha jaata hai, yaani andhere wali reaction. Galat naam ke liye angrezi mein ek shabd hai, misnomer: mis yaani galat, aur nomer yaani naam. Socho, phir aage badhenge: abhi wala prayog dekh kar batao, kya biosynthetic phase ko dark reaction kehna ek misnomer hai? Haan, biosynthetic phase ko dark reaction kehna ek misnomer hai, kyunki is kaam ko andhere ki koi zaroorat nahi. Prayog mein abhi dekha ki andhera hote hi yeh kaam bas thodi der chala, aur phir ruk gaya. Matlab andhera iski madad nahi karta; andhere mein to yeh der tak chal hi nahi sakta. Asal mein yeh din mein, light reactions ke saath-saath chalta hai. Tabhi to taaza ATP aur NADPH milte rehte hain. Yeh naam bas purane zamane se chala aa raha hai, yaani by convention. Naam andhere ka hai, par kaam roshni ke sahaare chalta hai. Isliye exam mein naam ke dhokhe mein mat aana. Yeh kaam andhere ka nahi, roshni ke banaye ATP aur NADPH ka hai.
Screen par: Wahi timeline, andhere wala hissa chamakta; label 'dark reaction'; shabd ke do tukde: mis = galat, nomer = naam; sawaal ka dabba 'kya misnomer hai?' aur chhoti ghadi; phir din ka sooraj, light reactions aur biosynthetic phase saath-saath chalte; 'raat mein hoti hai' par kaata nishaan; chhota label 'by convention: purana naam'.
Terms: misnomer = Galat naam; mis = galat, nomer = naam.
Kitaab se bahar: Misnomer ke hisse (mis + nomer); dark reactions aur by convention 11.3 se; exam ka jaal: dark reaction raat mein hoti hai.
Yaad: 11.3-k10
Jawab: Haan, misnomer hai: is kaam ko andhere ki zaroorat nahi; andhere mein yeh bache ATP aur NADPH khatam hote hi ruk jaata hai; din mein yeh light reactions ke saath chalta hai, kyunki roshni ke banaye ATP aur NADPH par tika hai.
Aam galti: Galat soch: andhere mein thodi der chalna dikhata hai ki yeh andhere wala kaam hai. Galat, kyunki woh sirf bache hue ATP aur NADPH se chalta hai aur jaldi ruk jaata hai.
p012-i8 Can we, hence, say that calling the biosynthetic phase as the dark reaction is a misnomer? Discuss this amongst yourselves.
samjhana11.7-k5
Ab aaj ke sawaal ka doosra hissa: stroma mein ATP aur NADPH ka istemal kaise hota hai? ATP aur NADPH theek kis kadam mein kitne lagte hain, yeh hum aage ginenge. Abhi ke liye bas itna jaan lo ki woh CO₂ ke carbon ko sugar banane mein lagte hain. Isliye pehle CO₂ ke carbon ka peechha karna hoga. Yaad karo, CO₂ paani ke saath milkar sugar banati hai. Pehle humne [CH₂O] dekha tha. Woh ek carbon, do hydrogen aur ek oxygen wala chhota hissa hai, poora molecule nahi. Sugar mein yahi hissa kai baar juda hota hai, isliye sugar ko (CH₂O)ₙ likhte hain. Neeche likha n batata hai ki yeh hissa kitni baar juda hai. Glucose mein yeh chhe baar juda hai, isliye glucose (CH₂O)₆ hai, yaani C₆H₁₂O₆. n teen bhi ho sakta hai aur chhe bhi; zyadatar saadhaaran sugar isi saanche ki hoti hain. Par equation sirf shuruaat aur aakhir dikhati hai: ek taraf CO₂ aur paani, doosri taraf sugar. Isse lagta hai jaise chhe CO₂ ek saath judkar seedhe glucose ban jaate hain. Toh kya sach mein aisa hota hai, ya carbon beech mein kahin rukta hai? Nahi, sach mein aisa nahi hota; CO₂ ka carbon kai padaavon se guzar kar sugar tak pahunchta hai. Isliye vaigyanik jaanna chahte the ki yeh reaction aage kaise badhti hai. Unka asli sawaal tha ki CO₂ jab pehli baar kisi reaction mein jaati hai, toh sabse pehle kaunsa molecule banta hai. Pichhli film mein humne fixing CO₂ dekha tha. Iska matlab hai CO₂ ke carbon ko kisi molecule ka pakka hissa bana dena. Toh CO₂ ke fix hone ke baad sabse pehle bane molecule ko first product kehte hain. Ise ek marathon se samjho, jo aaj ke baaki safar mein hamare saath chalega. Hazaaron dhavak start line se daudte hain, kai modon se guzarte hain, aur aakhir finish line par pahunchte hain. Yahan start line hai CO₂ ka stroma mein aana, raaste ke mod hain beech ke padaav, aur finish line hai sugar. Aur first product woh pehla mod hai, jahan dhavak sabse pehle pahunchte hain. Finish line par kaun pahuncha, yeh sab dekh lete hain. Par pehla mod kaunsa tha, yeh pakadna mushkil hai.
Screen par: Stroma mein CO₂ aur paani se sugar tak ek lamba teer; [CH₂O] ka chhota tukda, phir chhe tukde jud kar (CH₂O)₆ = C₆H₁₂O₆, neeche 'n = kitni baar'; teer ke beech dhundhle padaav aur '?'; 'chhe CO₂ seedhe glucose?' par kaata nishaan; label 'first product = fix hone ke baad pehla molecule'; phir marathon (apni tasveer): start line par CO₂, raaste mein kai mod, finish line par sugar, pehle mod par 'first product?'.
Terms: (CH₂O)ₙ = Sugar ka saancha: [CH₂O] wala hissa n baar juda; glucose mein n = 6.; first product = CO₂ ke fix hone ke baad sabse pehle banne wala molecule.
Kitaab se bahar: [CH₂O] 11.2 se, n ka matlab; fixing CO₂ 11.6.3 se; ATP aur NADPH ki ginti aage (11.7.2); marathon ki misaal, jo baaki heading mein saath chalti hai.
Yaad: 11.2-k9, 11.6.3-k18
p012-i9 Let us now see how the ATP and NADPH are used in the biosynthetic phase. We saw earlier that CO₂ is combined with H₂O to produce (CH₂O)ₙ or sugars. It was of interest to scientists to find out how this reaction proceeded, or rather what was the first product formed when CO₂ is taken into a reaction or fixed.
kahani11.7-k6
Yeh pehla mod dhoondhne ka kaam Melvin Calvin ne kiya, jinki kahani hum shuru mein sun chuke hain. Yaad karo, World War II ke theek baad duniya ne radio-activity ki tabaahi dekhi thi. Tab radioactive isotopes ko achhe kaam mein lagane ki kai koshishein hui. Unme Calvin ka kaam misaal ke laayak, yaani exemplary, tha. Inhi radioactive isotopes ko chhote mein radioisotopes kehte hain. Radio ka matlab hai dikhai na dene wali radiation chhodne wale, aur isotopes ka matlab hai ek hi tatva ke alag-alag wazan wale atom. Calvin ne radioactive carbon, ¹⁴C, ko apna nishaan banaya, jo paudhe mein aam carbon jaisa hi chalta hai. Unhone yeh prayog algae par kiya, isliye ise algal photosynthesis ka adhyayan kehte hain. Algae yaani paani mein rehne wale chhote, saral hare jeev. Ab marathon wali mushkil yaad karo: pehla mod pakda kaise jaaye? Socho, ¹⁴C wali CO₂ algae ko ek ghante tak di jaaye, toh nishaan kahan-kahan milega? Ek ghante mein ¹⁴C ka nishaan har padaav tak fail jaayega, aur sugar tak bhi pahunch jaayega. Bilkul waise hi, jaise race shuru hone ke ek ghante baad photo lo, toh dhavak poore raaste par bikhre dikhenge. Is tarah to pehla mod bheed mein chhup jaata hai. Ruko, pehle khud socho: pehla mod pakadna ho, toh ¹⁴C wali CO₂ kitni der deni chahiye? Aapne socha hoga ki bahut thodi der, aur aapne bilkul sahi socha. Calvin ne ise sirf kuch second ke liye diya. Kuch second mein ¹⁴C ko bas pehle mod tak pahunchne ka samay milta hai, usse aage nahi. Usi pal unhone algae ko garam alcohol mein daala, jisse unki saari reactions turant ruk gayin. Phir paper chromatography se algae ke molecule alag kiye, wahi tareeka jisse humne patti ke rang alag kiye the. Jis dhabbe mein ¹⁴C tha, usne X-ray film par kaala nishaan chhod diya. Kuch second wale prayog mein ¹⁴C sabse pehle ek teen carbon wale organic acid mein mila. Nimbu aur imli ka khattapan acid se aata hai. Carbon wale aise acid ko organic acid kehte hain. Is acid ka poora naam hai 3-phosphoglyceric acid, aur chhote mein ise PGA kehte hain. Yahi CO₂ ke fix hone ke baad pehchaana gaya pehla product tha, yaani hamari marathon ka pehla mod. Calvin yahin nahi ruke. Unhone aage ka poora raasta bhi khoja, jisse CO₂ ka carbon sugar tak pahunchta hai. Isliye is raaste ko unke naam par Calvin cycle kehte hain. Ise cycle kyun kehte hain, yeh aage dekhenge.
Screen par: Calvin ki prayogshala, World War II ke baad; label 'radioisotopes = radioactive isotopes'; ¹⁴C wali CO₂ (chamakte carbon) hare algae ke ghol mein; marathon ki do photo: ek ghante baad (laal topi wale dhavak poore raaste par bikhre) aur kuch second baad (sab pehle mod par); sawaal 'Ruko, pehle khud socho: kitni der?', bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk); phir ghadi par kuch second, algae garam alcohol mein, paper chromatography ka kaagaz, X-ray film par ek kaala dhabba; dhabbe par naam '3-phosphoglyceric acid (PGA)', teen carbon; kone mein nimbu aur imli (organic acid ka khattapan); aage sugar tak jaata raasta, label 'Calvin cycle'.
Terms: radioisotopes = Radioactive isotopes: radiation chhodne wale, ek hi element ke alag wazan wale atom.; ¹⁴C = Carbon ka radioactive isotope (6 proton, 8 neutron, wazan 14); C¹⁴ ka hi doosra likhne ka tareeka.; organic acid = Carbon wala acid; jaise nimbu aur imli ka khattapan dene wale acid.; PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: teen carbon wala organic acid, CO₂ fix hone ka pehla pehchaana product.; Calvin cycle = CO₂ ke carbon se sugar tak ka raasta, Melvin Calvin ke naam par.
Kitaab se bahar: Calvin ki kahani shuruaat se (World War II, isotopes, C¹⁴); Calvin ka kuch second wala prayog: garam alcohol, paper chromatography (11.4 se), X-ray film par dhabba (kitaab mein nahi); ruko, pehle khud socho isi mod par; organic acid ka khattapan (nimbu, imli); cycle kyun, aage 11.7.2 mein.
Yaad: 0-k7, 0-k9, 11.4-k1
p012-i10 Just after world war II, among the several efforts to put radioisotopes to beneficial use, the work of Melvin Calvin is exemplary. The use of radioactive ¹⁴C by him in algal photosynthesis studies led to the discovery that the first CO₂ fixation product was a 3-carbon organic acid. He also contributed to working out the complete biosynthetic pathway; hence it was called Calvin cycle after him. The first product identified was 3-phosphoglyceric acid or in short PGA.
example11.7-k7
Ab ¹⁴C wale ek carbon ka peechha karke dekho, kyunki isse ek zaroori sawaal hal hota hai. Sawaal yeh hai: PGA ke ek molecule mein kitne carbon atom hain? PGA ke ek molecule mein teen carbon atom hain. Naam se samjho: PGA ka G hai glyceric acid, aur glyceric acid ki reedh teen carbon ki hoti hai. Aapne glycerine dekhi hogi, jo sardi mein phate hothon par lagate hain. Uski reedh mein bhi teen carbon hain, aur glyceric acid usi parivaar ka hai. P ka matlab phospho, yaani is par ek phosphate laga hai, aur A ka matlab acid. Ab ek baareek baat yaad rakho. Naam ke shuru ka 3 carbon nahi ginta; woh batata hai ki phosphate teesre carbon par laga hai. Teen carbon ki ginti to glyceric acid ki reedh se aati hai. Ab ¹⁴C par lauto. Kuch second wale prayog mein PGA ke teen carbon mein se nishaan sirf ek carbon par mila. Woh carbon PGA ke acid wale sire par tha, aur yahi carbon seedha CO₂ se aaya tha. Matlab CO₂ ne sirf ek carbon diya, aur PGA ke baaki do carbon kisi aur molecule se aaye.
Screen par: PGA ka molecule paas se: teen carbon ki reedh, ek sire par acid wala hissa, teesre carbon par phosphate; naam ke tukde: P = phospho, G = glyceric, A = acid; kone mein glycerine ki bottle aur uski teen carbon ki reedh; '3 = carbon ki ginti?' par kaata nishaan, teesre carbon par teer 'phosphate yahan'; phir ¹⁴C ka nishaan sirf acid wale sire ke carbon par chamakta, baaki do carbon par '?'.
Kitaab se bahar: PGA ke naam ke hisse; glycerine ki teen carbon ki reedh; exam ki baareek baat: naam ka 3 phosphate ki jagah hai, carbon ki ginti nahi; ¹⁴C sirf acid wale sire ke ek carbon par (teacher ki taraf se), jo agle sawaal ki taraf le jaata hai.
p012-i11 How many carbon atoms does it have?
samjhana11.7-k8
Calvin ne PGA algae mein dhoondha tha; toh kya har paudhe mein CO₂ ka pehla product PGA hi hota hai? Nahi, har paudhe mein pehla product PGA hi nahi hota. Yeh jaanne ke liye vaigyanikon ne bahut tarah ke paudhon par prayog kiye. Socho, duniya mein laakhon tarah ke paudhe hain, thande pahaadon se garam registaan tak. Toh sabka tareeka ek jaisa ho, yeh zaroori nahi. Zyadatar paudhon mein pehla product PGA hi nikla. Par paudhon ka ek aur group mila, jisme pehla product bhi ek organic acid tha, magar usme chaar carbon the. Is acid ka naam oxaloacetic acid hai. Chhote mein ise OAA likhte hain, yaani oxalo, acetic aur acid ke pehle akshar. Iske naam ke hisse jaane-pehchaane hain. Oxalo aata hai oxalic acid se, jo palak mein milta hai, aur acetic yaani sirke ka acid. Yahan ek shabd par gaur karo: stable, yaani tikau, jo banne ke baad turant toot na jaaye. CO₂ fix hone ke baad jo pehla tikau molecule pakda ja sake, use first stable product kehte hain. Gas wali CO₂ ke carbon ka aise kisi molecule mein pakka bandh jaana hi CO₂ fixation hai. Yeh wahi fixing CO₂ hai, jo hum dekh chuke hain. Shuru mein humne assimilation ko khaane ke pach kar shareer ka hissa banne se samjha tha. Waise hi CO₂ ka carbon jab paudhe ke apne molecule ka hissa ban jaata hai, toh ise CO₂ assimilation kehte hain. Tab se photosynthesis mein CO₂ assimilation ko do mukhya prakaar ka maana gaya. Jin paudhon mein CO₂ fixation ka pehla product C₃ acid, yaani PGA, hai, unka raasta C₃ pathway kehlaata hai. Aur jahan pehla tikau product chaar carbon wala C₄ acid, yaani OAA, nikle, wahan ke raaste ko C₄ pathway kehte hain. Ab ek baareek baat: C₃ ka 3 aur C₄ ka 4 pehle tikau product ke carbon ginte hain, CO₂ ke molecule nahi. Ise do marathon ki tarah socho, jinka pehla mod alag hai. Ek mein pehla mod PGA hai, aur doosri mein OAA. Gehun aur chawal jaise paudhe C₃ hain, aur makka aur ganna jaise paudhe C₄. Par sirf bahar se dekh kar pakka nahi kaha ja sakta ki paudha C₃ hai ya C₄. Kyunki asli pehchaan andar bane pehle product se hoti hai, jo aankh se dikhta nahi. In dono groups mein aur bhi jude hue farq hain, jaise patti ki banawat. Woh hum aage C₄ wale hisse mein dekhenge.
Screen par: Duniya bhar ke paudhe ek ke baad ek (gehun, chawal, makka, ganna); zyadatar ke saath PGA (teen moti), ek group ke saath oxaloacetic acid (chaar moti), label OAA; naam ke tukde: oxalo (palak), acetic (sirka); label 'first stable product = pehla tikau molecule'; labels 'CO₂ fixation' aur 'CO₂ assimilation' (kone mein khana pach kar shareer ka hissa); do khaane: C₃ pathway (PGA, 3 carbon) aur C₄ pathway (OAA, 4 carbon); '3 = CO₂ ki ginti?' par kaata nishaan; do marathon ke raaste, alag pehla mod; patti ki banawat par '?', aage.
Terms: oxaloacetic acid = Chaar carbon wala organic acid; C₄ paudhon mein CO₂ fixation ka pehla stable product.; OAA = Oxaloacetic acid ka chhota naam: oxalo, acetic aur acid ke pehle akshar.; first stable product = CO₂ fix hone ke baad bana pehla tikau molecule, jo turant toot na jaaye.; CO₂ fixation = CO₂ ke carbon ka kisi molecule mein pakka bandh jaana.; CO₂ assimilation = CO₂ ke carbon ka paudhe ke apne molecules ka hissa ban jaana.; C₃ pathway = Jin paudhon mein CO₂ fixation ka pehla product teen carbon wala PGA hai, unka raasta.; C₄ acid = Chaar carbon wala acid; yahan OAA.; C₄ pathway = Jin paudhon mein pehla product chaar carbon wala OAA hai, unka raasta.
Kitaab se bahar: Zyadatar paudhe C₃; oxalo aur acetic ke hisse (palak, sirka); assimilation shuruaat se; exam ki baareek baat: C₃ aur C₄ ka ank pehle tikau product ke carbon ginta hai; gehun aur chawal C₃, makka aur ganna C₄ (aage 11.8); bahar se dekh kar pehchaan nahi (exercise 1).
Yaad: 0-k13
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p021-i14
p013-i1 Scientists also tried to know whether all plants have PGA as the first product of CO₂ fixation, or whether any other product was formed in other plants. Experiments conducted over a wide range of plants led to the discovery of another group of plants, where the first stable product of CO₂ fixation was again an organic acid, but one which had 4 carbon atoms in it. This acid was identified to be oxaloacetic acid or OAA. Since then CO₂ assimilation during photosynthesis was said to be of two main types: those plants in which the first product of CO₂ fixation is a C₃ acid (PGA), i.e., the C₃ pathway, and those in which the first product was a C₄ acid (OAA), i.e., the C₄ pathway. These two groups of plants showed other associated characteristics that we will discuss later.
poori kahani dobara11.7-k9
Ab aaj ki saari baatein ek dhaage mein piro lo. Light reaction se O₂, ATP aur NADPH nikle. O₂ bahar diffuse ho gayi, aur ATP aur NADPH stroma mein sugar banane mein lag gaye. Sugar wale is daur ko biosynthetic phase kehte hain. Ise ATP aur NADPH ke saath CO₂ aur H₂O bhi chahiye. Andhera hote hi yeh kuch der chala, phir ruka, aur roshni lautte hi phir chal pada. Isliye dark reaction ek misnomer hai. Phir humne Calvin ke saath carbon ka peechha kiya. Kuch second ki ¹⁴C wali CO₂ ne pehla mod dikha diya. Pehla product nikla teen carbon wala PGA, aur doosre group mein chaar carbon wala OAA. Inhi se C₃ aur C₄ pathway ke naam bane. Toh ab shuru ka sawaal phir se lo. Kya sugar banane ko roshni seedhe chahiye, aur hawa ka carbon pehle kis molecule mein pakda jaata hai? Sugar banane ko roshni seedhe nahi chahiye. Use roshni ke banaye ATP aur NADPH chahiye, isliye yeh kaam roshni par ghuma kar tika hai. Aur hawa ka carbon sabse pehle teen carbon wale acid, PGA, mein pakda jaata hai. C₄ paudhon mein woh pehle chaar carbon wale OAA mein pakda jaata hai. Marathon ki zubaan mein: finish line sugar hai, par pehla mod PGA hai. Par ek baat abhi khuli hai: PGA ke teen carbon mein se CO₂ ne to sirf ek diya tha. CO₂ ka yeh carbon kis molecule se judkar PGA banata hai, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Aaj ki kahani ek patti par: thylakoid se O₂ bahar, ATP aur NADPH stroma mein; label biosynthetic phase; roshni on-off-on ki timeline; 'dark reaction = misnomer'; Calvin ka ¹⁴C, kuch second ki ghadi, pehla mod PGA (teen carbon) aur doosre group mein OAA (chaar carbon); C₃ aur C₄; shuru ka sawaal laut kar, jawab: roshni seedhe nahi, ATP aur NADPH ke zariye; carbon pehle PGA mein; marathon ka pehla mod; aakhir mein PGA ke teen carbon mein se ek par ¹⁴C, baaki do par '?', jo khula rehta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Shuru ka sawaal phir se, jawab ke saath; agli film (11.7.1) ka sawaal khula chhoda: CO₂ ka carbon kis molecule se judkar PGA banata hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
11.7.1 The Primary Acceptor of CO₂ · 6 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 5 min
Maqsad: Ab PGA ki pehchaan se aage badhkar CO₂ lene wala starting molecule khojenge, aur uske carbon count ko product ke carbon count se jodenge.
sawaal11.7.1-k1
Ab PGA ki pehchaan se aage badhkar CO₂ lene wala starting molecule khojenge, aur uske carbon count ko product ke carbon count se jodenge. Yahaan incoming CO₂ jis starting molecule se judta hai, use khojna hai; electron receive karne wala primary acceptor isse alag hai. PGA mein teen carbon atoms hain, jaisa hum abhi pehchaan chuke hain. Scientists ka sawaal bhi yahi tha: ek-carbon CO₂ judne ke baad three-carbon PGA mile, toh starting molecule mein kitne carbons honge? Teen mein se ek ghataakar two-carbon starting molecule sochna natural hai, lekin kya product ke sirf ek molecule ko ginana kaafi hai? Dekho, starting molecule ka carbon count nikaalne ke liye product molecules ki total count bhi chahiye, isliye sirf subtraction se identity decide nahi hogi.
Screen par: Chloroplast ke stromal region par CO₂ ka one-carbon model aur ek three-carbon PGA model; starting molecule ki jagah question mark, saath product molecules ki count ke liye khaali boxes.
Yaad: 11.7-k7
Kya kis jaisa: Ek PGA ke three carbons se starting molecule ka size decide nahi hota; total product molecules bhi ginne honge.
Kahan tootti hai: 3 − 1 = 2 tabhi valid inference hota jab ek starting molecule aur ek CO₂ se sirf ek PGA banta.
p013-i3 Let us now ask ourselves a question that was asked by the scientists who were struggling to understand the ‘dark reaction’. How many carbon atoms would a molecule have which after accepting (fixing) CO₂, would have 3 carbons (of PGA)?
samjhana11.7.1-k2
Experiments ka unexpected answer five-carbon ketose sugar ribulose bisphosphate, ya RuBP, tha. Incoming CO₂ isi starting sugar molecule se judta hai. RuBP mein five-carbon skeleton aur do alag positions par phosphate groups hote hain; bisphosphate naam in do phosphate groups ko batata hai. Ketose sugar ka matlab aisa sugar hai jiski open-chain form mein ketone group hota hai. Ketone group mein ek carbon oxygen se double bond banata hai aur dono taraf carbon se juda hota hai. Five-carbon starting molecule se three-carbon product kaise milega? Dekho, carbon conservation sahi rehti hai; humein reaction ke saare product molecules ka hisaab jodna hoga.
Screen par: Usi stromal picture mein RuBP ka five-carbon molecular model; terminal positions par do phosphate tags aur open-chain structure mein internal C=O highlight.
Terms: ribulose bisphosphate (RuBP) = Five-carbon sugar jiske do alag positions par phosphate groups lage hote hain; incoming CO₂ isse judta hai.; ketose sugar = Sugar jiski open-chain form mein ketone group hota hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Ketone group ki basic bonding aur bisphosphate naam ke parts ki explanation clarity ke liye jodi gayi hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p013-i4 The studies very unexpectedly showed that the acceptor molecule was a 5-carbon ketose sugar – ribulose bisphosphate (RuBP).
kahani11.7.1-k3
Agar tumne two-carbon acceptor guess kiya, toh ghabraao mat; scientists ne bhi pehle yahi possibility sochi thi. Unhone many years ek 2-carbon compound, yaani do carbon atoms wale compound, ko acceptor ke roop mein dhoondhne mein lagaaye. Bahut experiments ke baad five-carbon RuBP ki identity mili. Galat guess itna convincing kyun laga? Aksar log sochte hain ki three-carbon PGA milne par starting acceptor two-carbon hi hoga, par asal mein ek product molecule ka size poore reaction ka carbon total nahi hota. Dekho, saare product molecules ginne par five-carbon RuBP ka hisaab milta hai.
Screen par: Research timeline: three-carbon PGA se two-carbon acceptor ka initial hypothesis; multiple experimental observations ke baad five-carbon RuBP ki confirmed identity.
Terms: 2-carbon compound = Aisa compound jiske ek molecule mein do carbon atoms hon.
p013-i5 Did any of you think of this possibility? Do not worry; the scientists also took a long time and conducted many experiments to reach this conclusion. They also believed that since the first product was a C₃ acid, the primary acceptor would be a 2-carbon compound; they spent many years trying to identify a 2-carbon compound before they discovered the 5-carbon RuBP.
example11.7.1-k4
Ab ek RuBP molecule ke five carbon counters lo, aur incoming CO₂ ka ek carbon counter unke saath jodo. Balanced equations mein atoms conserve hote hain, jaisa hum overall photosynthesis equation mein dekh chuke hain. Total six carbons ko do three-carbon groups mein baanto; har group ek PGA molecule ka carbon skeleton dikhata hai. Carbon ledger hua: 5 + 1 = 3 + 3. Kya six-carbon total ka matlab ek stable six-carbon product hi milega? Dekho, total carbon count six hai, lekin stable products do PGA molecules hain; ek molecule ka size aur saare products ka total alag quantities hain.
Screen par: Pehle wale RuBP model ke five carbon counters mein ek labelled CO₂ carbon judta hai; wahi six counters do PGA groups mein bantte hain, neeche 5 + 1 = 3 + 3.
Kitaab se bahar: Do PGA molecules ka carbon ledger agle heading ki reaction ko samajhne ke liye yahaan briefly khola gaya hai.
Yaad: 11.2-k19
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
analogy11.7.1-k5
Socho, packing table par five toy blocks pehle se rakhe hain aur courier ek aur block laata hai. Worker six blocks ko do parcels mein pack karta hai, har parcel mein three blocks. Tum sirf ek parcel kholkar bolo ki three blocks mein se one naya tha, isliye table par pehle sirf two blocks honge. Yeh conclusion kyun galat hoga? Dekho, doosre parcel ke three blocks bhi same starting collection se aaye hain, isliye poori shipment ginne par starting five blocks ka hisaab milta hai. Yahaan starting five blocks RuBP ke carbons hain, naya block CO₂ ka carbon hai, aur two parcels do PGA molecules hain.
Screen par: Packing table ke five toy blocks, ek arriving block, phir do three-block parcels; unke neeche corresponding RuBP, CO₂ aur two PGA carbon groups.
Kitaab se bahar: Parcel analogy carbon conservation aur product count ka distinction samjhaati hai.
Kya kis jaisa: Five starting blocks = RuBP ke five carbons; incoming block = CO₂ ka carbon; two three-block parcels = do PGA molecules.
Kahan tootti hai: Toy blocks unchanged objects hain; actual reaction mein atoms ke chemical bonds rearrange hote hain.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
ruko, socho11.7.1-k6
Socho, phir aage badhenge: two RuBP molecules two CO₂ molecules accept karein, toh kitne PGA molecules ke liye carbon available hoga? Pehle starting aur incoming carbons ka total nikaalo, phir three-carbon groups gino. Dekho, two RuBP ke ten carbons aur two CO₂ ke two carbons milkar twelve hote hain, isliye four three-carbon PGA molecules ka carbon account banta hai. Isse acceptor aur first stable product ka farq clear rakho: acceptor RuBP five-carbon hai, jabki product PGA three-carbon hai.
Screen par: Do RuBP carbon groups, do incoming CO₂ carbons, aur learner ke liye khaali PGA boxes; phir twelve counters four three-carbon groups mein.
Jawab: 2 × 5 + 2 × 1 = 12 carbons; 12 ÷ 3 = 4 PGA molecules.
Aam galti: Two CO₂ se sirf two PGA ginne par six carbons missing ho jaate hain; har RuBP acceptance se do PGA ka account banta hai.
