(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Ab acceptor aur products ka carbon hisaab mil gaya; Calvin cycle mein dekhenge ki reaction kaise chalti hai aur RuBP dobara kaise taiyaar hota hai.
11.7.2 The Calvin Cycle · 13 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 23 min
Maqsad: Har CO₂ ko pakadne mein ek RuBP kharch ho jaata hai; toh patti din bhar CO₂ kaise pakadti rehti hai, uska RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?
Misaal: dukaan ki poonji: RuBP poonji ki thaili (paanch carbon = paanch rupaye), har CO₂ ka carbon ek naya rupaya, triose phosphate teen rupaye ka hissa, ATP aur NADPH bijli-mazdoori ka kharcha, bahar nikli sugar kamaai
Kitab ki baatein (57): kitab kehti hai 15 · shabd ka matlab 14 · pehle ki zaroori baat 8 · figure ka nishaan 11 · teacher ki extra baat 9
f1 kitab kehti hai Calvin aur unke saathiyon ne poora raasta khoja aur dikhaya ki yeh raasta cycle ki tarah, yaani gol chakkar mein, chalta hai, aur RuBP dobara banta hai (regenerated).
f2 shabd ka matlab: regenerated Regenerated: use hone ke baad phir se banaya gaya; re = phir se, generate = banana.
f3 pehle ki zaroori baat RuBP paanch carbon wala sugar hai jo CO₂ ko pakadta hai, yaani CO₂ acceptor; CO₂ mein ek hi carbon atom hai; RuBP + CO₂ se teen-teen carbon ke do PGA bante hain (5 + 1 = 3 + 3).
f4 pehle ki zaroori baat Cycle: aisa raasta jo ghoom kar apne starting component par return karta hai; ek chakkar ke baad shuruaat wali cheez wapas mil jaati hai.
f5 teacher ki extra baat Melvin Calvin ko paudhon mein CO₂ assimilation ki is khoj ke liye 1961 mein Chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar mila.
f6 kitab kehti hai Ab dekhna hai ki Calvin pathway kaise chalta hai aur sugar kahan banti hai; Calvin pathway sabhi photosynthetic paudhon mein hota hai, chahe unka raasta C₃ ho, C₄ ho ya koi aur.
f7 shabd ka matlab: Calvin pathway Calvin pathway: Calvin cycle ka hi naam; pathway = raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta.
f8 pehle ki zaroori baat C₃ aur C₄ classification pehle stable product ke carbon ginkar: C₃ mein PGA (teen carbon), C₄ mein oxaloacetic acid (chaar carbon).
f9 pehle ki zaroori baat Stroma: chloroplast mein thylakoid ki thailiyon ke chaaron taraf ka gaadha ras; thylakoid mein bane ATP aur NADPH yahin aate hain, aur sugar banane wale enzymes yahin hain.
f10 teacher ki extra baat C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle ek alag tareeke se pakadte hain par aakhir mein Calvin cycle ko hi dete hain; CO₂ se sugar banane ka kaam sirf Calvin cycle karta hai.
f11 kitab kehti hai Samajhne mein aasaani ke liye Calvin cycle ko teen stages mein baanta jaata hai: carboxylation, reduction aur regeneration.
f12 kitab kehti hai Kadam 1, carboxylation: CO₂ ko ek stable organic intermediate mein fix karna; yeh Calvin cycle ka sabse crucial kadam hai, jahan CO₂ se RuBP ka carboxylation hota hai.
f13 kitab kehti hai Yeh reaction enzyme RuBP carboxylase catalyse karta hai, aur isse 3-PGA ke do molecule bante hain.
f14 kitab kehti hai Is enzyme mein oxygenation activity bhi hai, isliye ise RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase ya RuBisCO kehna zyada sahi hai.
f15 shabd ka matlab: Carboxylation Carboxylation: molecule par carboxyl group (COOH) jodna, jo CO₂ se banta hai; yahan RuBP par CO₂ judna.
f16 shabd ka matlab: stable organic intermediate Stable organic intermediate: stable = tikau, turant na tootne wala; organic = carbon wala molecule; intermediate = beech ka padaav, aakhri product nahi.
f17 shabd ka matlab: 3-PGA 3-PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: teen carbon ka glyceric acid, jiske teesre carbon par phosphate laga hai; COOH group ise acid banata hai.
f18 shabd ka matlab: catalysed Catalysed: enzyme ne reaction tez karaya, aur khud badla nahi.
f19 shabd ka matlab: oxygenation activity Oxygenation activity: CO₂ ki jagah O₂ ko RuBP par jodne ki kaabiliyat.
f20 shabd ka matlab: RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase (RuBisCO): RuBP par carboxylation bhi aur oxygenation bhi karne wala enzyme; -ase = enzyme.
f21 teacher ki extra baat RuBP aur CO₂ pehle chhe carbon ka ek molecule banate hain jo turant do 3-PGA mein toot jaata hai; isliye pehla stable molecule 3-PGA hai.
f22 teacher ki extra baat RuBisCO dharti par sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein hai.
f23 pehle ki zaroori baat Enzyme: protein jo reaction tez karta hai aur khud nahi badalta; naam aksar -ase par khatam hota hai.
f24 kitab kehti hai Kadam 2, reduction: reactions ki series jo glucose banne ki taraf le jaati hai; per CO₂ molecule fixed 2 ATP phosphorylation ke liye aur 2 NADPH reduction ke liye lagte hain.
f25 kitab kehti hai Six CO₂ ka fixation aur cycle ke 6 turns se ek glucose molecule banta hai.
f26 shabd ka matlab: per CO₂ molecule fixed Per CO₂ molecule fixed: har ek pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se.
f27 shabd ka matlab: turn Turn: cycle ka ek poora ghumaav, jisme ek CO₂ fix hota hai; turns = aise kai ghumaav.
f28 pehle ki zaroori baat Phosphorylation = phosphate jodna (ATP apna phosphate deta hai); reduction = electron aur hydrogen milna (NADPH deta hai).
f29 teacher ki extra baat Reduction mein 3-PGA (acid) ATP se phosphate aur NADPH se electron lekar triose phosphate (teen carbon ki sugar) banta hai; tri = teen, ose = sugar.
f30 pehle ki zaroori baat Glucose mein chhe carbon hote hain; CO₂ mein ek.
f31 kitab kehti hai Kadam 3, regeneration: CO₂ acceptor RuBP ka dobara banna crucial hai taaki cycle uninterrupted chale; RuBP banane ki phosphorylation mein ek ATP lagta hai.
f32 shabd ka matlab: CO₂ acceptor CO₂ acceptor: CO₂ ko lene wala molecule (accept = lena), yahan RuBP; light reaction ke electron lene wale primary acceptor se alag.
f33 shabd ka matlab: uninterrupted Uninterrupted: bina ruke, lagaataar.
f34 teacher ki extra baat Paanch triose phosphate (15 carbon) jod-tod se teen RuBP (15 carbon) bante hain; RuBP ka doosra phosphate ATP jodta hai, isliye RuBP mein do phosphate (bis).
f35 teacher ki extra baat Teen CO₂ par: 3 RuBP (15 C) + 3 CO₂ (3 C) = 18 C, phir 6 3-PGA, phir 6 triose phosphate; 5 wapas (15 C se 3 RuBP), 1 bahar (3 C); chhe CO₂ par 2 bahar = 6 C = ek glucose.
f36 kitab kehti hai Har CO₂ jo Calvin cycle mein aata hai, uske liye 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH lagte hain.
f37 kitab kehti hai ATP aur NADPH ki sankhya ka yeh farq poora karne ke liye shayad cyclic phosphorylation hota hai.
f38 shabd ka matlab: cyclic phosphorylation Cyclic phosphorylation: cyclic photophosphorylation, jisme electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi.
f39 pehle ki zaroori baat Non-cyclic raasta ATP aur NADPH dono banata hai; cyclic sirf ATP.
f40 kitab kehti hai Ek glucose molecule banane ke liye cycle ke 6 turns chahiye.
f41 kitab kehti hai Ek glucose ke liye kitne ATP aur NADPH lagenge, yeh khud nikaalna hai: chhe turns × 3 ATP = 18 ATP, chhe × 2 NADPH = 12 NADPH.
f42 kitab kehti hai Calvin cycle mein kya andar jaata hai aur kya bahar aata hai, yeh dekhna madad karta hai: In: six CO₂, 18 ATP, 12 NADPH; Out: one glucose, 18 ADP, 12 NADP.
f43 kitab kehti hai Figure 11.8: Calvin cycle teen stages mein: (1) carboxylation, CO₂ ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate se judta hai; (2) reduction, photochemically made ATP aur NADPH ke kharche par carbohydrate banta hai; (3) regeneration, CO₂ acceptor ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate phir banta hai taaki cycle chalta rahe.
f44 shabd ka matlab: photochemically made Photochemically made: roshni se chalne wali reactions mein bana; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
f45 figure ka nishaan: Atmosphere Upar Atmosphere: CO₂ hawa se aata hai.
f46 figure ka nishaan: CO₂ + H₂O Andar aate teer par CO₂ + H₂O, jo carboxylation mein jaate hain.
f47 figure ka nishaan: Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate RuBP ka poora naam; pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate, bis = do.
f48 figure ka nishaan: Carboxylation 1 Carboxylation ka dabba, pehla stage.
f49 figure ka nishaan: 3-phosphoglycerate Carboxylation ke baad bana compound; 3-PGA ka doosra naam.
f50 figure ka nishaan: Reduction 2 Reduction ka dabba, doosra stage.
f51 figure ka nishaan: ATP + NADPH Reduction mein andar aate hain.
f52 figure ka nishaan: ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺ Reduction se bahar jaate hain: kharch hue ATP aur NADPH ke bache roop.
f53 figure ka nishaan: Triose phosphate Reduction ke baad bana; yahan se raasta do mein bantta hai.
f54 figure ka nishaan: Sucrose, starch Triose phosphate se neeche ka teer: sucrose aur starch.
f55 figure ka nishaan: Regeneration 3 Regeneration ka dabba; ATP andar, ADP bahar, aur raasta wapas RuBP par.
f56 teacher ki extra baat Sucrose ganne aur cheeni wali sugar hai jo paudhe mein ek jagah se doosri jagah jaati hai; starch jama ki gayi sugar hai, jaise aaloo, chawal, gehun mein.
f57 teacher ki extra baat Carboxylation mein ATP ya NADPH nahi lagta; RuBP mein regeneration wale ATP ki energy pehle se hoti hai, isliye CO₂ ka judna apne aap aage badhne wali reaction hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.7.2-k1
Pichhli baar humne woh molecule pehchaana jo CO₂ ko pakadta hai: paanch carbon wala sugar, RuBP. RuBP ke paanch carbon mein CO₂ ka ek carbon juda, kul chhe hue. Inse teen-teen carbon ke do PGA bane. Yaad rakhein: CO₂ pakadte hi woh RuBP, RuBP nahi rehta; woh toot kar PGA ban jaata hai. Aage hum dekhenge ki yahi ek baat ek badi paheli khadi kar deti hai. Ab socho: har CO₂ ko pakadne mein ek RuBP kharch ho jaata hai. Aur patti din bhar laakhon CO₂ pakadti rehti hai. Toh patti CO₂ kaise pakadti rehti hai, uska RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota? RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota, isi sawaal ka jawab Melvin Calvin aur unke saathiyon ne dhoondha. Yeh wahi Calvin hain jinke radioactive carbon wale prayog se humne pehla product PGA pehchaana tha. Ab unhone kadam-dar-kadam poora raasta khoj nikala. Is kaam ke liye Calvin ko 1961 mein chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar mila. Dekho, unhone dikhaya ki yeh raasta seedhi line nahi, ek gol chakkar hai: jahan se shuru hota hai, ghoom kar wahin lautta hai. Isliye ise cycle kehte hain: chakkar ki shuruaat mein RuBP hai, aur chakkar ke aakhir mein RuBP phir se ban jaata hai. Use hone ke baad phir se banna, isi ko regenerated hona kehte hain: re yaani phir se, generate yaani banana. Toh RuBP kharch to hota hai, par chakkar use dobara bana deta hai. Isliye woh kabhi khatam nahi hota. Bilkul ek achhi dukaan ki tarah: dukaandar roz apni poonji lagata hai, par shaam ko poonji wapas thaili mein rakh leta hai. Ghar sirf uski kamaai jaati hai. Poonji roz lagti hai, phir bhi khatam nahi hoti, kyunki kamaai mein se hi wapas bhar jaati hai. Toh samjho, is chakkar mein RuBP wahi poonji hai, aur jo sugar bahar nikalti hai, woh kamaai. Par RuBP dobara banta kis cheez se hai, aur is kaam mein kharcha kitna lagta hai? RuBP dobara kaise banta hai aur kharcha kitna lagta hai, yeh hum chakkar ke kadam khol kar dekhenge.
Screen par: Pichhla milan: paanch moti ki RuBP maala mein ek CO₂ moti judta hai, chhe moti teen-teen ke do PGA mein bantte hain, aur RuBP ki maala wahan nahi bachti; neeche 'Yaad rakhein: CO₂ pakadte hi RuBP toot kar PGA'. Phir ek hari patti par din bhar aate CO₂ ke teer, aur sawaal 'RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?'. Melvin Calvin ka naam aur '1961 Nobel'. Ek gol chakkar ka teer jo RuBP se chalkar wapas RuBP par lautta hai, us par 'regenerated'. Phir dukaan: dukaandar ki thaili se poonji nikalti, shaam ko wapas thaili mein, aur alag se 'kamaai' ghar jaati; neeche jod: RuBP = poonji, sugar = kamaai.
Terms: regenerated = Use hone ke baad phir se banaya gaya; re = phir se, generate = banana.
Kitaab se bahar: Melvin Calvin ko 1961 mein chemistry ka Nobel puraskaar (paudhon mein CO₂ assimilation ki khoj); dukaan ki poonji ki misaal.
Yaad: 11.7.1-k2, 11.7.1-k4, 11.7-k6
Kahan tootti hai: Dukaan ki poonji sirf picture hai: RuBP ke carbon sikkon ki tarah jyon ke tyon nahi lautte; woh nayi molecules mein jod-tod hokar RuBP banate hain.
p013-i7 Calvin and his co-workers then worked out the whole pathway and showed that the pathway operated in a cyclic manner; the RuBP was regenerated.
samjhana11.7.2-k2
Yeh chakkar chalta kahan hai? Yeh chakkar chalta hai chloroplast ke stroma mein, yaani thylakoid ki thailiyon ke chaaron taraf bhare gaadhe ras mein. Yaad karo, thylakoid mein roshni se ATP aur NADPH bante hain, aur dono stroma mein hi chhode jaate hain. Isliye sugar bhi yahin stroma mein banti hai: CO₂, ATP aur NADPH, teeno ek hi jagah milte hain. Ab C₃ aur C₄ ko yaad karo: yeh naam pehle stable product ke carbon ginkar diye gaye the. C₃ paudhon mein pehla product teen carbon wala PGA hai, aur C₄ paudhon mein chaar carbon wala oxaloacetic acid. Aksar log sochte hain ki Calvin cycle sirf C₃ paudhon mein chalta hai, kyunki PGA wahi banate hain. Par aisa nahi hai. Dekho, har photosynthesis karne wale paudhe mein yeh chakkar chalta hai, chahe woh C₃ ho, C₄ ho, ya koi aur raasta rakhta ho. Kyun? Kyunki CO₂ ke carbon se sugar banane ka kaam sirf yahi chakkar karta hai. C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle ek alag tareeke se pakadte hain. Par aakhir mein use isi chakkar ko saunp dete hain; yeh hum aage C₄ wale hisse mein dekhenge. Isliye is raaste ko Calvin pathway bhi kehte hain: pathway yaani raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta. Ab yeh chakkar ek saath kai kaam karta hai, isliye samajhne mein aasaani ke liye ise teen kadamon mein baanta jaata hai. Yaad rakho: pehla kadam carboxylation hai, doosra reduction, aur teesra regeneration. Seedhe shabdon mein: pehle kadam mein CO₂ andar aata hai, doosre mein usse sugar banti hai, aur teesre mein RuBP phir banta hai. Dukaan ki zubaan mein: pehle naya paisa aata hai, phir kharcha karke maal taiyaar hota hai. Aakhir mein poonji wapas thaili mein jaati hai.
Screen par: Chloroplast ka cutaway: hari thylakoid thailiyon ke dher, unke chaaron taraf stroma ka gaadha ras roshan; thylakoid se ATP aur NADPH ke chhote moti stroma mein aate, saath bahar se CO₂ andar aata. Phir do paudhe: C₃ (PGA, teen moti) aur C₄ (oxaloacetic acid, chaar moti); 'sirf C₃ mein?' par kaata hua nishaan, aur dono paudhon se teer ek hi gol chakkar 'Calvin pathway' tak. Phir gol chakkar teen hisson mein: 1 carboxylation (CO₂ andar), 2 reduction (sugar banti), 3 regeneration (RuBP wapas); kone mein dukaan ke teen chhote chitra: naya paisa, maal taiyaar, poonji thaili mein.
Terms: Calvin pathway = Calvin cycle ka hi doosra naam; pathway = raasta, Calvin ka khoja hua raasta, jo har photosynthetic paudhe mein chalta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko pehle alag tareeke se pakad kar aakhir mein Calvin cycle ko hi dete hain (aage 11.8).
Yaad: 11.6.3-k13, 11.7-k8
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p013-i8 Let us now see how the Calvin pathway operates and where the sugar is synthesised. Let us at the outset understand very clearly that the Calvin pathway occurs in all photosynthetic plants; it does not matter whether they have C₃ or C₄ (or any other) pathways (Figure 11.8).
p013-i9 For ease of understanding, the Calvin cycle can be described under three stages: carboxylation, reduction and regeneration.
samjhana11.7.2-k3
Pehla kadam wahi milan hai jo humne pichhli baar gina tha: RuBP aur CO₂ ka judna. CO₂ ek gas hai jo hawa mein udti phirti hai. Is kadam mein uska carbon ek molecule mein pakka bandh jaata hai. Gas ke carbon ko aise pakka baandh dena hi CO₂ fixation hai, jise hum pehle mil chuke hain. Ab is kadam ka naam samjho: carboxyl ek chhota group hai, COOH, jo CO₂ se banta hai. Molecule par CO₂ jodkar yeh carboxyl group banana, isi ko carboxylation kehte hain. Toh carboxylation mein CO₂ ka carbon ek stable organic intermediate mein fix ho jaata hai. Stable yaani tikau, jo turant na toote; organic yaani carbon wala; intermediate yaani beech ka padaav, aakhri manzil nahi. Ise tikau kyun kaha? Ise tikau isliye kaha, kyunki RuBP aur CO₂ milkar pehle chhe carbon ka ek molecule banate hain. Woh molecule pal bhar bhi nahi tikta, aur turant teen-teen carbon ke do hisson mein toot jaata hai. Yahi tikau hissa 3-PGA hai, aur ek RuBP aur ek CO₂ se iske do molecule bante hain. Ab 3-PGA ka naam todo: poora naam 3-phosphoglyceric acid hai, aur glyceric acid teen carbon ka ek acid hai. Phospho yaani us par ek phosphate laga hai, aur shuru ka 3 batata hai ki phosphate teesre carbon par baitha hai. Aur ise acid isliye kehte hain, kyunki CO₂ se aaya COOH group hi ise acid banata hai. Yeh poore chakkar ka sabse zaroori, yaani most crucial, kadam hai. Kyunki yahin hawa ka carbon pehli baar jeevit duniya mein aata hai. Par yeh milan apne aap bahut dheere hota hai, isliye ek enzyme ise tez karta hai. Enzyme ek protein hai jo reaction ko tez karata hai aur khud nahi badalta; aise naam aksar -ase par khatam hote hain. Enzyme ke is tez karaane ko kehte hain ki reaction catalysed hua; yahan reaction RuBP carboxylase se catalysed hota hai. Naam hi kaam batata hai: RuBP par carboxylation karne wala, aur aakhir ka -ase yaani enzyme. Par iska poora naam isse bhi lamba hai; aisa kyun? Poora naam isliye lamba hai, kyunki yahi enzyme RuBP par CO₂ ki jagah O₂ bhi jod sakta hai. O₂ jodne ki is kaabiliyat ko oxygenation activity kehte hain. Isliye ise RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase kehna zyada sahi hai, aur chhote mein RuBisCO. Dekho, RuBisCO dharti par sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein hai; har hari patti mein yeh bhara hota hai. O₂ judne se kya nuksaan hota hai, yeh aage photorespiration mein dekhenge. Dukaan mein yeh naya paisa aane ka kadam hai: har CO₂ ek naya rupaya hai jo poonji ke saath judta hai.
Screen par: Stroma mein paas se: paanch moti wala RuBP aur ek moti wala CO₂ ek-doosre ki taraf; beech mein RuBisCO ka bada protein. Mile to chhe moti ka molecule pal bhar chamakta hai aur turant teen-teen moti ke do 3-PGA mein toot jaata hai; ek sire par 'COOH' aur teesre moti par phosphate ka tag. Naam ke hisse alag-alag chamakte: carboxyl + ation; stable, organic, intermediate; 3 + phospho + glyceric acid. Enzyme par label 'RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase = RuBisCO', aur ek chhota O₂ bhi enzyme ki taraf aata dikhe. Kone mein dukaan ki thaili mein naya rupaya judta hua.
Terms: Carboxylation = Molecule par CO₂ se bana carboxyl group (COOH) jodna; yahan RuBP par CO₂ ka judna, Calvin cycle ka pehla kadam.; stable organic intermediate = Tikau (turant na tootne wala), carbon wala, beech ka molecule jo aakhri product nahi; yahan 3-PGA.; 3-PGA = 3-phosphoglyceric acid: teen carbon ka acid jiske teesre carbon par phosphate hai; figure mein 3-phosphoglycerate.; catalysed = Enzyme dwara tez karayi gayi (reaction), jisme enzyme khud nahi badalta.; oxygenation activity = RuBP par CO₂ ki jagah O₂ jodne ki enzyme ki kaabiliyat.; RuBisCO = RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase: RuBP par carboxylation bhi aur oxygenation bhi karne wala enzyme.
Kitaab se bahar: RuBP aur CO₂ ka chhe carbon wala pal bhar ka molecule jo turant do 3-PGA mein tootta hai; carboxyl = COOH; RuBisCO dharti ka sabse zyada paya jaane wala protein.
Yaad: 11.7.1-k2, 11.7-k8
Exercise ke liye: p022-i1
p013-i10 1. Carboxylation – Carboxylation is the fixation of CO₂ into a stable organic intermediate. Carboxylation is the most crucial step of the Calvin cycle where CO₂ is utilised for the carboxylation of RuBP. This reaction is catalysed by the enzyme RuBP carboxylase which results in the formation of two molecules of 3-PGA. Since this enzyme also has an oxygenation activity it would be more correct to call it RuBP carboxylase-oxygenase or RuBisCO.
samjhana11.7.2-k4
Ab 3-PGA ko dhyan se dekho: carbon to isme aa gaya, par yeh abhi sugar nahi, ek acid hai. Acid ko sugar banane ke liye do cheezein chahiye: thodi energy, aur electron. Yaad karo, phosphate jodne ko phosphorylation kehte hain, aur ATP apna ek phosphate dekar energy deta hai. Aur electron, saath mein hydrogen, milne ko reduction kehte hain; NADPH yahi electron aur hydrogen dene wala molecule hai. Pehle har 3-PGA par ATP ek aur phosphate jod deta hai, aur molecule mein energy bhar jaati hai. Phir NADPH use electron aur hydrogen deta hai, aur acid wala sira sugar wala sira ban jaata hai. Ab aap samajh gaye honge ki is kadam ka naam reduction kyun hai: yahan electron milne se hi badlaav hota hai. Jo banta hai, use triose phosphate kehte hain: tri yaani teen, ose yaani sugar, yaani teen carbon ki sugar jis par phosphate laga hai. Yahi is chakkar ki asli sugar hai. Ise banane mein kai reactions ek ke baad ek chalti hain, aur yahi series aage glucose banne tak le jaati hai. Ab kharcha gino: ek CO₂ jodne se do 3-PGA bane the, aur har 3-PGA ko ek ATP aur ek NADPH chahiye. Toh har pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se is kadam mein 2 ATP aur 2 NADPH lagte hain. Isi 'har pakde gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se' ko per CO₂ molecule fixed kehte hain. Ab ek sawaal: CO₂ mein sirf ek carbon hai, aur glucose mein chhe. Toh ek glucose ke liye kitne CO₂ pakadne padenge? Ek glucose ke liye chhe CO₂ pakadne padenge, kyunki har CO₂ sirf ek carbon laata hai. Aur chakkar ke ek poore ghumaav mein ek hi CO₂ pakda jaata hai; is ek ghumaav ko turn kehte hain. Isliye yaad rakho: chhe CO₂ ka fixation, yaani cycle ke 6 turns, milkar ek glucose molecule banate hain. Dukaan ki tarah socho: maal taiyaar karne mein bijli aur mazdoori lagti hai. Chakkar mein yeh kharcha ATP aur NADPH se chukaya jaata hai.
Screen par: Ek 3-PGA (teen moti, ek sire par COOH, teesre moti par phosphate): pehle ATP aata hai aur ek aur phosphate tag judta hai, ATP se ADP bachta hai; phir NADPH electron aur H deta hai, COOH wala sira sugar ke sire mein badalta hai, NADPH se NADP⁺ bachta hai; ab naam 'triose phosphate' (tri = 3, ose = sugar). Neeche ginti: 1 CO₂ → 2 3-PGA → 2 ATP + 2 NADPH. Phir glucose ke chhe moti, chhe alag CO₂ ke ek-ek moti, aur chakkar ke 6 ghumaav ke gol nishaan. Kone mein dukaan ka bijli-mazdoori ka bill.
Terms: triose phosphate = Teen carbon ki sugar jis par phosphate laga hai (tri = teen, ose = sugar); reduction ka product.; per CO₂ molecule fixed = Har ek pakde (fix kiye) gaye CO₂ ke hisaab se.; turn = Cycle ka ek poora ghumaav, jisme ek CO₂ fix hota hai; kai ghumaav = turns.
Kitaab se bahar: 3-PGA par pehle ATP se phosphate judta hai, phir NADPH se electron aur hydrogen; acid wala sira sugar wala sira banta hai (triose phosphate).
Yaad: 11.6.2-k2, 11.6.3-k13
p014-i16 2. Reduction – These are a series of reactions that lead to the formation of glucose. The steps involve utilisation of 2 molecules of ATP for phosphorylation and two of NADPH for reduction per CO₂ molecule fixed. The fixation of six molecules of CO₂ and 6 turns of the cycle are required for the formation of one molecule of glucose from the pathway.
samjhana11.7.2-k5
Ab lautte hain us sawaal par jo shuruaat mein chhoda tha: RuBP dobara banta kis cheez se hai? RuBP kis cheez se banega, iska ek clue hai: chakkar mein bahar se sirf CO₂ aata hai, aur reduction ke baad triose phosphate bante hain. Ruko, pehle khud socho: RuBP ke paanch carbon aakhir aayenge kahan se? Aapne socha hoga ki triose phosphate se hi; bilkul sahi. Dekho, reduction ke baad bane triose phosphate mein se zyadatar wapas chakkar mein lautte hain. Unke carbon jod-tod kar phir se paanch carbon wale RuBP ban jaate hain. Isliye is kadam ka naam regeneration hai: wahi regenerated wala shabd, yaani dobara banana. Jo molecule CO₂ ko pakadta hai, use CO₂ acceptor kehte hain: accept yaani lena, acceptor yaani lene wala. Yaad karo, light reaction mein primary acceptor electron leta tha; yeh CO₂ acceptor CO₂ leta hai, aur yahan woh RuBP hai. Is kadam ka kharcha bhi hum gin lete hain: har CO₂ par ek ATP. Kyunki RuBP mein do phosphate hote hain, bis yaani do, aur doosra phosphate ATP hi jodta hai; yeh bhi phosphorylation hai. Par yeh kadam itna zaroori kyun hai? Yeh kadam zaroori isliye hai, kyunki RuBP dobara na bane to agla CO₂ pakadne wala koi nahi bachega. Tab ATP aur NADPH bhare pade rahenge, phir bhi chakkar ruk jaayega. RuBP banta rahe to chakkar bina ruke chalta rehta hai; bina ruke chalne ko uninterrupted kehte hain. Yahi dukaan ka niyam hai: jo dukaandar poonji kha jaaye, uski dukaan agle din nahi khulti.
Screen par: Chakkar ka teesra hissa: sawaal 'RuBP ke paanch carbon kahan se?' ke saath bada '?' aur chhoti ghadi (teen second ruk). Phir reduction ke baad bane chhe triose phosphate (teen-teen moti); paanch wapas chakkar ki taraf mudte hain, unke moti khulkar teen paanch-paanch moti ki RuBP maala ban jaate hain; har maala ke doosre sire par ATP se phosphate tag judta, ATP → ADP. Label 'CO₂ acceptor = RuBP', aur alag chhote dabbe mein light reaction ka 'primary acceptor: electron'. Phir RuBP na bane to aate CO₂ ke teer khaali laut jaate aur chakkar ka teer ruk jaata; RuBP bane to teer lagaataar ghoomta. Kone mein dukaan ka band shutter.
Terms: CO₂ acceptor = CO₂ ko lene (accept karne) wala molecule; Calvin cycle mein RuBP. Light reaction ke electron lene wale primary acceptor se alag.; uninterrupted = Bina ruke, lagaataar chalne wala.
Kitaab se bahar: Paanch triose phosphate ke 15 carbon jod-tod se teen RuBP ke 15 carbon bante hain; RuBP ka doosra phosphate ATP jodta hai (bis = do).
Yaad: 11.5-k5, 11.7.1-k2
p014-i17 3. Regeneration – Regeneration of the CO₂ acceptor molecule RuBP is crucial if the cycle is to continue uninterrupted. The regeneration steps require one ATP for phosphorylation to form RuBP.
example11.7.2-k6
Chalo, ab gin kar dekhte hain ki chakkar sach mein poora ghoom kar band hota hai ya nahi. Hisaab aasaan rahe, isliye teen CO₂ lekar chalte hain. Socho, teen RuBP hain, har ek mein paanch carbon: kul pandrah carbon. Teen CO₂ aaye, har ek mein ek carbon, toh ab kul athaarah carbon ho gaye. Carboxylation ke baad yeh athaarah carbon chhe 3-PGA mein bante hain, har ek mein teen. Reduction ke baad yahi chhe triose phosphate ban jaate hain; carbon abhi bhi athaarah hi hain. Ab batao, inme se kitne triose phosphate wapas jaayenge, aur kitne bahar? Wapas paanch triose phosphate jaayenge, aur bahar sirf ek. Paanch triose phosphate ke pandrah carbon jod-tod kar teen RuBP bana dete hain, yaani jitne RuBP the, utne hi wapas. Bacha ek triose phosphate, teen carbon wala, is chakkar ki asli kamaai hai; yahi sugar banne ke liye bahar nikalta hai. Dhyan do, yeh teen carbon wahi hain jo teen CO₂ lekar aaye the. Chakkar ne jitna carbon pakda, utna hi bahar diya. Chhe CO₂ par yahi hisaab do baar chalta hai: do triose phosphate bahar, yaani chhe carbon, ek glucose jitne. Dukaan mein bhi yahi hota: pandrah rupaye ki poonji, teen naye rupaye, kul athaarah; pandrah wapas thaili mein, aur teen rupaye kamaai.
Screen par: Moti gin kar: teen RuBP maala (5 + 5 + 5 = 15 moti) aur teen CO₂ moti (+3), kul 18. Phir chhe 3-PGA (6 × 3) aur chhe triose phosphate (6 × 3 = 18). Paanch triose phosphate ke 15 moti mud kar teen RuBP maala banate; ek triose phosphate (3 moti) bahar 'kamaai' ki taraf. Neeche ginti: 15 + 3 = 18 → 15 wapas + 3 bahar. Phir chhe CO₂ wala chitra: do triose phosphate bahar = 6 moti = ek glucose. Saath mein dukaan ke sikke: ₹15 poonji + ₹3 = ₹18; ₹15 thaili mein, ₹3 kamaai.
Kitaab se bahar: Teen CO₂ par carbon ki ginti: 15 + 3 = 18 carbon, chhe triose phosphate, paanch se teen RuBP, ek bahar; chhe CO₂ par do bahar = ek glucose.
Yaad: 11.7.1-k4
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
figure11.7.2-k7
Ab Figure 11.8 dekho; isme yahi poora chakkar ek gol raaste ki tarah bana hai. Upar dekho, likha hai Atmosphere, yaani hawa; wahan se teer isliye aata hai, kyunki CO₂ hawa se hi patti mein aata hai. Us teer par likha hai CO₂ + H₂O, jo pehle dabbe, 1 Carboxylation, mein jaate hain. Upar ke dabbe mein likha hai Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate; yeh RuBP ka poora naam hai. Naam mein 1,5 isliye hai, kyunki paanch carbon mein se pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate lage hain; bis yaani do phosphate. Carboxylation se teer ghadi ki sui ki disha mein 3-phosphoglycerate tak jaata hai; yeh wahi 3-PGA hai. Ab ungli aage le chalo: dabba hai 2 Reduction, aur isme andar aate teer par likha hai ATP + NADPH. Bahar jaate teer par likha hai ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺; kharch hone ke baad ATP aur NADPH ke yahi bache roop hain. Reduction ke baad aata hai Triose phosphate, aur yahan se raasta do mein bant jaata hai. Neeche ki taraf ka teer kehta hai Sucrose, starch; kamaai wali sugar isi taraf jaati hai. Doosra raasta 3 Regeneration se hokar lautta hai; yahan ATP andar jaata hai aur ADP bahar aata hai. Aur teer phir se Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate par pahunch kar chakkar band kar deta hai. Figure ke neeche likha hai ki reduction mein carbohydrate, photochemically made ATP aur NADPH ke kharche par banta hai. Photochemically made yaani roshni wali reactions mein bane hue: photo yaani roshni, chemical yaani rasayanik. Isliye yeh chakkar roshni ke bina zyada der nahi chal sakta; iska kharcha roshni ki kamaai se hi chalta hai.
Screen par: Figure 11.8 ka saaf, bada chitra; ungli ka nishaan har label par baari-baari: upar Atmosphere aur CO₂ + H₂O ka teer → 1 Carboxylation ke paas Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate (pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate chamakte) → ghadi ki disha mein 3-phosphoglycerate → 2 Reduction (andar ATP + NADPH, bahar ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺) → Triose phosphate → neeche Sucrose, starch, aur doosra teer 3 Regeneration (ATP andar, ADP bahar) → wapas Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate. Aakhir mein figure ke neeche ki line 'photochemically made ATP and NADPH' roshan, saath thylakoid mein roshni se ATP aur NADPH bante dikhane wala chhota chitra.
Terms: photochemically made = Roshni se chalne wali reactions (light reaction) mein bana hua; photo = roshni, chemical = rasayanik.
Kitaab se bahar: Ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate mein 1,5 = pehle aur paanchve carbon par phosphate.
p014-i1 Calvin cycle: atmospheric CO₂ + H₂O enters stage 1, Carboxylation, with ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate. The clockwise path proceeds to 3-phosphoglycerate, then stage 2, Reduction, then triose phosphate. ATP + NADPH enter reduction; ADP + Pᵢ + NADP⁺ leave it. A downward branch from triose phosphate leads to sucrose, starch. The continuing cycle passes through stage 3, Regeneration, where ATP enters and ADP leaves, and returns to ribulose-1,5-bisphosphate.
asli duniya11.7.2-k8
Yeh kamaai wali sugar aapko roz ki zindagi mein milti hai. Patti triose phosphate se sucrose banati hai, jo poore paudhe mein bheji ja sakti hai. Aapke ghar ki cheeni aur ganne ka meetha ras, dono yahi sucrose hain. Aur jo sugar turant kaam nahi aati, use paudha starch banakar jama karta hai. Aaloo, chawal aur gehun ka aata zyadatar starch hi hai. Toh aaloo ka starch aaloo mein bana kaise, jabki aaloo mitti ke neeche andhere mein hai? Aaloo ka starch patti mein bani sugar se bana: sucrose patti se aaloo tak pahunchi, aur wahan starch banakar jama ho gayi. Yaad karo, chapter ki shuruaat wale prayog mein patti ke jis hisse ko CO₂ nahi mili, wahan iodine se starch nahi dikha. Kyunki CO₂ ke bina yeh chakkar chal hi nahi sakta, aur chakkar ke bina na sugar banti hai, na starch.
Screen par: Patti se tane aur jad ki taraf behti sugar ki dhaara (sucrose); ek taraf ganna aur cheeni ki katori, doosri taraf mitti ke neeche aaloo ke andar starch ke daane, saath chawal aur aata. Phir chapter ki shuruaat wala prayog: tube ke andar wala patti ka hissa iodine ke baad bhoora, bahar wala hissa neela-kaala.
Terms: sucrose = Ganne aur cheeni wali sugar; paudha ise patti se baaki hisson tak bhejta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Ganne ka ras aur cheeni sucrose hain; aaloo, chawal aur gehun mein starch; aaloo ka starch patti se aayi sucrose se banta hai.
Yaad: 11.1-k6
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.7.2-k9
Ab poore chakkar ka kharcha jodne se pehle ek baat socho: kya CO₂ ko pakadne mein bhi energy lagti hai? CO₂ ko pakadne mein koi energy nahi lagti; carboxylation mein na ATP kharch hota hai, na NADPH. RuBP mein regeneration wale ATP ki energy pehle se bhari hoti hai. Isliye CO₂ se judna uske liye dhalaan se neeche ludhakne jaisa aasaan hai; RuBisCO bas ise tez karta hai. Matlab poora kharcha baaki do kadamon mein hai: sugar banane wale reduction mein, aur RuBP ko phir taiyaar karne wale regeneration mein. Dukaan mein bhi naya paisa aane ka koi kharcha nahi; kharcha maal banane aur poonji sambhalne mein hai.
Screen par: Chakkar ke teen hisse ek line mein: 1 carboxylation par '0 ATP, 0 NADPH', RuBP ki maala ek dhalaan ke upar se CO₂ ki taraf aasaani se ludhakti; 2 reduction par ATP aur NADPH ke moti; 3 regeneration par ATP ka moti, jo RuBP ki maala mein energy bhar kar use dhalaan ke upar pahunchata. Kone mein dukaan: paisa aane par koi bill nahi, maal banane aur thaili sambhalne par bill.
Kitaab se bahar: Carboxylation mein ATP ya NADPH nahi lagta; regeneration ke ATP ki energy RuBP mein rehti hai, isliye CO₂ ka judna apne aap aage badhne wali reaction hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.7.2-k10
Ab bill jodo: reduction ke 2 ATP aur regeneration ka 1 ATP milkar har CO₂ par 3 ATP hue, aur NADPH sirf 2. Yaad karo, seedhe raaste, yaani non-cyclic, mein ATP aur NADPH dono saath-saath bante hain. Aur gol raaste, yaani cyclic, mein electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai, aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi. Ab socho, chakkar ki ATP ki yeh zyada maang kaun poori karta hoga? ATP ki zyada maang poori karne ke liye hi shayad cyclic phosphorylation hota hai, jo NADPH badhaaye bina sirf ATP deta hai. Kyunki agar sirf seedha raasta chale, to har ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banega, aur ATP kam pad jaayega. Yahan cyclic phosphorylation wahi gol raaste wala cyclic photophosphorylation hai, jo humne light reaction mein dekha tha.
Screen par: Bill ki parchi: reduction 2 ATP + 2 NADPH, regeneration 1 ATP; neeche total 3 ATP + 2 NADPH har CO₂ par, tarazu mein ATP ka palda bhaari. Phir thylakoid par do raaste: seedha raasta (PS II → PS I → NADP⁺) ATP aur NADPH dono deta; gol raasta (PS I se ghoom kar wapas PS I) sirf ATP deta. Gol raaste se aata ATP tarazu ko barabar karta.
Terms: cyclic phosphorylation = Cyclic photophosphorylation: electron PS I se ghoom kar wahin lautta hai aur sirf ATP banta hai, NADPH nahi.
Kitaab se bahar: Sirf seedhe raaste se ATP ke saath NADPH bhi banta hai, isliye 3 : 2 ki maang ke liye ATP kam padta hai.
Yaad: 11.6.2-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p015-i1 Hence for every CO₂ molecule entering the Calvin cycle, 3 molecules of ATP and 2 of NADPH are required. It is probably to meet this difference in number of ATP and NADPH used in the dark reaction that the cyclic phosphorylation takes place.
ruko, socho11.7.2-k11
Hum gin chuke hain ki ek glucose ke chhe carbon ke liye cycle ke 6 turns chahiye. Ab khud nikaalo: ek glucose banane mein kitne ATP aur kitne NADPH lagenge? Kitne ATP lagenge, iska clue yeh hai ki har turn ka kharcha aapko pata hai; bas use chhe se guna karo. Har turn mein 3 ATP, toh chhe turns mein 6 × 3 = 18 ATP. Aur har turn mein 2 NADPH, toh 6 × 2 = 12 NADPH. Aksar bachche sochte hain ki 12 ATP lagenge, kyunki woh sirf reduction gin lete hain; par asal mein regeneration ke 6 ATP bhi judte hain. Aur yaad rakho, chhe turns se chhe glucose nahi, milkar ek hi glucose banta hai.
Screen par: Sawaal ka dabba 'Ek glucose = kitne ATP, kitne NADPH?' aur chhoti ghadi. Phir chhe gol chakkar ek line mein, har chakkar ke neeche 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH ke moti; jod: 18 ATP, 12 NADPH. Ek galat jawab '12 ATP' par kaata hua nishaan, aur regeneration ke 6 ATP judkar 18. Aakhir mein chhe chakkar → ek glucose.
Jawab: Ek glucose ke liye 6 turns; har turn mein 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH, toh 6 × 3 = 18 ATP aur 6 × 2 = 12 NADPH (reduction ke 12 ATP + regeneration ke 6 ATP = 18).
Aam galti: Sirf reduction ginne par 12 ATP aata hai, regeneration ke 6 ATP chhoot jaate hain; aur 6 turns se 1 glucose banta hai, 6 glucose nahi.
p015-i2 To make one molecule of glucose 6 turns of the cycle are required.
p015-i3 Work out how many ATP and NADPH molecules will be required to make one molecule of glucose through the Calvin pathway.
activity11.7.2-k12
Yeh sab ek nazar mein dekhna ho, to dekho ki Calvin cycle mein kya andar jaata hai aur kya bahar aata hai. Andar jaate hain chhe CO₂, aur bahar aata hai ek glucose. Andar jaate hain 18 ATP, aur bahar aate hain 18 ADP. Andar jaate hain 12 NADPH, aur bahar aate hain 12 NADP⁺. ATP aur NADPH kharch hokar ADP aur NADP⁺ ban jaate hain. Yeh thylakoid mein laut kar roshni se phir chaarj ho jaate hain. Par is hisaab mein RuBP kahin nahi dikhta; kyun? RuBP hisaab mein isliye nahi dikhta, kyunki jitna lagta hai, utna hi chakkar mein wapas ban jaata hai. Dukaan ke hisaab mein bhi poonji nahi likhi jaati; sirf kamaai aur kharcha likhe jaate hain.
Screen par: Do khaane ki table: In | Out; Six CO₂ | One glucose; 18 ATP | 18 ADP; 12 NADPH | 12 NADP⁺; har row baari-baari roshan. Side mein ADP aur NADP⁺ ke moti thylakoid ki taraf laut kar roshni mein phir ATP aur NADPH bante. Table ke bahar RuBP ka band gol chakkar jo khud mein ghoomta rehta hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Table mein NADP ko NADP⁺ kaha; RuBP table mein isliye nahi kyunki woh regenerate hota hai.
p015-i4 It might help you to understand all of this if we look at what goes in and what comes out of the Calvin cycle.
poori kahani dobara11.7.2-k13
Ab poora chakkar ek saath dohra lo. Pehle kadam, carboxylation, mein RuBisCO RuBP par CO₂ jodta hai, aur do 3-PGA bante hain. Doosre kadam, reduction, mein ATP aur NADPH ke kharche se 3-PGA triose phosphate ban jaate hain. Teesre kadam, regeneration, mein zyadatar triose phosphate ek ATP aur lagakar wapas RuBP ban jaate hain. Har teen CO₂ par ek triose phosphate kamaai bankar bahar jaata hai, aur usse sucrose aur starch bante hain. Har CO₂ ka kharcha 3 ATP aur 2 NADPH hai, aur ek glucose ka 18 ATP aur 12 NADPH. Ab shuru wala sawaal phir se: har CO₂ par ek RuBP kharch hota hai, toh patti ka RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota? RuBP khatam isliye nahi hota, kyunki jitna RuBP pehle kadam mein lagta hai, utna hi teesre kadam mein wapas ban jaata hai. Bilkul wahi dukaan jo shuru mein dekhi: poonji roz lagti hai, shaam ko wapas thaili mein aa jaati hai, aur ghar sirf kamaai jaati hai. Aur yeh chakkar har hare paudhe mein chalta hai, chahe woh C₃ ho ya C₄. Toh aap poochhoge, agar chakkar sab mein ek hai, to C₄ paudhe alag kis baat mein hain? C₄ paudhe alag kis baat mein hain, yeh aage hum dekhenge.
Screen par: Figure 11.8 jaisa poora gol chakkar, teeno kadam baari-baari roshan: RuBisCO par RuBP + CO₂ → do 3-PGA; ATP + NADPH se triose phosphate; paanch wapas RuBP, ek bahar sucrose aur starch ki taraf. Neeche parchi: 1 CO₂ = 3 ATP + 2 NADPH; 1 glucose = 18 ATP + 12 NADPH. Phir shuru ka sawaal 'RuBP khatam kyun nahi hota?' laut kar aata hai: pehle kadam mein ek RuBP lagta, teesre kadam mein utna hi RuBP wapas banta; kone mein dukaan ki thaili mein poonji wapas jaati aur kamaai ghar jaati. Aakhir mein do paudhe, C₃ aur C₄, dono ka teer isi chakkar tak, aur C₄ par sawaal ka nishaan jo khula rehta hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Calvin cycle har hare paudhe mein ek hi hai; agle heading mein dekhenge ki C₄ paudhe CO₂ ko is chakkar tak pahunchane ka kaun sa khaas tareeka rakhte hain.
11.8 THE C₄ PATHWAY · 18 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 15 min
Maqsad: Ab shared Calvin cycle tak C₄ plants ki carbon delivery follow karenge, aur samjhenge ki leaf ki special anatomy is delivery ko kaise support karti hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.8-k1
Ab shared Calvin cycle tak C₄ plants ki carbon delivery follow karenge, aur samjhenge ki leaf ki special anatomy is delivery ko kaise support karti hai. Anatomy pehchaanne ke baad process ke chaar steps follow karenge: initial carbon fixation, four-carbon compound ki delivery aur CO₂ release, three-carbon remainder ki return, aur acceptor regeneration. C₄ oxaloacetic acid wahi pehle pehchaana four-carbon first stable product hai, aur dono groups ka shared Calvin cycle bhi hum jaan chuke hain. C₄ plants dry tropical regions, yaani garam ilaakon ki paani-kam conditions, ke liye adapted ho sakte hain. Inka main biosynthetic pathway, yaani organic substances banane wala connected reaction route, Calvin cycle hi hai. Phir C₃ plants se inka farq kya hai? Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants Calvin cycle use nahi karte, par asal mein unki initial carbon capture aur delivery alag hai, sugar-making Calvin cycle shared hai.
Screen par: Realistic maize leaf se internal tissue tak zoom; four-carbon oxaloacetic acid entry route ke aage shared Calvin-cycle symbol. Side par chaar numbered steps: fixation, delivery/release, return, regeneration.
Terms: C₄ oxaloacetic acid = Pehle pehchaana four-carbon oxaloacetic acid; C₄ initial fixation ka first stable product.; dry tropical regions = Garam tropical ilaake jahaan paani ki availability kam ho sakti hai.; biosynthetic pathway = Organic substances banane wali connected reactions ka route.
Yaad: 11.7-k8, 11.7.2-k1
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p015-i10 Plants that are adapted to dry tropical regions have the C₄ pathway mentioned earlier. Though these plants have the C₄ oxaloacetic acid as the first CO₂ fixation product they use the C₃ pathway or the Calvin cycle
p015-i11 as the main biosynthetic pathway. Then, in what way are they different from C₃ plants? This is a question that you may reasonably ask.
samjhana11.8-k2
C₄ plants ki paanch khas baatein jodo: special leaf anatomy, higher-temperature tolerance, high light intensities par achchha response, photorespiration ka practically absent hona, aur greater biomass productivity. Leaf anatomy leaf ke andar tissues aur cells ka arrangement hai. Photorespiration RuBisCO ki O₂ wali activity se shuru hone wala route hai, jisme fixed carbon aur energy ka loss hota hai; iska detail agle heading mein kholenge. RuBisCO ki CO₂ aur O₂, dono wali activities hum pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Productivity of biomass ka matlab fixed time mein naya plant material banne ki rate hai. Photorespiration ko C₄ plants mein absent kyun maana jaata hai? Dekho, inka carbon-delivery system RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhata hai, jisse photorespiration bahut strongly suppress hoti hai. Pehle anatomy samjhenge, phir delivery route se in features ka connection jodenge.
Screen par: Maize leaf ke paas paanch features: internal tissue ring, warmth, strong light, suppressed photorespiration aur biomass gain.
Terms: leaf anatomy = Leaf ke andar tissues aur cells ki banawat aur arrangement.; photorespiration = RuBisCO ki O₂ wali activity se shuru carbon aur energy kharch karne wala route.; productivity of biomass = Ek fixed time mein naye plant material ke production ki rate.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k3
Exercise ke liye: p021-i16
p015-i12 C₄ plants are special: They have a special type of leaf anatomy, they tolerate higher temperatures, they show a response to high light intensities, they lack a process called photorespiration and have greater productivity of biomass. Let us understand these one by one.

Mesophyll ke layers bhi compare karo, lekin har C₃ leaf mein ek jaisi layer differentiation maan lena sahi nahi hai.

analogy11.8-k4
Socho, ek central stand ke chaaron taraf flowers ki wreath, yaani gol maala, lagi hai. Har flower central stand ko gherne wale ring ka hissa hai; isi surrounding arrangement ko leaf mein pehchaano. Vascular bundles leaf ki veins ke andar water aur food transport karne wale xylem–phloem groups hain. In bundles ko gherne wale cells bundle sheath cells kehlaate hain. Typical C₄ leaf mein particularly large sheath cells ka wreath-like arrangement Kranz anatomy kehlaata hai; Kranz ka meaning wreath hai. Yahaan central stand vascular bundle hai, aur flower-ring large bundle sheath cells hain. Kranz naam leaf ke baahari shape ko kyun nahi batata? Dekho, naam internal cells ki bundle ko gherne wali arrangement se aaya hai, poori leaf ki outline se nahi.

Kitaab se bahar: Xylem–phloem ka basic transport role prerequisite ke roop mein khola.

Exercise ke liye: p021-i15, p022-i12
p015-i14 The particularly large cells around the vascular bundles of the C₄ plants are called bundle sheath cells, and the leaves which have such anatomy are said to have ‘Kranz’ anatomy. ‘Kranz’ means ‘wreath’ and is a reflection of the arrangement of cells.
samjhana11.8-k5
Bundle sheath ki ring mein several layers ho sakti hain, lekin multiple layers compulsory nahi hain. In cells mein numerous chloroplasts, thick walls aur cells ke beech intercellular spaces ki absence khas features hain. Intercellular spaces neighbouring cells ke beech ki khaali jagahen hain. Walls ko impervious to gaseous exchange kehte hain, yaani gases ka freely aar-paar jaana strongly restricted hota hai. Gas escape restrict karna useful kyun hoga? Dekho, sheath ke andar delivered CO₂ retain hogi, aur numerous chloroplasts ke stromal reactions us carbon ko use kar sakenge. Chloroplast stroma ka sugar-making role hum pehle samajh chuke hain.
Screen par: Wahi realistic maize section enlarged: tightly packed sheath cells, many chloroplasts, thick walls aur restricted outward CO₂ arrows.
Terms: intercellular spaces = Paas ke cells ke beech ki khaali jagahen.; impervious to gaseous exchange = Gases ke freely cross karne ko strongly restrict karne wala.
Yaad: 11.3-k6
Exercise ke liye: p021-i15, p022-i12
p015-i15 The bundle sheath cells may form several layers around the vascular bundles; they are characterised by having a large number of chloroplasts, thick walls impervious to gaseous exchange and no intercellular spaces.

Dekho, dono named C₄ examples mein bundle, surrounding specialised sheath aur outer mesophyll ka relationship match karna hai. Result sirf green cells dekhna nahi, balki unki organised positions pehchaanana hai.

Dekho, actual harvest paani, light aur doosri growing conditions par bhi depend karta hai, isliye C₄ adaptation universal yield guarantee nahi hai.

pehle poori jhalak11.8-k9
Ab Figure 11.9 ka Hatch and Slack Pathway chaar steps mein follow karenge: initial fixation, four-carbon compound ki delivery aur CO₂ release, three-carbon return, aur acceptor renewal. Hatch and Slack Pathway C₄ carbon-capture aur delivery route ka doosra naam hai. Yeh cyclic process hai, kyunki starting acceptor dobara available hota hai; acceptor renewal ka logic Calvin cycle mein bhi dekha tha. Dono cycles ka acceptor kya same hoga? Dekho, Calvin cycle ka acceptor RuBP tha, lekin is initial delivery cycle ka acceptor ab alag pehchaanenge.

Yaad: 11.7.2-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p016-i1 Now study the pathway shown in Figure 11.9. This pathway that has been named the Hatch and Slack Pathway, is again a cyclic process. Let us study the pathway by listing the steps.
samjhana11.8-k10
Mesophyll mein primary acceptor phosphoenol pyruvate, ya PEP, three-carbon molecule hai. PEP carboxylase, ya PEPcase, initial carbon fixation catalyse karta hai, aur four-carbon OAA banta hai. OAA wahi pehle khola oxaloacetic acid hai; carbon ledger three plus one equals four hai. Figure mein incoming inorganic carbon HCO₃⁻, bicarbonate, ke roop mein dikhta hai; PEPcase isi form ko use karta hai. Is typical C₄ arrangement ke mesophyll cells mein PEPcase hai, lekin RuBisCO nahi hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ ka primary acceptor four-carbon hoga, par asal mein PEP three-carbon acceptor aur OAA four-carbon product hai. Acceptor aur enzyme ko alag kyun rakhna hai? Dekho, PEP carbon skeleton receive karta hai, jabki PEPcase reaction catalyse karta hai; enzyme ko three-carbon acceptor kehna galat hoga.
Screen par: Wahi mesophyll cutaway: atmospheric CO₂ se HCO₃⁻, phir PEP ke three carbon beads mein one carbon add hokar OAA four beads; PEPcase label.
Terms: phosphoenol pyruvate (PEP) = C₄ initial fixation ka three-carbon acceptor molecule.; PEP carboxylase or PEPcase = PEP aur bicarbonate se initial four-carbon product banane wali reaction ka enzyme.; OAA = Pehle pehchaane oxaloacetic acid ka short name; four-carbon first stable product.; HCO₃⁻ = Bicarbonate ion; PEPcase ko milne wala inorganic carbon ka form.
Kitaab se bahar: PEPcase ke actual bicarbonate substrate ko figure ke HCO₃⁻ label se clarify kiya.
Yaad: 11.7-k8, 11.7.2-k3
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p016-i2 The primary CO₂ acceptor is a 3-carbon molecule phosphoenol pyruvate (PEP) and is present in the mesophyll cells. The enzyme responsible for this fixation is PEP carboxylase or PEPcase. It is important to register that the mesophyll cells lack RuBisCO enzyme. The C₄ acid OAA is formed in the mesophyll cells.
samjhana11.8-k11
Mesophyll mein OAA se malic acid ya aspartic acid jaise other four-carbon compounds bante hain. In four-carbon organic acids ko yahaan C₄ acids keh rahe hain; yeh captured carbon neighbouring bundle sheath cells tak transport karte hain. Sheath mein inka breakdown CO₂ release karta hai aur three-carbon remainder deta hai; CO₂ nikalne ko decarboxylation kehte hain. Four carbons ka balance kahaan gaya? Dekho, four-carbon carrier se one-carbon CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder milta hai, isliye four equals one plus three rehta hai.
Screen par: Same cells par OAA se malic/aspartic acid; four-carbon cargo sheath mein enters, phir one-carbon CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder mein splits.
Terms: malic acid or aspartic acid = Four-carbon compounds jo captured carbon mesophyll se bundle sheath tak pahunchate hain.; C₄ acid = Yahaan four-carbon organic acid jo carbon delivery mein kaam aata hai.; decarboxylation = Kisi compound se CO₂ release hona.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i1, p021-i16, p022-i10
p016-i3 It then forms other 4-carbon compounds like malic acid or aspartic acid in the mesophyll cells itself, which are transported to the bundle sheath cells. In the bundle sheath cells these C₄ acids are broken down to release CO₂ and a 3-carbon molecule.
samjhana11.8-k12
Ab three-carbon remainder bundle sheath se mesophyll mein return hota hai, jahaan usse PEP dobara banta hai. Regeneration ka meaning wahi starting working molecule ka renewal hai; yahaan renew hone wala acceptor PEP hai, RuBP nahi. Kya return route ko free carbon recycling samajh sakte hain? Dekho, PEP renewal energy maangta hai, isliye carbon-concentrating system ka extra energy bill bhi hota hai. Typical C₄ accounting mein har delivered CO₂ ke liye two additional ATP equivalents lagte hain.
Screen par: Same cell cutaways par three-carbon return arrow aur mesophyll mein PEP regeneration; energy badge: +2 ATP equivalents per CO₂.
Kitaab se bahar: PEP regeneration ka extra cost: two ATP equivalents per CO₂; commonly ATP-to-AMP conversion ki energy accounting.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p016-i4 The 3-carbon molecule is transported back to the mesophyll where it is converted to PEP again, thus, completing the cycle.
samjhana11.8-k13
Bundle sheath mein released CO₂ ab C₃ ya Calvin pathway ko feed karti hai. RuBisCO ke through RuBP carboxylation ka reaction hum pehle follow kar chuke hain. Yahaan CO₂ preliminary four-carbon compound ki decarboxylation se milti hai; Calvin cycle CO₂ release nahi karti. Calvin cycle mein fixed carbon ka net output triose phosphate sugar ke roop mein nikalta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki initial C₄ fixation hi sugar-making Calvin cycle ko replace karti hai, par asal mein yeh usse concentrated CO₂ supply karti hai. Do fixation events ko kyun alag pehchaanenge? Dekho, pehla event carbon delivery ke liye capture karta hai, aur doosra shared Calvin pathway mein carbohydrate formation ko feed karta hai.
Screen par: Same bundle sheath cutaway par four-carbon compound se released CO₂ ka arrow chloroplast stroma ke Calvin-cycle inset tak; net triose-phosphate output alag highlighted.

p016-i5 The CO₂ released in the bundle sheath cells enters the C₃ or the Calvin pathway, a pathway common to all plants. The bundle sheath cells are

p016-i6 Two connected cells show the Hatch and Slack pathway. The upper mesophyll cell receives atmospheric CO₂, shown as HCO₃⁻ joining phosphoenol-pyruvate at Fixation. samjhana11.8-k15
Ab enzyme-location map complete karo: C₄ mesophyll mein PEPcase aur bundle sheath mein abundant RuBisCO hota hai. Is arrangement ke bundle sheath cells mein PEPcase nahi hota; initial capture aur Calvin fixation ki jobs spatially separated hain. C₃ leaves mein Calvin pathway mesophyll cells mein chalta hai, jabki typical C₄ leaf mein woh bundle sheath cells tak restricted hai. Same Calvin pathway hone par location ka difference important kyun hai? Dekho, four-carbon compounds CO₂ sheath mein release karte hain, isliye RuBisCO aur Calvin cycle wahin rakhne se delivered CO₂ seedhe use ho sakti hai. Dono groups sugars ke precursors shared Calvin pathway se banate hain; difference uski identity mein nahi, carbon supply aur cellular location mein hai.
Screen par: Two-column enzyme-location matrix: C₃ mesophyll—RuBisCO/Calvin; C₄ mesophyll—PEPcase, bundle sheath—RuBisCO/Calvin; corresponding tissue cells highlighted.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k1
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10, p022-i12, p021-i16, p022-i1
p017-i1 rich in an enzyme Ribulose bisphosphate carboxylase-oxygenase (RuBisCO), but lack PEPcase. Thus, the basic pathway that results in the formation of the sugars, the Calvin pathway, is common to the C₃ and C₄ plants.
p017-i2 Did you note that the Calvin pathway occurs in all the mesophyll cells of the C₃ plants? In the C₄ plants it does not take place in the mesophyll cells but does so only in the bundle sheath cells.
example11.8-k16
Ab maize ke ek incoming carbon ko follow karo: bicarbonate ka carbon three-carbon PEP ke saath four-carbon OAA ke account mein judta hai. Malic-acid delivery ke baad sheath mein one-carbon CO₂ release hoti hai aur three-carbon skeleton mesophyll lautkar PEP renew karta hai. Starting three-carbon acceptor pool wapas mila; net delivered carbon kitna raha? Dekho, ek incoming carbon Calvin cycle ko mila, jabki delivery skeleton regenerate hua; delivery loop khud extra carbon create nahi karta. Six delivered CO₂ ke liye Calvin cycle ka pehle joda bill eighteen ATP aur twelve NADPH hai. Typical C₄ delivery ke twelve extra ATP equivalents jodne par glucose-equivalent ka total thirty ATP equivalents aur twelve NADPH hota hai. Yeh energy investment CO₂ concentration ke liye hai; C₄ naam ka matlab four CO₂ molecules ek saath fix hona nahi.

ruko, socho11.8-k17
Socho, phir aage badhenge: four-carbon compound sheath mein CO₂ chhod de, lekin three-carbon remainder mesophyll lautna band ho jaaye, toh continuous delivery ka kya hoga? Kya sheath mein Calvin cycle hone se missing return route apne-aap theek ho jaayega? Dekho, return rukne par PEP acceptor pool renew nahi hoga, isliye initial capture aur sustained CO₂ delivery ghatengi, chahe sheath ki Calvin machinery present ho. Delivery loop aur Calvin cycle alag acceptors renew karte hain; ek ki renewal doosre ki missing regeneration ko replace nahi karti.

poori kahani dobara11.8-k18
Poora route phir jodo: Kranz leaf mein mesophyll large chloroplast-rich bundle sheath ko surround karta hai, aur sheath vascular bundle ko gherati hai. Mesophyll ka PEPcase three-carbon PEP ke saath incoming inorganic carbon fix karke four-carbon OAA banata hai. Malic ya aspartic acid carbon sheath tak laata hai; decarboxylation one CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder deti hai. CO₂ sheath ke RuBisCO aur Calvin cycle ko feed karti hai, jabki remainder mesophyll lautkar energy-dependent PEP renewal karta hai. Is arrangement se growth ko fayda kyun mil sakta hai? Dekho, CO₂ RuBisCO ke paas concentrate hoti hai aur photorespiratory loss suppress hota hai, isliye comparatively few Calvin-performing sheath cells bhi productive carbon assimilation support kar sakte hain. Ab O₂ wali competing RuBisCO activity ka poora carbon aur energy consequence samjhenge.

→ C₄ delivery RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhati hai; ab photorespiration mein dekhenge ki O₂ wali reaction ka loss kya hai aur concentrated CO₂ usse kaise rokti hai.
11.9 PHOTORESPIRATION · 12 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 11 min
Maqsad: Ab RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ka carbon aur energy loss samjhenge, aur dekhenge ki C₄ ki concentrated CO₂ supply is loss ko kaise dabaati hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.9-k1
Ab RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ka carbon aur energy loss samjhenge, aur dekhenge ki C₄ ki concentrated CO₂ supply is loss ko kaise dabaati hai. Yahaan teen links follow karenge: gases ki competition, oxygenation ka carbon–energy account, aur C₄ plants ka protective CO₂ concentration. Normal carboxylation ka pehle joda reference yaad karo: RuBP + CO₂ se RuBisCO ki madad se two 3PGA molecules bante hain. RuBP wahi five-carbon acceptor aur 3PGA wahi three-carbon PGA hai; 2 × 3PGA ka matlab do molecules, har ek mein three carbons. Comparison ka baseline kya hai? Dekho, five plus one se six carbons, yaani three plus three, milte hain; ab gas badalne par isi account ko compare karenge.
Screen par: Realistic chloroplast-stroma close-up: RuBisCO ke paas familiar carboxylation arrow, RuBP 5C + CO₂ 1C → 2 × 3PGA, total 6C; oxygenation branch abhi nahi.
Terms: 2 × 3PGA = 3PGA ke do molecules; har molecule mein teen carbon atoms.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k3
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p017-i4 Let us try and understand one more process that creates an important difference between C₃ and C₄ plants – Photorespiration. To understand photorespiration we have to know a little bit more about the first step of the Calvin pathway – the first CO₂ fixation step. This is the reaction where RuBP combines with CO₂ to form 2 molecules of 3PGA, that is catalysed by RuBisCO.
p017-i5 RuBP + CO₂ ⟶[RuBisCo] 2 × 3PGA
samjhana11.9-k2
RuBisCO duniya ka most abundant enzyme hai; photosynthetic carbon fixation ki enormous demand ke liye plants mein iski bahut badi total quantity milti hai. Itna enzyme kyun chahiye? Dekho, bahut bade scale par carbon fixation hoti hai aur RuBisCO comparatively slow catalyst hai, isliye considerable enzyme investment chahiye. Active site enzyme ka reaction karane wala region hai; RuBisCO ke isi catalytic region par CO₂ aur O₂ alternative reactions mein participate kar sakte hain. Ek enzyme dono gases ke saath kaise kaam kar sakta hai? Dekho, enzyme specificity hamesha absolute nahi hoti; RuBisCO ki catalytic chemistry RuBP ke saath dono alternative gas reactions allow karti hai. Carboxylase-oxygenase naam wahi pehle kholi dual activity batata hai; affinity kisi molecule se judne ka jhukaav hai.
Screen par: Ussi stromal RuBisCO ka enlarged active site; RuBP present, CO₂ aur O₂ ke alternative entry arrows, dono ek saath reaction karte hue nahi.
Terms: active site = Enzyme ka region jahaan substrate ki catalysed reaction hoti hai.; affinity = Kisi molecule ke saath judne ka jhukaav.
Kitaab se bahar: RuBisCO ki abundance samjhaane ke liye comparatively slow catalysis aur enzyme specificity ka non-absolute hona joda.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k3
Exercise ke liye: p022-i1
p017-i6 RuBisCO that is the most abundant enzyme in the world (Do you wonder why?) is characterised by the fact that its active site can bind to both CO₂ and O₂ – hence the name. Can you think how this could be possible? RuBisCO has a much greater affinity for CO₂ when the CO₂: O₂
samjhana11.9-k3
CO₂ aur O₂ ki concentrations comparable hon, toh RuBisCO ka jhukaav CO₂ ki taraf kaafi zyada hota hai. Agar yeh preference na hoti, toh kya hota? Dekho, comparable concentrations par oxygenation ko zyada opportunity milti aur productive carbon fixation ka advantage kam hota. Binding competitive hai, yaani dono gases alternative reactions ke liye compete karti hain. Relative concentration ka matlab ek gas ki concentration doosri ke mukaable kitni hai; CO₂:O₂ isi concentration ratio ko likhta hai. Aksar lagta hai ki greater CO₂ affinity O₂ binding ko impossible bana degi, par asal mein reaction balance local gas concentrations par bhi depend karta hai. CO₂ kam hone par O₂ wali reaction ka relative chance badh sakta hai.
Screen par: Same active-site view: pehle comparable gas concentrations, phir CO₂ particles kam; carboxylation aur oxygenation arrows ki relative widths badalti hain.
Terms: competitive = CO₂ aur O₂ ka alternative RuBisCO reactions ke liye muqaabla.; relative concentration = Ek substance ki concentration doosre ke mukaable kitni hai.; CO₂:O₂ = CO₂ concentration ka O₂ concentration se ratio.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i1
p017-i7 is nearly equal. Imagine what would happen if this were not so! This binding is competitive. It is the relative concentration of O₂ and CO₂ that determines which of the two will bind to the enzyme.
analogy11.9-k4
Socho, ek service counter par do queues se candidates aa rahe hain, aur clerk ek waqt par ek candidate process karta hai. Clerk ko pehli queue ko preference dene ki aadat hai, lekin us queue se log aana bahut kam ho jaayein. Kya preference hone se doosri queue ki service bilkul band rahegi? Dekho, available candidates badalne par doosri queue ko bhi zyada turns mil sakte hain. Ab doosri queue ki arrival same rakho aur pehli queue se arrivals badha do; preferred queue ko phir zyada service opportunities milengi. Yahaan counter RuBisCO ka active site hai, preferred queue CO₂ hai, doosri queue O₂ hai, aur arrivals unki local concentrations hain.
Screen par: Ek counter, CO₂ aur O₂ labelled do queues; O₂ arrivals constant rehte hue CO₂ arrivals badhti hain.
Kya kis jaisa: Counter active site hai; queues CO₂ aur O₂ hain; preference affinity aur arrivals local concentrations ko map karte hain.
Kahan tootti hai: Enzyme conscious choice nahi karta; molecular reaction probabilities affinity aur conditions se badalti hain.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
example11.9-k5
Ab C₃ leaf mein ek five-carbon RuBP molecule ka O₂ wala reaction follow karo. Oxygenation se one three-carbon phosphoglycerate, wahi PGA, aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate banta hai. Phosphoglycolate oxygenation ka two-carbon product hai, jiska processing photorespiratory route mein hota hai. Carbon ledger yahaan five equals three plus two hai; O₂ koi naya carbon nahi laati. Normal route se difference kya mila? Dekho, carboxylation one external carbon jodkar two PGA deti hai, jabki oxygenation external carbon jode bina one PGA aur one phosphoglycolate deti hai. Isliye O₂ reaction ki opportunity badhne se productive CO₂ fixation ghatati hai; isi diversion se pehle pehchaana photorespiration route shuru hota hai.
Screen par: Ussi stromal picture mein normal branch shaant rehti hai; nayi branch RuBP 5C + O₂ → PGA 3C + phosphoglycolate 2C, carbon dots counted.
Terms: phosphoglycolate (2 Carbon) = RuBP oxygenation se banne wala two-carbon compound.
Yaad: 11.8-k2
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p017-i8 In C₃ plants some O₂ does bind to RuBisCO, and hence CO₂ fixation is decreased. Here the RuBP instead of being converted to 2 molecules of PGA binds with O₂ to form one molecule of phosphoglycerate and phosphoglycolate (2 Carbon) in a pathway called photorespiration.
samjhana11.9-k6
Photorespiratory processing mein ATP utilise hota hai aur CO₂ release hoti hai; utilisation of ATP ka matlab energy kharch hona hai. Is route se net sugar synthesis, ATP synthesis ya NADPH synthesis nahi milti. Kya oxygenation ke turant first step mein hi CO₂ nikal gayi thi? Dekho, first split mein saare five carbons three plus two products mein the; CO₂ baad ke photorespiratory processing mein release hoti hai. Isliye carbon gain par double pressure hai: initial CO₂ fixation ki opportunity ghati aur processing mein pehle fixed carbon ka kuch hissa bhi chhoota. Photorespiration ko sirf useless kehna bhi poori biological picture nahi hai; yeh phosphoglycolate se carbon recover karne ka salvage route hai. Iske protective roles bhi samjhe gaye hain, isliye biological function bilkul unknown kehna sahi nahi; yahaan focus photosynthetic carbon aur energy cost hai.
Screen par: Oxygenation ke 2C product se processing arrow aage badhe: ATP → ADP expense aur outward CO₂ arrow; separate net-output ledger mein sugar, ATP, NADPH production zero.
Terms: utilisation of ATP = ATP ki energy ka use hona; ATP ka production nahi.
Kitaab se bahar: Outdated function-unknown statement ki jagah carbon-salvage aur protective roles ki modern clarification; first oxygenation aur later CO₂ release ko alag kiya.
Exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i10
p017-i9 In the photorespiratory pathway, there is neither synthesis of sugars, nor of ATP. Rather it results in the release of CO₂ with the utilisation of ATP. In the photorespiratory pathway there is no synthesis of ATP or NADPH. The biological function of photorespiration is not known yet.
ruko, socho11.9-k7
Socho, phir aage badhenge: two RuBP molecules oxygenation karein, toh immediate products mein kitna carbon aur kitna naya atmospheric carbon hoga? Kya photorespiration naam dekhkar ise ATP banane wali ordinary respiration maan sakte hain? Dekho, ten starting carbons two PGA ke six aur two phosphoglycolate ke four carbons mein milenge, aur koi naya atmospheric carbon add nahi hoga. Baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karke CO₂ release karta hai, isliye is route ko ATP-producing ordinary respiration samajhna galat hoga.
Screen par: Two RuBP ke 10 carbon dots → two PGA, 6C total + two phosphoglycolate, 4C total; incoming carbon zero, later ATP-expense marker.
Jawab: Immediate oxygenation carbon conserve karti hai: 10 = 6 + 4; external CO₂ carbon zero add hota hai. Later processing ATP consume aur CO₂ release karti hai.
Aam galti: Immediate oxygenation mein carbon missing nahi hota; CO₂ loss later processing ka hai. Photorespiration ATP-producing respiration nahi hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.9-k8
Ab C₄ ka cure jodo: mesophyll ke C₄ acids bundle sheath mein breakdown hokar CO₂ release karte hain, jaisa hum delivery route mein follow kar chuke hain. Isse intracellular concentration, yaani cell ke andar per volume CO₂ ki amount, RuBisCO ke paas badhti hai. O₂ ko hataye bina iska fayda kaise milega? Dekho, local CO₂:O₂ ratio badhne se RuBisCO ki carboxylase activity favour hoti hai aur oxygenase activity minimum ki taraf jaati hai. Minimising oxygenase activity ka matlab O₂ wali reaction ko strongly reduce karna hai, enzyme ki ability mita dena nahi. Isi strong suppression ke kaaran C₄ plants ko practically photorespiration-absent kehte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants mein RuBisCO hi nahi hota, par asal mein sheath ka RuBisCO concentrated CO₂ ke saath kaam karta hai.
Screen par: Realistic C₄ mesophyll–bundle-sheath pair: familiar C₄-acid delivery, sheath mein CO₂ release; same O₂ density par CO₂ density badhe aur carboxylation arrow widen ho.
Terms: intracellular concentration = Cell ke andar kisi substance ki amount per unit volume.; minimising the oxygenase activity = RuBisCO ki O₂ wali reaction ko bahut kam karna.
Yaad: 11.8-k11, 11.8-k15
Exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10
p017-i10 In C₄ plants photorespiration does not occur. This is because they have a mechanism that increases the concentration of CO₂ at the enzyme site. This takes place when the C₄ acid from the mesophyll is broken down in the bundle sheath cells to release CO₂ – this results in increasing the intracellular concentration of CO₂. In turn, this ensures that the RuBisCO functions as a carboxylase minimising the oxygenase activity.
asli duniya11.9-k9
Warm, bright conditions mein maize aur sorghum ki C₄ carbon concentration crop productivity ko support kar sakti hai. Productivity wahi naya organic material banne ki rate hai; yield crop se milne wali useful harvested upaj hai. Tolerance to higher temperatures ka matlab comparatively warmer conditions mein bhi functioning maintain karne ki capacity hai. Extra ATP kharch karne ke bawajood C₄ system faydemand kyun ho sakta hai? Dekho, delivery ka energy bill photorespiratory carbon aur energy losses ko bacha sakta hai, isliye suitable conditions mein net growth aur yield better ho sakte hain. Higher-temperature tolerance bhi C₄ plants ki khas feature hai, lekin har environment mein higher harvest ki guarantee nahi hai.
Screen par: Maize aur sorghum field ke saath two budgets: C₃ photorespiratory carbon loss aur C₄ delivery-energy expense ke badle retained growth carbon; harvest grain alag output.
Terms: yield = Crop se milne wali useful harvested upaj.; tolerance to higher temperatures = Comparatively warmer conditions mein functioning maintain karne ki capacity.
Kitaab se bahar: Favourable warm, bright conditions aur environment-dependent yield ka qualification.
Yaad: 11.8-k8, 11.8-k16
Exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i10
p017-i11 Now that you know that the C₄ plants lack photorespiration, you probably can understand why productivity and yields are better in these plants. In addition these plants show tolerance to higher temperatures.
activity11.9-k10
Ab C₃–C₄ comparison table bharo; pehle locations aur carbon identities ki ten rows complete karo. Calvin cycle C₃ mein mesophyll aur C₄ mein bundle sheath mein hota hai, lekin initial carboxylation dono mein mesophyll se shuru hoti hai. CO₂-fixing cell-type categories C₃ mein one, mesophyll, aur C₄ mein two, mesophyll plus bundle sheath, hain; palisade aur spongy dono mesophyll categories ke andar aate hain. Primary acceptors C₃ mein five-carbon RuBP aur C₄ mein three-carbon PEP hain; first products respectively three-carbon PGA aur four-carbon OAA hain. Dono plants mein RuBisCO hai, lekin photosynthetic location C₃ mein mesophyll aur C₄ mein bundle sheath hai. PEPcase wali row mein kya likhoge? Dekho, initial photosynthetic fixation ke context mein C₃ ke liye No aur C₄ ke liye Yes likhenge; iska matlab C₃ plant mein kisi doosre metabolic role ke liye PEPcase kabhi nahi hota, yeh nahi hai.
Screen par: Table 11.1, rows 1–10, columns Characteristic | C₃ | C₄: Calvin site | Mesophyll | Bundle sheath; Initial carboxylation | Mesophyll | Mesophyll; Fixing cell types | One: mesophyll | Two: mesophyll and bundle sheath; Primary acceptor | RuBP | PEP; Acceptor carbons | 5 | 3; Primary product | PGA | OAA; Product carbons | 3 | 4; RuBisCO present | Yes | Yes; Photosynthetic initial-fixation PEPcase | No | Yes; RuBisCO cells | Mesophyll | Bundle sheath.
Kitaab se bahar: PEPcase ke C₃ non-photosynthetic metabolic roles ka clarification; table ka No initial photosynthetic fixation ke context mein hai.
Yaad: 11.8-k10, 11.8-k15, 11.8-k7
Kya kis jaisa: Screen ki ten completed rows; enzyme presence ko uske photosynthetic role aur cell location se alag pehchaano.
Kahan tootti hai: C₄ plants RuBisCO rakhte hain; acceptor PEP hai, PEPcase enzyme hai.

activity11.9-k11
Ab remaining seven rows bharo; environmental comparisons ko comparable conditions mein samjho, universal fixed rates ki tarah nahi. High light par suitable temperature aur gas supply mein relative fixation C₃ ke liye Low aur C₄ ke liye High choose karte hain. Photorespiration ke low-light row mein C₃ ke liye Sometimes aur C₄ ke liye Negligible, jabki high-light row mein C₃ High aur C₄ Negligible rakho. Light akeli oxygenation decide kyun nahi karti? Dekho, local gas ratio aur temperature bhi matter karte hain, isliye C₃ ka low-light Sometimes conditional response hai, guaranteed absence nahi. Low CO₂ par C₃ High aur C₄ Negligible; high CO₂ par dono Negligible choose karo, kyunki available CO₂ oxygenation ke against carboxylation favour karti hai. Typical temperature optimum C₃ ke liye 20–25°C aur C₄ ke liye 30–40°C rakho; optimum best-working temperature hai, aur temperature ka mechanism aage kholenge. Examples mein C₃ wheat aur rice, C₄ maize aur sorghum likho; collected sections ko characteristic Kranz organisation ke comparison se classify karo.
Screen par: Same table, rows 11–17: High-light fixation | Low | High; Low-light photorespiration | Sometimes | Negligible; High-light photorespiration | High | Negligible; Low-CO₂ photorespiration | High | Negligible; High-CO₂ photorespiration | Negligible | Negligible; Typical temperature optimum | 20–25°C | 30–40°C; Examples | Wheat, rice | Maize, sorghum. Side-by-side labelled leaf sections connect example classification to Kranz organisation.
Kitaab se bahar: Table ke inferred environmental entries ko conditional banaya; wheat aur rice examples aur typical temperature ranges jode. Severe CO₂ deprivation mein C₄ concentration bhi unlimited protection nahi deti.
Yaad: 11.8-k2, 11.8-k7

poori kahani dobara11.9-k12
Pehle samjhi productive carboxylation ko reference rakho; ab uske competing diversion aur C₄ ke protection ko ek saath jodo. O₂ compete kare, toh five-carbon RuBP se one three-carbon PGA aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate banta hai; naya carbon add nahi hota. Baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karta aur CO₂ release karta hai, net sugar, ATP ya NADPH production nahi deta. C₄ mein mesophyll se aayi acids bundle sheath mein CO₂ release karti hain, jisse RuBisCO ki carboxylation favour aur oxygenation strongly suppress hoti hai. Few Calvin-performing cells bhi productive kyun ho sakte hain? Dekho, concentrated carbon supply aur reduced photorespiratory loss un cells ko effective assimilation support dete hain; cell-count akela productivity decide nahi karta.
Screen par: Connected final comparison: carboxylation 5+1→3+3; oxygenation 5→3+2 with later ATP expense and CO₂ loss; C₄ delivery raises sheath CO₂ and directs flow toward Calvin assimilation.
Yaad: 11.8-k18, 11.7.2-k13
