→ Ab carbon retention ka farq clear hai; agle heading mein dekhenge ki light, CO₂, temperature, water aur plant ki internal features photosynthesis ki actual rate ko kaise control karte hain.
11.10 FACTORS AFFECTING PHOTOSYNTHESIS · 7 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 6 min
Maqsad: Ab photosynthesis ki machinery se aage badhkar samjhenge ki plant ki internal features aur external conditions mein se kaunsi cheez uski actual rate ko rok rahi hai.
samjhana11.10-k1
Ab photosynthesis ki machinery se aage badhkar samjhenge ki plant ki internal features aur external conditions mein se kaunsi cheez uski actual rate ko rok rahi hai. Yield ko useful harvested upaj pehchaan chuke hain; photosynthesis ki rate crop ke liye available organic material aur yield ko strongly influence karti hai. Internal plant factors mein leaves ki number, size, age aur orientation, mesophyll cells aur chloroplasts ki features, internal CO₂ concentration aur chlorophyll ki amount aati hain. Orientation leaf ki direction aur angle hai; isse leaf par light pahunchne ka pattern badal sakta hai. Same sunlight mein do plants ka photosynthesis alag kyun ho sakta hai? Dekho, unki light collect karne aur carbon fix karne wali internal machinery ki quantity aur arrangement alag ho sakti hai. Yeh internal factors genetic predisposition, yaani inherited traits se judi tendencies, aur plant ki growth par depend karte hain.
Screen par: Crop plant se leaf, mesophyll cell aur chloroplast tak connected zoom; side inventory: leaf number, size, age, orientation; mesophyll and chloroplast features; internal CO₂; chlorophyll amount. Genetics aur growth se inventory tak arrows.
Terms: orientation = Leaf kis direction aur angle par positioned hai.; genetic predisposition = Inherited traits ki wajah se plant ki structure aur functioning ki tendencies.
Yaad: 11.9-k9
p019-i2 An understanding of the factors that affect photosynthesis is necessary. The rate of photosynthesis is very important in determining the yield of plants including crop plants. Photosynthesis is under the influence of several factors, both internal (plant) and external. The plant factors include the number, size, age and orientation of leaves, mesophyll cells and chloroplasts, internal CO₂ concentration and the amount of chlorophyll. The plant or internal factors are dependent on the genetic predisposition and the growth of the plant.
samjhana11.10-k2
Ab external conditions jodo: sunlight ki availability, temperature, external CO₂ concentration aur water photosynthesis ko influence karte hain. Yeh factors ek-ek karke duty par nahi aate; photosynthesising leaf par sabka influence simultaneously hota hai. Phir actual rate kaun tay karta hai? Dekho, usually koi ek factor sub-optimal, yaani process ki need ke liye kam anukool, hota hai aur wahi immediate rate ko restrict karta hai. Us immediate restriction ko limiting factor kehte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki sab factors influence karte hain, toh sab equally limiting honge, par asal mein influence hona aur abhi bottleneck hona alag baatein hain.
Screen par: Pehle plant picture ke around sunlight, thermometer, atmospheric CO₂ aur water supply ke four inputs; CO₂ input narrow hota hai aur carbon-fixation output restricted dikhta hai.
Terms: sub-optimal = Process ki need ke liye sabse suitable condition se kam anukool.; limiting factor = Woh factor jo abhi process ki rate ko directly restrict kar raha hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
p019-i3 The external factors would include the availability of sunlight, temperature, CO₂ concentration and water. As a plant photosynthesises, all these factors will simultaneously affect its rate. Hence, though several factors interact and simultaneously affect photosynthesis or CO₂ fixation, usually one factor is the major cause or is the one that limits the rate. Hence, at any point the rate will be determined by the factor available at sub-optimal levels.
samjhana11.10-k3
Blackman ne 1905 mein isi relation ko Law of Limiting Factors ke roop mein express kiya. Kai factors se affected chemical process ki rate us factor se determine hoti hai jo apni minimal value ke nearest hai. Minimal value yahaan process ki requirement ke relative sabse restrictive level hai. Kya temperature ke degrees aur CO₂ ke percentage mein jo number chhota ho, use limiting kahenge? Dekho, different units ke raw numbers compare nahi karte; har factor ko process ki apni requirement ke mukaable assess karte hain. Limiting factor ko favourable direction mein badalne se rate directly respond karti hai, jabki already adequate factor badhana us shortage ko replace nahi karta.
Screen par: Blackman — 1905 — Law of Limiting Factors; four separate requirement gauges mein ek restrictive gauge highlighted. Us gauge ko improve karne par photosynthesis-rate indicator rises.
Terms: Law of Limiting Factors = Kai influencing factors mein jo abhi sabse restrictive hai, wahi process ki rate determine karta hai.; minimal value = Process ki requirement ke relative factor ka sabse restrictive level.
Kitaab se bahar: Different units ke raw numbers compare na karne ka clarification.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
p019-i4 When several factors affect any [bio] chemical process, Blackman’s (1905) Law of Limiting Factors comes into effect. This states the following: If a chemical process is affected by more than one factor, then its rate will be determined by the factor which is nearest to its minimal value: it is the factor which directly affects the process if its quantity is changed.
analogy11.10-k4
Socho, toy-car factory ko har car ke liye four wheels aur one body chahiye. Ek ghante mein forty wheels aur sirf six bodies milti hain, toh maximum six cars assemble hongi. Ab wheels forty se eighty kar do; kya twelve cars ban jaayengi? Dekho, six bodies abhi bhi sirf six cars allow karti hain, isliye extra wheels bottleneck nahi hataate. Bodies six se ten karo, toh forty wheels ke saath ten cars ban sakti hain; ab wheels agla constraint ban sakte hain. Yahaan factory output photosynthesis ki rate hai, parts ki supplies environmental requirements hain, aur body shortage current limiting factor hai.
Screen par: Assembly table: 40 wheels + 6 bodies → 6 toy cars; then 80 wheels + 6 bodies → 6 cars; finally 40 wheels + 10 bodies → 10 cars.
Kitaab se bahar: Numerical toy-car factory analogy for requirement-relative limitation and shifting bottlenecks.
Kya kis jaisa: Factory output photosynthetic rate ko, parts supplies influencing factors ko, aur insufficient bodies immediate limiting factor ko map karti hain; shortage relieve hone par constraint shift ho sakta hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Photosynthesis ke environmental factors toy parts ki tarah fixed stoichiometric supplies nahi hain; temperature reaction conditions ko influence karta hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
example11.10-k5
Ab ek green leaf lo, jise optimal light aur CO₂ mil rahe hain, lekin temperature bahut low hai. Enzymatic reactions ko hum enzyme-assisted chemical changes pehchaan chuke hain; bahut low temperature unki activity strongly slow kar sakta hai. Isliye adequate light aur CO₂ ke bawajood leaf ki photosynthesis itni restricted ho sakti hai ki practically chalti na dikhe. Is setup mein kya change karna useful hoga? Dekho, light aur CO₂ fixed rakhkar temperature ko optimal temperature tak laane se restriction relieve hogi aur leaf photosynthesis shuru kar sakti hai. Optimal temperature woh temperature hai jis par us plant ki photosynthesis ke liye conditions sabse favourable hoti hain.
Screen par: Same realistic green leaf in controlled chamber: light and CO₂ controls fixed at suitable levels. Thermometer very low se optimal range tak moves; photosynthesis indicator low se active hota hai.
Terms: optimal temperature = Woh temperature jis par us plant ki photosynthesis sabse favourably chalti hai.
Kitaab se bahar: Very low temperature se enzyme-mediated reactions slow hone ka brief mechanistic explanation.
Yaad: 11.3-k6
Exercise ke liye: p022-i7
p019-i5 For example, despite the presence of a green leaf and optimal light and CO₂ conditions, the plant may not photosynthesise if the temperature is very low. This leaf, if given the optimal temperature, will start photosynthesising.
ruko, socho11.10-k6
Socho, phir aage badhenge: isi cold leaf ko extra lamp dein, lekin temperature bahut low hi rakhein, toh kya main restriction hat jaayegi? Apna answer leaf ke green hone se nahi, uske current limiting factor se jodo. Dekho, light pehle hi adequate hai aur restriction low temperature ki hai, isliye extra light temperature wali limitation ko remove nahi karegi. Temperature favourable karne ke baad kya wahi factor hamesha limiting rahega? Dekho, pehli restriction hatne par CO₂ ya koi doosra factor next bottleneck ban sakta hai, isliye limiting factor conditions ke saath badal sakta hai.
Screen par: Controlled leaf chamber mein extra lamp turns on while thermometer remains low; rate unchanged. Phir temperature improves aur rate rises, with another input becoming the narrowest constraint.
Jawab: Adequate light badhane se low-temperature restriction nahi hategi; temperature improve karne par rate respond kar sakti hai aur phir doosra factor limiting ho sakta hai.
Aam galti: Low photosynthetic rate ka matlab automatically light shortage nahi; limiting factor ko controlled change ke response se identify karte hain.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
asli duniya11.10-k7
Thandi subah khet mein bright sunshine ho, phir bhi crop ki carbon assimilation restricted reh sakti hai. Dhoop dikhna high photosynthetic rate ki guarantee kyun nahi hai? Dekho, leaf ka temperature unfavourably low ho sakta hai, isliye plenty of light ke saath bhi biochemical work slow reh sakta hai. Field performance samajhne ke liye visible sunshine ke saath plant ki internal capacity aur baaki conditions bhi dekhni padti hain.
Screen par: Bright cold morning mein crop field; sunlit leaf ke saath low leaf-temperature reading aur restrained carbon-assimilation indicator.
Kitaab se bahar: Cold, sunny crop-field conditions mein limiting-factor principle ka practical application.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Ab limiting factor pehchaanne ka rule mil gaya; light ko badalkar dekhenge ki rate kab badhti hai aur kab doosri requirement usse rok deti hai.
11.10.1 Light · 10 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 8 min
Maqsad: Ab limiting-factor rule ko light par lagaakar samjhenge ki roshni badhne se photosynthesis kab badhti hai, kab rukti nahi balki constant rehti hai, aur kab ghat sakti hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.10.1-k1
Ab limiting-factor rule ko light par lagaakar samjhenge ki roshni badhne se photosynthesis kab badhti hai, kab rukti nahi balki constant rehti hai, aur kab ghat sakti hai. Blackman ka rule yaad rakho: current bottleneck badalne par hi rate directly respond karti hai. Light ke teen controls alag hain: light quality uska colour ya wavelength, light intensity unit area par per unit time pahunchne wali light, aur duration of exposure light milne ka total samay hai. Lamp ka colour, brightness aur timer badalne mein kya same condition badalti hai? Dekho, colour quality badalta hai, brightness intensity badalti hai, aur timer exposure duration badalta hai; inhe ek-doosre ka naam nahi denge. Ab intensity response ke teen regions follow karenge: low-light rise, transition, aur saturation ka constant-rate region.
Screen par: Ek leaf ke saamne lamp; alag colour selector, brightness slider aur timer. Saath mein khaali graph axes: Light intensity aur Rate of photosynthesis.
Terms: light quality = Light ka wavelength ya colour composition.; light intensity = Unit area par per unit time pahunchne wali light ki quantity.; duration of exposure = Plant ko light milne ka total samay.
Yaad: 11.10-k3
p019-i7 We need to distinguish between light quality, light intensity and the duration of exposure to light, while discussing light as a factor that affects photosynthesis.
samjhana11.10.1-k2
Low light intensity par incident light badhaane se CO₂ fixation ki rate approximately seedhi line ke pattern mein badhti hai. Isse linear relationship kehte hain; graph mein intensity ke equal increments par rate ke approximately equal increments milte hain. Shuru mein light badhaana effective kyun hai? Dekho, light current limiting supply hai, isliye uski availability badhne se photosynthetic work badh sakta hai. Higher intensity par curve dheere-dheere bend karti hai, kyunki CO₂ supply ya biochemical reactions ki capacity jaise doosre factors limiting hone lagte hain. Aksar log sochte hain ki light badhaate rehne se photosynthesis hamesha badhegi, par asal mein doosre factors limiting hone par extra light rate ko aur nahi badhaati. Limiting factor conditions ke saath badal sakta hai, jaisa hum pehle samjhe the.
Screen par: Wahi leaf aur graph; brightness badhne ke saath pehle rising straight segment, phir sirf bend add ho. Colour aur timer fixed dikhte rahein.
Terms: linear relationship = Aisa sambandh jiska graph approximately seedhi line ho.
Kitaab se bahar: Biochemical capacity ko finite reaction capacity ke roop mein briefly clarify kiya.
Yaad: 11.10-k2
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7
p019-i8 There is a linear relationship between incident light and CO₂ fixation rates at low light intensities. At higher light intensities, gradually the rate does not show further increase as other factors become limiting (Figure 11.10).
analogy11.10.1-k3
Socho, hall mein hundred seats hain, aur pehle sirf twenty interested guests tak invitation pahunchi hai. Invitations forty aur phir sixty guests tak pahunchao, toh occupied seats badh sakti hain. Ab hundred seats bhar gayi hain; two hundred guests ko invite karne se kya occupied seats two hundred ho jaayengi? Dekho, seats ki capacity hundred hai, isliye extra invitations ab occupied seats nahi badhaati. Yahaan invitations ki supply light intensity hai, occupied seats photosynthetic rate hain, aur hall ki seating capacity doosre factors ki imposed limit hai. Pehle invitation shortage bottleneck thi, lekin baad mein seat capacity bottleneck ban gayi.
Screen par: Hall mein 20, 40, 60 aur 100 occupied seats ke successive views; invitations badhti rahein, full hall ki occupied-seat count 100 par rahe.
Kitaab se bahar: Numerical audience analogy for changing limitation.
Kya kis jaisa: Invitations light supply, occupied seats photosynthetic rate, aur seating capacity non-light constraints ko represent karti hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Leaf ki biochemical capacity permanently fixed nahi hoti; CO₂, temperature aur plant condition badalne par saturation rate badal sakti hai.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.10.1-k4
Light saturation woh intensity hai jiske baad aur light dene se photosynthetic rate aur nahi badhti. Is general response ke liye saturation ko full sunlight ke lagbhag 10 per cent par yaad rakho; har species aur condition ka exact threshold identical nahi hota. Full sunlight ko 100 relative units lo, toh 10 per cent ka matlab 10 units hoga, one unit nahi. Nature mein light rarely limiting kyun hoti hai? Dekho, available light aksar saturation requirement se kaafi hoti hai, isliye extra light ke bajay doosri conditions rate restrict kar sakti hain. Shade aur dense forests ke plants important exceptions hain, jahaan light ki supply hi kam pad sakti hai.
Screen par: Full sunlight ka 100-unit bar aur uska 10-unit portion highlighted; side mein exposed leaf aur canopy ke neeche shaded leaf.
Terms: light saturation = Light ka woh level jiske baad extra light photosynthetic rate ko aur nahi badhaati.
Kitaab se bahar: Saturation threshold ki species aur conditions par dependence clarify ki.
Kahan tootti hai: 10 per cent ko 1 per cent na samjhein; rarely limiting ka matlab never limiting nahi.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6
p019-i9 What is interesting to note is that light saturation occurs at 10 per cent of the full sunlight. Hence, except for plants in shade or in dense forests, light is rarely a limiting factor in nature.
figure11.10.1-k5
Figure 11.10 mein horizontal axis Light intensity aur vertical axis Rate of photosynthesis hai; arrowheads increasing directions dikhate hain, aur numerical scale nahi hai. A rising low-light region ko mark karta hai; B woh transition hai jahaan curve bend hokar flatten hone lagti hai. C horizontal plateau region hai; plateau ka matlab intensity badhne par bhi rate constant rehna hai. D tak vertical dashed projection saturation-region ki light intensity ko horizontal axis par locate karti hai. E tak horizontal dashed projection plateau ki photosynthetic rate ko vertical axis par locate karti hai. D aur E mein kya farq hai? Dekho, D light intensity ki reading hai aur E photosynthetic rate ki reading hai, isliye dono alag axes ki quantities hain. D saturation ki intensity dikhata hai, zero gas-exchange balance nahi; is unscaled graph se exact intensity ya rate calculate nahi kar sakte.
Screen par: Wahi graph ab complete: rising region A, bend B, horizontal C; curve se x-axis par dashed projection D aur y-axis par dashed projection E. Dono axis arrowheads aur koi numerical ticks nahi.
Terms: plateau = Graph ka horizontal region jahaan x badhne par y constant rehta hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Actual figure mein A rising region hai, plateau nahi; C plateau hai aur D saturation-region intensity, compensation point nahi.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i6, p022-i7, p022-i8
p019-i11 Graph of rate of photosynthesis against light intensity. Both axes have arrowheads in their positive directions and no numerical scale. The response rises approximately linearly from the origin, bends through a transition near B, and becomes horizontal toward C. A marks the rising region. A dashed horizontal line at E projects the plateau rate to the vertical axis; a dashed vertical line at D projects the saturation-region intensity to the horizontal axis. Thin directional arrows alongside the rising and plateau portions distinguish A, B and C regions.
example11.10.1-k6
Ab ek leaf ke intensity-response ka numerical case lo: relative light 10 se 20 karne par rate 2 se 4 units hoti hai. Yeh rising response batata hai ki is range mein extra light effective thi. Higher range mein relative light 40 se 50 karo, lekin rate 6 se 6 units hi rahe. Kya doosri reading mein photosynthesis band ho gayi? Dekho, rate ab bhi 6 units hai, isliye photosynthesis chal rahi hai; bas extra light uski rate aur nahi badha rahi. Yeh difference rising A aur plateau C ke patterns ko samjhata hai, unke exact numerical values ko nahi.
Screen par: Leaf ke paas alag numerical table: Light 10→20, rate 2→4; Light 40→50, rate 6→6. Printed unscaled graph alag rahe.
Kitaab se bahar: Arbitrary relative-unit worked example; numbers figure ki readings nahi hain.

ruko, socho11.10.1-k7
Socho, phir aage badhenge: plateau par extra light se rate nahi badhti; kya isse prove hota hai ki leaf ko CO₂ ki hi kami hai? Blackman ka rule lagao, lekin missing factor ka naam bina test ke mat chuno. Dekho, plateau light ke alawa restriction ki taraf ishara karta hai, lekin CO₂ supply, temperature ya doosri capacity mein kaunsa factor limiting hai, yeh additional comparison se jaanenge. Aksar constant rate ko zero photosynthesis samajh lete hain, par plateau par positive rate continue rehti hai.

asli duniya11.10.1-k8
Dense forest mein upper canopy bahut si incoming light intercept kar leti hai, isliye neeche ki leaves tak kam light pahunchti hai. Yahaan healthy leaf mein pigments hone ke bawajood light limiting ho sakti hai. Canopy mein gap se zyada light aaye, toh kya rate badh sakti hai? Dekho, agar light current bottleneck thi aur baaki conditions adequate hain, toh increased illumination photosynthesis badha sakti hai.
Screen par: Dense forest canopy se attenuated light forest-floor leaf tak; canopy gap se usi leaf par zyada light pahunchti dikhe.
Kitaab se bahar: Forest canopy interception ko shade limitation ke real mechanism se joda.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.10.1-k9
Ab saturation se alag excessive-light damage samjho: incident light bahut zyada hone par chlorophyll breakdown aur photosynthesis mein decrease ho sakta hai. Accessory pigments ki photo-oxidation se protection wali role hum pehle samajh chuke hain, lekin protection unlimited nahi hoti. Saturation aur damage ko alag kyun rakhenge? Dekho, saturation mein rate constant rehti hai, jabki damaging excess mein light-capturing machinery ko nuksaan pahunchne se rate ghat sakti hai. Figure 11.10 ka printed curve plateau tak hai; excessive-light decline ko uske explanation se alag response ke roop mein samjho.
Screen par: Printed plateau graph unchanged rahe; alag leaf close-up mein excessive illumination, chlorophyll loss aur photosynthetic-rate indicator ka decline.
Yaad: 11.4-k12
Kahan tootti hai: Plateau ko chlorophyll damage ka evidence na maanein; printed graph mein downturn nahi hai.
p020-i1 incident light beyond a point causes the breakdown of chlorophyll and a decrease in photosynthesis.
poori kahani dobara11.10.1-k10
Poora response jodo: quality aur duration se intensity alag rakhi, phir low light par rising fixation, bend aur saturation plateau follow kiya. A rise, B transition aur C plateau hain; D intensity projection aur E plateau-rate projection hai. Light saturate hone par photosynthesis kyun continue rehti hai? Dekho, available light ongoing work support karti hai, lekin doosri restriction extra light se rate badhne nahi deti. Shade mein light kam pad sakti hai, jabki excessive illumination chlorophyll ko damage karke rate ghata sakti hai.
Screen par: Teen quiet panels: rising response, positive plateau, aur separate excessive-light damage; central graph par A–E apni correct positions par.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Ab light adequate hone par carbon supply bottleneck ban sakti hai; CO₂ concentration mein dekhenge ki extra carbon dioxide kab photosynthesis badhaati hai.
11.10.2 Carbon dioxide Concentration · 6 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 5 min
Maqsad: Ab light ki limitation se aage badhkar samjhenge ki extra CO₂ kab photosynthesis badhaati hai aur C₃–C₄ plants ka response kyun alag hota hai.
samjhana11.10.2-k1
Ab light ki limitation se aage badhkar samjhenge ki extra CO₂ kab photosynthesis badhaati hai aur C₃–C₄ plants ka response kyun alag hota hai. Pehle dekha tha ki light plateau par carbon supply bhi bottleneck ho sakti hai; CO₂ photosynthesis ka ek major limiting factor hai. Concentration ka matlab total hawa mein CO₂ ka kitna hissa hai. Reference atmospheric range 0.03–0.04 per cent lo; yeh historical range hai, aaj ki measured concentration ka claim nahi. CO₂ concentration lagbhag 0.05 per cent tak badhaane se fixation ki rate badh sakti hai. Kya isse zyada CO₂ hamesha aur fayda degi? Dekho, higher concentrations ka prolonged exposure damaging ho sakta hai, isliye enrichment ko unlimited benefit ya har crop ke liye fixed prescription nahi samjhenge.
Screen par: Leaf tak pahunchti CO₂ aur carbon-fixation arrow; concentration cards: historical atmospheric reference 0.03–0.04%, stimulation up to 0.05%, prolonged higher exposure se possible damage.
Terms: concentration = Total hawa mein CO₂ ka proportion ya hissa.
Kitaab se bahar: Atmospheric range ko historical reference aur enrichment values ko condition-dependent samajhne ki clarification.
Yaad: 11.10.1-k7
Exercise ke liye: p022-i7
p020-i3 Carbon dioxide is the major limiting factor for photosynthesis. The concentration of CO₂ is very low in the atmosphere (between 0.03 and 0.04 per cent). Increase in concentration upto 0.05 per cent can cause an increase in CO₂ fixation rates; beyond this the levels can become damaging over longer periods.
analogy11.10.2-k2
Socho, construction site par har ghante ten wall-panels jitni bricks aa rahi hain, lekin available powered equipment sirf four panels assemble kar sakta hai. Bricks double karne se kya eight panels ban jaayenge? Dekho, equipment ki current capacity four panels hi hai, isliye extra material akela production nahi badhaayega. Ab equipment ten panels handle kare, lekin bricks sirf six panels ki hon, toh material supply badhaana useful hoga. Yahaan bricks CO₂ ka carbon material hain, powered equipment ki capacity light-supported work hai, aur completed panels photosynthetic output hain. Blackman ke rule ki tarah, extra supply tab useful hai jab wahi current restriction ko relieve kare.
Screen par: Construction comparison: material for 10 panels plus powered capacity 4 gives 4; powered capacity 10 plus material for 6 gives 6.
Kitaab se bahar: Numerical construction analogy se interacting light aur carbon limitations samjhaayi gayi hain.
Yaad: 11.10-k3
Kya kis jaisa: Bricks CO₂ supply hain, powered assembly light-supported capacity hai, aur panel production photosynthetic rate hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Photosynthesis mein literal panels ya fixed brick-like molecular production ratios nahi hote.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.10.2-k3
C₃ and C₄ plants wahi pehle pehchaane groups hain, jinke initial first stable products respectively three-carbon PGA aur four-carbon OAA hain; C₄ ki local CO₂-concentrating supply bhi hum samajh chuke hain. Low light mein neither group extra CO₂ se rate badhaata hai, kyunki light ki restriction abhi bani rehti hai. High light mein dono groups CO₂ badhne par photosynthetic rate badha sakte hain, lekin unki saturation alag levels par aati hai. CO₂ saturation ka matlab ab aur CO₂ dene par rate ka appreciably na badhna hai. C₄ plants about 360 µlL⁻¹ par saturate karte hain, jabki C₃ plants ka saturation 450 µlL⁻¹ se beyond dikhta hai. µlL⁻¹ ka matlab har litre hawa mein CO₂ ke microlitres hai. C₄ ka extra external CO₂ response pehle flatten kyun hota hai? Dekho, uska concentrating system RuBisCO ko pehle se effective CO₂ supply deta hai, jabki C₃ plants mein reference atmospheric levels par carbon supply zyada restrictive reh sakti hai.
Screen par: Pehle low-light C₃ aur C₄ responses extra CO₂ par nearly flat; phir separate high-light qualitative curves, C₄ plateau near 360 µL L⁻¹ aur C₃ plateau beyond 450 µL L⁻¹.
Terms: C₃ and C₄ plants = Pehle pehchaane plant groups: initial first stable fixation product C₃ mein three-carbon PGA aur C₄ mein four-carbon OAA hota hai.; saturation = Yahaan CO₂ ka woh level jiske baad extra CO₂ se photosynthetic rate appreciably nahi badhti.; µlL⁻¹ = Har litre hawa mein CO₂ ke microlitres.
Kitaab se bahar: Saturation values ko universal sharp thresholds ke bajay stated comparison ke approximate response levels ke roop mein samjhaaya gaya hai.
Yaad: 11.8-k15, 11.9-k8
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p020-i4 The C₃ and C₄ plants respond differently to CO₂ concentrations. At low light conditions neither group responds to high CO₂ conditions. At high light intensities, both C₃ and C₄ plants show increase in the rates of photosynthesis. What is important to note is that the C₄ plants show saturation at about 360 µlL⁻¹ while C₃ responds to increased CO₂ concentration and saturation is seen only beyond 450 µlL⁻¹. Thus, current availability of CO₂ levels is limiting to the C₃ plants.
example11.10.2-k4
Ab 0.04 per cent CO₂ ko µlL⁻¹ mein convert karke dekho. Ek litre mein one million microlitres hote hain, aur 0.04 per cent ka fraction 0.0004 hai. One million ko 0.0004 se multiply karne par 400 µlL⁻¹ milta hai. High-light comparison mein 400 ko dono saturation levels se kaise jodoge? Dekho, 400 C₄ ke about 360 se upar hai, lekin C₃ ke beyond-450 saturation level se neeche hai, isliye C₃ mein further CO₂ response ki gunjaaish samajh aati hai. Yeh approximate response comparison hai; plant aur growing conditions ke saath exact saturation badal sakti hai.
Screen par: 0.04/100 × 1,000,000 µL per litre = 400 µL L⁻¹; number line par 360, 400, 450 aur C₃ saturation ka beyond-450 region.
Kitaab se bahar: Percentage se volume concentration ka worked conversion aur saturation ki condition-dependence.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
ruko, socho11.10.2-k5
Socho, phir aage badhenge: low-light tomato greenhouse mein sirf CO₂ badha di, toh photosynthetic rate zaroor badhegi? Apne answer mein extra carbon ki quantity ke saath current limiting factor bhi pehchaano. Dekho, low light abhi energy-side restriction hai, isliye extra CO₂ akeli rate nahi badhaayegi; pehle light limitation relieve honi chahiye. Light adequate hone par bhi kya CO₂ enrichment har baar useful hogi? Dekho, enrichment tab useful hogi jab CO₂ restrictive ho aur temperature jaise doosre factors us benefit ko block na kar rahe hon.
Screen par: Tomato greenhouse: low-light control fixed, CO₂ dial badhta hai, rate indicator unchanged; adequate-light case mein carbon-limited rate badhti hai.
Yaad: 11.10-k3
Jawab: Low light limiting ho toh extra CO₂ rate nahi badhaati; adequate light aur doosri suitable conditions mein carbon limitation relieve hone se rate badh sakti hai.
Aam galti: CO₂ raw material hai, lekin zyada raw material light se milne wali energy ki kami replace nahi karta.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i7
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
asli duniya11.10.2-k6
Isi response ka use tomatoes aur bell pepper jaise C₃ greenhouse crops mein hota hai. Carbon dioxide enriched atmosphere woh hawa hai jisme CO₂ ka proportion badhaaya gaya ho. Suitable light aur doosri growing conditions mein enrichment photosynthesis badhaakar greater productivity aur higher yields support kar sakti hai. Harvest ka carbon extra CO₂ se kaise judta hai? Dekho, increased carbon fixation plant ko zyada organic material banaane deti hai, aur suitable crop management mein iska benefit useful harvested upaj tak pahunch sakta hai. Yield wahi useful crop harvest hai, jise hum productivity ki rate se alag pehchaan chuke hain.
Screen par: Tomato aur bell-pepper greenhouse mein regulated CO₂ supply; leaf fixation se growing crop aur harvested fruits tak connected carbon-flow arrows.
Terms: carbon dioxide enriched atmosphere = Aisi hawa jisme CO₂ ki concentration badhaayi gayi ho.
Yaad: 11.9-k9
p020-i5 The fact that C₃ plants respond to higher CO₂ concentration by showing increased rates of photosynthesis leading to higher productivity has been used for some greenhouse crops such as tomatoes and bell pepper. They are allowed to grow in carbon dioxide enriched atmosphere that leads to higher yields.
→ CO₂ supply badhne ka fayda functioning reactions par depend karta hai; ab temperature mein dekhenge ki garmi ya thand un reactions ki capacity kaise badalti hai.
11.10.3 Temperature · 5 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 4 min
Maqsad: Ab CO₂ supply se aage badhkar samjhenge ki temperature reactions ki capacity kaise badalta hai, aur alag plants ka best-working temperature kyun alag hota hai.
samjhana11.10.3-k1
Ab CO₂ supply se aage badhkar samjhenge ki temperature reactions ki capacity kaise badalta hai, aur alag plants ka best-working temperature kyun alag hota hai. Enzymatic ka meaning enzyme-assisted hai, jise hum stromal sugar-making reactions mein pehle samajh chuke hain. Temperature badalne se enzymes ki reaction karane ki activity badalti hai, isliye dark reactions strongly temperature-controlled hoti hain. Kya adequate light aur CO₂ is temperature effect ko hata denge? Dekho, supplies available hone par bhi enzymes ki working capacity restrictive ho sakti hai, isliye unfavourable temperature carbon assimilation ko limit kar sakta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki light reactions temperature-independent hain, par asal mein woh bhi temperature-sensitive hain; unka response carbon reactions ke mukaable much lesser extent ka hota hai.
Screen par: Chloroplast cutaway mein stromal carbon reactions aur thylakoid light reactions ke saath ek thermometer; temperature change par stromal reaction-speed indicator mein bada aur light-reaction indicator mein chhota badlav.
Yaad: 11.3-k6, 11.10-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i7
p020-i7 The dark reactions being enzymatic are temperature controlled. Though the light reactions are also temperature sensitive they are affected to a much lesser extent.
example11.10.3-k2
Ab comparable light aur CO₂ conditions mein ek C₃ aur ek C₄ leaf ka temperature response compare karo. Temperature optimum wahi best-working temperature hai, jise hum optimal temperature ke naam se pehle pehchaan chuke hain. Temperature ko horizontal aur photosynthetic rate ko vertical axis par rakho; C₃ ka peak cooler side aur C₄ ka peak warmer side par aata hai. C₄ plants comparatively higher temperatures par higher photosynthetic rates maintain kar sakte hain, jabki C₃ plants ka optimum lower hota hai. C₄ ki warmer performance mein carbon delivery ka kya contribution hai? Dekho, RuBisCO ke paas concentrated CO₂ photorespiratory loss suppress karti hai, isliye warmer conditions mein productive carbon assimilation ko support milta hai. Pehle bhari comparison table ke typical ranges C₃ ke liye 20–25°C aur C₄ ke liye 30–40°C the; har individual plant ka exact optimum identical nahi hota.
Screen par: Same light aur CO₂ controls ke neeche qualitative C₃–C₄ temperature-response curves; C₃ peak cooler aur C₄ peak warmer position par, numerical axis ke bina.
Yaad: 11.9-k8, 11.9-k11, 11.10-k5
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p020-i8 The C₄ plants respond to higher temperatures and show higher rate of photosynthesis while C₃ plants have a much lower temperature optimum.
analogy11.10.3-k3
Socho, do musical instruments alag performance conditions ke liye tune kiye gaye hain. Dono same room mein rakhe hon, phir bhi jis condition mein pehla sabse achchha perform kare, doosre ka best performance wahin milna zaroori nahi. Kya ek instrument ka best setting sab par laga denge? Dekho, har instrument ki tuning alag hai, isliye uska best-working range bhi alag ho sakta hai. Yahaan instruments alag plants hain, tuning unki physiological adaptation hai, aur best performance photosynthesis ka temperature optimum hai.
Screen par: Do instruments ke alag best-setting markers se linked do plants ke temperature-response peaks.
Kitaab se bahar: Alag tuning wale instruments ka everyday comparison temperature optima ka difference samjhane ke liye.
Kya kis jaisa: Instruments plants hain, tuning physiological adaptation hai, aur best-performance setting temperature optimum hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Plants manually tune nahi hote; unki temperature response biological adaptation aur functioning se banti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
asli duniya11.10.3-k4
Pathway ke alawa plant ka habitat bhi matter karta hai; habitat woh jagah aur environmental conditions hain jahaan plant rehta hai. Tropical plants garam tropical regions se adapted plants hain. Temperate climates tropical regions ke mukaable generally cooler aur seasonal climates hain. In habitats ke adapted plants ka photosynthetic optimum same kyun nahi hota? Dekho, alag thermal conditions ke liye adaptation ke kaaran tropical plants ka temperature optimum temperate-adapted plants se generally higher hota hai. Isliye warm tropical habitat aur cooler temperate habitat ke plants ko ek hi universal photosynthetic temperature optimum nahi de sakte. Tropical hona aur C₄ hona same classification nahi hai; habitat aur carbon-fixation pathway ko separate comparisons rakho.
Screen par: Warm tropical habitat aur cooler temperate habitat ke plants ke saath separate qualitative optimum markers; habitat comparison pathway comparison se alag panel mein.
Terms: habitat = Plant ke rehne ki jagah aur wahaan ki environmental conditions.; Tropical = Earth ke garam tropical regions se sambandhit.; temperate climates = Tropical regions se generally cooler, seasonal climates.
Kitaab se bahar: Habitat category ko C₃–C₄ pathway category ke barabar na maanne ki clarification.
p020-i9 The temperature optimum for photosynthesis of different plants also depends on the habitat that they are adapted to. Tropical plants have a higher temperature optimum than the plants adapted to temperate climates.
ruko, socho11.10.3-k5
Socho, phir aage badhenge: light aur CO₂ adequate hain, toh kya temperature badhaate rehne se har plant ki photosynthesis badhti rahegi? Apne answer mein optimum aur plants ke beech ka difference dono jodo. Dekho, warming tab favourable ho sakti hai jab plant apne optimum ki taraf jaa raha ho, lekin optimum se aage jaana further improvement ki guarantee nahi deta. Kya tropical plant ka optimum dekhkar use C₄ declare kar sakte hain? Dekho, habitat adaptation bhi optimum influence karti hai, isliye temperature response akela carbon-fixation pathway identify nahi karta.
Screen par: Temperature-response curve par cooler position se optimum tak marker aage badhe, phir peak ke beyond lower-rate position; separate habitat aur pathway labels.
Kitaab se bahar: Optimum ke beyond temperature increase ko unlimited benefit na maanne ki response-curve clarification.
Yaad: 11.10-k3
Jawab: Temperature increase optimum ki taraf favourable ho sakta hai, lekin beyond-optimum warming unlimited rate increase nahi deti; optimum pathway aur habitat adaptation dono se influenced hai.
Aam galti: Higher C₄ optimum ka matlab unlimited heat tolerance nahi; tropical origin akela C₄ identity establish nahi karta.
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Temperature reactions ki capacity badalta hai; ab water mein dekhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise restrict karti hai.
11.10.4 Water · 6 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 4 min
Maqsad: Ab temperature se aage badhkar samjhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise ghataati hai.
pehle poori jhalak11.10.4-k1
Ab temperature se aage badhkar samjhenge ki paani ki kami leaf ki condition aur CO₂ entry badalkar photosynthesis ko kaise ghataati hai. Water ko hum light reaction ka reactant aur leaf ke pores ko gas exchange ke raaste pehchaan chuke hain. Water stress ka matlab plant ki zaroorat ke mukaable paani ki availability kam padna hai. Iske do effects follow karenge: stomatal closure se carbon supply ghatna, aur wilting se leaf ki functioning ghatna. Kya problem sirf water splitting ke liye reactant kam hone ki hai? Aksar log sochte hain ki paani ki kami sirf water splitting ka substrate ghataati hai, par asal mein iska major effect plant ki condition ke through indirect hota hai.
Screen par: Healthy aur water-stressed potted plant ka paired view; stressed plant se do abhi khaali branches, CO₂ entry aur leaf functioning.
Terms: water stress = Plant ki zaroorat ke mukaable paani ki availability kam padna.
Yaad: 11.6.1-k4, 0-k1
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
samjhana11.10.4-k2
Stomata wahi leaf ke gas-exchange pores hain; opening ke guard cells unhe regulate karte hain, jaisa hum shuru mein dekh chuke hain. Water stress mein stomata close hone se water vapour ka loss kam hota hai, lekin CO₂ ki entry bhi restrict hoti hai. Paani bachaane se photosynthesis kyun ghategi? Dekho, closed pores ke through kam CO₂ available hoti hai, isliye carbon fixation restrict hoti hai. Doosri branch mein leaves wilt karti hain, yaani paani ki kami se dheeli padkar jhuk jaati hain. Cells ke andar water pressure leaf ko firm rakhta hai; yeh support ghatne par leaf droop kar sakti hai. Wilting se light ko effectively exposed leaf area ghat sakta hai, aur metabolic activity, yaani cells ki chemical reactions ki working, bhi kam hoti hai. Isliye water reactant hone ke bawajood uska major limiting effect stomatal CO₂ supply aur leaf functioning ke through samjhenge.
Screen par: Ussi stressed plant par pehli branch: closed stomatal pore → kam incoming CO₂ → reduced fixation; doosri branch: drooping leaf → reduced exposed area aur metabolic activity.
Terms: stomata = Pehle pehchaane stomatal pores ka plural naam; leaf ke gas-exchange raaste.; wilt = Paani ki kami se leaf ka dheela padkar jhukna.; metabolic activity = Cells mein chalne wali chemical reactions ki working.
Kitaab se bahar: Cell water pressure se firmness aur wilting ka sambandh; reduced surface area ko reduced effectively exposed area ke roop mein clarify kiya.
Yaad: 0-k1
Exercise ke liye: p022-i7
p020-i11 Even though water is one of the reactants in the light reaction, the effect of water as a factor is more through its effect on the plant, rather than directly on photosynthesis. Water stress causes the stomata to close hence reducing the CO₂ availability. Besides, water stress also makes leaves wilt, thus, reducing the surface area of the leaves and their metabolic activity as well.
analogy11.10.4-k3
Socho, ek workshop ke gate se raw material andar aata hai, lekin khule gate se andar ki thandi hawa bhi bahar nikalti hai. Cooling bachaane ke liye gate band karte ho, toh raw material ki delivery bhi restrict ho jaati hai. Ek protective action production ko kyun ghata sakta hai? Dekho, gate dono movements ka shared raasta hai, isliye ek loss rokne par useful incoming supply bhi kam ho jaati hai. Yahaan gate stomatal pore hai, cooling ka loss water-vapour loss hai, aur incoming raw material CO₂ hai. Stomatal closure paani bachaati hai, lekin carbon supply ka trade-off bhi laati hai.
Screen par: Workshop gate par inward raw-material arrow aur outward cooling-loss arrow; saath mein stomatal pore par inward CO₂ aur outward water-vapour arrows.
Kitaab se bahar: Shared workshop gate ki analogy.
Kya kis jaisa: Gate stomatal pore hai; cooling loss water-vapour loss aur incoming material CO₂ hai. Gate closure loss ke saath useful entry bhi restrict karti hai.
Kahan tootti hai: Stomata literal gates nahi hain; analogy sirf shared exchange route aur closure ke trade-off ko samjhati hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
example11.10.4-k4
Ab balcony ke water-stressed tomato plant ko lo: sunlight available hai, lekin leaves droop kar rahi hain. Pehli branch mein stomatal closure CO₂ entry ghataati hai; doosri mein wilting effective light exposure aur metabolic functioning ko impair karti hai. Sirf extra lamp lagaane se kya dono problems solve hongi? Dekho, light badhaana closed pores ki carbon restriction ya water-stressed leaf ki impaired functioning ko directly theek nahi karta. Limiting-factor rule ke mutaabik current restriction ko relieve karna zaroori hai, na ki already available supply ko aur badhaana.
Screen par: Balcony tomato plant par adequate sunlight; dono causal branches visible, extra lamp ke bawajood closed pore aur drooping leaf unchanged.
Kitaab se bahar: Tomato plant ka worked application.
Yaad: 11.10-k3
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
asli duniya11.10.4-k5
Khet mein dry spell ke dauraan crop ki carbon gain visible wilting se pehle bhi ghat sakti hai. Leaf abhi seedhi dikhe, toh kya water shortage ka photosynthesis par effect nahi hua hoga? Dekho, stomatal closure visible wilting se pehle CO₂ entry restrict kar sakti hai, isliye sirf drooping dekhna water stress ke impact ka poora measure nahi hai.
Screen par: Dry field ki abhi upright crop leaf ka close-up; narrowed stomatal pore aur reduced incoming CO₂, paas mein baad ki wilting wali leaf.
Kitaab se bahar: Stomatal restriction visible wilting se pehle bhi carbon gain ghata sakti hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobara11.10.4-k6
Ab dono branches ek saath jodo: water stress stomata close karata hai, jisse CO₂ availability aur carbon fixation ghat sakti hai. Saath hi wilting leaf ka effective exposed area aur metabolic activity ghataakar photosynthetic functioning impair karti hai. Water ka direct reactant role apni jagah hai, lekin factor ke roop mein uska major effect poore plant ki condition ke through aata hai.
Screen par: Ussi potted plant ke completed two-branch diagram ko saaf rakho: CO₂ restriction aur wilting-related functional decline, dono se lower photosynthesis.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
→ Ab environmental limits bhi jud gayi hain; recap mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko poore photosynthetic system ki ek tasveer mein jodenge.
recap Poore chapter ki tasveer (summary) · 11 gyan-bindu · lagbhag 9 min
Maqsad: Ab poore photosynthetic system mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko jodkar dekhenge ki plant food kaise banata hai.
pehle poori jhalakrecap-k1
Ab poore photosynthetic system mein light ki energy, water ke electrons aur CO₂ ke carbon ko jodkar dekhenge ki plant food kaise banata hai. Is connected picture mein chaar dhaage follow karenge: energy, electrons, protons aur carbon. Atmospheric CO₂ stomata se leaf mein aati hai, aur uska carbon photosynthesis se carbohydrates, jaise glucose aur starch, ke account mein pahunchta hai. Carbon aur energy ke sources alag kyun rakhenge? Dekho, CO₂ carbon material deti hai aur sunlight energy deti hai; light khud food ke carbon atoms nahi banati.
Screen par: Sunlit leaf ka realistic close-up; stomatal pore se ek marked CO₂ carbon andar aata hai, aur sunlight ka separate energy arrow leaf par padta hai.
Yaad: 0-k17, 11.2-k14, 11.10.4-k2
p021-i2 Green plants make their own food by photosynthesis. During this process carbon dioxide from the atmosphere is taken in by leaves through stomata and used for making carbohydrates, principally glucose and starch.
saarrecap-k2
Ab isi leaf se mesophyll cell aur uske numerous chloroplasts tak zoom karke dono working regions jodo. Photosynthesis mainly leaves mein, aur doosre chlorophyll-containing green parts mein bhi hoti hai. Chloroplast ki thylakoid membranes light reactions sambhaalti hain, jabki stroma biosynthetic carbon-fixing reactions ka working region hai. Carbon fixing reactions wahi reactions hain jo CO₂ ke carbon ko organic compounds mein incorporate karti hain. Dono stages alag hote hue independent kyun nahi hain? Dekho, membrane reactions ATP aur NADPH supply karti hain, aur stromal reactions in supplies ko carbon assimilation mein use karti hain; dark naam darkness ki requirement nahi batata.
Screen par: Ussi leaf se mesophyll aur realistic chloroplast cutaway tak zoom; internal membranes aur surrounding stroma ke do clear working regions, unke beech ATP–NADPH supply arrows.
Yaad: 11.3-k2, 11.3-k5, 11.3-k6, 11.7-k4
p021-i3 Photosynthesis takes place only in the green parts of the plants, mainly the leaves. Within the leaves, the mesophyll cells have a large number of chloroplasts that are responsible for CO₂ fixation. Within the chloroplasts, the membranes are sites for the light reaction, while the chemosynthetic pathway [biosynthetic carbon-fixing pathway] occurs in the stroma.
p021-i4 Photosynthesis has two stages: the light reaction and the carbon fixing reactions.
saarrecap-k3
Ab membrane ke photosystem mein antenna pigments se absorbed light ki excitation energy reaction-centre chlorophyll a tak pahunchti hai. Reaction centre chlorophylls wahi special chlorophyll a sites hain jo excitation ko electron donation se jodti hain. PS I ka centre P700, 700 nm par, aur PS II ka centre P680, 680 nm red light par characteristic absorption dikhata hai. Kya antenna se centre tak energy transfer aur centre se acceptor tak electron transfer same event hain? Dekho, antenna excitation energy funnel karti hai, jabki reaction centre electron donate karta hai; collection aur photochemical conversion complementary jobs hain. I aur II discovery ke names hain, electron flow ka order nahi.
Screen par: Ussi chloroplast ki thylakoid membrane par photosystem enlarge ho; antenna se energy flashes centre tak converge hon, phir P680–PS II aur P700–PS I ki wavelength pairings dikhein.
Yaad: 11.5-k3, 11.5-k5, 11.5-k7
Exercise ke liye: p022-i2
p021-i5 In the light reaction the light energy is absorbed by the pigments present in the antenna, and funnelled to special chlorophyll a molecules called reaction centre chlorophylls.
p021-i6 There are two photosystems, PS I and PS II. PS I has a 700 nm absorbing chlorophyll a P700 molecule at its reaction centre, while PS II has a P680 reaction centre that absorbs red light at 680 nm.
saarrecap-k4
Non-cyclic route mein light PS II ka electron excite karti hai; acceptor aur transport chain use PS I tak pahunchate hain. PS I par doosra light boost milta hai, phir acceptor pathway electrons NADP⁺ reduction tak laata hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki yahaan NAD se NADH banta hai, par asal mein photosynthetic route NADP⁺ ko reduce karke NADPH banata hai. Ek NADPH ke liye two electrons aur one stromal H⁺ chahiye. Electron chain mein kharch kyun nahi ho jaata? Dekho, carriers electrons aage transfer karte hain, destroy nahi karte; unki energy ka conversion aur electrons ka matter ledger alag sambhalte hain.
Screen par: Ussi membrane par sirf electron route highlight ho: PS II → carriers → PS I → NADP⁺; two light boosts aur NADP⁺ + 2e⁻ + H⁺ → NADPH ka small ledger.
Yaad: 11.6-k5, 11.6-k10
p021-i7 After absorbing light, electrons are excited and transferred through PS II and PS I and finally to NAD [NADP⁺] forming NADH [NADPH].
saarrecap-k5
Electron transport ke saath lumen mein high H⁺ aur stroma mein relatively low H⁺ ka gradient banta hai. Water splitting lumen mein H⁺ add karti hai, carrier transport stromal H⁺ andar laata hai, aur NADP⁺ reduction stromal H⁺ use karti hai. Ab ATP ke liye energy kahaan se milegi? Dekho, pehle samjha controlled proton return yaad karo: gradient ki energy ATP-making machinery ko chalati hai. F₀ isi membrane-embedded proton-channel part ka short label hai, jise chloroplast mein CF₀ ke naam se pehchaana tha. Gradient breakdown mein difference kam hota hai, protons destroy nahi hote.
Screen par: Ussi thylakoid cutaway par electron route quiet rahe; lumen H⁺ pool, stromal H⁺ depletion aur CF₀ se outward return highlight ho, stromal CF₁ ke paas ADP + Pi → ATP.
Terms: F₀ = Pehle pehchaane CF₀ proton-channel part ka short label; ATP synthase ka membrane-embedded hissa.
Yaad: 11.6.3-k2, 11.6.3-k9, 11.6.3-k10
Exercise ke liye: p022-i11
p021-i8 During this process a proton gradient is created across the membrane of the thylakoid. The breakdown of the protons [proton] gradient due to movement through the F₀ part of the ATPase enzyme releases enough energy for synthesis of ATP.
examplerecap-k6
Ab do water molecules ka batch lekar electron supply aur oxygen output ko isi picture mein fit karo: 2H₂O → 4H⁺ + O₂ + 4e⁻. PS II-associated lumen-facing water splitting four replacement electrons, four lumen H⁺ aur one oxygen molecule deti hai. Yeh four electrons aage two NADP⁺ ko two NADPH banane ki supply de sakte hain. Released O₂ ka source CO₂ kyun nahi hai? Dekho, water oxidation se oxygen milti hai, aur isotope evidence bhi uska water-source establish karta hai; CO₂ ka main tracked contribution carbon assimilation hai. O₂ baahar diffuse ho sakti hai, jabki water ke electrons reducing-power pathway aur H⁺ gradient-building se judte hain.
Screen par: Ussi membrane ke PS II par two water models; four electrons electron route mein, four H⁺ lumen mein aur one O₂ outward diffusion ki taraf; NADPH endpoint par two-carrier tally.
Yaad: 11.6.1-k4, 11.6.1-k6, 11.6-k5
p021-i9 Splitting of water molecules is associated with PS II resulting in the release of O₂, protons and transfer of electrons to PS II.
saarrecap-k7
Ab stromal carbon ledger kholo: RuBisCO five-carbon RuBP ke saath one-carbon CO₂ fix karke two three-carbon PGA molecules banata hai. Reduction ATP aur NADPH use karke triose-phosphate pool banati hai; uska kuch carbon output mein aur baaki regeneration mein jaata hai. Three CO₂ aur three RuBP ka total eighteen-carbon pool six three-carbon intermediates deta hai. Inmein three carbons net output hain, jabki fifteen carbons three RuBP regenerate karte hain; iska bill nine ATP aur six NADPH hai. RuBP regenerate karna zaroori kyun hai? Dekho, next CO₂ ko acceptor chahiye, isliye carbon output ke saath starting pool ki renewal bhi essential hai. Glucose-equivalent ka pehle joda eighteen ATP–twelve NADPH budget yaad rakho; spent ADP aur NADP⁺ supplies renew hone ke liye membrane reactions se dobara judte hain.
Screen par: Ussi chloroplast ke stroma mein carbon route stepwise: 3 RuBP + 3 CO₂ → 6 PGA → triose pool; three-carbon output alag ho, fifteen-carbon return RuBP tak close ho; spent carriers membranes ki taraf.
Yaad: 11.7.2-k3, 11.7.2-k6, 11.7.2-k12
Exercise ke liye: p022-i10
p021-i10 In the carbon fixation cycle, CO₂ is added by the enzyme, RuBisCO, to a 5-carbon compound RuBP that is converted to 2 molecules of 3-carbon PGA. This is then converted to sugar by the Calvin cycle, and the RuBP is regenerated. During this process ATP and NADPH synthesised in the light reaction are utilised.
saarrecap-k8
Ab isi productive route ki competing branch yaad karo: RuBisCO RuBP ki oxygenation bhi catalyse kar sakta hai. Oxygenation wahi O₂ wali alternative reaction hai, jisme five-carbon RuBP one three-carbon PGA aur one two-carbon phosphoglycolate deta hai. Is branch mein naya carbon gain kyun nahi hua? Dekho, O₂ carbon nahi laayi, aur baad ka photorespiratory processing ATP kharch karke CO₂ release karta hai; net sugar, ATP ya NADPH synthesis nahi milti. Photorespiration ordinary ATP-producing respiration nahi hai; productive carbon assimilation ke comparison mein iska carbon aur energy cost hai.
Screen par: Stromal RuBP site par small competing O₂ branch; 5C → 3C + 2C tally, phir downstream ATP expenditure aur CO₂-release arrows.
Yaad: 11.9-k5, 11.9-k6
p021-i11 RuBisCO also catalyses a wasteful oxygenation reaction in C₃ plants: photorespiration.
saarrecap-k9
Kuch tropical plants ka C₄ pathway isi competition ke against carbon-concentrating delivery system deta hai. Mesophyll mein three-carbon PEP initial carbon capture se four-carbon OAA banata hai; C₄ acids bundle sheath tak carbon le jaati hain. Sheath mein four-carbon cargo one CO₂ aur three-carbon remainder deta hai; remainder mesophyll lautkar energy-dependent PEP renewal karta hai. Delivered CO₂ bundle sheath ke Calvin cycle mein carbohydrates ke formation ko feed karti hai. Is delivery ko carbon ka final destination kyun nahi kahenge? Dekho, sheath tak pahunchna sirf supply step hai; delivered carbon ko carbohydrate account mein laane ka kaam Calvin cycle karta hai. Aksar log sochte hain ki C₄ plants Calvin cycle nahi chalaate, par asal mein initial C₄ capture usi cycle ko concentrated CO₂ supply karta hai. High local CO₂ RuBisCO ki oxygenation suppress karke pehle samjha photorespiratory loss ghataati hai.
Screen par: Leaf ke neighbouring mesophyll aur bundle sheath cells ka realistic cutaway; 3C PEP → 4C cargo → 1C CO₂ + 3C return; sheath chloroplast mein Calvin route aur local CO₂ supply highlight ho.
Yaad: 11.8-k10, 11.8-k11, 11.8-k12, 11.8-k15, 11.9-k8
Exercise ke liye: p021-i16, p022-i1, p022-i10
p021-i12 Some tropical plants show a special type of photosynthesis called C₄ pathway. In these plants the first product of CO₂ fixation that takes place in the mesophyll, is a 4-carbon compound. In the bundle sheath cells the Calvin pathway is carried out for the synthesis of carbohydrates.
ruko, sochorecap-k10
Socho, phir poori picture close karenge: chloroplast mein light aur CO₂ available hain, lekin RuBP regeneration ruk gayi, toh kya sugar production continuously chalti rahegi? Kya ATP aur NADPH ki supply missing acceptor ko replace kar degi? Dekho, available RuBP pool use hone ke baad next fixation restrict hogi, kyunki energy aur reducing power carbon acceptor ki renewal ka replacement nahi hain. Isi tarah poore plant mein adequate light akeli insufficient CO₂, unfavourable temperature ya water-stressed leaf ki restriction nahi hataati.
Screen par: Pehle bana stromal loop; regeneration arrow rukne par RuBP pool ghatta dikhe, jabki ATP, NADPH aur CO₂ supply arrows present rahen.
Yaad: 11.7-k2, 11.7.2-k5, 11.10-k3
Jawab: Dekho, regeneration rukne se RuBP acceptor pool replenish nahi hota, isliye remaining RuBP use hone ke baad sustained CO₂ fixation restrict hoti hai; adequate energy supplies acceptor ko replace nahi karti.
Aam galti: ATP aur NADPH available hone ko sufficient na maanein; continuous fixation ke liye RuBP regeneration bhi essential hai.
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
poori kahani dobararecap-k11
Ab end-to-end tasveer jodo: antenna light energy collect karti hai, reaction centres electron transfer shuru karte hain, aur water replacement electrons ke saath O₂ deta hai. Electron flow NADPH supply aur proton-gradient formation se judta hai; H⁺ ka controlled return ATP synthase ko ATP banane ki energy deta hai. Stroma mein CO₂ carbon RuBP se PGA aur carbohydrate intermediates tak jaata hai, jabki carbon ka returning hissa acceptor regenerate karta hai. ATP energy deta hai aur NADPH reducing electrons deta hai; unke spent carrier forms dobara light-reaction supply se jud sakte hain. C₄ delivery RuBisCO ke paas CO₂ badhaakar photorespiratory loss dabaati hai, lekin actual rate plant ki capacity aur current environmental bottleneck par bhi depend karti hai. Poore chapter ka central sambandh kya raha? Dekho, sunlight ki energy water ke electron flow aur proton gradient ke through usable chemical supplies banti hai, aur woh supplies atmospheric carbon ko plant ke organic food mein badalne ka kaam support karti hain.
Screen par: Sunlit whole plant ke saath four uncluttered linked tracks: sunlight → ATP support, water → electrons → NADPH, lumen H⁺ → ATP synthase, atmosphere CO₂ → carbohydrate; returning carriers aur acceptor loops apne respective regions mein.
Yaad: 11.6-k10, 11.6.3-k12, 11.7.2-k13, 11.8-k18, 11.10.4-k6
(NCERT line nahi: jodne wali baat)
